# Parallel-Safe Cloud Run Test Runner Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** Execute this plan task-by-task. Every behavior task
> produces final reviewable code; no task commits an implementation that a later
> task is expected to invalidate.

## User Request

### Requested result
Implement and verify `freshell#sbnj`, the mandatory first child of master `freshell#589e`: replace the unsafe shared Cloud Run test lifecycle with a trustworthy, parallel-safe runner used by the existing Vitest and E2E entrypoints. Finish the branch to the repository's pre-PR standard, then stop so the parent can propose what to do next.

### Explicit constraints
- Use the repository's `the usual` workflow.
- Before creating a worktree, verify the repo-supported baseline on current `origin/main` with the test backend explicitly local; if it is not green, stop and report the failing command and summary.
- Work in a fresh `.worktrees/` worktree and feature branch from `origin/main`; do not implement from the stale `the-usual/cloud-run-jobs` worktree or branch.
- Follow Red-Green-Refactor TDD and provide unit plus integration/end-to-end coverage appropriate to the runner.
- Enforce source provenance before any cloud mutation: clean symbolic branch, configured upstream, and live upstream tip exactly equal to `HEAD`; build from an immutable `git archive HEAD` snapshot.
- Give every invocation unique temporary source, image tag, job name, and exact execution identity; resolve and execute an immutable image digest; use structured command output; never use mutable `latest`, a fixed shared job, a job update, an execution-list/latest fallback, or another invocation's result.
- Require exact expected-part completion and fail closed for missing, partial, unknown, malformed, or unreadable results.
- Clean up only run-owned jobs, image tags, and temporary files on success, failure, and interruption; never delete a potentially shared digest; preserve primary and cleanup failures.
- Keep the new abstraction minimal: one plain shared TypeScript Cloud Run lifecycle with an injected command-execution seam and thin compatibility adapters/facades for Vitest and E2E. Do not add provider-neutral interfaces, plugins, a workflow engine, persistent run history, self-hashing machinery, or a global resource janitor.
- Derive local and cloud Vitest execution from the same client/default, server, and non-visual Electron suite definitions; ensure required Electron source/assets are included in cloud build contexts.
- Resolve cloud versus local routing before the heavy local coordinator gate. Cloud submissions must not acquire or wait on that gate; broad local suites remain coordinated. Invalid non-empty backend values fail.
- Cloud failure must never silently start heavy local work; print the exact equivalent explicit-local command and return nonzero.
- Migrate the existing E2E Cloud Run lifecycle to the safe shared runner while preserving public commands; browser inventory expansion and skip removal remain deferred to `freshell#p9ma`.
- Acceptance evidence must include focused automated provenance/lifecycle/result/cleanup/concurrency/coordinator tests; a complete explicit-local supported check; cloud-default proof that no heavy local Vitest child starts; two simultaneous real-cloud runs from separate clean pushed worktrees with distinct resources and exact self-attribution; owned cleanup proof; and a focused real E2E cloud smoke through the migrated facade.
- Push the feature branch before real-cloud acceptance, and rerun cloud evidence if code changes afterward.
- Preserve all pre-existing user work, including the untracked `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-13-coding-agent-resource-containment.md` in the main checkout.
- Do not restart or deploy the live self-hosted Freshell server.
- Do not create or open a PR without the user's separate explicit approval. Preparing, committing, and pushing the feature branch is allowed.
- Update existing project guidance/documentation when the test workflow or architecture changes, including `AGENTS.md` where its always-loaded instructions would otherwise become stale.
- After `sbnj` is implementation-complete and verified, stop. Do not automatically start `p9ma`, `n799`, `qmyw`, `ncyn`, `a9nh`, `q2xt`, or `k68e`.

### Accepted tradeoffs and residuals
- `sbnj` is intentionally one safety-complete change; landing only immutable tags, only unique jobs, or another partial hardening is unacceptable because false attribution would remain possible.
- “Pushed source” means exact equality with the configured upstream tip, rather than merely being contained by any remote ref.
- The shared typed module is justified only at the Cloud Run lifecycle boundary; the existing shell entrypoints remain compatibility facades.
- Migrating E2E lifecycle safety is included in `sbnj`, while complete browser coverage parity remains in `p9ma`.
- The rest of master `589e`, final acceptance `q2xt`, and session-list task `k68e` remain explicitly out of scope for this run.

**Goal:** Replace both unsafe Cloud Run test paths with one provenance-gated, invocation-owned, digest-pinned lifecycle while preserving explicit local execution and the existing Vitest and E2E command surfaces.

**Architecture:** A single `scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts` module owns Git provenance, immutable source staging, build/image/job/execution identity, structured result validation, and owned cleanup through one injected `RunCommand` function. Data-only Vitest suite definitions feed both the local planner and a thin Vitest adapter; a thin E2E adapter retains Playwright-specific argument and sharding policy, while the current shell scripts become quoted `exec` facades.

**Tech Stack:** Node.js >=22.5.0, TypeScript 5.7 in ESM/NodeNext mode, `tsx`, Vitest 3.2, Zod, POSIX shell, Git, Docker, Google Cloud CLI (Cloud Build, Artifact Registry, Cloud Run Jobs, Cloud Logging), and Playwright.

## Execution Gates

### Gate A — explicit-local baseline before a replacement worktree

**Status: unsatisfied; implementation is forbidden in this worktree.** The
workspace created `the-usual/kata-sbnj` before the required baseline. Its later
baseline was not explicitly local and failed the existing
`FreshAgentView.test.tsx` 429-backoff test (expected two snapshot calls, observed
one; the server child exited 143 and Electron did not run).

The workspace stage must execute this whole handoff from the main checkout. It
fails before mutation if tracked/index work exists; snapshots, archives,
temporarily evacuates, restores, and verifies every pre-existing Git-reported
untracked root; pins and tests the fetched base with explicit-local backends;
rejects base movement; creates a collision-resistant replacement; copies only
this committed plan; and records the replacement identity outside every
worktree. The replacement is not created until restoration and integrity checks
pass.

```bash
set -euo pipefail
MAIN=/home/dan/code/freshell
PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT=/home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/kata-sbnj
PLAN_REL=docs/plans/2026-08-15-kata-sbnj.md
CONTAINMENT_REL=docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-13-coding-agent-resource-containment.md
GATE_RECORD="$MAIN/.worktrees/.the-usual-logs/kata-sbnj/gate-a.env"
GATE_DIR="$(dirname -- "$GATE_RECORD")"

# A tracked-dirty main checkout cannot be a pristine origin/main baseline.
# Stop before fetch/merge rather than stashing, resetting, or rewriting user work.
test "$(git -C "$MAIN" symbolic-ref --quiet --short HEAD)" = main
if [[ -n "$(git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=no)" ]]; then
  printf '%s\n' 'main has tracked/index changes; refusing Gate A before any mutation' >&2
  exit 1
fi
test -e "$MAIN/$CONTAINMENT_REL" || test -L "$MAIN/$CONTAINMENT_REL"
test -z "$(git -C "$PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT" status --porcelain=v1 -- "$PLAN_REL")"
PLAN_SOURCE_SHA="$(git -C "$PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT" rev-parse "HEAD:$PLAN_REL")"
test "$(git -C "$PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT" hash-object "$PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT/$PLAN_REL")" = \
  "$PLAN_SOURCE_SHA"

mkdir -p -- "$GATE_DIR"
PRESERVE_ROOT="$(mktemp -d "$GATE_DIR/gate-a-preserve.XXXXXX")"
test "$(stat -c %d -- "$MAIN")" = "$(stat -c %d -- "$PRESERVE_ROOT")"
PRESERVE_TREE="$PRESERVE_ROOT/tree"
PRESERVE_CONFLICTS="$PRESERVE_ROOT/conflicts"
EXPECTED_STATUS="$PRESERVE_ROOT/expected-status.z"
POST_MERGE_STATUS="$PRESERVE_ROOT/post-merge-status.z"
FINAL_STATUS="$PRESERVE_ROOT/final-status.z"
UNTRACKED_PATHS="$PRESERVE_ROOT/untracked-paths.z"
UNTRACKED_ARCHIVE="$PRESERVE_ROOT/untracked.tar"
mkdir -p -- "$PRESERVE_TREE" "$PRESERVE_CONFLICTS"

git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal >"$EXPECTED_STATUS"
: >"$UNTRACKED_PATHS"
CONTAINMENT_COVERED=0
while IFS= read -r -d '' record; do
  if [[ ${record:0:3} != '?? ' ]]; then
    printf 'unexpected non-untracked Gate A status record: %q\n' "$record" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  rel=${record:3}
  rel=${rel%/}
  if [[ -z $rel || $rel = /* || $rel = .. || $rel = ../* || $rel = */../* ]]; then
    printf 'unsafe Gate A path: %q\n' "$rel" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  printf '%s\0' "$rel" >>"$UNTRACKED_PATHS"
  if [[ $CONTAINMENT_REL = "$rel" || $CONTAINMENT_REL = "$rel/"* ]]; then
    CONTAINMENT_COVERED=1
  fi
done <"$EXPECTED_STATUS"
test "$CONTAINMENT_COVERED" -eq 1

tar --create --file="$UNTRACKED_ARCHIVE" --directory="$MAIN" \
  --sort=name --null --verbatim-files-from --files-from="$UNTRACKED_PATHS"
tar --compare --file="$UNTRACKED_ARCHIVE" --directory="$MAIN"

git -C "$MAIN" fetch origin main
GATE_A_BASE_SHA="$(git -C "$MAIN" rev-parse 'refs/remotes/origin/main^{commit}')"
# Keep untracked paths in place for the ff-only update: Git must reject a base
# that would overwrite one of them. Then require the complete pre-fetch snapshot
# to remain byte-for-byte and archive-for-filesystem identical.
git -C "$MAIN" merge --ff-only "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA"
test "$(git -C "$MAIN" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA"
if [[ -n "$(git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=no)" ]]; then
  printf '%s\n' 'tracked/index state changed while pinning Gate A' >&2
  exit 1
fi
git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal \
  >"$POST_MERGE_STATUS"
cmp -s "$EXPECTED_STATUS" "$POST_MERGE_STATUS"
tar --compare --file="$UNTRACKED_ARCHIVE" --directory="$MAIN"

PRESERVATION_ACTIVE=1
restore_preserved_paths() {
  local rc=0 rel src dst conflict
  while IFS= read -r -d '' rel; do
    src="$PRESERVE_TREE/$rel"
    dst="$MAIN/$rel"
    if [[ -e $src || -L $src ]]; then
      if [[ -e $dst || -L $dst ]]; then
        conflict="$PRESERVE_CONFLICTS/$rel"
        mkdir -p -- "$(dirname -- "$conflict")" || rc=1
        if ! mv -- "$dst" "$conflict"; then
          rc=1
          continue
        fi
        printf 'preserved a colliding post-baseline path at %q\n' "$conflict" >&2
        rc=1
      fi
      mkdir -p -- "$(dirname -- "$dst")" || rc=1
      mv -- "$src" "$dst" || rc=1
    fi
  done <"$UNTRACKED_PATHS"

  git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal \
    >"$FINAL_STATUS" || rc=1
  cmp -s "$EXPECTED_STATUS" "$FINAL_STATUS" || rc=1
  tar --compare --file="$UNTRACKED_ARCHIVE" --directory="$MAIN" || rc=1
  return "$rc"
}

on_gate_a_exit() {
  local primary=$? restore=0
  trap - EXIT INT TERM
  set +e
  if (( PRESERVATION_ACTIVE )); then
    restore_preserved_paths
    restore=$?
  fi
  if (( restore != 0 )); then
    printf 'Gate A preservation failed; retained recovery state at %q\n' \
      "$PRESERVE_ROOT" >&2
  fi
  if (( primary != 0 )); then
    exit "$primary"
  fi
  exit "$restore"
}
trap on_gate_a_exit EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM

while IFS= read -r -d '' rel; do
  src="$MAIN/$rel"
  dst="$PRESERVE_TREE/$rel"
  if [[ ! -e $src && ! -L $src ]]; then
    printf 'untracked path disappeared before preservation: %q\n' "$src" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  mkdir -p -- "$(dirname -- "$dst")"
  mv -- "$src" "$dst"
done <"$UNTRACKED_PATHS"

# The baseline now sees only tracked origin/main content (plus ordinary ignored
# dependencies/caches), and any broad run waits on the shared coordinator.
git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal \
  >"$PRESERVE_ROOT/pre-baseline-status.z"
test ! -s "$PRESERVE_ROOT/pre-baseline-status.z"
FRESHELL_TEST_SUMMARY="sbnj explicit-local origin-main baseline" \
FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=local \
FRESHELL_E2E_BACKEND=local \
env --chdir="$MAIN" npm run check

git -C "$MAIN" fetch origin main
test "$(git -C "$MAIN" rev-parse 'refs/remotes/origin/main^{commit}')" = \
  "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA"
test "$(git -C "$MAIN" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA"
git -C "$MAIN" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal \
  >"$PRESERVE_ROOT/post-baseline-status.z"
test ! -s "$PRESERVE_ROOT/post-baseline-status.z"

RESTORE_RC=0
restore_preserved_paths || RESTORE_RC=$?
PRESERVATION_ACTIVE=0
trap - EXIT INT TERM
if (( RESTORE_RC != 0 )); then
  printf 'Gate A preservation failed; retained recovery state at %q\n' \
    "$PRESERVE_ROOT" >&2
  exit "$RESTORE_RC"
fi
rm -rf -- "$PRESERVE_ROOT"
REPLACEMENT_SLUG="kata-sbnj-impl-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)-$$-$(node -e \
  'process.stdout.write(require("node:crypto").randomBytes(4).toString("hex"))')"
REPLACEMENT_ROOT="$MAIN/.worktrees/$REPLACEMENT_SLUG"
REPLACEMENT_BRANCH="the-usual/$REPLACEMENT_SLUG"
test "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" != /home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/kata-sbnj
test "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH" != the-usual/kata-sbnj
test "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH" != the-usual/cloud-run-jobs
test ! -e "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT"
git -C "$MAIN" worktree add -b "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH" \
  "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA"

install -D -m 0644 "$PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT/$PLAN_REL" "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT/$PLAN_REL"
cmp -s "$PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT/$PLAN_REL" "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT/$PLAN_REL"
git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" add -- "$PLAN_REL"
mapfile -t HANDOFF_PATHS < <(
  git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" diff --cached --name-only
)
test "${#HANDOFF_PATHS[@]}" -eq 1
test "${HANDOFF_PATHS[0]}" = "$PLAN_REL"
git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" commit -m "docs(plan): add sbnj implementation plan"

test -z "$(git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" status --short)"
test "$(git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" symbolic-ref --quiet --short HEAD)" = \
  "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH"
git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" merge-base --is-ancestor "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA" HEAD
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$GATE_RECORD")"
GATE_TMP="$(mktemp "$GATE_RECORD.tmp.XXXXXX")"
chmod 0600 "$GATE_TMP"
printf 'GATE_A_BASE_SHA=%q\nREPLACEMENT_ROOT=%q\nREPLACEMENT_BRANCH=%q\n' \
  "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA" "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH" >"$GATE_TMP"
mv "$GATE_TMP" "$GATE_RECORD"
```

If it is red, report the command and summary and stop. Do not repair that
unrelated failure, reuse this branch/worktree, or use the stale
`the-usual/cloud-run-jobs` branch. Expected RED tests after Gate A are ordinary
TDD evidence and return to their owning task; they are not a new workflow gate.
If `origin/main` moves after the baseline, rerun the baseline against the new
tip; never create from an untested SHA. The plan-only handoff may descend from
the tested base, but must not merge, rebase, cherry-pick, or otherwise import the
stale planning branch's history or source changes.

Every catchable exit restores the complete pre-Gate untracked set before
returning. A restore collision preserves both versions under the printed
owner-only recovery root and fails closed. Recovery state is removed only after
exact status and archive comparison succeeds. SIGKILL, OOM, or host loss cannot
run shell traps; in that case the archive and any already-evacuated roots remain
under the `gate-a-preserve.*` recovery directory for explicit inspection.

### Gate A preservation fixture — must pass before real Gate A

The block is self-contained, creates only `/tmp` repositories, verifies the production-prefix SHA before four exact substitutions, sends INT/TERM to an isolated process group, and never includes the replacement-worktree suffix. The complete block is 185 lines and SHA-256 `0ee39492ee933658ac4dc8efdcbf195141cd1d9c05fab006766bb4b9606a2333` including its final newline.

````bash
timeout --signal=KILL 180s bash <<'GATE_A_FIXTURE'
set -euo pipefail

PLAN=/home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/kata-sbnj/docs/plans/2026-08-15-kata-sbnj.md
EXPECTED_PREFIX_SHA=30fd429e6c32dcf49c9825fd0dc20fd67bd83ca4d0c1114a39dc8d85c14da0bc
FIXTURE_ROOT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/kata-sbnj-gate-a-fixture.XXXXXX)"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$FIXTURE_ROOT"' EXIT

git_quiet() {
  git -C "$1" "${@:2}" >/dev/null 2>&1
}

make_case() {
  local case_name=$1
  local case_root="$FIXTURE_ROOT/$case_name"
  local main="$case_root/main"
  local origin="$case_root/origin.git"
  local plan_source="$case_root/plan-source"
  local gate_record="$main/.worktrees/.the-usual-logs/kata-sbnj/gate-a.env"
  local baseline="$case_root/baseline.sh"
  local gate="$case_root/gate-prefix.sh"
  local expected_status="$case_root/expected-status.z"
  local expected_paths="$case_root/expected-paths.z"
  local expected_archive="$case_root/expected.tar"
  local output="$case_root/gate.out"
  local errors="$case_root/gate.err"
  local newline_name=$'line\nbreak.txt'

  mkdir -p -- "$case_root"
  git -c init.defaultBranch=main init --bare "$origin" >/dev/null
  git init -b main "$main" >/dev/null
  git -C "$main" config user.name 'Gate Fixture'
  git -C "$main" config user.email gate-fixture@example.invalid
  printf '%s\n' '.worktrees/' >"$main/.gitignore"
  printf '%s\n' 'tracked base' >"$main/tracked.txt"
  git_quiet "$main" add .gitignore tracked.txt
  git_quiet "$main" commit -m base
  git_quiet "$main" remote add origin "$origin"
  git_quiet "$main" push -u origin main

  git init -b plan "$plan_source" >/dev/null
  git -C "$plan_source" config user.name 'Gate Fixture'
  git -C "$plan_source" config user.email gate-fixture@example.invalid
  mkdir -p -- "$plan_source/docs/plans"
  cp -- "$PLAN" "$plan_source/docs/plans/2026-08-15-kata-sbnj.md"
  git_quiet "$plan_source" add docs/plans/2026-08-15-kata-sbnj.md
  git_quiet "$plan_source" commit -m plan

  mkdir -p -- "$main/docs/superpowers/plans" "$main/untracked-root/empty-child"
  printf '%s\n' 'leading dash original' >"$main/-o"
  printf '%s\n' 'containment original' \
    >"$main/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-13-coding-agent-resource-containment.md"
  printf '%s\n' 'session original' \
    >"$main/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-16-session-index-event-driven.md"
  printf '%s\n' 'space original' >"$main/space name.txt"
  printf '%s\n' 'newline original' >"$main/$newline_name"
  printf '%s\n' 'nested original' >"$main/untracked-root/nested.txt"
  ln -s nested.txt "$main/untracked-root/link"
  ln -s missing.txt "$main/untracked-root/dangling"

  git -C "$main" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal >"$expected_status"
  : >"$expected_paths"
  while IFS= read -r -d '' record; do
    test "${record:0:3}" = '?? '
    local rel=${record:3}
    rel=${rel%/}
    printf '%s\0' "$rel" >>"$expected_paths"
  done <"$expected_status"
  tar --create --file="$expected_archive" --directory="$main" \
    --sort=name --null --verbatim-files-from --files-from="$expected_paths"

  cat >"$baseline" <<'BASELINE'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
case "$1" in
  success) exit 0 ;;
  failure) exit 23 ;;
  collision)
    printf '%s\n' 'collision version' > ./-o
    exit 0
    ;;
  int)
    trap 'exit 0' INT
    pgid="$(ps -o pgid= -p "$PPID" | tr -d ' ')"
    kill -INT -- "-$pgid"
    sleep 1
    exit 0
    ;;
  term)
    trap 'exit 0' TERM
    pgid="$(ps -o pgid= -p "$PPID" | tr -d ' ')"
    kill -TERM -- "-$pgid"
    sleep 1
    exit 0
    ;;
  *) exit 97 ;;
esac
BASELINE
  chmod 0700 "$baseline"

  python3 - "$PLAN" "$gate" "$main" "$plan_source" "$gate_record" "$baseline" "$case_name" "$EXPECTED_PREFIX_SHA" <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import shlex
import sys

plan_path, output_path, main, plan_source, gate_record, baseline, case_name, expected_sha = sys.argv[1:]
text = Path(plan_path).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
anchor = text.index('### Gate A')
start = text.index('```bash\nset -euo pipefail', anchor) + len('```bash\n')
end = text.index('\nREPLACEMENT_SLUG=', start)
prefix = text[start:end]
assert hashlib.sha256(prefix.encode()).hexdigest() == expected_sha
baseline_block = '''FRESHELL_TEST_SUMMARY="sbnj explicit-local origin-main baseline" \\
FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=local \\
FRESHELL_E2E_BACKEND=local \\
env --chdir="$MAIN" npm run check'''
replacements = [
    ('MAIN=/home/dan/code/freshell', f'MAIN={shlex.quote(main)}'),
    ('PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT=/home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/kata-sbnj',
     f'PLAN_SOURCE_ROOT={shlex.quote(plan_source)}'),
    ('GATE_RECORD="$MAIN/.worktrees/.the-usual-logs/kata-sbnj/gate-a.env"',
     f'GATE_RECORD={shlex.quote(gate_record)}'),
    (baseline_block,
     f'env --chdir="$MAIN" bash {shlex.quote(baseline)} {shlex.quote(case_name)}'),
]
for old, new in replacements:
    assert prefix.count(old) == 1, old
    prefix = prefix.replace(old, new)
assert len(replacements) == 4
assert 'REPLACEMENT_SLUG=' not in prefix
Path(output_path).write_text(prefix + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
PY
  chmod 0700 "$gate"

  local expected_exit
  case "$case_name" in
    success) expected_exit=0 ;;
    failure) expected_exit=23 ;;
    int) expected_exit=130 ;;
    term) expected_exit=143 ;;
    collision) expected_exit=1 ;;
    *) return 97 ;;
  esac

  set +e
  timeout --signal=KILL 20s setsid bash "$gate" >"$output" 2>"$errors"
  local actual_exit=$?
  set -e
  if [[ $actual_exit -ne $expected_exit ]]; then
    printf 'case %s: expected exit %s, got %s\n' \
      "$case_name" "$expected_exit" "$actual_exit" >&2
    cat "$errors" >&2
    return 1
  fi

  cmp -s "$expected_status" \
    <(git -C "$main" status --porcelain=v1 -z --untracked-files=normal)
  tar --compare --file="$expected_archive" --directory="$main"
  test "$(<"$main/-o")" = 'leading dash original'
  test "$(<"$main/space name.txt")" = 'space original'
  test "$(<"$main/$newline_name")" = 'newline original'
  test -L "$main/untracked-root/link"
  test -L "$main/untracked-root/dangling"
  test -d "$main/untracked-root/empty-child"
  test -z "$(git -C "$main" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=no)"
  test -z "$(git -C "$main" branch --list 'the-usual/kata-sbnj-impl-*')"
  test -z "$(find "$main/.worktrees" -maxdepth 1 -type d -name 'kata-sbnj-impl-*' -print 2>/dev/null)"

  if [[ $case_name == collision ]]; then
    local recovery
    recovery="$(sed -n 's/^Gate A preservation failed; retained recovery state at //p' "$errors" | tail -n 1)"
    test -n "$recovery"
    test -d "$recovery"
    test "$(<"$recovery/conflicts/-o")" = 'collision version'
    grep -Fq 'preserved a colliding post-baseline path' "$errors"
  fi

  printf 'Gate A fixture %s: PASS (exit %s)\n' "$case_name" "$actual_exit"
}

for case_name in success failure int term collision; do
  make_case "$case_name"
done
GATE_A_FIXTURE
````

### Replacement execution context

Tasks 1–11 and Verification Task 20 run only in the recorded replacement. Before
each task, execute these assertions (or source one executor-owned helper with
exactly this body):

```bash
GATE_RECORD=/home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/.the-usual-logs/kata-sbnj/gate-a.env
test -r "$GATE_RECORD"
# shellcheck disable=SC1090
source "$GATE_RECORD"
test "$(git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = \
  "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT"
test "$(git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" symbolic-ref --quiet --short HEAD)" = \
  "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH"
test "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" != /home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/kata-sbnj
test "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH" != the-usual/kata-sbnj
test "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH" != the-usual/cloud-run-jobs
git -C "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT" merge-base --is-ancestor "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA" HEAD
cd "$REPLACEMENT_ROOT"
```

Every relative path and every unqualified `npm`, `node`, `bash`, Docker, and
non-acceptance `git` command in Tasks 1–11 and 20 executes from this `cd`. Never
implement, commit, or run Task 20 from the old planning worktree or main
checkout.

### Gate B — dedicated identities before cloud mutation

**Status: unsatisfied; read-only inventory found no eligible identities.**
Tasks 1–11 are local implementation work and may proceed after Gate A. Source
upload, Cloud Build, registry push, Job mutation, live characterization, and
real-cloud acceptance additionally require separately authorized provisioning
and a passing read-only preflight proving:

1. `FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` and
   `FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` are distinct, enabled, dedicated
   service accounts in the configured project.
2. The caller has `iam.serviceAccounts.actAs` on both and
   `iam.serviceAccounts.getAccessToken` only on the Build service account. No
   project-wide Token Creator or default/broad identity fallback is accepted.
3. The Build identity has only exact source-bucket object read, configured
   Artifact Registry repository write, and log write. The runtime identity has
   no project role unless a workload API need is demonstrated and added with a
   focused failing test.
4. Neither worker has Owner, Editor, Secret Manager, broad Run, project-wide
   Token Creator, conditional/custom/ancestor/sibling grants, or unrelated
   roles. `FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE` must name exactly one containing
   `organizations/<positive-decimal-id>` authority root. Project ancestry and
   organization-scoped group-expanded/effective analysis must be complete and
   readable; there is no project/folder fallback.
5. Build logging is `CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY` (or a separately approved explicit
   user-owned destination), the source bucket and repository already exist, and
   non-regional `ongoing_builds` quota is at least two.
6. Every returned Build, Job, and Execution echoes the configured Build/runtime
   identity before it can be trusted.

Provisioning and IAM changes are outside this plan and require separate user
authorization. There is no broad-identity fallback.

## Final Architecture and Dependency Order

Only final implementations are committed. Dependencies flow in this order:

1. final detached POSIX command/process and catchable-signal primitive;
2. canonical suites, CSPRNG identity, and captured-SHA extracted source;
3. dedicated-identity/capacity preflight;
4. generation-owned source, global Build/local rebuild, and immutable digest;
5. provider schemas plus a closed-purpose Cloud Run v2 child command;
6. final ownership receipt and automatic exact cleanup;
7. assembled lifecycle;
8. container wrapper and Electron context;
9. Vitest adapter and classifier-first coordinator routing;
10. E2E adapter and compatibility facade;
11. acceptance validator, fixture replay, and guidance;
20. non-destructive/local verification; then
21. authorized real-cloud acceptance.

Task IDs 20 and 21 are retained for the existing verification/acceptance
references; numbering jumps directly from final behavior Task 11 to those
stages, with no hidden work in the gap.

The fixed Node child is **not** a provider abstraction. It accepts only four
validated Cloud Run v2 operations (`get-job`, `run-job`, `delete-job`, and
`get-operation`), constructs those endpoints internally, and cannot receive a
URL or HTTP verb. Artifact Registry and Storage inspection/deletion use their
own structured `gcloud` commands. `RunCommand` remains the sole injected seam.
Opt-in contract characterization uses a bounded `ProviderContractCapture`
created after the lifecycle's pre-aborted guard and passed explicitly through
that invocation's validated provider reads and cleanup. It has no module-global
active state, cannot issue commands, flushes before the terminal receipt, and
is released by the lifecycle's outer `finally` on every post-activation path.

The exact pushed commit, including the local lifecycle running with the
configured caller's orchestration authority, is trusted against deliberate
subversion. Dedicated worker identities constrain Build/runtime workers; they
do not sandbox malicious caller-authorized local orchestration. Wrapper output
mediation prevents accidental/foreign marker confusion; it is not a hostile-code
sandbox. Automatic cleanup covers success,
observable failure, timeout, and catchable signals while the supervisor lives.
SIGKILL, OOM, or host loss may leave exact residuals named by progressive
redacted ownership events. Hard-loss output is diagnostic fact only and grants
no post-loss deletion authority. This is not persistent recovery state or a
janitor. Artifact Registry tag deletion has no CAS; the accepted
bounded policy is an internal 128-bit tag, restricted writers, terminal Build,
exact digest recheck, name deletion, post-check absence, digest retention, and
fail/retain/report on ambiguity.

Read-only evidence supports global `E2_HIGHCPU_32` Builds (10 concurrent global
Builds and seven recent successful global 32-vCPU pushes to the regional
registry). The regional default-pool quota is 10 CPUs and cannot support even
one regional 32-vCPU Build. Therefore `buildRegion` is always `global`, while
`runRegion` defaults to `us-west1` and remains configurable.

## Task 1: Final POSIX Process-Group and Signal Primitive

**Files:** create `scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts`, the dedicated sandbox config
and process test; create `tsconfig.scripts.json`; modify all three standard
Vitest configs and `package.json`.

**Interfaces:** the GREEN block defines `CommandResult`, `RunCommandOptions`,
`RunCommand`, `CloudInvocationOrigin`, `LifecycleBudgets`,
`CloudRunLifecycleInput`, `ExecutionCounters`, `CloudPartResult`,
`CleanupDisposition`, and `ResidualResource` once in `lifecycle.ts`.
`defaultRunCommand` is final in this task. It spawns `detached: true`, records
parent `exit` independently from stdio `close`, starts group closure on either
parent exit or failure, and settles only after parent terminal state, process
group absence, and stdio close are all observed within one settlement deadline.

```ts
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
```

```ts
// test/sandbox/cloud-run-process-groups.test.ts
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process'
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs'
import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { afterEach, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

type TestRunCommand = (
  command: string,
  args: readonly string[],
  options: {
    signal?: AbortSignal
    output:
      | { kind: 'capture'; maxStdoutBytes: number; maxStderrBytes: number }
      | { kind: 'inherit' }
    timeoutMs: number
    terminateGraceMs: number
    settlementTimeoutMs: number
  },
) => Promise<{
  stdout: string
  stderr: string
  exitCode: number
  signal: NodeJS.Signals | null
  timedOut: boolean
}>

const insideSandbox = existsSync('/.dockerenv') &&
  process.env.FRESHELL_DESTRUCTIVE_SANDBOX === '1'
const roots: string[] = []
afterEach(async () => Promise.all(
  roots.splice(0).map((root) => rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true })),
))

async function fixture(scriptBody: string) {
  const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'cloud-pgroup-'))
  roots.push(root)
  const script = join(root, 'parent.sh')
  const pidFile = join(root, 'descendant.pid')
  await writeFile(script, `#!/bin/sh\nset -eu\n${scriptBody}\n`, { mode: 0o700 })
  return { script, pidFile }
}

function expectGone(pid: number): void {
  try {
    process.kill(pid, 0)
  } catch (error) {
    expect((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code).toBe('ESRCH')
    return
  }
  throw new Error(`owned descendant ${pid} is still alive`)
}

const bounded = {
  output: { kind: 'capture', maxStdoutBytes: 4096, maxStderrBytes: 4096 } as const,
  timeoutMs: 10_000,
  terminateGraceMs: 100,
  settlementTimeoutMs: 2_000,
}

let defaultRunCommand: TestRunCommand
beforeAll(async () => {
  const modulePath = '../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
  const loaded = await import(modulePath).catch(() => ({})) as Record<string, unknown>
  expect(
    loaded.defaultRunCommand,
    'the final owned-process runner must be installed before sandbox cases execute',
  ).toBeTypeOf('function')
  defaultRunCommand = loaded.defaultRunCommand as TestRunCommand
})

describe.skipIf(!insideSandbox)('final owned POSIX process groups', () => {
  it('settles a zero-exit parent whose descendant keeps captured pipes open', async () => {
    const { script, pidFile } = await fixture(`
( while :; do sleep 1; done ) &
child=$!
echo "$child" >"${pidFile}"
exit 0`)
    const started = Date.now()
    const result = await defaultRunCommand(script, [], bounded)
    expect(result).toMatchObject({ exitCode: 0, signal: null, timedOut: false })
    expect(Date.now() - started).toBeLessThan(3_000)
    const pid = Number((await readFile(pidFile, 'utf8')).trim())
    expectGone(pid)
  })

  it('times out and kills a TERM-resistant parent and descendant', async () => {
    const { script, pidFile } = await fixture(`
trap '' TERM
( trap '' TERM; while :; do sleep 1; done ) &
child=$!
echo "$child" >"${pidFile}"
wait`)
    await expect(defaultRunCommand(script, [], {
      ...bounded,
      timeoutMs: 150,
    })).rejects.toThrow('timed out')
    expectGone(Number((await readFile(pidFile, 'utf8')).trim()))
  })

  it('bounds output and kills the writer group', async () => {
    const { script, pidFile } = await fixture(`
( while :; do printf '0123456789'; done ) &
child=$!
echo "$child" >"${pidFile}"
wait`)
    await expect(defaultRunCommand(script, [], {
      ...bounded,
      output: { kind: 'capture', maxStdoutBytes: 128, maxStderrBytes: 128 },
    })).rejects.toThrow('stdout exceeded 128 bytes')
    expectGone(Number((await readFile(pidFile, 'utf8')).trim()))
  })

  it('removes its abort listener after settlement', async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    const add = vi.spyOn(controller.signal, 'addEventListener')
    const remove = vi.spyOn(controller.signal, 'removeEventListener')
    await defaultRunCommand('/bin/true', [], { ...bounded, signal: controller.signal })
    const listener = add.mock.calls.find(([type]) => type === 'abort')?.[1]
    expect(listener).toBeDefined()
    expect(remove).toHaveBeenCalledWith('abort', listener)
  })

  it('does not signal an unrelated process group', async () => {
    const unrelated = spawn('/bin/sh', ['-c', 'while :; do sleep 1; done'], {
      detached: true,
      stdio: 'ignore',
    })
    unrelated.unref()
    try {
      await defaultRunCommand('/bin/true', [], bounded)
      expect(() => process.kill(unrelated.pid!, 0)).not.toThrow()
    } finally {
      process.kill(-unrelated.pid!, 'SIGKILL')
    }
  })

  it('fails before spawn for an already-aborted signal', async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    controller.abort()
    await expect(defaultRunCommand('/bin/false', [], {
      ...bounded,
      signal: controller.signal,
    })).rejects.toThrow('aborted before spawn')
  })
})
```

Use `child=$!` for the descendant identity; it is deliberately left with the
parent's captured stdout/stderr. There is no redirection in the zero-exit case.

```ts
// scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts
import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from 'node:child_process'

export type CommandResult = {
  stdout: string
  stderr: string
  exitCode: number
  signal: NodeJS.Signals | null
  timedOut: boolean
}

export type RunCommandOptions = {
  cwd?: string
  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv
  stdin?: string | Uint8Array
  signal?: AbortSignal
  output:
    | { kind: 'capture'; maxStdoutBytes: number; maxStderrBytes: number }
    | { kind: 'inherit' }
  timeoutMs: number
  terminateGraceMs: number
  settlementTimeoutMs: number
}

export type RunCommand = (
  command: string,
  args: readonly string[],
  options: RunCommandOptions,
) => Promise<CommandResult>

export type CloudInvocationOrigin =
  | { surface: 'coordinator-test' | 'coordinator-check' | 'coordinator-verify' | 'coordinator-test-all'; argv: readonly string[] }
  | { surface: 'vitest-run' | 'vitest-build' | 'vitest-push'; argv: readonly string[] }
  | { surface: 'e2e-run' | 'e2e-build' | 'e2e-push'; argv: readonly string[] }

export type LifecycleBudgets = {
  overallMs: number
  provenanceMs: number
  sourceMs: number
  buildMs: number
  imageMs: number
  jobMs: number
  executionMs: number
  resultReadMs: number
  cleanupMs: number
  commandMs: number
  terminateGraceMs: number
  settlementMs: number
}

export type ExecutionCounters = {
  succeededCount: number
  failedCount: number
  cancelledCount: number
  runningCount: number
}

export type CloudPartResult = {
  marker: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT'
  schemaVersion: 1
  kind: 'vitest' | 'e2e'
  runId: string
  execution: string
  partId: string
  taskIndex: number
  taskCount: number
  taskAttempt: 0
  status: 'passed' | 'failed'
  exitCode: number
  testCount?: number
}

export type CleanupDisposition = {
  kind: 'execution' | 'job' | 'build' | 'source' | 'tag' | 'local-image' | 'docker-config' | 'temp-root'
  identity: string
  outcome: 'deleted' | 'absent' | 'terminal' | 'retained' | 'failed'
  precondition?: string
  error?: string
}

export type ResidualResource = {
  kind: CleanupDisposition['kind']
  identity: string
  reason: string
}

export type CloudRunLifecycleInput = {
  origin: CloudInvocationOrigin
  kind: 'vitest' | 'e2e'
  repoRoot: string
  account: string
  projectId: string
  iamAnalysisScope: string
  buildRegion: 'global'
  runRegion: string
  sourceBucket: string
  repository: string
  imageName: string
  jobPrefix: string
  buildServiceAccount: string
  runtimeServiceAccount: string
  buildMode: 'cloud' | 'local'
  operation: 'run' | 'build'
  taskCount: number
  // Canonical positive whole-second protobuf duration, e.g. "1800s"; max 86400s.
  taskTimeout: string
  memory: string
  cpu: number
  workloadEnv: Readonly<Record<string, string>>
  expectedParts: readonly string[]
  budgets: LifecycleBudgets
  signal?: AbortSignal
}

function groupExists(pid: number | undefined): boolean {
  if (!pid) return false
  try {
    process.kill(-pid, 0)
    return true
  } catch (error) {
    const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code
    if (code === 'ESRCH') return false
    if (code === 'EPERM') return true
    throw error
  }
}

function signalGroup(pid: number | undefined, signal: NodeJS.Signals): void {
  if (!pid) return
  try {
    process.kill(-pid, signal)
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'ESRCH') throw error
  }
}

function appendBounded(
  chunks: Buffer[],
  used: { value: number },
  chunk: Buffer,
  maximum: number,
  stream: 'stdout' | 'stderr',
  fail: (error: Error) => void,
): void {
  if (used.value + chunk.byteLength > maximum) {
    fail(new Error(`${stream} exceeded ${maximum} bytes`))
    return
  }
  used.value += chunk.byteLength
  chunks.push(chunk)
}

export const defaultRunCommand: RunCommand = (command, args, options) => {
  if (options.signal?.aborted) {
    return Promise.reject(new Error('Command aborted before spawn'))
  }
  if (process.platform === 'win32') {
    return Promise.reject(new Error(
      'Cloud lifecycle command ownership requires POSIX process groups',
    ))
  }
  if (options.settlementTimeoutMs <= options.terminateGraceMs) {
    return Promise.reject(new Error(
      'settlementTimeoutMs must exceed terminateGraceMs',
    ))
  }

  return new Promise<CommandResult>((resolve, reject) => {
    const capture = options.output.kind === 'capture'
    const child: ChildProcess = spawn(command, [...args], {
      cwd: options.cwd,
      env: options.env,
      shell: false,
      detached: true,
      stdio: [
        options.stdin === undefined ? 'inherit' : 'pipe',
        capture ? 'pipe' : 'inherit',
        capture ? 'pipe' : 'inherit',
      ],
    })
    const stdout: Buffer[] = []
    const stderr: Buffer[] = []
    const stdoutBytes = { value: 0 }
    const stderrBytes = { value: 0 }
    let parentExit: { code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null } | undefined
    let parentError: Error | undefined
    let closeSeen = false
    let timedOut = false
    let primary: Error | undefined
    let settlementStarted = false
    let settled = false
    let killSent = false
    let groupAbsent = false
    let settlementDeadline = 0
    let settlementTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined
    let escalationTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined
    let groupPollTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined

    const clearAll = () => {
      clearTimeout(commandTimer)
      if (settlementTimer) clearTimeout(settlementTimer)
      if (escalationTimer) clearTimeout(escalationTimer)
      if (groupPollTimer) clearTimeout(groupPollTimer)
      options.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
    }

    const result = (): CommandResult => ({
      stdout: Buffer.concat(stdout).toString('utf8'),
      stderr: Buffer.concat(stderr).toString('utf8'),
      exitCode: parentExit?.code ?? 1,
      signal: parentExit?.signal ?? null,
      timedOut,
    })

    const finishIfReady = () => {
      if (settled || !settlementStarted) return
      const parentTerminal = parentExit !== undefined || parentError !== undefined
      if (!parentTerminal || !groupAbsent || !closeSeen) return
      settled = true
      clearAll()
      if (primary || parentError) {
        reject(primary && parentError
          ? new AggregateError([primary, parentError], 'Command and parent process failed')
          : primary ?? parentError)
        return
      }
      resolve(result())
    }

    const pollGroup = () => {
      if (settled) return
      try {
        groupAbsent = !groupExists(child.pid)
      } catch (error) {
        primary = primary
          ? new AggregateError([primary, error], 'Process-group probe failed')
          : error as Error
        groupAbsent = false
      }
      finishIfReady()
      if (!settled && Date.now() < settlementDeadline) {
        groupPollTimer = setTimeout(pollGroup, 10)
      }
    }

    const beginSettlement = (failure?: Error) => {
      if (failure && !primary) primary = failure
      if (settlementStarted) return
      settlementStarted = true
      clearTimeout(commandTimer)
      settlementDeadline = Date.now() + options.settlementTimeoutMs
      try {
        if (groupExists(child.pid)) signalGroup(child.pid, 'SIGTERM')
        else groupAbsent = true
      } catch (error) {
        primary = primary
          ? new AggregateError([primary, error], 'TERM group closure failed')
          : error as Error
      }
      escalationTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (settled) return
        try {
          if (!groupExists(child.pid)) return
          killSent = true
          signalGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL')
        } catch (error) {
          primary = primary
            ? new AggregateError([primary, error], 'KILL group closure failed')
            : error as Error
        }
      }, options.terminateGraceMs)
      settlementTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (settled) return
        let groupStillPresent = true
        try {
          groupStillPresent = groupExists(child.pid)
          if (groupStillPresent && !killSent) {
            signalGroup(child.pid, 'SIGKILL')
            groupStillPresent = groupExists(child.pid)
          }
        } catch (error) {
          primary = primary
            ? new AggregateError([primary, error], 'Final group signal failed')
            : error as Error
        }
        const missing = [
          parentExit === undefined && parentError === undefined ? 'parent terminal event' : '',
          groupStillPresent ? 'process-group absence' : '',
          closeSeen ? '' : 'stdio close',
        ].filter(Boolean).join(', ')
        settled = true
        clearAll()
        reject(new AggregateError(
          [...(primary ? [primary] : []), new Error(`Missing: ${missing}`)],
          `Command settlement exceeded ${options.settlementTimeoutMs}ms`,
        ))
      }, options.settlementTimeoutMs)
      pollGroup()
      finishIfReady()
    }

    const onAbort = () => beginSettlement(new Error('Command aborted'))
    const commandTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      timedOut = true
      beginSettlement(new Error(`Command timed out after ${options.timeoutMs}ms`))
    }, options.timeoutMs)
    options.signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
    if (options.signal?.aborted) onAbort()

    child.once('exit', (code, signal) => {
      parentExit = { code, signal }
      // This event is intentionally distinct from `close`: descendants may
      // still hold captured pipes. Parent exit starts group closure now.
      beginSettlement()
      finishIfReady()
    })
    child.once('close', () => {
      closeSeen = true
      finishIfReady()
    })
    child.once('error', (error) => {
      parentError = error
      beginSettlement()
      finishIfReady()
    })

    if (capture) {
      child.stdout!.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => appendBounded(
        stdout,
        stdoutBytes,
        chunk,
        options.output.kind === 'capture' ? options.output.maxStdoutBytes : 0,
        'stdout',
        beginSettlement,
      ))
      child.stderr!.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => appendBounded(
        stderr,
        stderrBytes,
        chunk,
        options.output.kind === 'capture' ? options.output.maxStderrBytes : 0,
        'stderr',
        beginSettlement,
      ))
    }
    if (child.stdin) {
      child.stdin.once('error', (error) => {
        if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'EPIPE') beginSettlement(error)
      })
      child.stdin.end(options.stdin)
    }
  })
}

export async function superviseCatchableSignals(
  work: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<number>,
): Promise<number> {
  const controller = new AbortController()
  let caught: 'SIGINT' | 'SIGTERM' | 'SIGHUP' | undefined
  const listeners = new Map<NodeJS.Signals, () => void>()
  for (const signal of ['SIGINT', 'SIGTERM', 'SIGHUP'] as const) {
    const listener = () => {
      caught ??= signal
      controller.abort(new Error(`Caught ${signal}`))
    }
    listeners.set(signal, listener)
    process.on(signal, listener)
  }
  try {
    try {
      const code = await work(controller.signal)
      return caught ? signalExitCode(caught) : code
    } catch (error) {
      if (caught) return signalExitCode(caught)
      throw error
    }
  } finally {
    for (const [signal, listener] of listeners) {
      process.removeListener(signal, listener)
    }
  }
}

export function signalExitCode(signal: 'SIGINT' | 'SIGTERM' | 'SIGHUP') {
  return signal === 'SIGINT' ? 130 : signal === 'SIGTERM' ? 143 : 129
}
```

```ts
type SettlementState = {
  parentTerminal: boolean
  groupAbsent: boolean
  closeSeen: boolean
}

function settlementMissing(state: SettlementState): string[] {
  return [
    state.parentTerminal ? '' : 'parent terminal event',
    state.groupAbsent ? '' : 'process-group absence',
    state.closeSeen ? '' : 'stdio close',
  ].filter(Boolean)
```

```ts
    return defaultRunCommand(command, args, options)

  expect(await readFile(join(repo, 'sentinel.txt'), 'utf8')).toBe('B\n')
  expect(await readFile(join(repo, 'docker/cloud-run/cloudbuild.yaml'), 'utf8'))
    .toBe('description: B\n')
  expect((await execFileAsync(

  )).stdout).toBe('A\n')
  expect((await execFileAsync(
    'tar', ['-xOf', snapshot.sourceArchive, 'docker/cloud-run/cloudbuild.yaml'],
  )).stdout).toBe('description: A\n')
  expect(await readFile(join(snapshot.sourceDir, 'sentinel.txt'), 'utf8')).toBe('A\n')
  expect(await readFile(snapshot.buildTemplatePath, 'utf8')).toBe('description: A\n')
})
```

The same RED file uses this bounded command fixture for every provenance
cardinality falsification:

```ts
const gitOk = (stdout = ''): CommandResult => ({
  stdout, stderr: '', exitCode: 0, signal: null, timedOut: false,
})

function provenanceFaultRunner(
  fault: 'two-remotes' | 'two-merges' | 'zero-rows' | 'two-rows' |
    'dirty' | 'detached' | 'tip-mismatch',
): RunCommand {
  const head = 'a'.repeat(40)
  return async (command, args) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (command !== 'git') throw new Error(`unexpected command ${text}`)
    if (args.includes('symbolic-ref')) {
      return fault === 'detached'
        ? { ...gitOk(), exitCode: 1, stderr: 'not symbolic' }
        : gitOk('feature\n')
    }
    if (args.includes('status')) return gitOk(fault === 'dirty' ? ' M file\0' : '')
    if (args.includes('rev-parse')) return gitOk(`${head}\n`)
    if (args.includes('--get-all') && text.includes('.remote')) {
      return gitOk(fault === 'two-remotes' ? 'origin\0backup\0' : 'origin\0')
    }
    if (args.includes('--get-all') && text.includes('.merge')) {
      return gitOk(fault === 'two-merges'
        ? 'refs/heads/feature\0refs/heads/other\0'
        : 'refs/heads/feature\0')
    }
    if (args.includes('ls-remote')) {
      if (fault === 'zero-rows') return gitOk('')
      if (fault === 'two-rows') return gitOk(
        `${head}\trefs/heads/feature\n${head}\trefs/heads/feature\n`,
      )
      return gitOk(
        `${fault === 'tip-mismatch' ? 'b'.repeat(40) : head}\trefs/heads/feature\n`,
      )
    }
    throw new Error(`mutation/archive reached after provenance fault: ${text}`)
  }
}

it.each([
  'two-remotes', 'two-merges', 'zero-rows', 'two-rows',
  'dirty', 'detached', 'tip-mismatch',
] as const)('fails closed for provenance fault %s before archive', async (fault) => {
  await expect(prepareSourceSnapshot(
    '/repo', '1'.repeat(32), undefined, provenanceFaultRunner(fault),
  )).rejects.toThrow()
})
```

```ts
// scripts/testing/vitest-suites.ts
export const STANDARD_VITEST_SUITES = [
  { key: 'default', name: 'client', configPath: 'config/vitest/vitest.config.ts' },
  { key: 'server', name: 'server', configPath: 'config/vitest/vitest.server.config.ts' },
  { key: 'electron', name: 'electron', configPath: 'config/vitest/vitest.electron.config.ts' },
] as const

export type StandardVitestSuite = (typeof STANDARD_VITEST_SUITES)[number]
export type StandardVitestSuiteSelector = StandardVitestSuite['key'] | 'all'

export function selectStandardVitestSuites(selector: string) {
  if (selector === 'all') return STANDARD_VITEST_SUITES
  const suite = STANDARD_VITEST_SUITES.find(({ key }) => key === selector)
  if (!suite) throw new Error(
    `Invalid Vitest suite ${JSON.stringify(selector)}; expected default, server, electron, or all`,
  )
  return [suite]
}
```

```ts
// additions to lifecycle.ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
import { mkdir, mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'

const FULL_OBJECT_ID = /^(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})$/
const JOB_NAME = /^[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,47}[a-z0-9])?$/
const DOCKER_TAG = /^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$/

export function createRunId(): string {
  return randomBytes(16).toString('hex')
}

function createResourceIdentity(
  prefix: string,
  head: string,
  runId: string,
): InvocationIdentity {
  if (!FULL_OBJECT_ID.test(head)) throw new Error('head must be one full Git object ID')
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(runId)) {
    throw new Error('runId must preserve exactly 128 random bits')
  }
  if (prefix.length < 1 || prefix.length > 128) {
    throw new Error('Job prefix must contain 1 through 128 input characters')
  }
  const normalized = prefix.toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
    .slice(0, 16)
    .replace(/-+$/g, '')
  if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,15}$/.test(normalized)) {
    throw new Error('Job prefix must normalize to a lowercase letter-led name')
  }
  const value = {
    runId,
    sourceObject: `source/freshell/${runId}.tar.gz`,
    imageTag: `sbnj-${head.slice(0, 12)}-${runId}`,
    buildCorrelationTag: `freshell-${runId}`,
    jobName: `${normalized}-${runId}`,
  }
  if (!JOB_NAME.test(value.jobName) || value.jobName.length > 49) {
    throw new Error('Generated Cloud Run Job name violates the 49-character contract')
  }
  if (!DOCKER_TAG.test(value.imageTag) ||
      !/^[a-z0-9-]{1,64}$/.test(value.buildCorrelationTag) ||
      Buffer.byteLength(value.sourceObject) > 1024) {
    throw new Error('Generated registry/build/storage identity is invalid')
  }
  return value
}

export function createInvocationIdentity(
  prefix: string,
  head: string,
): InvocationIdentity {
  return createResourceIdentity(prefix, head, createRunId())
}

function oneNulValue(stdout: string, label: string): string {
  const values = stdout.split('\0').filter(Boolean)
  if (values.length !== 1) throw new Error(`${label} must have exactly one value`)
  return values[0]
}

export async function prepareSourceSnapshot(
  repoRoot: string,
  runId: string,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<SourceSnapshot> {
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(runId)) {
    throw new Error('snapshot runId must preserve exactly 128 random bits')
  }
  const options = (timeoutMs: number): RunCommandOptions => ({
    cwd: repoRoot,
    signal,
    output: { kind: 'capture', maxStdoutBytes: 64 * 1024, maxStderrBytes: 64 * 1024 },
    timeoutMs,
    terminateGraceMs: 1_000,
    settlementTimeoutMs: 5_000,
  })
  const git = async (args: readonly string[], timeoutMs = 30_000) => {
    const result = await runCommand('git', ['-C', repoRoot, ...args], options(timeoutMs))
    if (result.exitCode !== 0 || result.signal || result.timedOut) {
      throw new Error(`Git command failed: ${args[0]}`)
    }
    return result.stdout
  }
  const branch = (await git(['symbolic-ref', '--quiet', '--short', 'HEAD'])).trim()
  if (!branch) throw new Error('Cloud source requires a symbolic branch')
  if (await git(['status', '--porcelain=v1', '--untracked-files=all'])) {
    throw new Error('Cloud source worktree must be clean')
  }
  const head = (await git(['rev-parse', '--verify', 'HEAD^{commit}'])).trim()
  if (!FULL_OBJECT_ID.test(head)) throw new Error('Captured HEAD is not a full object ID')
  const upstreamRemote = oneNulValue(
    await git(['config', '--null', '--get-all', `branch.${branch}.remote`]),
    'upstream remote',
  )
  const upstreamMergeRef = oneNulValue(
    await git(['config', '--null', '--get-all', `branch.${branch}.merge`]),
    'upstream merge ref',
  )
  if (!/^refs\/heads\/[A-Za-z0-9._/-]+$/.test(upstreamMergeRef)) {
    throw new Error('Configured upstream merge value is not one branch ref')
  }
  const live = (await git([
    'ls-remote', '--refs', '--exit-code', upstreamRemote, upstreamMergeRef,
  ])).trimEnd().split('\n').filter(Boolean)
  if (live.length !== 1 || live[0] !== `${head}\t${upstreamMergeRef}`) {
    throw new Error('Live configured upstream tip must exactly equal captured HEAD')
  }

  const tempRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), `freshell-cloud-${runId}-`))
  const sourceArchive = join(tempRoot, 'source.tar.gz')
  const sourceDir = join(tempRoot, 'source')
  const dockerConfigDir = join(tempRoot, 'docker-config')
  try {
    await mkdir(sourceDir, { mode: 0o700 })
    await mkdir(dockerConfigDir, { mode: 0o700 })
    await git(['archive', '--format=tar.gz', `--output=${sourceArchive}`, head], 120_000)
    const extract = await runCommand('tar', ['-xzf', sourceArchive, '-C', sourceDir], options(120_000))
    if (extract.exitCode !== 0 || extract.signal || extract.timedOut) {
      throw new Error('Captured source archive extraction failed')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    await rm(tempRoot, { recursive: true, force: true })
    throw error
  }
  return {
    branch,
    upstreamRemote,
    upstreamMergeRef,
    head,
    tempRoot,
    sourceArchive,
    sourceDir,
    buildTemplatePath: join(sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run/cloudbuild.yaml'),
    dockerConfigDir,
  }
}
```

- [ ] **REFACTOR while green.** Extract `runCheckedGit` with the exact options
below and use it for all Git calls; rerun GREEN. It accepts argv, never a shell
string, and never rereads mutable `HEAD` after capture.

```ts
async function runCheckedGit(
  repoRoot: string,
  args: readonly string[],
  signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  timeoutMs = 30_000,
): Promise<string> {
  const result = await runCommand('git', ['-C', repoRoot, ...args], {
    cwd: repoRoot,
    signal,
    output: { kind: 'capture', maxStdoutBytes: 64 * 1024, maxStderrBytes: 64 * 1024 },
    timeoutMs,
    terminateGraceMs: 1_000,
    settlementTimeoutMs: 5_000,
  })
  if (result.exitCode !== 0 || result.signal || result.timedOut) {
    throw new Error(`Git command failed: ${args[0]}`)
  }
  return result.stdout
}
```

Retain every Task 2 test and shared declaration. Replace **only** the import
prologue of `cloud-run-lifecycle.test.ts` with this exact cumulative Task 3
block. `execFileAsync`, `roots`, its one `afterEach`, the Task 2 dynamic loader,
and all Task 2 tests remain exactly once below it. Every response is literal
provider-shaped JSON and every mutation command is rejected by the fixture
itself:

```ts
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process'
import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { promisify } from 'node:util'
import { afterEach, beforeAll, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { defaultRunCommand } from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
import type {
  CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  CommandResult,
  RunCommand,
} from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
import * as lifecycleModule from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'

type GcloudJson = <T>(
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
  args: readonly string[],
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  timeoutMs?: number,
) => Promise<T>
type VerifyCloudPrerequisites = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
) => Promise<unknown>
type ReadEffectiveReport = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  permission?: string,
) => Promise<unknown>

function requiredLifecycleFunction<T extends (...args: never[]) => unknown>(
  name: string,
): T {
  const candidate = (lifecycleModule as Record<string, unknown>)[name]
  expect(candidate, `${name} final behavior must be installed`).toBeTypeOf('function')
  return candidate as T
}

const gcloudJson: GcloudJson = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<GcloudJson>('gcloudJson')(...args)
const verifyCloudPrerequisites: VerifyCloudPrerequisites = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<VerifyCloudPrerequisites>(
    'verifyCloudPrerequisites',
  )(...args)
const readEffectiveReport: ReadEffectiveReport = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ReadEffectiveReport>('readEffectiveReport')(...args)

const ok = (value: unknown): CommandResult => ({
  stdout: `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`,
  stderr: '',
  exitCode: 0,
  signal: null,
  timedOut: false,
})
const failed = (stderr = 'gcloud emitted an undocumented diagnostic'): CommandResult => ({
  stdout: '',
  stderr,
  exitCode: 1,
  signal: null,
  timedOut: false,
})

type RunCommandMock = ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>>

function commandTranscript(runCommand: RunCommand): string {
  return (runCommand as RunCommandMock).mock.calls
    .map(([command, args]) => `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`)
    .join('\n')
}

const BASE_INPUT: CloudRunLifecycleInput = {
  origin: { surface: 'vitest-run', argv: [] },
  kind: 'vitest',
  repoRoot: '/repo',
  account: 'caller@example.com',
  projectId: 'test-project',
  iamAnalysisScope: 'organizations/123456789',
  buildRegion: 'global',
  runRegion: 'us-west1',
  sourceBucket: 'test-source-bucket',
  repository: 'freshell-e2e',
  imageName: 'freshell-vitest',
  jobPrefix: 'freshell-vitest',
  buildServiceAccount: 'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  runtimeServiceAccount: 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  buildMode: 'cloud',
  operation: 'run',
  taskCount: 4,
  taskTimeout: '1800s',
  memory: '4Gi',
  cpu: 4,
  workloadEnv: {},
  expectedParts: ['client:1/4'],
  budgets: {
    overallMs: 8_000_000,
    provenanceMs: 30_000,
    sourceMs: 120_000,
    buildMs: 4_500_000,
    imageMs: 120_000,
    jobMs: 120_000,
    executionMs: 2_100_000,
    resultReadMs: 120_000,
    cleanupMs: 300_000,
    commandMs: 30_000,
    terminateGraceMs: 1_000,
    settlementMs: 5_000,
  },
}

function effectiveReport(
  identity: string,
  grants: readonly (readonly [string, string])[],
  permission?: string,
) {
  return {
    fullyExplored: true,
    mainAnalysis: {
      analysisQuery: {
        scope: BASE_INPUT.iamAnalysisScope,
        identitySelector: { identity },
        options: { expandGroups: true, expandRoles: true },
        ...(permission === undefined
          ? {}
          : { accessSelector: { permissions: [permission] } }),
      },
      ...(grants.length === 0 ? {} : {
        analysisResults: grants.map(([resource, role]) => ({
          accessControlLists: [{
            accesses: permission === undefined ? [{ role }] : [{ permission }],
            resources: [{ fullResourceName: resource }],
          }],
          attachedResourceFullName: resource,
          fullyExplored: true,
          iamBinding: { members: [identity], role },
          identityList: { identities: [{ name: identity }] },
        })),
      }),
      fullyExplored: true,
    },
  }
}

function exactGateBRunner(overrides: {
  buildGrants?: (readonly [string, string])[]
  runtimeGrants?: (readonly [string, string])[]
  quota?: unknown
  fault?: string
} = {}): RunCommand {
  const caller = 'user:caller@example.com'
  const build = BASE_INPUT.buildServiceAccount
  const runtime = BASE_INPUT.runtimeServiceAccount
  const buildGrants = overrides.buildGrants ?? [
    ['//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/test-project',
      overrides.fault === 'build Secret Accessor'
        ? 'roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor'
        : 'roles/logging.logWriter'],
    ['//storage.googleapis.com/projects/_/buckets/test-source-bucket', 'roles/storage.objectViewer'],
    ['//artifactregistry.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e', 'roles/artifactregistry.writer'],
  ]
  const runtimeGrants = overrides.runtimeGrants ?? (
    overrides.fault === 'runtime Editor'
      ? [['//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/test-project', 'roles/editor']]
      : []
  )
  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (/storage cp|builds submit|docker push|run jobs create/.test(text)) {
      throw new Error(`unexpected mutation in preflight: ${text}`)
    }
    if (overrides.fault === 'unreadable analysis' &&
        args.includes('analyze-iam-policy')) {
      return failed('ERROR: permission denied by gcloud')
    }
    if (args[0] === 'projects' && args[1] === 'describe') {
      return ok({ projectId: 'test-project', projectNumber: '123456789' })
    }
    if (args[0] === 'projects' && args[1] === 'get-ancestors') {
      return ok(overrides.fault === 'wrong organization ancestry'
        ? [
            { id: 'test-project', type: 'project' },
            { id: '999999999', type: 'organization' },
          ]
        : [
            { id: 'test-project', type: 'project' },
            { id: '123456789', type: 'organization' },
          ])
    }
    if (args[0] === 'iam' && args[1] === 'service-accounts' && args[2] === 'describe') {
      const requested = String(args[3])
      const email = overrides.fault === 'same worker identity' && requested === runtime
        ? build
        : overrides.fault === 'default Compute worker' && requested === build
          ? '123456789-compute@developer.gserviceaccount.com'
          : requested
      return ok({
        name: `projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${email}`,
        email,
        disabled: false,
      })
    }
    if (args[0] === 'asset' && args[1] === 'analyze-iam-policy') {
      expect(args).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
        '--organization=123456789',
        '--expand-groups',
        '--expand-roles',
        '--show-response',
        '--account=caller@example.com',
        '--billing-project=test-project',
        '--format=json',
      ]))
      expect(args.some((arg) => arg.startsWith('--scope='))).toBe(false)
      expect(args.some((arg) => arg.startsWith('--project='))).toBe(false)
      const identityArg = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--identity='))!
      const identity = identityArg.slice('--identity='.length)
      const permissionArg = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--permissions='))
      if (permissionArg) {
        const permission = permissionArg.slice('--permissions='.length)
        const actAsResources = [
          `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${build}`,
          `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${runtime}`,
        ]
        const resources = permission === 'iam.serviceAccounts.actAs'
          ? (overrides.fault === 'missing caller actAs'
              ? actAsResources.slice(0, 1)
              : overrides.fault === 'extra caller actAs'
                ? [...actAsResources, '//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project']
                : actAsResources)
          : (overrides.fault === 'project-wide Token Creator'
              ? ['//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project']
              : overrides.fault === 'extra token resource'
                ? actAsResources
                : [actAsResources[0]])
        const role = permission === 'iam.serviceAccounts.actAs'
          ? 'roles/iam.serviceAccountUser'
          : 'roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator'
        return ok(effectiveReport(
          caller,
          resources.map((resource) => [resource, role]),
          permission,
        ))
      }
      const grants = identity === `serviceAccount:${build}` ? buildGrants : runtimeGrants
      const report = effectiveReport(identity, grants)
      const first = report.mainAnalysis.analysisResults?.[0]
      if (overrides.fault === 'conditional grant' && first) {
        ;(first.iamBinding as Record<string, unknown>).condition = {
          expression: 'true',
        }
      }
      if (overrides.fault === 'incomplete group expansion') report.fullyExplored = false
      if (overrides.fault === 'custom role' && first) {
        first.iamBinding.role = 'projects/test-project/roles/custom'
      }
      if (overrides.fault === 'duplicate grant path' && first) {
        report.mainAnalysis.analysisResults!.push(structuredClone(first))
      }
      return ok(report)
    }
    if (args[0] === 'storage' && args[1] === 'buckets' && args[2] === 'list') {
      return ok(overrides.fault === 'missing bucket'
        ? []
        : [{ name: 'test-source-bucket', location: 'US' }])
    }
    if (args[0] === 'artifacts' && args[1] === 'repositories' && args[2] === 'list') {
      return ok([{
        name: overrides.fault === 'wrong repository'
          ? 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/wrong'
          : 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e',
        format: 'DOCKER',
      }])
    }
    if (JSON.stringify(args) === JSON.stringify([
      'beta', 'quotas', 'info', 'describe', 'OngoingBuildsPerProject',
      '--service=cloudbuild.googleapis.com',
      `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
      `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
      '--format=json(metric,quotaId,dimensions,dimensionsInfos)',
    ])) {
      if (overrides.quota !== undefined) return ok(overrides.quota)
      return ok({
        metric: 'cloudbuild.googleapis.com/ongoing_builds',
        quotaId: 'OngoingBuildsPerProject',
        dimensionsInfos: [{
          applicableLocations: overrides.fault === 'regional-only quota'
            ? ['us-west1']
            : ['global'],
          ...(overrides.fault === 'regional-only quota'
            ? { dimensions: { region: 'us-west1' } }
            : {}),
          details: {
            value: overrides.fault === 'quota below two' ? '1' : '10',
          },
        }],
      })
    }
    throw new Error(`unhandled read: ${text}`)
  })
}

it('accepts only two dedicated workers and exact effective grant sets', async () => {
  const runner = exactGateBRunner({
    buildGrants: [
      ['//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/test-project', 'roles/logging.logWriter'],
      ['//storage.googleapis.com/projects/_/buckets/test-source-bucket', 'roles/storage.objectViewer'],
      ['//artifactregistry.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e', 'roles/artifactregistry.writer'],
    ],

  await expect(verifyCloudPrerequisites(BASE_INPUT, runner)).resolves.toMatchObject({
    projectId: 'test-project',

    globalOngoingBuildLimit: 10,

  const quotaCalls = (runner as RunCommandMock).mock.calls.filter(([, args]) =>
    args[0] === 'beta' && args[1] === 'quotas')
  expect(quotaCalls).toHaveLength(1)
  expect(quotaCalls[0][1]).toEqual([

    '--service=cloudbuild.googleapis.com',
    '--project=test-project',
    '--account=caller@example.com',
    '--format=json(metric,quotaId,dimensions,dimensionsInfos)',
  ])
})

        resources: Array<{ analysisState?: unknown }>

  ['wrong echoed scope', (value: MutableIamFixture) => {
    value.mainAnalysis.analysisQuery.scope = 'organizations/999'
  }],
  ['wrong echoed identity', (value: MutableIamFixture) => {
    value.mainAnalysis.analysisQuery.identitySelector.identity =
      'serviceAccount:foreign@example.com'
  }],
  ['wrong options', (value: MutableIamFixture) => {
    value.mainAnalysis.analysisQuery.options.expandGroups = false
  }],
  ['missing ACL', (value: MutableIamFixture) => {
    value.mainAnalysis.analysisResults[0].accessControlLists = []
  }],
  ['missing identity', (value: MutableIamFixture) => {
    value.mainAnalysis.analysisResults[0].identityList.identities = []
  }],
  ['unknown authority field', (value: MutableIamFixture) => {
    value.mainAnalysis.analysisQuery.folder = 'folders/1'
  }],
] as const)('rejects raw analyzer fault: %s', async (_name, mutate) => {
  const fixture = JSON.parse(await readFile(
    'test/fixtures/cloud-run/iam-analysis-contracts.json',
    'utf8',
  )) as { worker: MutableIamFixture }
  mutate(fixture.worker)
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockResolvedValue(ok(fixture.worker))
  await expect(readEffectiveReport(
    BASE_INPUT,
    `serviceAccount:${BASE_INPUT.buildServiceAccount}`,
    runner,
  )).rejects.toThrow()
})

it('rejects a wrong echoed permission', async () => {
  const fixture = JSON.parse(await readFile(
    'test/fixtures/cloud-run/iam-analysis-contracts.json',

  fixture.permission.mainAnalysis.analysisQuery.accessSelector!.permissions = [

it.each([
  'same worker identity',
  'default Compute worker',
```

The same RED file exercises only objectively preserved command-transport
classification. It proves that abort, nonzero gcloud exits (including
JSON-looking stderr), and malformed stdout are never retried. Only a
`RunCommand` timeout or a thrown characterized OS transport code can consume
the bounded three-attempt read budget; provider presence/absence is represented
by successful exact read output:

```ts
it('retries an objectively returned RunCommand timeout within one deadline', async () => {
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
    .mockResolvedValueOnce({
      stdout: '', stderr: '', exitCode: null, signal: 'SIGKILL', timedOut: true,
    })
    .mockResolvedValueOnce(ok({ projectId: 'test-project', projectNumber: '123456789' }))
  await expect(gcloudJson(
    z.object({ projectId: z.string(), projectNumber: z.string() }).strict(),
    ['projects', 'describe', 'test-project', '--format=json'],
    BASE_INPUT,
    runner,
    1_000,
  )).resolves.toMatchObject({ projectId: 'test-project' })
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
})

it.each(['ECONNRESET', 'ETIMEDOUT', 'EAI_AGAIN'] as const)(
  'retries only characterized thrown transport failure %s',
  async (code) => {
    const transport = Object.assign(new Error(code), { code })
    const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(transport)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(ok({ value: 'read' }))
    await expect(gcloudJson(
      z.object({ value: z.literal('read') }).strict(),
      ['projects', 'describe', 'test-project', '--format=json'],
      BASE_INPUT,
      runner,
      1_000,
    )).resolves.toEqual({ value: 'read' })
    expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
  },
)

it.each([
  'plain diagnostic',
  JSON.stringify({
    error: { code: 404, status: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'invented stderr' },
  }),
  JSON.stringify({
    error: { code: 429, status: 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED', message: 'invented stderr' },
  }),
] as const)('does not parse or retry gcloud stderr: %s', async (stderr) => {
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockResolvedValue(failed(stderr))
  await expect(gcloudJson(
    z.unknown(),
    ['projects', 'describe', 'test-project', '--format=json'],
    BASE_INPUT,
    runner,
    1_000,
  )).rejects.toThrow('gcloud exact read failed')
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
})

it('does not retry malformed JSON or an aborted lifecycle', async () => {
  const malformed = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockResolvedValue({
    ...ok({}), stdout: '{not-json',
  })
  await expect(gcloudJson(
    z.unknown(), ['projects', 'describe', 'test-project'], BASE_INPUT, malformed,
  )).rejects.toThrow('non-JSON')
  expect(malformed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)

  const controller = new AbortController()
  controller.abort(new Error('stop'))
  const aborted = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
  await expect(gcloudJson(
    z.unknown(),
    ['projects', 'describe', 'test-project'],
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal },
    aborted,
  )).rejects.toThrow('aborted')
  expect(aborted).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
})
```
