Add these complete schemas and bodies to `lifecycle.ts`:

```ts
const forbiddenWorkerRoles = new Set([
  'roles/owner',
  'roles/editor',
  'roles/secretmanager.admin',
  'roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor',
  'roles/run.admin',
  'roles/run.developer',
  'roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator',
])

function allowedWorkerGrantKeys(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
): readonly string[] {
  return [
    `//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/${input.projectId}\0roles/logging.logWriter`,
    `//storage.googleapis.com/projects/_/buckets/${input.sourceBucket}\0roles/storage.objectViewer`,
    `//artifactregistry.googleapis.com/projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}/repositories/${input.repository}\0roles/artifactregistry.writer`,
  ]
}

function grantKey(resource: string, role: string): string {
  return `${resource}\0${role}`
}

const projectSchema = z.object({
  projectId: z.string().min(1),
  projectNumber: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+$/),
}).strict()
const serviceAccountSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1),
  email: z.string().email(),
  disabled: z.boolean().optional(),
}).passthrough()
const bucketSchema = z.object({ name: z.string(), location: z.string() }).passthrough()
const repositorySchema = z.object({ name: z.string(), format: z.literal('DOCKER') }).passthrough()

function phaseOptions(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  timeoutMs: number,
  output: RunCommandOptions['output'] = {
    kind: 'capture',
    maxStdoutBytes: 1024 * 1024,
    maxStderrBytes: 64 * 1024,
  },
): RunCommandOptions {
  return {
    cwd: input.repoRoot,
    signal: input.signal,
    output,
    timeoutMs,
    terminateGraceMs: input.budgets.terminateGraceMs,
    settlementTimeoutMs: input.budgets.settlementMs,
  }
}

function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
}

function errorChain(error: unknown): string[] {
  const values = [errorMessage(error)]
  if (error instanceof AggregateError) {
    for (const child of error.errors) values.push(...errorChain(child))
  } else if (error instanceof Error && error.cause !== undefined) {
    values.push(...errorChain(error.cause))
  }
  return values
}

function transportErrorCode(error: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') return undefined
  const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code
  return typeof code === 'string' ? code : undefined
}

const retryableHttpStatusCodes = new Set([429, 500, 502, 503, 504])
const retryableTransportCodes = new Set([
  'ECONNRESET',
  'ETIMEDOUT',
  'EAI_AGAIN',
  'UND_ERR_SOCKET',
  'UND_ERR_CONNECT_TIMEOUT',
])

async function waitForRetry(
  milliseconds: number,
  signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
): Promise<void> {
  if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Lifecycle aborted during read retry')
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const finish = () => {
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort)
      resolve()
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(finish, milliseconds)
    const abort = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer)
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort)
      reject(new Error('Lifecycle aborted during read retry'))
    }
    signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true })
  })
}

export async function gcloudJson<T>(
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
  args: readonly string[],
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  timeoutMs = input.budgets.commandMs,
): Promise<T> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs
  let lastFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt += 1) {
    if (input.signal?.aborted) {
      throw new Error(`Lifecycle aborted before idempotent read: ${args.slice(0, 3).join(' ')}`)
    }
    const remaining = deadline - Date.now()
    if (remaining <= 0) throw new Error('Idempotent read deadline exhausted')
    try {
      const result = await runCommand(
        'gcloud',
        [...args],
        phaseOptions(input, remaining),
      )
      if (result.exitCode === 0 && result.signal === null && !result.timedOut) {
        let decoded: unknown
        try {
          decoded = JSON.parse(result.stdout)
        } catch (error) {
          throw new Error(`Read returned non-JSON output: ${errorMessage(error)}`)
        }
        return schema.parse(decoded)
      }
      lastFailure = result.timedOut
        ? Object.assign(
            new Error('Idempotent gcloud read timed out'),
            { code: 'ETIMEDOUT' },
          )
        : new Error(
            `gcloud exact read failed (exit=${String(result.exitCode)}, ` +
            `signal=${String(result.signal)}); stderr is diagnostic only: ` +
            args.slice(0, 3).join(' '),
          )
      if (result.timedOut) {
        // The final RunCommand has already closed its owned process group.
      } else {
        throw lastFailure
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (input.signal?.aborted) throw error
      const code = transportErrorCode(error)
      if (!code || !retryableTransportCodes.has(code)) throw error
      lastFailure = error
    }
    if (attempt === 3 || Date.now() >= deadline) break
    await waitForRetry(Math.min(250 * attempt, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())), input.signal)
  }
  throw new AggregateError(
    lastFailure === undefined ? [] : [lastFailure],
    `Idempotent gcloud read exhausted three attempts: ${args.slice(0, 3).join(' ')}`,
  )
}

async function checkedCommand(
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  command: string,
  args: readonly string[],
  options: RunCommandOptions,
): Promise<CommandResult> {
  const result = await runCommand(command, args, options)
  if (result.exitCode !== 0 || result.signal !== null || result.timedOut) {
    throw new Error(`${command} ${args.slice(0, 3).join(' ')} failed`)
  }
  return result
}
```

```ts
const healthyAnalysisStateSchema = z.object({
  code: z.literal('OK').optional(),
  cause: z.never().optional(),
}).strict()
const rawAccessSchema = z.object({
  analysisState: healthyAnalysisStateSchema.optional(),
  permission: z.string().min(1).optional(),
  role: z.string().min(1).optional(),
}).strict().refine(
  ({ permission, role }) => (permission === undefined) !== (role === undefined),
  'access must contain exactly one permission or role',
)
const rawResourceSchema = z.object({
  analysisState: healthyAnalysisStateSchema.optional(),
  fullResourceName: z.string().min(1),
}).strict()
const rawAccessControlListSchema = z.object({
  accesses: z.array(rawAccessSchema).min(1),
  resources: z.array(rawResourceSchema).min(1),
  conditionEvaluation: z.never().optional(),
  resourceEdges: z.never().optional(),
}).strict()
const rawIdentitySchema = z.object({
  analysisState: healthyAnalysisStateSchema.optional(),
  name: z.string().min(1),
}).strict()
const rawAnalysisResultSchema = z.object({
  accessControlLists: z.array(rawAccessControlListSchema).min(1),
  attachedResourceFullName: z.string().min(1),
  fullyExplored: z.literal(true),
  iamBinding: z.object({
    members: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1),
    role: z.string().min(1),
    condition: z.never().optional(),
  }).strict(),
  identityList: z.object({
    identities: z.array(rawIdentitySchema).min(1),
    groupEdges: z.never().optional(),
  }).strict(),
}).strict()
const rawAnalysisQuerySchema = z.object({
  scope: z.string().regex(/^organizations\/[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  identitySelector: z.object({ identity: z.string().min(1) }).strict(),
  options: z.object({
    expandGroups: z.literal(true),
    expandRoles: z.literal(true),
  }).strict(),
  accessSelector: z.object({
    permissions: z.tuple([z.string().min(1)]),
    roles: z.never().optional(),
  }).strict().optional(),
  conditionContext: z.never().optional(),
  resourceSelector: z.never().optional(),
}).strict()
const rawAnalyzeIamPolicyResponseSchema = z.object({
  fullyExplored: z.literal(true),
  mainAnalysis: z.object({
    analysisQuery: rawAnalysisQuerySchema,
    analysisResults: z.array(rawAnalysisResultSchema).optional(),
    denyAnalysisResults: z.tuple([]).optional(),
    fullyExplored: z.literal(true),
    nonCriticalErrors: z.tuple([]).optional(),
  }).strict(),
  serviceAccountImpersonationAnalysis: z.tuple([]).optional(),
}).strict()
const ancestrySchema = z.array(z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  type: z.enum(['project', 'folder', 'organization']),
}).strict()).max(64)
const emptyDimensionsSchema = z.record(z.string(), z.string())
  .refine((value) => Object.keys(value).length === 0)
const quotaInfoSchema = z.object({
  metric: z.literal('cloudbuild.googleapis.com/ongoing_builds'),
  quotaId: z.literal('OngoingBuildsPerProject'),
  dimensions: z.tuple([]).optional(),
  dimensionsInfos: z.tuple([
    z.object({
      applicableLocations: z.tuple([z.literal('global')]),
      dimensions: emptyDimensionsSchema.optional(),
      details: z.object({
        value: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+$/),
      }).strict(),
    }).strict(),
  ]),
}).strict()

function callerMember(account: string): string {
  if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+$/.test(account)) throw new Error('Caller account must be an email')
  return account.endsWith('.gserviceaccount.com')
    ? `serviceAccount:${account}`
    : `user:${account}`
}

function requireDedicatedServiceAccount(
  value: z.infer<typeof serviceAccountSchema>,
  expectedEmail: string,
  label: string,
): void {
  const expectedName = `projects/${expectedEmail.split('@')[1].split('.')[0]}/serviceAccounts/${expectedEmail}`
  if (value.email !== expectedEmail || value.name !== expectedName || value.disabled === true) {
    throw new Error(`${label} is not the exact enabled configured service account`)
  }
  if (/developer\.gserviceaccount\.com$/.test(value.email) ||
      /(?:compute|cloudbuild|appspot)/i.test(value.email.split('@')[0])) {
    throw new Error(`${label} must be a dedicated non-default identity`)
  }
}

function requireExactMultiset(
  actual: readonly string[],
  expected: readonly string[],
  label: string,
): void {
  const values = [...actual].sort()
  const wanted = [...expected].sort()
  if (JSON.stringify(values) !== JSON.stringify(wanted)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} mismatch: ${JSON.stringify(values)}`)
  }
}

export async function readEffectiveReport(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  permission?: string,
): Promise<z.infer<typeof rawAnalyzeIamPolicyResponseSchema>> {
  const organizationId =
    input.iamAnalysisScope.slice('organizations/'.length)
  const report = await gcloudJson(rawAnalyzeIamPolicyResponseSchema, [
    'asset', 'analyze-iam-policy',
    `--organization=${organizationId}`,
    `--identity=${identity}`,
    ...(permission === undefined ? [] : [`--permissions=${permission}`]),
    '--expand-groups',
    '--expand-roles',
    '--show-response',
    `--account=${input.account}`,
    `--billing-project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ], input, runCommand)
  const query = report.mainAnalysis.analysisQuery
  if (query.scope !== input.iamAnalysisScope ||
      query.identitySelector.identity !== identity ||
      query.options.expandGroups !== true ||
      query.options.expandRoles !== true) {
    throw new Error('Cloud Asset analysis did not echo exact scope/identity/options')
  }
  const echoedPermissions =
    query.accessSelector?.permissions
  if (permission === undefined
    ? echoedPermissions !== undefined
    : JSON.stringify(echoedPermissions) !== JSON.stringify([permission])) {
    throw new Error('Cloud Asset analysis did not echo exact permission selector')
  }
  for (const result of report.mainAnalysis.analysisResults ?? []) {
    if (result.identityList.identities.length !== 1 ||
        result.identityList.identities[0].name !== identity ||
        !result.iamBinding.role.startsWith('roles/')) {
      throw new Error('Cloud Asset result has foreign identity or custom role')
    }
    if (permission === undefined) {
      if (forbiddenWorkerRoles.has(result.iamBinding.role)) {
        throw new Error(`Forbidden worker role ${result.iamBinding.role}`)
      }
      for (const acl of result.accessControlLists) {
        if (!acl.accesses.every(({ role }) =>
            role === result.iamBinding.role) ||
            acl.resources.length !== 1 ||
            acl.resources[0].fullResourceName !==
              result.attachedResourceFullName) {
          throw new Error('Worker ACL differs from attached binding/resource')
        }
      }
    } else {
      if (!result.iamBinding.members.includes(identity)) {
        throw new Error('Effective permission is not a direct caller binding')
      }
      for (const acl of result.accessControlLists) {
        if (!acl.accesses.some(({ permission: value }) => value === permission)) {
          throw new Error('Effective permission result omitted requested permission')
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return report
}

async function readEffectiveWorkerGrants(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  email: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<readonly string[]> {
  const identity = `serviceAccount:${email}`
  const report = await readEffectiveReport(input, identity, runCommand)
  return (report.mainAnalysis.analysisResults ?? [])
    .map((row) => grantKey(
      row.attachedResourceFullName,
      row.iamBinding.role,
    ))
    .sort()
}

async function readEffectivePermissionResources(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  permission: 'iam.serviceAccounts.actAs' | 'iam.serviceAccounts.getAccessToken',
  role: 'roles/iam.serviceAccountUser' | 'roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator',
): Promise<readonly string[]> {
  const member = callerMember(input.account)
  const report = await readEffectiveReport(input, member, runCommand, permission)
  return (report.mainAnalysis.analysisResults ?? []).map((result) => {
    if (result.iamBinding.role !== role ||
        !result.iamBinding.members.includes(member)) {
      throw new Error(`Effective ${permission} used a foreign role/member`)
    }
    const resources = result.accessControlLists.flatMap((acl) =>
      acl.resources.map(({ fullResourceName }) => fullResourceName))
    if (resources.length !== 1 ||
        resources[0] !== result.attachedResourceFullName) {
      throw new Error(`Effective ${permission} resource projection is ambiguous`)
    }
    return resources[0]
  }).sort()
}

function exactRepositoryResource(input: CloudRunLifecycleInput): string {
  return `projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}/repositories/${input.repository}`
}

export async function verifyCloudPrerequisites(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<CloudPrerequisites> {
  if (input.buildRegion !== 'global') throw new Error('Build region must be global')
  if (!/^organizations\/[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(input.iamAnalysisScope)) {
    throw new Error('IAM analysis scope must be one organization authority root')
  }
  if (input.buildServiceAccount === input.runtimeServiceAccount) {
    throw new Error('Build and runtime service accounts must be distinct')
  }
  const [project, buildAccount, runtimeAccount] = await Promise.all([
    gcloudJson(projectSchema, [
      'projects', 'describe', input.projectId,
      `--account=${input.account}`,
      '--format=json(projectId,projectNumber)',
    ], input, runCommand),
    gcloudJson(serviceAccountSchema, [
      'iam', 'service-accounts', 'describe', input.buildServiceAccount,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
      '--format=json(name,email,disabled)',
    ], input, runCommand),
    gcloudJson(serviceAccountSchema, [
      'iam', 'service-accounts', 'describe', input.runtimeServiceAccount,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
      '--format=json(name,email,disabled)',
    ], input, runCommand),
  ])
  if (project.projectId !== input.projectId) throw new Error('Project ID mismatch')
  requireDedicatedServiceAccount(buildAccount, input.buildServiceAccount, 'Build identity')
  requireDedicatedServiceAccount(runtimeAccount, input.runtimeServiceAccount, 'Runtime identity')

  const member = callerMember(input.account)
  const ancestry = await gcloudJson(ancestrySchema, [
    'projects', 'get-ancestors', input.projectId,
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json(id,type)',
  ], input, runCommand)
  const organizations = ancestry.filter(({ type }) => type === 'organization')
  if (organizations.length !== 1 ||
      `organizations/${organizations[0].id}` !== input.iamAnalysisScope) {
    throw new Error('Project ancestry does not match the configured organization authority')
  }

  const [buildGrants, runtimeGrants, actAsResources, tokenMintResources] =
    await Promise.all([
    readEffectiveWorkerGrants(input, input.buildServiceAccount, runCommand),
    readEffectiveWorkerGrants(input, input.runtimeServiceAccount, runCommand),
    readEffectivePermissionResources(
      input,
      runCommand,
      'iam.serviceAccounts.actAs',
      'roles/iam.serviceAccountUser',
    ),
    readEffectivePermissionResources(
      input,
      runCommand,
      'iam.serviceAccounts.getAccessToken',
      'roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator',
    ),
  ])
  requireExactMultiset(
    buildGrants,
    [...allowedWorkerGrantKeys(input)],
    'Build effective grants',
  )
  requireExactMultiset(runtimeGrants, [], 'Runtime effective grants')
  const buildServiceAccountResource =
    `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/${input.projectId}` +
    `/serviceAccounts/${input.buildServiceAccount}`
  const runtimeServiceAccountResource =
    `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/${input.projectId}` +
    `/serviceAccounts/${input.runtimeServiceAccount}`
  requireExactMultiset(
    actAsResources,
    [buildServiceAccountResource, runtimeServiceAccountResource],
    'caller actAs resources',
  )
  requireExactMultiset(
    tokenMintResources,
    [buildServiceAccountResource],
    'caller token-mint resources',
  )

  const [bucketRows, repositoryRows, quotaInfo] = await Promise.all([
    gcloudJson(z.array(bucketSchema).max(2), [
      'storage', 'buckets', 'list',
      `--filter=name="${input.sourceBucket}"`, '--limit=2',
      `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
      '--format=json(name,location)',
    ], input, runCommand),
    gcloudJson(z.array(repositorySchema).max(2), [
      'artifacts', 'repositories', 'list',
      `--filter=name="${exactRepositoryResource(input)}"`, '--limit=2',
      `--location=${input.runRegion}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`, '--format=json(name,format)',
    ], input, runCommand),
    gcloudJson(quotaInfoSchema, [
      'beta', 'quotas', 'info', 'describe', 'OngoingBuildsPerProject',
      '--service=cloudbuild.googleapis.com',
      `--project=${input.projectId}`,
      `--account=${input.account}`,
      '--format=json(metric,quotaId,dimensions,dimensionsInfos)',
    ], input, runCommand),
  ])
  if (bucketRows.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
      `Source bucket exact list returned ${bucketRows.length} rows`,
    )
  }
  const bucket = bucketRows[0]
  if (bucket.name !== input.sourceBucket || bucket.location !== 'US') {
    throw new Error('Source bucket must be the exact pre-existing US bucket')
  }
  const repositoryResource = exactRepositoryResource(input)
  if (repositoryRows.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
      `Artifact repository exact list returned ${repositoryRows.length} rows`,
    )
  }
  const repository = repositoryRows[0]
  if (repository.name !== repositoryResource || repository.format !== 'DOCKER') {
    throw new Error('Artifact Registry repository mismatch')
  }
  const limit = Number(quotaInfo.dimensionsInfos[0].details.value)
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 2) {
    throw new Error('Non-regional ongoing_builds quota >= 2 is required')
  }
  return {
    projectId: project.projectId,
    projectNumber: project.projectNumber,
    iamAnalysisScope: input.iamAnalysisScope,
    sourceBucket: bucket.name,
    sourceBucketLocation: 'US',
    repositoryResource,
    globalOngoingBuildLimit: limit,
    buildServiceAccount: buildAccount.email,
    runtimeServiceAccount: runtimeAccount.email,
    effectiveIam: {
      analysisScope: input.iamAnalysisScope,
      caller: member,
      buildEffectiveGrantKeys: [...buildGrants].sort(),
      runtimeEffectiveGrantKeys: [...runtimeGrants].sort(),
      actAsResources,
      tokenMintResources,
    },
    quota: {
      metric: 'cloudbuild.googleapis.com/ongoing_builds',
      dimensions: {},
      limit,
    },
  }
}
```

```ts
function serviceAccountPrincipal(email: string): string {
  if (!email.endsWith('.gserviceaccount.com')) {
    throw new Error('Worker principal must be a service account')
  }
  return `serviceAccount:${email}`
}
```

**Interfaces produced:**

```ts
export type SourceIdentity = {
  bucket: string
  object: string
  generation: string
  size: string
  commit: string
}
export type ImageIdentity = {
  taggedImage: string
  tag: string
  tagResourceName: string
  digest: string
  immutableImage: string
  digestVersionResourceName: string
  localTags: readonly string[]
}
export type BuildAuthority = {
  projectId: string
  projectNumber: string
}
export type ActiveBuildStatus = 'PENDING' | 'QUEUED' | 'WORKING'
export type TerminalBuildStatus =
  | 'STATUS_UNKNOWN'
  | 'SUCCESS'
  | 'FAILURE'
  | 'INTERNAL_ERROR'
  | 'CANCELLED'
  | 'TIMEOUT'
  | 'EXPIRED'
export type BuildStatus = ActiveBuildStatus | TerminalBuildStatus
export type CorrelatedBuildIdentity = {
  correlationTag: string
  id: string
  resourceName: string
  projectId: string
  projectNumber: string
  createTime: string
  startTime: string
  finishTime?: string
  serviceAccount: string
  status: BuildStatus
  normalizedConfig: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>
}
export type BuildIdentity = Omit<
  CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
  'finishTime' | 'status'
> & {
  finishTime: string
  status: TerminalBuildStatus
}
export type CorrelationWindow = {
  notBeforeMs: number
  notAfterMs: number
}
export type Clock = () => number
export type BuildTemplate = {
  steps: readonly {
    name: string
    args: readonly string[]
  }[]
  images: readonly ['${_IMAGE}']
  options: {
    machineType: 'E2_HIGHCPU_32'
    diskSizeGb: 200
    logging: 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY'
  }
  timeout: '3600s'
}
export type BuiltImage = {
  build: BuildIdentity
  image: ImageIdentity
}
```

```ts
const uploadOwnedSource: UploadOwnedSource = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<UploadOwnedSource>('uploadOwnedSource')(...args)
const readStrictBuildTemplate: ReadStrictBuildTemplate = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ReadStrictBuildTemplate>('readStrictBuildTemplate')(...args)
const startBuildFromGeneration: StartBuildFromGeneration = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<StartBuildFromGeneration>('startBuildFromGeneration')(...args)
const finishBuildFromGeneration: FinishBuildFromGeneration = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<FinishBuildFromGeneration>('finishBuildFromGeneration')(...args)
const buildIsActive: BuildIsActive = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<BuildIsActive>('buildIsActive')(...args)
const readExactBuild: ReadExactBuild = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ReadExactBuild>('readExactBuild')(...args)
const waitForBuildTerminal: WaitForBuildTerminal = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<WaitForBuildTerminal>('waitForBuildTerminal')(...args)
const resolveProducedImage: ResolveProducedImage = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ResolveProducedImage>('resolveProducedImage')(...args)
const buildAndPushLocally: BuildAndPushLocally = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<BuildAndPushLocally>('buildAndPushLocally')(...args)

const commandOk = (value: unknown = {}): CommandResult => ({
  stdout: `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`,
  stderr: '',
  exitCode: 0,
  signal: null,
  timedOut: false,
})
type CommandStep = {
  command: string
  includes: readonly string[]
  exactArgs?: readonly string[]
  result?: CommandResult
  error?: Error
  assertOptions?: (options: RunCommandOptions) => void
}
function sequenceRunner(steps: readonly CommandStep[]): RunCommand {
  const queue = [...steps]
  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    const step = queue.shift()
    if (!step) throw new Error(`unexpected command: ${command} ${args.join(' ')}`)
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    expect(command).toBe(step.command)
    for (const token of step.includes) expect(text).toContain(token)
    if (step.exactArgs) expect(args).toEqual(step.exactArgs)
    step.assertOptions?.(options)
    if (step.error) throw step.error
    return step.result ?? commandOk()
  })
}

timeout: 3600s

async function makeSnapshot() {
  const tempRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'cloud-build-test-'))
  roots.push(tempRoot)
  const sourceDir = join(tempRoot, 'source')
  const dockerConfigDir = join(tempRoot, 'docker-config')
  await writeFile(join(tempRoot, 'source.tar.gz'), 'abc')
  await writeFile(join(tempRoot, 'cloudbuild.yaml'), TEMPLATE_TEXT)
  await mkdir(sourceDir, { mode: 0o700 })
  await mkdir(dockerConfigDir, { mode: 0o700 })
  await chmod(tempRoot, 0o700)
  return {
    branch: 'feature',
    upstreamRemote: 'origin',
    upstreamMergeRef: 'refs/heads/feature',
    head: 'a'.repeat(40),
    tempRoot,
    sourceArchive: join(tempRoot, 'source.tar.gz'),
    sourceDir,
    buildTemplatePath: join(tempRoot, 'cloudbuild.yaml'),
    dockerConfigDir,
  }
}

function buildRow(input = BASE_INPUT, identity = IDENTITY, source = SOURCE) {
  return {

        object: source.object,

        sourceFetcher: 'GSUTIL',
      },
      sourceProvenance: {
        resolvedStorageSource: {
          bucket: source.bucket,
          object: source.object,
          generation: source.generation,
        },
      },
    },
    substitutions: { _IMAGE: `${IMAGE_PATH}:${identity.imageTag}` },
    steps: [
      {
        name: `gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker@sha256:${'6'.repeat(64)}`,
        args: ['build', '--no-cache', '--file', 'docker/cloud-run/Dockerfile', '--tag', '${_IMAGE}', '.'],
        id: 'build-image',
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        timing: { startTime: testIso(2_000), endTime: testIso(40_000) },
        pullTiming: { startTime: testIso(1_000), endTime: testIso(2_000) },
      },
      {
        name: `gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker@sha256:${'6'.repeat(64)}`,
        args: ['push', '${_IMAGE}'],

        status: 'SUCCESS',

      },
    ],
    images: ['${_IMAGE}'],
    options: { machineType: 'E2_HIGHCPU_32', diskSizeGb: 200, logging: 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY' },
    timeout: '3600s',
    results: { images: [{

    }],
    buildStepImages: [`sha256:${'e'.repeat(64)}`],
    artifactManifest: 'gs://provider-owned/result.json',
    },
    timing: { BUILD: { startTime: testIso(1_000), endTime: testIso(60_000) } },
  }
}
function correlatedBuildRows(
  fault:
    | 'zero'
    | 'two'
    | 'wrong-source'
    | 'wrong-image'
    | 'wrong-sa'
    | 'too-early'
    | 'too-late',
  input = BASE_INPUT,
  _snapshot: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof makeSnapshot>>,
  identity = IDENTITY,
  source = SOURCE,
) {
  if (fault === 'zero') return []
  const row = buildRow(input, identity, source)
  if (fault === 'two') {
    return [row, {
      ...row,
      id: 'build-2',
      name:
        `projects/${BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber}/locations/global/builds/build-2`,
    }]
  }
  if (fault === 'wrong-source') row.source.storageSource.generation = 'foreign'
  if (fault === 'wrong-image') row.substitutions._IMAGE = `${IMAGE_PATH}:foreign`
  if (fault === 'wrong-sa') row.serviceAccount = 'projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/foreign@example.com'
  if (fault === 'too-early') row.createTime = testIso(-5_001)
  if (fault === 'too-late') row.createTime = testIso(5_001)
  return [row]
}
function terminalBuildFixture(input = BASE_INPUT, identity = IDENTITY, source = SOURCE) {
  return {
    correlationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    id: 'build-1',
    resourceName:
      `projects/${BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber}/locations/global/builds/build-1`,
    projectId: BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectId,
    projectNumber: BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber,
    createTime: testIso(0),
    startTime: testIso(1_000),
    finishTime: testIso(60_000),
    serviceAccount: input.buildServiceAccount,
    status: 'SUCCESS' as const,
    normalizedConfig: buildRow(input, identity, source),
  }
}
function activeBuildFixture(input = BASE_INPUT, identity = IDENTITY, source = SOURCE) {
  const { finishTime: _finishTime, ...terminalFields } =
    buildRow(input, identity, source)
  const row = { ...terminalFields, status: 'WORKING' as const }
  return {
    correlationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    id: row.id,
    resourceName: row.name,
    projectId: BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectId,
    projectNumber: BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber,
    createTime: row.createTime,
    startTime: row.startTime,
    serviceAccount: input.buildServiceAccount,
    status: 'WORKING' as const,
    normalizedConfig: row,
  }
}
function localPushRunner(
  input: typeof BASE_INPUT,
  snapshot: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof makeSnapshot>>,
  identity = IDENTITY,
): RunCommand {
  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (command === 'docker') {
      expect(options.env?.DOCKER_CONFIG).toBe(snapshot.dockerConfigDir)
      if (args[0] === 'build') return commandOk()
      if (args[0] === 'login') {
        expect(String(options.stdin)).toBe('short-lived-token\n')
        expect(text).not.toContain('short-lived-token')
        return commandOk()
      }
      if (args[0] === 'push' || args[0] === 'image') return commandOk()
    }
    if (command === 'gcloud' && args.includes('print-access-token')) {
      return { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'short-lived-token\n' }
    }
    if (command === 'gcloud' && args.includes('tags') && args.includes('list')) {
      return commandOk([artifactTagRow()])
    }
    if (command === 'gcloud' && args.includes('versions') && args.includes('list')) {
      return commandOk([{
        name: `projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}/repositories/${input.repository}/packages/${input.imageName}/versions/sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      }])
    }
    throw new Error(`unhandled local push command ${text}`)
  })
}

it.each([
  ['timeout', Object.assign(new Error('timed out'), { code: 'ETIMEDOUT' }), false],
  ['abort', new Error('Command aborted'), true],
  ['spawn', Object.assign(new Error('spawn failed'), { code: 'ENOENT' }), false],
  ['overflow', new Error('stdout exceeded 65536 bytes'), false],
  ['settlement', new Error('Command settlement exceeded 5000ms'), false],
] as const)(
  'reconciles a possibly accepted source upload %s rejection without retrying',
  async (_case, commandFailure, abortDuringCopy) => {
    const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
    const controller = new AbortController()
    const runner = sequenceRunner([
      {
        command: 'gcloud',
        includes: ['storage', 'cp', '--if-generation-match=0'],
        error: commandFailure,
        assertOptions: () => {
          if (abortDuringCopy) controller.abort(new Error('caught SIGTERM'))
        },
      },
      {
        command: 'gcloud',
        includes: [
          'storage', 'objects', 'list', IDENTITY.sourceObject, '--limit=2',
        ],
        assertOptions: (options) => expect(options.signal).toBeUndefined(),
        result: commandOk([{
          bucket: BASE_INPUT.sourceBucket,
          name: IDENTITY.sourceObject,
          generation: '1786766419256749',
          size: '3',
          custom_fields: {
            'freshell-run-id': IDENTITY.runId,
            'freshell-head': snapshot.head,
          },
        }]),
      },
    ])
    await expect(uploadOwnedSource(
      { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal }, snapshot, IDENTITY, runner,
    )).resolves.toEqual(SOURCE)
    expect((commandTranscript(runner).match(/storage cp/g) ?? [])).toHaveLength(1)
  },
)

it('aggregates upload ambiguity and never retries create', async () => {
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  const runner = sequenceRunner([
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['storage', 'cp'], error: new Error('timeout') },
    {
      command: 'gcloud',
      includes: ['storage', 'objects', 'list', '--limit=2'],
      result: commandOk([{
        bucket: BASE_INPUT.sourceBucket,
        name: IDENTITY.sourceObject,
        generation: '9',
        size: '999',
        custom_fields: { 'freshell-run-id': 'foreign', 'freshell-head': snapshot.head },
      }]),
    },
  ])
  await expect(uploadOwnedSource(BASE_INPUT, snapshot, IDENTITY, runner))
    .rejects.toThrow('Source upload response was lost and ownership is ambiguous')
  expect((commandTranscript(runner).match(/storage cp/g) ?? [])).toHaveLength(1)
})

it('strictly rejects malformed extracted Build config before any mutation command', async () => {
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  await writeFile(snapshot.buildTemplatePath, 'steps: []\n')
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
  await expect(readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath))
    .rejects.toThrow('Build template')
  expect(runner).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
})

  ['STATUS_UNKNOWN', false],
  ['PENDING', true],
  ['QUEUED', true],
  ['WORKING', true],
  ['SUCCESS', false],
  ['FAILURE', false],

  ['EXPIRED', false],
] as const)('classifies documented Build status %s exactly', (status, active) => {
  expect(buildIsActive(status)).toBe(active)
})

it('parses full nested Build data and polls PENDING before terminal authority', async () => {
  const {
    finishTime: terminalFinishTime,
    ...pendingFields
  } = buildRow()
  expect(terminalFinishTime).toBeDefined()
  const pending = { ...pendingFields, status: 'PENDING' as const }
  const terminal = buildRow()
  const runner = sequenceRunner([
    {
      command: 'gcloud',
      includes: ['builds', 'list', terminal.id, '--region=global'],
      result: commandOk([pending]),
    },
    {
      command: 'gcloud',
      includes: ['builds', 'list', terminal.id, '--region=global'],
      result: commandOk([terminal]),
    },
  ])
  await expect(waitForBuildTerminal(
    BASE_INPUT,
    BUILD_AUTHORITY,
    terminal.id,
    runner,
  )).resolves.toMatchObject({
    id: terminal.id,
    name: terminal.name,
    projectId: BASE_INPUT.projectId,
    status: 'SUCCESS',
    source: {
      storageSource: { sourceFetcher: 'GSUTIL' },
      sourceProvenance: expect.any(Object),
    },
    steps: [
      expect.objectContaining({ id: 'build-image', timing: expect.any(Object) }),
      expect.objectContaining({ id: 'push-image', timing: expect.any(Object) }),
    ],
    results: {
      images: [expect.objectContaining({ pushTiming: expect.any(Object) })],
      buildStepImages: expect.any(Array),
    },
  })
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
})

it.each([
  ['foreign project ID', { projectId: 'foreign-project' }],
  ['numeric name segment', {
    name: 'projects/999999999/locations/global/builds/build-1',
  }],
  ['foreign Build ID', {
    id: 'build-foreign',
    name:
      `projects/${BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber}/locations/global/builds/build-foreign`,
  }],
] as const)('rejects %s on every exact Build read', async (_label, patch) => {
  const row = { ...buildRow(), ...patch }
  const runner = sequenceRunner([{
    command: 'gcloud',
    includes: ['builds', 'list', 'build-1'],
    result: commandOk([row]),
  }])
  await expect(readExactBuild(
    BASE_INPUT, BUILD_AUTHORITY, 'build-1', runner,
  )).rejects.toThrow('foreign ID/project/name authority')
})

it('recovers one accepted Build by the complete tuple despite lifecycle abort', async () => {
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  const template = await readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath)
  const created = buildRow(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY, SOURCE)
  const controller = new AbortController()
  const reconciled = (options: RunCommandOptions) =>
    expect(options.signal).toBeUndefined()
  const runner = sequenceRunner([
    {
      command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'submit', '--no-source'],
      assertOptions: () => controller.abort(new Error('caught SIGTERM')),
      error: new Error('settlement lost'),
    },
    {
      command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'list', IDENTITY.buildCorrelationTag],
      assertOptions: reconciled, result: commandOk([created]),
    },
    {
      command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'list', 'build-1'],
      assertOptions: reconciled, result: commandOk([created]),
    },
    {
      command: 'gcloud', includes: ['artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list'],
      assertOptions: reconciled,
      result: commandOk([artifactTagRow()]),
    },
    {
      command: 'gcloud', includes: ['artifacts', 'versions', 'list'],
      assertOptions: reconciled,
      result: commandOk([{
        name: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e/packages/freshell-vitest/versions/sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      }]),
    },
  ])
  const correlated = await startBuildFromGeneration(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal },
    BUILD_AUTHORITY, snapshot, IDENTITY, SOURCE, template, runner, TEST_WINDOW,
  )
  const built = await finishBuildFromGeneration(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal },
    IDENTITY,
    correlated,
    runner,
  )
  expect(built.build).toMatchObject({ id: 'build-1', status: 'SUCCESS' })
  expect((commandTranscript(runner).match(/builds submit/g) ?? [])).toHaveLength(1)
})

it('returns one fully correlated active Build before terminal waiting', async () => {
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  const template = await readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath)
  const { finishTime: _finishTime, ...activeFields } = buildRow()
  const active = { ...activeFields, status: 'WORKING' as const }
  const startRunner = sequenceRunner([{
    command: 'gcloud',
    includes: ['builds', 'submit', '--async'],
    result: commandOk(active),
  }])
  const correlated = await startBuildFromGeneration(
    BASE_INPUT,
    BUILD_AUTHORITY,
    snapshot,
    IDENTITY,
    SOURCE,
    template,
    startRunner,
    TEST_WINDOW,
  )
  expect(correlated).toMatchObject({
    id: active.id,
    resourceName: active.name,
    projectId: BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectId,
    projectNumber: BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber,
    correlationTag: IDENTITY.buildCorrelationTag,
    serviceAccount: BASE_INPUT.buildServiceAccount,
    status: 'WORKING',
  })
  expect(correlated.finishTime).toBeUndefined()
  expect(commandTranscript(startRunner)).not.toMatch(/builds list/)

  const controller = new AbortController()
  controller.abort(new Error('caught SIGTERM after active acquisition'))
  const finishRunner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (_command, _args, options) => {
    expect(options.signal).toBe(controller.signal)
    throw options.signal!.reason
  })
  await expect(finishBuildFromGeneration(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal },
    IDENTITY,
    correlated,
    finishRunner,
  )).rejects.toThrow('caught SIGTERM after active acquisition')
  expect(correlated).toMatchObject({ id: active.id, status: 'WORKING' })
  expect(correlated.finishTime).toBeUndefined()
})
```

```ts
const storageObjectSchema = z.object({
  bucket: z.string().min(1),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  size: z.string().regex(/^(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/),
  custom_fields: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
}).strict()
const storageObjectRowsSchema = z.array(storageObjectSchema).max(2)

async function listExactSourceObjects(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  bucket: string,
  object: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  timeoutMs = input.budgets.sourceMs,
): Promise<readonly z.infer<typeof storageObjectSchema>[]> {
  return gcloudJson(storageObjectRowsSchema, [
    'storage', 'objects', 'list', `gs://${bucket}`,
    `--filter=name="${object}"`, '--limit=2',
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json(bucket,name,generation,size,custom_fields)',
  ], input, runCommand, timeoutMs)
}

function mutationReconciliationInput(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
): CloudRunLifecycleInput {
  const copy: CloudRunLifecycleInput = { ...input }
  delete copy.signal
  return copy
}

function exactSourceIdentity(
  row: z.infer<typeof storageObjectSchema>,
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  expectedSize: string,
): SourceIdentity {
  if (row.bucket !== input.sourceBucket || row.name !== identity.sourceObject ||
      row.size !== expectedSize ||
      row.custom_fields['freshell-run-id'] !== identity.runId ||
      row.custom_fields['freshell-head'] !== snapshot.head) {
    throw new Error('Uploaded source identity did not exact-match')
  }
  return {
    bucket: row.bucket,
    object: row.name,
    generation: row.generation,
    size: row.size,
    commit: snapshot.head,
  }
}

export async function uploadOwnedSource(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<SourceIdentity> {
  const uri = `gs://${input.sourceBucket}/${identity.sourceObject}`
  const expectedSize = String((await stat(snapshot.sourceArchive)).size)
  let possiblyAcceptedFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    const copy = await runCommand('gcloud', [
      'storage', 'cp', snapshot.sourceArchive, uri,
      '--if-generation-match=0',
      `--custom-metadata=freshell-run-id=${identity.runId},freshell-head=${snapshot.head}`,
      `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.sourceMs))
    if (copy.exitCode !== 0 || copy.signal !== null || copy.timedOut) {
      possiblyAcceptedFailure = new Error('Source upload returned an unreadable outcome')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    possiblyAcceptedFailure = error
  }

  try {
    const reconciliationInput = mutationReconciliationInput(input)
    const rows = await listExactSourceObjects(
      reconciliationInput,
      input.sourceBucket,
      identity.sourceObject,
      runCommand,
    )
    const exact = rows.filter((row) =>
      row.bucket === input.sourceBucket &&
      row.name === identity.sourceObject)
    if (exact.length !== 1) {
      throw new Error(
        `Source reconciliation requires one exact list row; got ${exact.length}`,
      )
    }
    const row = exact[0]
    return exactSourceIdentity(row, input, snapshot, identity, expectedSize)
  } catch (reconciliationFailure) {
    if (possiblyAcceptedFailure === undefined) throw reconciliationFailure
    throw new AggregateError(
      [possiblyAcceptedFailure, reconciliationFailure],
      'Source upload response was lost and ownership is ambiguous',
    )
  }
}
```

The template parser and request generator are asynchronous, strict, and freeze
the extracted bytes before any mutation:

```ts
const pinnedDockerBuilder = /^gcr\.io\/cloud-builders\/docker@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/
const buildTemplateSchema: z.ZodType<BuildTemplate> = z.object({
  steps: z.tuple([
    z.object({
      name: z.string().regex(pinnedDockerBuilder),
      args: z.tuple([
        z.literal('build'), z.literal('--no-cache'), z.literal('--file'),
        z.literal('docker/cloud-run/Dockerfile'), z.literal('--tag'),
        z.literal('${_IMAGE}'), z.literal('.'),
      ]),
    }).strict(),
    z.object({
      name: z.string().regex(pinnedDockerBuilder),
      args: z.tuple([z.literal('push'), z.literal('${_IMAGE}')]),
    }).strict(),
  ]),
  images: z.tuple([z.literal('${_IMAGE}')]),
  options: z.object({
    machineType: z.literal('E2_HIGHCPU_32'),
    diskSizeGb: z.literal(200),
    logging: z.literal('CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY'),
  }).strict(),
  timeout: z.literal('3600s'),
}).strict()

function deepFreeze<T>(value: T): Readonly<T> {
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Object.isFrozen(value)) {
    Object.freeze(value)
    for (const child of Object.values(value as Record<string, unknown>)) deepFreeze(child)
  }
  return value
}

export async function readStrictBuildTemplate(path: string): Promise<Readonly<BuildTemplate>> {
  const raw = await readFile(path, 'utf8')
  let decoded: unknown
  try {
    decoded = parseYaml(raw)
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(`Build template is not YAML: ${errorMessage(error)}`)
  }
  try {
    return deepFreeze(buildTemplateSchema.parse(decoded))
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(`Build template violates the exact cache-free contract: ${errorMessage(error)}`)
  }
}

export function generateBuildRequest(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  source: SourceIdentity,
  template: Readonly<BuildTemplate>,
): Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> {
  buildTemplateSchema.parse(template)
  const taggedImage = `${imagePath(input)}:${identity.imageTag}`
  return deepFreeze({
    ...template,
    source: { storageSource: {
      bucket: source.bucket,
      object: source.object,
      generation: source.generation,
    } },
    serviceAccount:
      `projects/${input.projectId}/serviceAccounts/${input.buildServiceAccount}`,
    tags: [identity.buildCorrelationTag, `freshell-run-${identity.runId}`],
    substitutions: { _IMAGE: taggedImage },
  })
}

const buildImageRowSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1),
  digest: z.string().regex(/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/),
}).passthrough()
const buildStatusSchema = z.enum([
  'STATUS_UNKNOWN',
  'PENDING',
  'QUEUED',
  'WORKING',
  'SUCCESS',
  'FAILURE',
  'INTERNAL_ERROR',
  'TIMEOUT',
  'CANCELLED',
  'EXPIRED',
])
const activeBuildStatusSchema = z.enum(['PENDING', 'QUEUED', 'WORKING'])
const terminalBuildStatusSchema = z.enum([
  'STATUS_UNKNOWN',
  'SUCCESS',
  'FAILURE',
  'INTERNAL_ERROR',
  'TIMEOUT',
  'CANCELLED',
  'EXPIRED',
])
const buildRowSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$/),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  projectId: z.string().min(1),
  status: buildStatusSchema,
  createTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),
  startTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }).optional(),
  finishTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }).optional(),
  serviceAccount: z.string(),
  tags: z.array(z.string()),
  source: z.object({ storageSource: z.object({
    bucket: z.string(),
    object: z.string(),
    generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  }).passthrough() }).passthrough(),
  substitutions: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
  steps: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    args: z.array(z.string()),
  }).passthrough()).optional(),
  images: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
  options: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()).optional(),
  timeout: z.string().optional(),
  results: z.object({
    images: z.array(buildImageRowSchema),
  }).passthrough().optional(),
}).passthrough()
type BuildRow = z.infer<typeof buildRowSchema>

export function correlationWindow(now: Clock = Date.now): CorrelationWindow {
  const instant = now()
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(instant)) throw new Error('Clock returned an invalid instant')
  return { notBeforeMs: instant - 5_000, notAfterMs: instant + 5_000 }
}

export function buildIsActive(status: z.infer<typeof buildStatusSchema>): boolean {
  return activeBuildStatusSchema.safeParse(status).success
}

function buildResourceName(authority: BuildAuthority, id: string): string {
  if (authority.projectId.length === 0 ||
      !/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(authority.projectNumber) ||
      !/^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$/.test(id)) {
    throw new Error('Invalid Build authority')
  }
  return `projects/${authority.projectNumber}/locations/global/builds/${id}`
}

function buildHasAuthority(
  row: BuildRow,
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  expectedId = row.id,
): boolean {
  return authority.projectId === input.projectId &&
    row.id === expectedId &&
    row.projectId === authority.projectId &&
    row.name === buildResourceName(authority, expectedId)
}

function buildMatchesCorrelation(
  row: BuildRow,
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  source: SourceIdentity,
  taggedImage: string,
  window: CorrelationWindow,
): boolean {
  const created = Date.parse(row.createTime)
  return Number.isFinite(created) &&
    buildHasAuthority(row, input, authority) &&
    created >= window.notBeforeMs && created <= window.notAfterMs &&
    row.tags.includes(identity.buildCorrelationTag) &&
    row.tags.includes(`freshell-run-${identity.runId}`) &&
    row.source.storageSource.bucket === source.bucket &&
    row.source.storageSource.object === source.object &&
    row.source.storageSource.generation === source.generation &&
    row.substitutions._IMAGE === taggedImage &&
    row.serviceAccount ===
      `projects/${input.projectId}/serviceAccounts/${input.buildServiceAccount}`
}

function acceptedBuildRequest(
  row: BuildRow,
): Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> {
  return deepFreeze({
    source: { storageSource: {
      bucket: row.source.storageSource.bucket,
      object: row.source.storageSource.object,
      generation: row.source.storageSource.generation,
    } },
    serviceAccount: row.serviceAccount,
    tags: [...row.tags],
    substitutions: { _IMAGE: row.substitutions._IMAGE },
    steps: row.steps?.map(({ name, args }) => ({ name, args })),
    images: row.images,
    options: row.options === undefined ? undefined : {
      machineType: row.options.machineType,
      diskSizeGb: row.options.diskSizeGb,
      logging: row.options.logging,
    },
    timeout: row.timeout,
  })
}

function correlatedBuildIdentity(
  row: BuildRow,
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  expectedRequest: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>,
): CorrelatedBuildIdentity {
  const status = buildStatusSchema.parse(row.status)
  if (!buildHasAuthority(row, input, authority)) {
    throw new Error('Correlated Build identity is outside exact project authority')
  }
  const acceptedRequest = acceptedBuildRequest(row)
  const requestComparable = {
    source: expectedRequest.source,
    serviceAccount: expectedRequest.serviceAccount,
    tags: expectedRequest.tags,
    substitutions: expectedRequest.substitutions,
    steps: expectedRequest.steps,
    images: expectedRequest.images,
    options: expectedRequest.options,
    timeout: expectedRequest.timeout,
  }
  if (JSON.stringify(acceptedRequest) !== JSON.stringify(requestComparable)) {
    throw new Error('Build accepted config differs from frozen request')
  }
  const accepted = deepFreeze(buildRowSchema.parse(row))
  return {
    correlationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    id: row.id,
    resourceName: row.name,
    projectId: row.projectId,
    projectNumber: authority.projectNumber,
    createTime: row.createTime,
    startTime: row.startTime ?? row.createTime,
    ...(row.finishTime === undefined ? {} : { finishTime: row.finishTime }),
    serviceAccount: row.serviceAccount.replace(/^projects\/[^/]+\/serviceAccounts\//, ''),
    status,
    normalizedConfig: accepted,
  }
}

function terminalBuildIdentity(
  correlated: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
): BuildIdentity {
  const status = terminalBuildStatusSchema.parse(correlated.status)
  if (correlated.finishTime === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`Terminal Build ${correlated.id} omitted finishTime`)
  }
  return {
    ...correlated,
    finishTime: correlated.finishTime,
    status,
  }
}

function immutableBuildOwnership(
  build: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
): Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const row = buildRowSchema.parse(build.normalizedConfig)
  return {
    correlationTag: build.correlationTag,
    id: build.id,
    resourceName: build.resourceName,
    projectId: build.projectId,
    projectNumber: build.projectNumber,
    createTime: build.createTime,
    serviceAccount: build.serviceAccount,
    request: acceptedBuildRequest(row),
  }
}

function sameImmutableBuildOwnership(
  left: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
  right: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
): boolean {
  return JSON.stringify(immutableBuildOwnership(left)) ===
    JSON.stringify(immutableBuildOwnership(right))
}

export async function readExactBuild(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  id: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  timeoutMs = input.budgets.commandMs,
): Promise<BuildRow> {
  const rows = await gcloudJson(z.array(buildRowSchema).max(2), [
    'builds', 'list', '--region=global',
    `--filter=id="${id}"`, '--limit=2',
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ], input, runCommand, timeoutMs)
  const exact = rows.filter((row) =>
    buildHasAuthority(row, input, authority, id))
  if (exact.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
      `Build exact list requires one authoritative row; got ${exact.length}`,
    )
  }
  const row = exact[0]
  if (!buildHasAuthority(row, input, authority, id)) {
    throw new Error('Build exact list returned foreign ID/project/name authority')
  }
  return row
}

export async function waitForBuildTerminal(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  id: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<BuildRow> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.buildMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const row = await readExactBuild(
      input, authority, id, runCommand, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now()),
    )
    if (!buildIsActive(row.status)) return row
    await waitForRetry(Math.min(2_000, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())), input.signal)
  }
  throw new Error(`Build ${id} did not become terminal within build budget`)
}

class TerminalBuildError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly build: BuildIdentity) {
    super(`Build ${build.id} ended ${build.status}`)
    this.name = 'TerminalBuildError'
  }
}

export async function startBuildFromGeneration(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  source: SourceIdentity,
  template: Readonly<BuildTemplate>,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  window: CorrelationWindow = correlationWindow(),
): Promise<CorrelatedBuildIdentity> {
  const request = generateBuildRequest(input, snapshot, identity, source, template)
  const configPath = join(snapshot.tempRoot, `build-${identity.runId}.json`)
  await writeFile(configPath, `${JSON.stringify(request)}\n`, { mode: 0o600 })
  const taggedImage = `${imagePath(input)}:${identity.imageTag}`
  let row: BuildRow | undefined
  let possiblyAcceptedFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    const result = await runCommand('gcloud', [
      'builds', 'submit', '--no-source', `--config=${configPath}`,
      `--service-account=projects/${input.projectId}/serviceAccounts/${input.buildServiceAccount}`,
      '--async', `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
      '--region=global', '--format=json',
    ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.commandMs))
    if (result.exitCode === 0 && result.signal === null && !result.timedOut) {
      try {
        row = buildRowSchema.parse(JSON.parse(result.stdout))
      } catch (error) {
        possiblyAcceptedFailure = error
      }
    } else {
      possiblyAcceptedFailure = new Error('Build create returned an unreadable outcome')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    possiblyAcceptedFailure = error
  }
  // Once create may have been accepted, only exact-create correlation ignores
  // the lifecycle signal. Terminal waiting begins after ownership is returned.
  const reconciliationInput = mutationReconciliationInput(input)
  if (!row) {
    let rows: BuildRow[]
    try {
      rows = await gcloudJson(z.array(buildRowSchema).max(2), [
        'builds', 'list', '--region=global', '--limit=2',
        `--filter=tags=${identity.buildCorrelationTag}`,
        `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
        '--format=json',
      ], reconciliationInput, runCommand, input.budgets.commandMs)
    } catch (reconciliationFailure) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        [possiblyAcceptedFailure, reconciliationFailure].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
        'Build create response was lost and correlation is unreadable',
      )
    }
    const matches = rows.filter((candidate) => buildMatchesCorrelation(
      candidate, input, authority, identity, source, taggedImage, window,
    ))
    if (matches.length !== 1) {
      throw new AggregateError(
        possiblyAcceptedFailure === undefined ? [] : [possiblyAcceptedFailure],
        `Build recovery requires exactly one fully correlated Build; got ${matches.length}`,
      )
    }
    row = matches[0]
  } else if (!buildMatchesCorrelation(
    row, input, authority, identity, source, taggedImage, window,
  )) {
    throw new Error('Readable Build create response does not match correlation tuple')
  }
  return correlatedBuildIdentity(row, input, authority, identity, request)
}

export async function finishBuildFromGeneration(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  correlatedBuild: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<BuiltImage> {
  const authority: BuildAuthority = {
    projectId: correlatedBuild.projectId,
    projectNumber: correlatedBuild.projectNumber,
  }
  let terminalCandidate = correlatedBuild
  if (buildIsActive(correlatedBuild.status)) {
    const terminalRow = await waitForBuildTerminal(
      input,
      authority,
      correlatedBuild.id,
      runCommand,
    )
    const original = buildRowSchema.parse(correlatedBuild.normalizedConfig)
    terminalCandidate = correlatedBuildIdentity(
      terminalRow,
      input,
      authority,
      identity,
      acceptedBuildRequest(original),
    )
    if (!sameImmutableBuildOwnership(correlatedBuild, terminalCandidate)) {
      throw new Error('Terminal Build immutable ownership differs from correlated Build')
    }
  }
  const build = terminalBuildIdentity(terminalCandidate)
  if (build.status !== 'SUCCESS') throw new TerminalBuildError(build)
  const image = await resolveProducedImage(
    mutationReconciliationInput(input), identity, build, runCommand,
  )
  return { build, image }
}
```

Artifact Registry reads stay dedicated, exact, bounded `gcloud` commands; the
fixed Run child is not reused:

```ts
const artifactTagRowSchema = z.object({
  image: z.string().min(1),
  tag: z.string().min(1),
  version: z.string().min(1),
}).strict()
const artifactVersionSchema = z.object({ name: z.string().min(1) }).strict()
const artifactVersionRowsSchema = z.array(artifactVersionSchema).max(2)

function normalizeDigest(value: string): string {
  const match = /(?:^|[/@])(?:sha256:)?([0-9a-f]{64})$/.exec(value)
  if (!match) throw new Error(`Invalid sha256 digest ${JSON.stringify(value)}`)
  return `sha256:${match[1]}`
}

function imagePath(input: CloudRunLifecycleInput): string {
  return `${input.runRegion}-docker.pkg.dev/${input.projectId}/${input.repository}/${input.imageName}`
}

export const imageIdentitySchema = z.object({
  taggedImage: z.string().min(1),
  tag: z.string().min(1),
  tagResourceName: z.string().min(1),
  digest: z.string().regex(/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/),
  immutableImage: z.string().min(1),
  digestVersionResourceName: z.string().min(1),
  localTags: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
}).strict()

export function requireCoherentImageIdentity(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  value: ImageIdentity,
): ImageIdentity {
  const image = imageIdentitySchema.parse(value)
  const base = imagePath(input)
  if (image.taggedImage !== `${base}:${image.tag}` ||
      image.tagResourceName !== artifactTagResourceName(input, image.tag) ||
      image.immutableImage !== `${base}@${image.digest}` ||
      image.digestVersionResourceName !==
        artifactVersionResourceName(input, image.digest)) {
    throw new Error('Image identity fields are not coherent')
  }
  return image
}

function digestFromBuild(build: BuildIdentity, taggedImage: string): string {
  const parsed = z.object({
    results: z.object({ images: z.array(buildImageRowSchema) }).strict(),
  }).passthrough().parse(build.normalizedConfig)
  const exact = parsed.results.images.filter(({ name }) => name === taggedImage)
  if (exact.length !== 1) throw new Error('Build must produce exactly one digest for invocation tag')
  return normalizeDigest(exact[0].digest)
}

export async function resolveProducedImage(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  build: BuildIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  localTags: readonly string[] = [],
): Promise<ImageIdentity> {
  if (build.status !== 'SUCCESS') throw new Error('Cannot resolve image before successful Build')
  const base = imagePath(input)
  const taggedImage = `${base}:${identity.imageTag}`
  const digest = digestFromBuild(build, taggedImage)
  const tagResource = artifactTagResourceName(input, identity.imageTag)
  const expectedVersion = artifactVersionResourceName(input, digest)
  const tagRows = await gcloudJson(z.array(artifactTagRowSchema).max(2), [
    'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list', base,
    exactTagFilter(input, identity.imageTag), '--limit=2',
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json(image,tag,version)',
  ], input, runCommand, input.budgets.imageMs)
  const exactTags = tagRows.filter(({ image, tag, version }) =>
    image === base && tag === tagResource && version === expectedVersion)
  if (exactTags.length !== 1) throw new Error('Invocation tag does not uniquely point to Build digest')
  const versions = await gcloudJson(artifactVersionRowsSchema, [
    'artifacts', 'versions', 'list',
    `--package=${input.imageName}`,
    `--filter=name="${expectedVersion}"`, '--limit=2',
    `--location=${input.runRegion}`,
    `--repository=${input.repository}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    `--account=${input.account}`, '--format=json(name)',
  ], input, runCommand, input.budgets.imageMs)
  const exactVersions = versions.filter(({ name }) => name === expectedVersion)
  if (exactVersions.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
      `Digest exact list requires one retained version; got ${exactVersions.length}`,
    )
  }
  const version = exactVersions[0]
  return requireCoherentImageIdentity(input, {
    taggedImage,
    tag: identity.imageTag,
    tagResourceName: tagResource,
    digest,
    immutableImage: `${base}@${digest}`,
    digestVersionResourceName: version.name,
    localTags: [...localTags],
  })
}

function dockerOptions(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  timeoutMs: number,
  stdin?: string,
): RunCommandOptions {
  return {
    ...phaseOptions(input, timeoutMs),
    env: { ...process.env, DOCKER_CONFIG: snapshot.dockerConfigDir },
    ...(stdin === undefined ? {} : { stdin }),
  }
}

export async function buildAndPushLocally(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<BuiltImage> {
  const remote = `${imagePath(input)}:${identity.imageTag}`
  const local = `freshell-${input.kind}:${identity.runId}`
  await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'docker', [
    'build', '--no-cache', '--file',
    join(snapshot.sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run/Dockerfile'),
    '--tag', local, '--tag', remote, snapshot.sourceDir,
  ], dockerOptions(input, snapshot, input.budgets.buildMs))

  const token = await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
    'auth', 'print-access-token',
    `--account=${input.account}`,
    `--impersonate-service-account=${input.buildServiceAccount}`,
  ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.commandMs))
  const accessToken = token.stdout.trim()
  if (!accessToken || /\s/.test(accessToken)) throw new Error('Invalid short-lived Build token')
  await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'docker', [
    'login', `${input.runRegion}-docker.pkg.dev`,
    '--username=oauth2accesstoken', '--password-stdin',
  ], dockerOptions(input, snapshot, input.budgets.commandMs, `${accessToken}\n`))
  await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'docker', ['push', remote],
    dockerOptions(input, snapshot, input.budgets.buildMs))

  const tagRows = await gcloudJson(z.array(artifactTagRowSchema).max(2), [
    'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list', imagePath(input),
    exactTagFilter(input, identity.imageTag), '--limit=2',
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json(image,tag,version)',
  ], input, runCommand, input.budgets.imageMs)
  const tagResource = artifactTagResourceName(input, identity.imageTag)
  if (tagRows.length !== 1 ||
      tagRows[0].image !== imagePath(input) ||
      tagRows[0].tag !== tagResource) {
    throw new Error('Local push did not create exactly one invocation tag')
  }
  const actualDigest = normalizeDigest(tagRows[0].version)
  if (tagRows[0].version !== artifactVersionResourceName(input, actualDigest)) {
    throw new Error('Local push tag returned a foreign version resource')
  }
  const completedAt = new Date().toISOString()
  const build: BuildIdentity = {
    correlationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    id: `local-build-${identity.runId}`,
    resourceName: `local-builds/${identity.runId}`,
    projectId: authority.projectId,
    projectNumber: authority.projectNumber,
    createTime: completedAt,
    startTime: completedAt,
    finishTime: completedAt,
    serviceAccount: input.buildServiceAccount,
    status: 'SUCCESS',
    normalizedConfig: deepFreeze({
      results: { images: [{ name: remote, digest: actualDigest }] },
      source: { localExtractedCommit: snapshot.head, context: snapshot.sourceDir },
      serviceAccount: input.buildServiceAccount,
      tags: [identity.buildCorrelationTag, `freshell-run-${identity.runId}`],
      substitutions: { _IMAGE: remote },
    }),
  }
  const image = await resolveProducedImage(input, identity, build, runCommand, [local, remote])
  return { build, image }
}

function sourceGenerationUri(source: SourceIdentity): string {
  return `gs://${source.bucket}/${source.object}#${source.generation}`
}
```

- [ ] **REFACTOR while green.** Extract the exact full-resource tag filter and
constructor family below, use it in every dedicated Artifact Registry tag read,
and rerun GREEN.

```ts
function artifactPackageResourceName(input: CloudRunLifecycleInput): string {
  return `projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}` +
    `/repositories/${input.repository}/packages/${input.imageName}`
}

function artifactTagResourceName(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  tag: string,
): string {
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/.test(tag)) {
    throw new Error('Artifact tag violates the accepted alphabet/length')
  }
  return `${artifactPackageResourceName(input)}/tags/${tag}`
}

function artifactVersionResourceName(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  digest: string,
): string {
  return `${artifactPackageResourceName(input)}/versions/${normalizeDigest(digest)}`
}

function exactTagFilter(input: CloudRunLifecycleInput, tag: string): string {
  return `--filter=tag="${artifactTagResourceName(input, tag)}"`
}
```

```ts
export type VerifiedRunJob = {
  name: string
  uid: string
  etag: string
  generation: string
  createTime: string
  labels: Readonly<Record<string, string>>
  template: {
    taskCount: number
    template: {
      serviceAccount: string
      containers: readonly [{
        image: string
        env: readonly { name: string; value: string }[]
        resources: { limits: Readonly<Record<string, string>> }
      }]
      maxRetries: number
      timeout: string
    }
  }
}

export type ExecutionIdentity = {
  operationName: string
  resourceName: string
  name: string
  uid: string
}

export type ExecutionRead = {
  metadata: { name: string; namespace: string; uid: string }
  spec: { taskCount: number; template: { spec: { serviceAccountName: string } } }
  status: {
    conditions: readonly { type: string; status: 'True' | 'False' | 'Unknown'; reason?: string }[]
    succeededCount?: number
    failedCount?: number
    cancelledCount?: number
    runningCount?: number
    startTime?: string
    completionTime?: string
  }
}

export type CanonicalExecution = {
  execution: ExecutionRead
  counters: ExecutionCounters
  omittedZeroCounters: readonly (keyof ExecutionCounters)[]
  completionStatus: 'True' | 'False'
}

export type ExactExecutionLogInput = {
  account: string
  projectId: string
  runRegion: string
  jobName: string
  executionName: string
  limit: number
  budgets: Pick<LifecycleBudgets, 'commandMs' | 'resultReadMs' | 'terminateGraceMs' | 'settlementMs'>
  signal?: AbortSignal
}

export type ResultCollectionInput = {
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput
  runId: string
  jobName: string
  executionName: string
  runCommand: RunCommand
}
```

```ts
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process'

type CanonicalizeTaskTimeout = (value: string) => string
type CanonicalizeExecution = (raw: unknown) => CanonicalExecution
type CollectExactPartResults = (

type CreateAndVerifyJob = (

  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  execution: ExecutionIdentity,
  projectNumber: string,

const canonicalizeTaskTimeout: CanonicalizeTaskTimeout = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CanonicalizeTaskTimeout>(
    'canonicalizeTaskTimeout',
  )(...args)
const canonicalizeExecution: CanonicalizeExecution = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CanonicalizeExecution>('canonicalizeExecution')(...args)
const collectExactPartResults: CollectExactPartResults = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CollectExactPartResults>('collectExactPartResults')(...args)
const createAndVerifyJob: CreateAndVerifyJob = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CreateAndVerifyJob>('createAndVerifyJob')(...args)
const deleteExactRunJob: DeleteExactRunJob = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<DeleteExactRunJob>('deleteExactRunJob')(...args)
const getExactRunJob: GetExactRunJob = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<GetExactRunJob>('getExactRunJob')(...args)
const getExactRunOperation: GetExactRunOperation = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<GetExactRunOperation>('getExactRunOperation')(...args)
const executionSucceeded: ExecutionSucceeded = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ExecutionSucceeded>('executionSucceeded')(...args)
const replayRawLiveContracts: ReplayRawLiveContracts = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ReplayRawLiveContracts>(
    'replayRawLiveContracts',
  )(...args)
const readExactExecutionLogs: ReadExactExecutionLogs = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ReadExactExecutionLogs>('readExactExecutionLogs')(...args)
const runExactJobOnce: RunExactJobOnce = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<RunExactJobOnce>('runExactJobOnce')(...args)
const waitForExecutionTerminal: WaitForExecutionTerminal = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<WaitForExecutionTerminal>(
    'waitForExecutionTerminal',
  )(...args)

function verifiedJob(createTime = testIso(0)) {
  return {
    name: `projects/${BASE_INPUT.projectId}/locations/${BASE_INPUT.runRegion}/jobs/${IDENTITY.jobName}`,
    uid: 'job-uid-1',
    etag: 'job-etag-1',
    generation: '7',
    createTime,
    updateTime: testIso(1_000),
    creator: BASE_INPUT.account,
    lastModifier: BASE_INPUT.account,
    latestCreatedExecution: {
      name: 'provider-field-that-is-never-used-for-attribution',
    },
    terminalCondition: { type: 'Ready', state: 'CONDITION_SUCCEEDED' },
    labels: { 'freshell-run-id': IDENTITY.runId },
    template: {
      taskCount: 1,
      parallelism: 1,
      labels: { provider: 'extra' },
      template: {
        serviceAccount: BASE_INPUT.runtimeServiceAccount,
        containers: [{
          image: `${IMAGE_PATH}@sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
          env: [
            { name: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND', value: 'vitest', providerExtra: 'kept' },
            { name: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID', value: IDENTITY.runId },
          ],
          resources: {
            limits: { cpu: '4', memory: '4Gi' },
            cpuIdle: false,
          },
          command: [],
        }],
        maxRetries: 0,
        timeout: '1800s',
        executionEnvironment: 'EXECUTION_ENVIRONMENT_GEN2',
      },
    },
    providerOnlyField: { safeToIgnore: true },
  } as const
}
const VERIFIED_JOB = verifiedJob()

  executionSuccessOmittedZeros: {
    apiVersion: 'run.googleapis.com/v1',
    kind: 'Execution',

      name: 'execution-owned', namespace: '123456789', uid: 'execution-uid-1',

    },

        },

    },

        message: 'All tasks completed', lastTransitionTime: testIso(2_000),
      }],

    },

        message: 'Task failed', lastTransitionTime: testIso(2_000),
      }],
      failedCount: 1,

      completionTime: testIso(2_000),
      logUri: 'https://console.cloud.google.com/logs/query',
      observedGeneration: 1,
    },
  },
}

function runTransportResult(httpStatus: number, body: unknown): CommandResult {
  return { ...commandOk(), stdout: JSON.stringify({ httpStatus, body }) }
}
function fixedRunTransportRunner(
  responses: readonly (CommandResult | Error)[] = [
    { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'short-lived-token\n' },
    runTransportResult(200, VERIFIED_JOB),
  ],
): ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>> {
  const queue = [...responses]
  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async () => {
    const next = queue.shift()
    if (!next) throw new Error('unexpected fixed Run transport call')
    if (next instanceof Error) throw next
    return next
  })
}

  'rejects invalid task timeout %s',

)

    },
    runner,
```
