})

async function withRedirectedRunServer<T>(
  handler: RequestListener,
  work: (runner: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>>) => Promise<T>,
): Promise<T> {
  const server = createServer(handler)
  server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1')
  await once(server, 'listening')
  const address = server.address()
  if (!address || typeof address === 'string') {
    throw new Error('Local fixed-child server did not bind TCP')
  }
  const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'closed-run-child-'))
  roots.push(root)
  const preload = join(root, 'redirect-fetch.mjs')
  const origin = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`
  await writeFile(preload, `
    const nativeFetch = globalThis.fetch
    const origin = ${JSON.stringify(origin)}
    globalThis.fetch = (input, init) =>
      nativeFetch(String(input).replace('https://run.googleapis.com', origin), init)
  `)
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    if (command === 'gcloud') {
      expect(args.slice(0, 3)).toEqual(['auth', 'print-access-token'])
      return { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'local-test-token\n' }
    }
    expect(command).toBe(process.execPath)
    return defaultRunCommand(command, args, {
      ...options,
      cwd: process.cwd(),
      env: {
        ...process.env,
        ...options.env,
        NODE_OPTIONS: `--import=${pathToFileURL(preload).href}`,
      },
    })
  })
  try {
    return await work(runner)
  } finally {
    server.close()
    await once(server, 'close')
  }
}

function providerEntryForStdout(line: string, execution = 'execution-owned') {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(line)
    if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) {
      return {
        resource: { type: 'cloud_run_job', labels: {
          project_id: BASE_INPUT.projectId,
          location: BASE_INPUT.runRegion,
          job_name: IDENTITY.jobName,
        } },
        labels: { 'run.googleapis.com/execution_name': execution },
        jsonPayload: value,
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return {
    resource: { type: 'cloud_run_job', labels: {
      project_id: BASE_INPUT.projectId,
      location: BASE_INPUT.runRegion,
      job_name: IDENTITY.jobName,
    } },
    labels: { 'run.googleapis.com/execution_name': execution },
    textPayload: line,
  }
}
function cloudResult(partId: string, taskIndex = 0) {
  return {
    marker: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT',
    schemaVersion: 1,
    kind: 'vitest',
    runId: IDENTITY.runId,
    execution: 'execution-owned',
    partId,
    taskIndex,
    taskCount: 1,
    taskAttempt: 0,
    status: 'passed',
    exitCode: 0,
    testCount: 3,
  }
}

it('exposes only the four fixed Run operations and keeps the token on stdin', async () => {
  const runner = fixedRunTransportRunner()
  await getExactRunJob(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, runner)
  const calls = runner.mock.calls
  expect(calls[0][1]).toContain(
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
  )
  expect(calls[1][0]).toBe(process.execPath)
  const envelope = JSON.parse(String(calls[1][2].stdin))
  expect(envelope).toMatchObject({
    command: {
      kind: 'get-job',
      projectId: 'test-project',
      region: 'us-west1',
      jobName: IDENTITY.jobName,
    },
    token: 'short-lived-token',
  })
  expect(JSON.stringify(calls.map(([, args]) => args))).not.toContain('short-lived-token')
  const source = await readFile('scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts', 'utf8')
  expect(source.match(/case '(?:get-job|run-job|delete-job|get-operation)'/g))
    .toHaveLength(4)
  expect(source).toContain("type ClosedRunCommand = z.infer<typeof closedRunCommandSchema>")
  expect(source).toContain('fixed Cloud Run envelope exceeds 64 KiB')
  expect(source).toContain('Cloud Run response exceeds 1 MiB')
  expect(source).not.toContain('export type ClosedRunCommand')
})

it.each([429, 500, 502, 503, 504])(
  'retries only a structured transient closed Job GET %s',
  async (code) => {
    const runner = fixedRunTransportRunner([
      { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token-one\n' },
      runTransportResult(code, {
        error: {
          code,
          status: code === 429 ? 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED' : 'INTERNAL',
          message: 'retry',
        },
      }),
      { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token-two\n' },
      runTransportResult(200, VERIFIED_JOB),
    ])
    await expect(getExactRunJob(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, runner))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ uid: VERIFIED_JOB.uid })
    expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4)
  },
)

it.each(['ECONNRESET', 'ETIMEDOUT', 'EAI_AGAIN'] as const)(
  'retries a characterized thrown closed Job GET transport %s',
  async (code) => {
    const runner = fixedRunTransportRunner([
      { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token-one\n' },
      Object.assign(new Error(code), { code }),
      { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token-two\n' },
      runTransportResult(200, VERIFIED_JOB),
    ])
    await expect(getExactRunJob(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, runner))
      .resolves.toMatchObject({ etag: VERIFIED_JOB.etag })
    expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(4)
  },
)

it('retries a real reset from the actual fixed child through defaultRunCommand', async () => {
  let requests = 0
  await withRedirectedRunServer((
    request: IncomingMessage,
    response: ServerResponse,
  ) => {
    requests += 1
    if (requests === 1) {
      request.socket.destroy()
      return
    }
    response.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' })
    response.end(JSON.stringify(VERIFIED_JOB))
  }, async (runner) => {
    await expect(getExactRunJob(
      BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, runner,
    )).resolves.toMatchObject({
      uid: VERIFIED_JOB.uid,
      generation: VERIFIED_JOB.generation,
    })
    expect(requests).toBe(2)
    const childCalls = runner.mock.calls.filter(
      ([command]) => command === process.execPath,
    )
    expect(childCalls).toHaveLength(2)
  })
})

it('never retries a real reset for run/delete mutations through the fixed child', async () => {
  let runAttempts = 0
  let deleteAttempts = 0
  let deleted = false
  const operationName =
    `projects/${BASE_INPUT.projectId}/locations/${BASE_INPUT.runRegion}/operations/op-owned`
  await withRedirectedRunServer((
    request: IncomingMessage,
    response: ServerResponse,
  ) => {
    const url = request.url ?? ''
    const send = (status: number, body: unknown) => {
      response.writeHead(status, { 'content-type': 'application/json' })
      response.end(JSON.stringify(body))
    }
    if (url.endsWith(`/${IDENTITY.jobName}:run`)) {
      runAttempts += 1
      request.socket.destroy()
      return
    }
    if (request.method === 'DELETE' && url.includes(`/jobs/${IDENTITY.jobName}`)) {
      deleteAttempts += 1
      deleted = true
      send(200, { name: operationName })
      return
    }
    if (url.endsWith('/operations/op-owned')) {
      send(200, { name: operationName, done: true })
      return
    }
    if (url.includes(`/jobs/${IDENTITY.jobName}`)) {
      if (deleted) {
        send(404, {
          error: { code: 404, status: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'absent' },
        })
      } else {
        send(200, VERIFIED_JOB)
      }
      return
    }
    send(500, {
      error: { code: 500, status: 'INTERNAL', message: 'unexpected route' },
    })
  }, async (runner) => {
    await expect(runExactJobOnce(
      BASE_INPUT, VERIFIED_JOB, runner,
    )).rejects.toThrow('Execution identity unknown')
    expect(runAttempts).toBe(1)
    expect(deleteAttempts).toBe(1)
  })
})

it('retries an exact Operation GET but never changes its identity', async () => {
  const name = `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/op-owned`
  const runner = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token-one\n' },
    runTransportResult(503, {
      error: { code: 503, status: 'UNAVAILABLE', message: 'retry' },
    }),
    { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token-two\n' },
    runTransportResult(200, { name, done: true, metadata: { provider: 'extra' } }),
  ])
  await expect(getExactRunOperation(BASE_INPUT, 'op-owned', runner))
    .resolves.toMatchObject({ name, done: true })
  const inputs = runner.mock.calls
    .filter(([command]) => command === process.execPath)
    .map(([, , options]) => JSON.parse(String(options.stdin)).command)
  expect(inputs).toEqual([
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'get-operation', operationId: 'op-owned' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'get-operation', operationId: 'op-owned' }),
  ])
})

it('does not retry closed 403/404, malformed transient, or pre-abort', async () => {
  const denied = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token\n' },
    runTransportResult(403, {
      error: { code: 403, status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED', message: 'denied' },
    }),
  ])
  await expect(getExactRunJob(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, denied))
    .rejects.toThrow('PERMISSION_DENIED')
  expect(denied).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)

  const absent = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token\n' },
    runTransportResult(404, {
      error: { code: 404, status: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'absent' },
    }),
  ])
  await expect(getExactRunJob(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, absent))
    .resolves.toBeUndefined()
  expect(absent).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)

  const malformed = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'token\n' },
    runTransportResult(503, { provider: 'not-a-structured-error' }),
  ])
  await expect(getExactRunJob(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.jobName, malformed))
    .rejects.toThrow()
  expect(malformed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)

  const controller = new AbortController()
  controller.abort(new Error('stop'))
  const aborted = fixedRunTransportRunner([])
  await expect(getExactRunJob(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal },
    IDENTITY.jobName,
    aborted,
  )).rejects.toThrow('aborted')
  expect(aborted).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
})

it('never retries run and contains after unreadable response plus abort', async () => {
  const token = { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'short-lived-token\n' }
  const operationName = `projects/${BASE_INPUT.projectId}/locations/${BASE_INPUT.runRegion}/operations/delete-op`
  const scripted = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    token,
    Object.assign(new Error('run response lost'), { code: 'ECONNRESET' }),
    token,
    runTransportResult(200, VERIFIED_JOB),
    token,
    runTransportResult(200, { name: operationName }),
    token,
    runTransportResult(200, { name: operationName, done: true }),
    token,
    runTransportResult(404, {
      error: { code: 404, status: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'absent' },
    }),
  ])
  const controller = new AbortController()
  let call = 0
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    call += 1
    if (call === 2) controller.abort(new Error('caught SIGTERM'))
    else if (call > 2) expect(options.signal).toBeUndefined()
    return scripted(command, args, options)
  })
  await expect(runExactJobOnce(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal }, VERIFIED_JOB, runner,
  )).rejects.toThrow(
    'Execution identity unknown; exact owned Job containment',
  )
  const calls = (runner as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls
  const childInputs = calls
    .filter(([command]) => command === process.execPath)
    .map(([, , options]) => String(options.stdin))
    .join('\n')
  expect((childInputs.match(/"kind":"run-job"/g) ?? [])).toHaveLength(1)
  expect(childInputs).toContain('"kind":"delete-job"')
  expect(childInputs).not.toMatch(
    /executions list|logging read|latestCreatedExecution|startExecutionToken|runExecutionToken/,
  )
})

it('canonicalizes all omitted zero counters only after exact terminal invariants', () => {
  const success = canonicalizeExecution(fixtures.executionSuccessOmittedZeros)
  expect(success).toMatchObject({
    counters: { succeededCount: 4, failedCount: 0, cancelledCount: 0, runningCount: 0 },
    omittedZeroCounters: ['failedCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount'],
    completionStatus: 'True',
  })
  expect(executionSucceeded(success)).toBe(true)
  const failure = canonicalizeExecution(fixtures.executionFailureOnlyFailedCount)
  expect(failure).toMatchObject({
    counters: { succeededCount: 0, failedCount: 1, cancelledCount: 0, runningCount: 0 },
    omittedZeroCounters: ['succeededCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount'],
    completionStatus: 'False',
  })
  expect(executionSucceeded(failure)).toBe(false)
  for (const key of ['succeededCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount']) {
    expect(Object.hasOwn(fixtures.executionFailureOnlyFailedCount.status, key)).toBe(false)
  }
})

it('returns a Completed=False failedCount-only Execution without polling again', async () => {
  const raw = fixtures.executionFailureOnlyFailedCount
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockResolvedValue(commandOk(raw))
  await expect(waitForExecutionTerminal(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, taskCount: 1 },
    {
      operationName: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/run-failed',
      resourceName: `${VERIFIED_JOB.name}/executions/${raw.metadata.name}`,
      name: raw.metadata.name,
      uid: raw.metadata.uid,
    },
    raw.metadata.namespace,
    runner,
  )).resolves.toMatchObject({
    completionStatus: 'False',
    counters: { succeededCount: 0, failedCount: 1, cancelledCount: 0, runningCount: 0 },
  })
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
})

it('replays the committed raw provider fixture through production parsers', async () => {

  expect(replayRawLiveContracts(value)).toEqual({

    providerVersion: 'Google Cloud SDK 576.0.0',
    successCounters: {
      succeededCount: 1,
      failedCount: 0,
      cancelledCount: 0,
      runningCount: 0,
    },
    failureCounters: {
      succeededCount: 0,
      failedCount: 1,
      cancelledCount: 0,
      runningCount: 0,
    },
    failureOmittedCounters: [
      'succeededCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount',
    ],
    successPartIds: ['server:1/1'],
    failureResultRejected: true,
  })
  expect(Object.hasOwn(value.executionFailure.status, 'succeededCount')).toBe(false)
  expect(Object.hasOwn(value.executionFailure.status, 'cancelledCount')).toBe(false)
  expect(Object.hasOwn(value.executionFailure.status, 'runningCount')).toBe(false)
})

it('accepts only bare JSON as jsonPayload and queries expectedParts plus one', async () => {
  const expectedParts = ['client:1/1', 'server:1/1', 'electron:1/1']
  const rows = [
    ...expectedParts.map((partId) => providerEntryForStdout(
      JSON.stringify(cloudResult(partId)),
    )),
    providerEntryForStdout(
      '[cloud-child stdout] {"marker":"FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT","partId":"forged"}',
    ),
  ]
  expect(rows[0]).toHaveProperty('jsonPayload.marker', 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT')
  expect(rows.at(-1)).toHaveProperty('textPayload')
  expect(rows.at(-1)).not.toHaveProperty('jsonPayload')
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockResolvedValue(commandOk(rows))
  await expect(collectExactPartResults({
    input: { ...BASE_INPUT, taskCount: 1, expectedParts },
    runId: IDENTITY.runId,
    jobName: IDENTITY.jobName,
    executionName: 'execution-owned',
    runCommand: runner,
  })).resolves.toHaveLength(3)
  expect((runner as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0][1]).toContain('--limit=4')
  expect((runner as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls[0][1].join(' '))
    .toContain('jsonPayload.marker="FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT"')
})

it.each([
  ['missing', [providerEntryForStdout(JSON.stringify(cloudResult('client:1/1')))]],
  ['duplicate', [
    providerEntryForStdout(JSON.stringify(cloudResult('client:1/1'))),
    providerEntryForStdout(JSON.stringify(cloudResult('client:1/1'))),
  ]],
  ['extra', [
    providerEntryForStdout(JSON.stringify(cloudResult('client:1/1'))),
    providerEntryForStdout(JSON.stringify(cloudResult('foreign:1/1'))),

] as const)('fails closed for %s result candidates', async (_name, rows) => {

    executionName: 'execution-owned',

  })).rejects.toThrow()
})

it('reconciles a thrown Job create after abort by exact name and full config', async () => {
  const token = { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'short-lived-token\n' }
  const scripted = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    Object.assign(new Error('create response lost'), { code: 'ECONNRESET' }),
    token,
    runTransportResult(200, VERIFIED_JOB),
  ])
  const controller = new AbortController()
  let call = 0
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    call += 1
    if (call === 1) controller.abort(new Error('caught SIGTERM'))
    else expect(options.signal).toBeUndefined()
    return scripted(command, args, options)
  })
  const input = { ...BASE_INPUT, taskCount: 1, signal: controller.signal }
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  await expect(createAndVerifyJob(
    input,
    IDENTITY,
    snapshot.tempRoot,
    VERIFIED_JOB.template.template.containers[0].image,
    {
      FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND: 'vitest',
      FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID: IDENTITY.runId,
    },
    runner,
    TEST_WINDOW,
  )).resolves.toEqual(VERIFIED_JOB)
  const createCalls = runner.mock.calls
    .filter(([command, args]) => command === 'gcloud' && args.includes('create'))
  expect(createCalls).toHaveLength(1)
  expect(createCalls[0][1]).toEqual([
    'run', 'jobs', 'create', IDENTITY.jobName,
    `--image=${VERIFIED_JOB.template.template.containers[0].image}`,
    `--service-account=${BASE_INPUT.runtimeServiceAccount}`,
    '--tasks=1',
    `--task-timeout=${BASE_INPUT.taskTimeout}`,
    '--max-retries=0',
    `--memory=${BASE_INPUT.memory}`,
    `--cpu=${BASE_INPUT.cpu}`,
    `--labels=freshell-run-id=${IDENTITY.runId}`,
    `--env-vars-file=${join(snapshot.tempRoot, `job-env-${IDENTITY.runId}.json`)}`,
    `--region=${BASE_INPUT.runRegion}`,
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ])
})

Task 5 keeps the exact cumulative Task 4 `lifecycle.ts` imports; no HTTP client,

```ts
const closedRunCommandSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal('get-job'),
    projectId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,61}[a-z0-9]$/),
    region: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/),
    jobName: z.string().regex(/^[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,47}[a-z0-9])?$/),
  }).strict(),
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal('run-job'),
    projectId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,61}[a-z0-9]$/),
    region: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/),
    jobName: z.string().regex(/^[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,47}[a-z0-9])?$/),
    etag: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
  }).strict(),
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal('delete-job'),
    projectId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,61}[a-z0-9]$/),
    region: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/),
    jobName: z.string().regex(/^[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,47}[a-z0-9])?$/),
    etag: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
  }).strict(),
  z.object({
    kind: z.literal('get-operation'),
    projectId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,61}[a-z0-9]$/),
    region: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/),
    operationId: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9._~-]+$/),
  }).strict(),
])
type ClosedRunCommand = z.infer<typeof closedRunCommandSchema>

const CLOSED_RUN_V2_CHILD = String.raw`
import { writeSync } from 'node:fs'
import process from 'node:process'
const raw = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  let text = ''
  process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8')
  process.stdin.on('data', (chunk) => {
    text += chunk
    if (Buffer.byteLength(text, 'utf8') > 64 * 1024) {
      reject(new Error('fixed Cloud Run envelope exceeds 64 KiB'))
      process.stdin.destroy()
    }
  })
  process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve(text))
  process.stdin.on('error', reject)
})
const envelope = JSON.parse(raw)
if (!envelope || typeof envelope !== 'object' || Array.isArray(envelope) ||
    JSON.stringify(Object.keys(envelope).sort()) !== JSON.stringify(['command', 'token'])) {
  throw new Error('invalid fixed Cloud Run envelope')
}
const command = envelope.command
const token = envelope.token
if (!command || typeof command !== 'object' || Array.isArray(command) ||
    typeof token !== 'string' || token.length === 0 || token.length > 16_384) {
  throw new Error('invalid fixed Cloud Run envelope values')
}
const project = command.projectId
const region = command.region
const job = command.jobName
const operation = command.operationId
const etag = command.etag
const projectPattern = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,61}[a-z0-9]$/
const regionPattern = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/
const jobPattern = /^[a-z](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,47}[a-z0-9])?$/
const operationPattern = /^[A-Za-z0-9._~-]+$/
const keysByKind = {
  'get-job': ['jobName', 'kind', 'projectId', 'region'],
  'run-job': ['etag', 'jobName', 'kind', 'projectId', 'region'],
  'delete-job': ['etag', 'jobName', 'kind', 'projectId', 'region'],
  'get-operation': ['kind', 'operationId', 'projectId', 'region'],
}
const expectedKeys = keysByKind[command.kind]
if (!expectedKeys || JSON.stringify(Object.keys(command).sort()) !== JSON.stringify(expectedKeys) ||
    !projectPattern.test(project) || !regionPattern.test(region) ||
    (job !== undefined && !jobPattern.test(job)) ||
    (operation !== undefined && !operationPattern.test(operation)) ||
    ((command.kind === 'run-job' || command.kind === 'delete-job') &&
      (typeof etag !== 'string' || etag.length < 1 || etag.length > 4096))) {
  throw new Error('invalid fixed Cloud Run command')
}
const root = 'https://run.googleapis.com/v2/projects/' + project + '/locations/' + region
const headers = {
  authorization: 'Bearer ' + token,
  'content-type': 'application/json',
}
async function readBoundedResponse(response) {
  if (!response.body) return ''
  const chunks = []
  let size = 0
  for await (const chunk of response.body) {
    const bytes = Buffer.from(chunk)
    size += bytes.byteLength
    if (size > 1024 * 1024) {
      throw new Error('Cloud Run response exceeds 1 MiB')
    }
    chunks.push(bytes)
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8')
}
let response
try {
  switch (command.kind) {
    case 'get-job':
      response = await fetch(root + '/jobs/' + job, { headers })
      break
    case 'run-job':
      response = await fetch(root + '/jobs/' + job + ':run', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify({ etag }),
      })
      break
    case 'delete-job':
      response = await fetch(
        root + '/jobs/' + job + '?etag=' + encodeURIComponent(etag),
        { method: 'DELETE', headers },
      )
      break
    case 'get-operation':
      response = await fetch(root + '/operations/' + operation, { headers })
      break
    default:
      throw new Error('unsupported fixed Cloud Run operation')
  }
} catch (error) {
  const direct = error && typeof error === 'object' ? error.code : undefined
  const cause = error && typeof error === 'object' &&
    error.cause && typeof error.cause === 'object'
    ? error.cause.code
    : undefined
  const code = typeof direct === 'string' ? direct : cause
  const characterized = new Set([
    'ECONNRESET',
    'ETIMEDOUT',
    'EAI_AGAIN',
    'UND_ERR_SOCKET',
    'UND_ERR_CONNECT_TIMEOUT',
  ])
  if (typeof code !== 'string' || !characterized.has(code)) throw error
  writeSync(1, JSON.stringify({
    transportError: {
      code,
      message: 'characterized Cloud Run fetch transport failure',
    },
  }))
  process.exit(0)
}
const text = await readBoundedResponse(response)
let body
try { body = text === '' ? {} : JSON.parse(text) } catch { throw new Error('non-JSON Cloud Run response') }
if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) {
  throw new Error('non-object Cloud Run response')
}
if (response.status >= 400) {
  const error = body.error
  if (!error || error.code !== response.status || typeof error.status !== 'string' ||
      typeof error.message !== 'string') throw new Error('malformed Cloud Run error')
} else if (command.kind === 'get-job') {
  if (typeof body.name !== 'string' || typeof body.uid !== 'string' ||
      typeof body.etag !== 'string') throw new Error('malformed Job response')
} else if (command.kind === 'get-operation') {
  if (typeof body.name !== 'string') throw new Error('malformed Operation response')
} else if (typeof body.name !== 'string') {
  throw new Error('mutation did not return an Operation')
}
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ httpStatus: response.status, body }))
`

async function invokeClosedRunCommand(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  command: ClosedRunCommand,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<{ httpStatus: number; body: unknown }> {
  const validated = closedRunCommandSchema.parse(command)
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.commandMs
  const token = await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
    'auth', 'print-access-token', `--account=${input.account}`,
  ], phaseOptions(input, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())))
  const result = await checkedCommand(runCommand, process.execPath, [
    '--input-type=module', '--eval', CLOSED_RUN_V2_CHILD,
  ], {
    ...phaseOptions(input, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())),
    stdin: JSON.stringify({ command: validated, token: token.stdout.trim() }),
  })
  const envelope = runTransportEnvelopeSchema.parse(JSON.parse(result.stdout))
  if ('transportError' in envelope) {
    throw Object.assign(
      new Error(
        `Cloud Run child transport failed: ${envelope.transportError.code}`,
      ),
      { code: envelope.transportError.code },
    )
  }
  return envelope
}
```

Define the transport envelope schema immediately before
`invokeClosedRunCommand`, then add all exact-purpose wrappers. The child cannot
target Artifact Registry, Storage, or a caller-selected endpoint:

```ts
const providerErrorSchema = z.object({
  error: z.object({
    code: z.number().int(),
    status: z.string(),
    message: z.string(),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()
const runTransportResponseSchema = z.object({
  httpStatus: z.number().int().min(100).max(599),
  body: z.unknown(),
}).strict()
const runTransportErrorSchema = z.object({
  transportError: z.object({
    code: z.enum([
      'ECONNRESET',
      'ETIMEDOUT',
      'EAI_AGAIN',
      'UND_ERR_SOCKET',
      'UND_ERR_CONNECT_TIMEOUT',
    ]),
    message: z.literal('characterized Cloud Run fetch transport failure'),
  }).strict(),
}).strict()
const runTransportEnvelopeSchema = z.union([
  runTransportResponseSchema,
  runTransportErrorSchema,
])
function parseTaskTimeoutMilliseconds(value: string): number {
  const match = /^([1-9][0-9]*)(s|m|h)$/.exec(value)
  if (!match) throw new Error('Task timeout must be a positive s, m, or h duration')
  const factor = match[2] === 's' ? 1_000 : match[2] === 'm' ? 60_000 : 3_600_000
  const milliseconds = Number(match[1]) * factor
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(milliseconds) || milliseconds > 24 * 3_600_000) {
    throw new Error('Task timeout must not exceed 24h')
  }
  return milliseconds
}

export function canonicalizeTaskTimeout(value: string): string {
  return `${parseTaskTimeoutMilliseconds(value) / 1_000}s`
}

function assertCanonicalTaskTimeout(value: string): void {
  if (canonicalizeTaskTimeout(value) !== value) {
    throw new Error(
      'CloudRunLifecycleInput.taskTimeout must be canonical positive whole seconds',
    )
  }
}

const runJobSchema: z.ZodType<VerifiedRunJob> = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1),
  uid: z.string().min(1),
  etag: z.string().min(1),
  generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  createTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),
  labels: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
  template: z.object({
    taskCount: z.number().int().positive(),
    template: z.object({
      serviceAccount: z.string().email(),
      containers: z.tuple([z.object({
        image: z.string().min(1),
        env: z.array(z.object({
          name: z.string(),
          value: z.string(),
        }).passthrough()),
        resources: z.object({
          limits: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
        }).passthrough(),
      }).passthrough()]),
      maxRetries: z.literal(0),
      timeout: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*s$/),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()
const operationSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1),
  done: z.boolean().optional(),
  error: z.object({ code: z.number().int(), message: z.string(), status: z.string().optional() })
    .strict().optional(),
  response: z.object({ name: z.string(), uid: z.string() }).passthrough().optional(),
}).passthrough()
const gcloudV1JobCreateSchema = z.object({
  apiVersion: z.string(),
  kind: z.literal('Job'),
  metadata: z.object({
    name: z.string().min(1),
    namespace: z.string().optional(),
    uid: z.string().optional(),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()

function structuredRunError(response: { httpStatus: number; body: unknown }): Error {
  const parsed = providerErrorSchema.parse(response.body)
  if (parsed.error.code !== response.httpStatus) throw new Error('Malformed Cloud Run error')
  return new Error(`${parsed.error.status} (${parsed.error.code}): ${parsed.error.message}`)
}

type ClosedRunReadCommand = Extract<
  ClosedRunCommand,
  { kind: 'get-job' | 'get-operation' }
>

function withClosedReadBudget(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  commandMs: number,
): CloudRunLifecycleInput {
  return { ...input, budgets: { ...input.budgets, commandMs } }
}

async function invokeClosedRunRead(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  command: ClosedRunReadCommand,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<z.infer<typeof runTransportResponseSchema>> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.commandMs
  let lastFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt += 1) {
    if (input.signal?.aborted) {
      throw new Error(`Lifecycle aborted before closed ${command.kind}`)
    }
    const remaining = deadline - Date.now()
    if (remaining <= 0) break
    try {
      const response = await invokeClosedRunCommand(
        withClosedReadBudget(input, remaining),
        command,
        runCommand,
      )
      if (!retryableHttpStatusCodes.has(response.httpStatus)) return response
      // Only a schema-valid structured 429/5xx is transient.
      providerErrorSchema.parse(response.body)
      lastFailure = structuredRunError(response)
    } catch (error) {
      if (input.signal?.aborted) throw error
      const code = transportErrorCode(error)
      if (!code || !retryableTransportCodes.has(code)) throw error
      lastFailure = error
    }
    if (attempt === 3 || Date.now() >= deadline) break
    await waitForRetry(
      Math.min(250 * attempt, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())),
      input.signal,
    )
  }
  throw new AggregateError(
    lastFailure === undefined ? [] : [lastFailure],
    `Closed ${command.kind} exhausted its absolute read deadline`,
  )
}

export async function getExactRunJob(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  jobName: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<VerifiedRunJob | undefined> {
  const response = await invokeClosedRunRead(input, {
    kind: 'get-job', projectId: input.projectId, region: input.runRegion, jobName,
  }, runCommand)
  if (response.httpStatus === 404) {
    const error = providerErrorSchema.parse(response.body).error
    if (error.status !== 'NOT_FOUND' || error.code !== 404) {
      throw new Error('Malformed Job absence response')
    }
    return undefined
  }
  if (response.httpStatus !== 200) throw structuredRunError(response)
  const job = runJobSchema.parse(response.body)
  const expectedName =
    `projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}/jobs/${jobName}`
  if (job.name !== expectedName) throw new Error('Job GET returned foreign name')
  return job
}

function sortedEnv(env: readonly { name: string; value: string }[]) {
  return [...env].sort((left, right) => left.name.localeCompare(right.name))
}

export function assertExactJobConfiguration(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  job: VerifiedRunJob,
  immutableImage: string,
  expectedEnv: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
): void {
  const expectedName =
    `projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}/jobs/${identity.jobName}`
  const container = job.template.template.containers[0]
  if (job.name !== expectedName || !job.uid || !job.etag ||
      !/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(job.generation) ||
      job.labels['freshell-run-id'] !== identity.runId ||
      job.template.taskCount !== input.taskCount ||
      job.template.template.serviceAccount !== input.runtimeServiceAccount ||
      container.image !== immutableImage ||
      job.template.template.maxRetries !== 0 ||
      job.template.template.timeout !== input.taskTimeout ||
      container.resources.limits.cpu !== String(input.cpu) ||
      container.resources.limits.memory !== input.memory ||
      JSON.stringify(sortedEnv(container.env)) !== JSON.stringify(sortedEnv(
        Object.entries(expectedEnv).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value })),
      ))) {
    throw new Error('Job does not exact-match invocation configuration')
  }
}

export async function createAndVerifyJob(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  tempRoot: string,
  immutableImage: string,
  expectedEnv: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  window: CorrelationWindow = correlationWindow(),
): Promise<VerifiedRunJob> {
  assertCanonicalTaskTimeout(input.taskTimeout)
  let createFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  const envFile = join(tempRoot, `job-env-${identity.runId}.json`)
  await writeFile(envFile, `${JSON.stringify(expectedEnv)}\n`, { mode: 0o600 })
  try {
    const result = await runCommand('gcloud', [
      'run', 'jobs', 'create', identity.jobName,
      `--image=${immutableImage}`,
      `--service-account=${input.runtimeServiceAccount}`,
      `--tasks=${input.taskCount}`,
      `--task-timeout=${input.taskTimeout}`,
      '--max-retries=0', `--memory=${input.memory}`, `--cpu=${input.cpu}`,
      `--labels=freshell-run-id=${identity.runId}`,
      `--env-vars-file=${envFile}`,
      `--region=${input.runRegion}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`, '--format=json',
    ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.jobMs))
    if (result.exitCode === 0 && result.signal === null && !result.timedOut) {
      try {
        const acknowledgement = gcloudV1JobCreateSchema.parse(JSON.parse(result.stdout))
        if (acknowledgement.metadata.name !== identity.jobName) {
          throw new Error('gcloud v1 create acknowledgement returned a foreign Job name')
        }
      } catch (error) {
        createFailure = error
      }
    } else {
      createFailure = new Error('Job create returned an unreadable outcome')
    }
  } catch (error) {
    createFailure = error
  } finally {
    await rm(envFile, { force: true })
  }

  // The create output is never authoritative. Every attempted mutation,
  // including a readable v1 acknowledgement and a lost response, crosses the
  // same exact v2 GET boundary before ownership can be acquired.
  const reconciliationInput = mutationReconciliationInput(input)
  let job: VerifiedRunJob | undefined
  try {
    job = await getExactRunJob(
      reconciliationInput, identity.jobName, runCommand,
    )
  } catch (reconciliationFailure) {
    throw new AggregateError(
      [createFailure, reconciliationFailure].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
      'Job create outcome and authoritative v2 GET could not be reconciled',
    )
  }
  if (!job) {
    throw new AggregateError(
      createFailure === undefined ? [] : [createFailure],
      'Job create was attempted but the authoritative v2 Job is absent',
    )
  }
  const created = Date.parse(job.createTime)
  if (!Number.isFinite(created) ||
      created < window.notBeforeMs || created > window.notAfterMs) {
    throw new Error('Job create time is outside invocation bounds')
  }
  assertExactJobConfiguration(input, identity, job, immutableImage, expectedEnv)
  return job
}

function operationIdFromName(name: string, input: CloudRunLifecycleInput): string {
  const prefix = `projects/${input.projectId}/locations/${input.runRegion}/operations/`
  if (!name.startsWith(prefix)) throw new Error('Operation is outside exact context')
  const id = name.slice(prefix.length)
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9._~-]+$/.test(id)) throw new Error('Invalid Operation ID')
  return id
}

export async function getExactRunOperation(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  operationId: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<z.infer<typeof operationSchema>> {
  const response = await invokeClosedRunRead(input, {
    kind: 'get-operation',
    projectId: input.projectId,
    region: input.runRegion,
    operationId,
  }, runCommand)
  if (response.httpStatus !== 200) throw structuredRunError(response)
  const operation = operationSchema.parse(response.body)
  if (operationIdFromName(operation.name, input) !== operationId) {
    throw new Error('Operation GET returned the wrong identity')
  }
  return operation
}

async function waitForRunOperation(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  name: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<z.infer<typeof operationSchema>> {
  const id = operationIdFromName(name, input)
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.jobMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const operation = await getExactRunOperation(input, id, runCommand)
    if (operation.done === true) {
      if (operation.error) throw new Error(
        `Operation ${name} failed: ${operation.error.status ?? operation.error.code}`,
      )
      return operation
    }
    await waitForRetry(Math.min(1_000, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())), input.signal)
  }
  throw new Error(`Operation ${name} did not finish within Job budget`)
}

type ExactJobDeleteResult = {
  outcome: 'deleted' | 'absent'
  preservedFailures: readonly unknown[]
}

export async function deleteExactRunJob(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  acknowledged: VerifiedRunJob,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<ExactJobDeleteResult> {
  const reconciliationInput = mutationReconciliationInput(input)
  const jobName = acknowledged.name.split('/').at(-1)!
  const fresh = await getExactRunJob(reconciliationInput, jobName, runCommand)
  if (!fresh) return { outcome: 'absent', preservedFailures: [] }
  if (fresh.uid !== acknowledged.uid ||
      fresh.generation !== acknowledged.generation) {
    throw new Error(
      'Fresh Job UID/generation differs from acknowledged ownership',
    )
  }

  let deleteFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    const response = await invokeClosedRunCommand(reconciliationInput, {
      kind: 'delete-job',
      projectId: input.projectId,
      region: input.runRegion,
      jobName,
      etag: fresh.etag,
    }, runCommand)
    if (response.httpStatus < 200 || response.httpStatus >= 300) {
      throw structuredRunError(response)
    }
    const operation = operationSchema.parse(response.body)
    await waitForRunOperation(reconciliationInput, operation.name, runCommand)
  } catch (error) {
    // A delete may have been accepted despite a transport/settlement/Operation
    // failure. Never retry it; exact-GET the internally generated name instead.
    deleteFailure = error
  }

  const remaining = await getExactRunJob(reconciliationInput, jobName, runCommand)
  if (!remaining) {
    return {
      outcome: 'deleted',
      preservedFailures: deleteFailure === undefined ? [] : [deleteFailure],
    }
  }
  if (remaining.uid !== acknowledged.uid ||
      remaining.generation !== acknowledged.generation) {
    throw new AggregateError(
      deleteFailure === undefined ? [] : [deleteFailure],
      'Job name now belongs to a foreign UID/generation after delete ambiguity',
    )
  }
  throw new AggregateError(
    deleteFailure === undefined ? [] : [deleteFailure],
    'Exact owned Job remains after delete reconciliation; delete was not retried',
  )
}

class ExplicitRunRejection extends Error {}

async function containAmbiguousRun(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  acknowledged: VerifiedRunJob,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<readonly unknown[]> {
  const fresh = await getExactRunJob(input, acknowledged.name.split('/').at(-1)!, runCommand)
  if (!fresh ||
      fresh.uid !== acknowledged.uid ||
      fresh.generation !== acknowledged.generation ||
      fresh.etag.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Cannot prove exact Job ownership for containment')
  }
  return (await deleteExactRunJob(input, acknowledged, runCommand)).preservedFailures
}

export async function runExactJobOnce(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  job: VerifiedRunJob,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<ExecutionIdentity> {
  const jobName = job.name.split('/').at(-1)!
  let primary: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    const response = await invokeClosedRunCommand(input, {
      kind: 'run-job',
      projectId: input.projectId,
      region: input.runRegion,
      jobName,
      etag: job.etag,
    }, runCommand)
    if (response.httpStatus >= 400 && response.httpStatus < 500) {
      throw new ExplicitRunRejection(structuredRunError(response).message)
    }
    if (response.httpStatus < 200 || response.httpStatus >= 300) {
      throw structuredRunError(response)
    }
    const accepted = operationSchema.parse(response.body)
    const terminal = await waitForRunOperation(input, accepted.name, runCommand)
    if (!terminal.response) throw new Error('Run Operation omitted Execution identity')
    const prefix = `${job.name}/executions/`
    if (!terminal.response.name.startsWith(prefix)) {
      throw new Error('Run Operation returned foreign Execution resource')
    }
    const name = terminal.response.name.slice(prefix.length)
    if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$/.test(name) || !terminal.response.uid) {
      throw new Error('Run Operation returned malformed Execution identity')
    }
    return {
      operationName: accepted.name,
      resourceName: terminal.response.name,
      name,
      uid: terminal.response.uid,
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ExplicitRunRejection) throw error
    primary = error
  }
  let containmentFailures: readonly unknown[]
  try {
    containmentFailures = await containAmbiguousRun(
      mutationReconciliationInput(input), job, runCommand,
    )
  } catch (containmentFailure) {
    throw new AggregateError(
      [primary, containmentFailure].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
      'Execution identity unknown; exact owned Job containment failed',
    )
  }
  throw new AggregateError(
    [primary, ...containmentFailures].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
    'Execution identity unknown; exact owned Job containment completed',
  )
}

const executionReadSchema: z.ZodType<ExecutionRead> = z.object({
  metadata: z.object({
    name: z.string().min(1),
    namespace: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+$/),
    uid: z.string().min(1),
  }).passthrough(),
  spec: z.object({
    taskCount: z.number().int().positive(),
    template: z.object({
      spec: z.object({
        serviceAccountName: z.string().email(),
      }).passthrough(),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).passthrough(),
  status: z.object({
    conditions: z.array(z.object({
      type: z.string(),
      status: z.enum(['True', 'False', 'Unknown']),
      reason: z.string().optional(),
    }).passthrough()),
    succeededCount: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    failedCount: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    cancelledCount: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    runningCount: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
    startTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }).optional(),
    completionTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }).optional(),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()
const counterKeys = [
  'succeededCount', 'failedCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount',
] as const

export function canonicalizeExecution(raw: unknown): CanonicalExecution {
  const execution = executionReadSchema.parse(raw)
  const completed = execution.status.conditions.filter(({ type }) =>
    type === 'Completed')
  if (completed.length !== 1 ||
      !['True', 'False'].includes(completed[0].status) ||
      !execution.status.completionTime) {
    throw new Error('Execution is not exact terminal Completed state')
  }
  const counters = Object.fromEntries(counterKeys.map((key) => [
    key, execution.status[key] ?? 0,
  ])) as ExecutionCounters
  if (counters.runningCount !== 0 ||
      counters.succeededCount + counters.failedCount + counters.cancelledCount !==
        execution.spec.taskCount) {
    throw new Error('Terminal Execution counters violate exact task arithmetic')
  }
  return {
    execution,
    counters,
    omittedZeroCounters: counterKeys.filter((key) => execution.status[key] === undefined),
    completionStatus: completed[0].status as 'True' | 'False',
  }
}

export function executionSucceeded(value: CanonicalExecution): boolean {
  return value.completionStatus === 'True' &&
    value.counters.succeededCount === value.execution.spec.taskCount &&
    value.counters.failedCount === 0 && value.counters.cancelledCount === 0 &&
    value.counters.runningCount === 0
}

export async function waitForExecutionTerminal(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  execution: ExecutionIdentity,
  projectNumber: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CanonicalExecution> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.executionMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const raw = await gcloudJson(executionReadSchema, [
      'run', 'jobs', 'executions', 'describe', execution.name,
      `--region=${input.runRegion}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`, '--format=json',
    ], input, runCommand, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now()))
    if (raw.metadata.name !== execution.name || raw.metadata.uid !== execution.uid ||
        raw.metadata.namespace !== projectNumber ||
        raw.spec.taskCount !== input.taskCount ||
        raw.spec.template.spec.serviceAccountName !== input.runtimeServiceAccount) {
      throw new Error('Execution describe returned foreign identity/configuration')
    }
    const completed = raw.status.conditions.filter(({ type }) => type === 'Completed')
    if (completed.length > 1) throw new Error('Execution has duplicate Completed conditions')
    if (completed.length === 1 && completed[0].status !== 'Unknown') {
      return canonicalizeExecution(raw)
    }
    await waitForRetry(Math.min(2_000, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())), input.signal)
  }
  throw new Error(`Execution ${execution.name} did not become terminal`)
}

const cloudPartResultSchema: z.ZodType<CloudPartResult> = z.object({
  marker: z.literal('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT'),
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  kind: z.enum(['vitest', 'e2e']),
  runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
  execution: z.string().min(1),
  partId: z.string().min(1),
  taskIndex: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  taskCount: z.number().int().positive(),
  taskAttempt: z.literal(0),
  status: z.enum(['passed', 'failed']),
  exitCode: z.number().int(),
  testCount: z.number().int().positive().optional(),
}).strict()
const loggingEntrySchema = z.object({
  resource: z.object({
    type: z.literal('cloud_run_job'),
    labels: z.object({
      project_id: z.string(),
      location: z.string(),
      job_name: z.string(),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).strict(),
  labels: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
  jsonPayload: z.unknown().optional(),
  textPayload: z.string().optional(),
}).passthrough()

function executionLogFilter(
  projectId: string,
  runRegion: string,
  jobName: string,
  executionName: string,
): string {
  return [
    'resource.type="cloud_run_job"',
    `resource.labels.project_id="${projectId}"`,
    `resource.labels.location="${runRegion}"`,
    `resource.labels.job_name="${jobName}"`,
    `labels."run.googleapis.com/execution_name"="${executionName}"`,
    'jsonPayload.marker="FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT"',
  ].join(' AND ')
}

export async function readExactExecutionLogs(
  input: ExactExecutionLogInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<readonly z.infer<typeof loggingEntrySchema>[]> {
  if (!Number.isInteger(input.limit) || input.limit < 1 || input.limit > 10_000) {
    throw new Error('Exact log limit must be in 1..10000')
  }
  const filter = executionLogFilter(
    input.projectId, input.runRegion, input.jobName, input.executionName,
  )
  const result = await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
    'logging', 'read', filter,
    `--limit=${input.limit}`, '--order=asc',
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ], {
    signal: input.signal,
    output: {
      kind: 'capture', maxStdoutBytes: 1024 * 1024, maxStderrBytes: 64 * 1024,
    },
    timeoutMs: input.budgets.resultReadMs,
    terminateGraceMs: input.budgets.terminateGraceMs,
    settlementTimeoutMs: input.budgets.settlementMs,
  })
  let decoded: unknown
  try { decoded = JSON.parse(result.stdout) }
  catch (error) { throw new Error(`Cloud Logging returned non-JSON: ${errorMessage(error)}`) }
  return z.array(loggingEntrySchema).max(input.limit).parse(decoded)
}

function validatePartCandidate(
  entry: z.infer<typeof loggingEntrySchema>,
  collection: ResultCollectionInput,
): CloudPartResult | undefined {
  if (entry.jsonPayload === undefined) {
    if (entry.textPayload !== undefined) return undefined
    throw new Error('Logging row has neither structured nor diagnostic payload')
  }
  const part = cloudPartResultSchema.parse(entry.jsonPayload)
  const { input, runId, jobName, executionName } = collection
  if (entry.resource.labels.project_id !== input.projectId ||
      entry.resource.labels.location !== input.runRegion ||
      entry.resource.labels.job_name !== jobName ||
      entry.labels['run.googleapis.com/execution_name'] !== executionName ||
      part.kind !== input.kind || part.runId !== runId ||
      part.execution !== executionName || part.taskCount !== input.taskCount ||
      part.taskIndex >= input.taskCount || part.status !== 'passed' || part.exitCode !== 0 ||
      (part.kind === 'vitest' ? part.testCount === undefined : part.testCount !== undefined)) {
    throw new Error(`Foreign or failed result candidate ${part.partId}`)
  }
  return part
}

export async function collectExactPartResults(
  collection: ResultCollectionInput,
): Promise<CloudPartResult[]> {
  const { input, jobName, executionName, runCommand } = collection
  const expected = [...input.expectedParts].sort()
  if (new Set(expected).size !== expected.length) throw new Error('Expected parts contain duplicates')
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.resultReadMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const rows = await readExactExecutionLogs({
      account: input.account,
      projectId: input.projectId,
      runRegion: input.runRegion,
      jobName,
      executionName,
      limit: expected.length + 1,
      budgets: input.budgets,
      signal: input.signal,
    }, runCommand)
    const parts = rows.map((row) => validatePartCandidate(row, collection))
      .filter((value): value is CloudPartResult => value !== undefined)
    const actual = parts.map(({ partId }) => partId).sort()
    if (new Set(actual).size !== actual.length) throw new Error('Duplicate result part')
    if (actual.some((part) => !expected.includes(part))) throw new Error('Unknown result part')
    if (JSON.stringify(actual) === JSON.stringify(expected)) return parts
    await waitForRetry(Math.min(2_000, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())), input.signal)
  }
  throw new Error('Exact expected result parts did not arrive before result-read deadline')
}
```

Add this closed raw-contract envelope and replay body to `lifecycle.ts`. It is
not a transport seam: it accepts exactly the committed Build/Job/Operation/
Execution/LogEntry bundle and routes each member through the same production
schema, authority check, canonicalizer, and result candidate validator used by
the lifecycle:

```ts
const rawLiveContractsEnvelopeSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  characterization: z.object({
    acceptedSha: z.string().regex(FULL_OBJECT_ID),
    capturedAt: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),
    providerVersion: z.string()
      .regex(/^Google Cloud SDK [0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$/),
    apiVersions: z.object({
      build: z.literal('cloudbuild.googleapis.com/v1'),
      job: z.literal('run.googleapis.com/v2'),
      operation: z.literal('run.googleapis.com/v2'),
      execution: z.literal('run.googleapis.com/v1'),
      logEntry: z.literal('logging.googleapis.com/v2'),
    }).strict(),
    fieldPresence: z.object({
      build: z.array(z.string()).min(8),
      job: z.array(z.string()).min(8),
      operation: z.array(z.string()).min(3),
      executionSuccess: z.array(z.string()).min(7),
      executionFailure: z.array(z.string()).min(6),
      logEntrySuccess: z.array(z.string()).min(7),
      logEntryFailure: z.array(z.string()).min(7),
    }).strict(),
  }).strict(),
  context: z.object({
    projectId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,61}[a-z0-9]$/),
    projectNumber: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+$/),
    runRegion: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]+$/),
    repository: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9._-]+$/),
    imageName: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]+$/),
    buildServiceAccount: z.string().email(),
    runtimeServiceAccount: z.string().email(),
    runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
    failureRunId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
    sourceBucket: z.string().min(1),
    sourceObject: z.string().min(1),
    sourceGeneration: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
    imageTag: z.string().min(1),
    immutableImage: z.string().regex(/@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/),
    jobName: z.string().min(1),
    failureJobName: z.string().min(1),
    executionSuccessName: z.string().min(1),
    executionFailureName: z.string().min(1),
    taskTimeout: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*s$/),
    memory: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*(?:Mi|Gi)$/),
    cpu: z.number().int().positive(),
    workloadEnv: z.record(
      z.string().regex(/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*$/),
      z.string(),
    ),
    expectedSuccessParts: z.tuple([z.literal('server:1/1')]),
  }).strict(),
  build: z.unknown(),
  job: z.unknown(),
  operation: z.unknown(),
  executionSuccess: z.unknown(),
  executionFailure: z.unknown(),
  logEntrySuccess: z.unknown(),
  logEntryFailure: z.unknown(),
}).strict()

export type RawLiveContractsReplay = {
  acceptedSha: string
  providerVersion: string
  successCounters: ExecutionCounters
  failureCounters: ExecutionCounters
  failureOmittedCounters: readonly (keyof ExecutionCounters)[]
  successPartIds: readonly string[]
  failureResultRejected: true
}

export function replayRawLiveContracts(value: unknown): RawLiveContractsReplay {
  if (Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(value), 'utf8') > 1024 * 1024) {
    throw new Error('Raw live-contract fixture exceeds 1 MiB')
  }
  if (/authorization|access[_-]?token|password/i.test(JSON.stringify(value))) {
    throw new Error('Raw live-contract fixture contains a credential-shaped key')
  }
  const raw = rawLiveContractsEnvelopeSchema.parse(value)
  const context = raw.context
  const build = buildRowSchema.and(z.object({
    name: z.string().min(1),
    projectId: z.string().min(1),
  }).passthrough()).parse(raw.build)
  const taggedImage =
    `${context.runRegion}-docker.pkg.dev/${context.projectId}/` +
    `${context.repository}/${context.imageName}:${context.imageTag}`
  const [immutableTagBase, immutableDigest] =
    context.immutableImage.split('@') as [string, string]
  const steps = build.steps ?? []
  const resultImages = build.results?.images ?? []
  const builder = steps[0]?.name
  if (build.projectId !== context.projectId ||
      build.name !==
        `projects/${context.projectNumber}/locations/global/builds/${build.id}` ||
      build.status !== 'SUCCESS' ||
      build.source.storageSource.bucket !== context.sourceBucket ||
      build.source.storageSource.object !== context.sourceObject ||
      build.source.storageSource.generation !== context.sourceGeneration ||
      build.substitutions._IMAGE !== taggedImage ||
      !build.tags.includes(`freshell-${context.runId}`) ||
      !build.tags.includes(`freshell-run-${context.runId}`) ||
      build.serviceAccount !==
        `projects/${context.projectId}/serviceAccounts/${context.buildServiceAccount}` ||
      !builder?.match(
        /^gcr\.io\/cloud-builders\/docker@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/,
      ) ||
      steps[1]?.name !== builder ||
      JSON.stringify(steps.map(({ args }) => args)) !== JSON.stringify([
        [
          'build', '--no-cache', '--file', 'docker/cloud-run/Dockerfile',
          '--tag', '${_IMAGE}', '.',
        ],
        ['push', '${_IMAGE}'],
      ]) ||
      JSON.stringify(build.images) !== JSON.stringify(['${_IMAGE}']) ||
      build.options?.machineType !== 'E2_HIGHCPU_32' ||
      build.options?.diskSizeGb !== 200 ||
      build.options?.logging !== 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY' ||
      build.timeout !== '3600s' ||
      immutableTagBase !==
        `${context.runRegion}-docker.pkg.dev/${context.projectId}/` +
        `${context.repository}/${context.imageName}` ||
      resultImages.length !== 1 ||
      resultImages[0].name !== taggedImage ||
      resultImages[0].digest !== immutableDigest) {
    throw new Error('Raw Build fixture fails authoritative identity/config checks')
  }

  const input: CloudRunLifecycleInput = {
    origin: { surface: 'vitest-run', argv: [] },
    kind: 'vitest',
    repoRoot: process.cwd(),
    account: 'caller@example.com',
    projectId: context.projectId,
    iamAnalysisScope: 'organizations/123456789',
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: context.runRegion,
    sourceBucket: context.sourceBucket,
    repository: context.repository,
    imageName: context.imageName,
    jobPrefix: 'freshell-vitest',
    buildServiceAccount: context.buildServiceAccount,
    runtimeServiceAccount: context.runtimeServiceAccount,
    buildMode: 'cloud',
    operation: 'run',
    taskCount: 1,
    taskTimeout: context.taskTimeout,
    memory: context.memory,
    cpu: context.cpu,
    workloadEnv: context.workloadEnv,
    expectedParts: context.expectedSuccessParts,
    budgets: {
      overallMs: 1, provenanceMs: 1, sourceMs: 1, buildMs: 1,
      imageMs: 1, jobMs: 1, executionMs: 1, resultReadMs: 1,
      cleanupMs: 1, commandMs: 1, terminateGraceMs: 1, settlementMs: 1,
    },
  }
  const identity: InvocationIdentity = {
    runId: context.runId,
    sourceObject: context.sourceObject,
    imageTag: context.imageTag,
    buildCorrelationTag: `freshell-${context.runId}`,
    jobName: context.jobName,
  }
  const job = runJobSchema.parse(raw.job)
  assertExactJobConfiguration(
    input,
    identity,
    job,
    context.immutableImage,
    {
      ...context.workloadEnv,
      FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND: 'vitest',
      FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID: context.runId,
    },
  )
  const operation = operationSchema.parse(raw.operation)
  if (!operation.done || !operation.response ||
      operation.response.name !== `${job.name}/executions/${context.executionSuccessName}`) {
    throw new Error('Raw Operation fixture omits exact Execution identity')
  }

  const success = canonicalizeExecution(raw.executionSuccess)
  const failure = canonicalizeExecution(raw.executionFailure)
  if (success.execution.metadata.name !== context.executionSuccessName ||
      failure.execution.metadata.name !== context.executionFailureName ||
      success.execution.metadata.namespace !== context.projectNumber ||
      failure.execution.metadata.namespace !== context.projectNumber ||
      success.execution.spec.template.spec.serviceAccountName !==
        context.runtimeServiceAccount ||
      failure.execution.spec.template.spec.serviceAccountName !==
        context.runtimeServiceAccount ||
      !executionSucceeded(success) || executionSucceeded(failure)) {
    throw new Error('Raw Execution fixtures violate success/failure authority')
  }
  const failureValue = raw.executionFailure as {
    status: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>
  }
  const requiredOmissions = [
    'succeededCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount',
  ] as const
  if (requiredOmissions.some((key) =>
      Object.hasOwn(failureValue.status, key))) {
    throw new Error('Failure raw fixture is not failedCount-only')
  }

  const successEntry = loggingEntrySchema.parse(raw.logEntrySuccess)
  const failureEntry = loggingEntrySchema.parse(raw.logEntryFailure)
  const successCollection: ResultCollectionInput = {
    input,
    runId: context.runId,
    jobName: context.jobName,
    executionName: context.executionSuccessName,
    runCommand: defaultRunCommand,
  }
  const accepted = validatePartCandidate(successEntry, successCollection)
  if (!accepted) throw new Error('Success LogEntry did not yield a result candidate')
  const failurePart = cloudPartResultSchema.parse(failureEntry.jsonPayload)
  if (failurePart.status !== 'failed' ||
      failurePart.exitCode === 0 ||
      failurePart.kind !== 'vitest' ||
      failurePart.runId !== context.failureRunId ||
      failurePart.execution !== context.executionFailureName ||
      failureEntry.resource.labels.project_id !== context.projectId ||
      failureEntry.resource.labels.location !== context.runRegion ||
      failureEntry.resource.labels.job_name !== context.failureJobName ||
      failureEntry.labels['run.googleapis.com/execution_name'] !==
        context.executionFailureName) {
    throw new Error('Failure LogEntry is not exact failed result data')
  }
  let failureResultRejected = false
  try {
    validatePartCandidate(failureEntry, {
      ...successCollection,
      runId: context.failureRunId,
      jobName: context.failureJobName,
      executionName: context.executionFailureName,
    })
  } catch {
    failureResultRejected = true
  }
  if (!failureResultRejected) {
    throw new Error('Failure LogEntry was incorrectly accepted as success')
  }
  return {
    acceptedSha: raw.characterization.acceptedSha,
    providerVersion: raw.characterization.providerVersion,
    successCounters: success.counters,
    failureCounters: failure.counters,
    failureOmittedCounters: failure.omittedZeroCounters,
    successPartIds: [accepted.partId],
    failureResultRejected: true,
  }
}
```

import type {
  BuildAuthority,

  BuiltImage,

  CorrelationWindow,
  CloudRunLifecycleInput,

type CreatePendingOwnership = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runId: string,
) => OwnedResources
type CleanupOwnedResources = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
) => Promise<unknown[]>
type AcquireSnapshotOwnership = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
) => void
type AcquireSourceOwnership = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  source: SourceIdentity,
) => void

  owned: OwnedResources,
  build: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
) => void
type AcquireBuildAndImageOwnership = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  built: BuiltImage,
) => void
