```ts
  owned: OwnedResources,
  job: VerifiedRunJob,
) => void
type AcquireExecutionOwnership = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  execution: ExecutionIdentity,
) => void
type AcquireTerminalExecution = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  terminal: CanonicalExecution,
) => void
type EmitOwnershipEvent = (
  write: (line: string) => unknown,
  phase: 'intent' | 'possibly-accepted' | 'acquired' | 'reconciled' | 'residual',
  value: unknown,
) => void
type EmitTerminalReceipt = (
  write: (line: string) => unknown,
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
) => void
type ParseOwnershipReceipt = (value: unknown) => OwnershipReceipt
type CreateSourceMutationIntent = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
) => Promise<SourceMutationIntent>
type CreateBuildMutationIntent = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  source: SourceIdentity,
  template: Readonly<BuildTemplate>,
  window: CorrelationWindow,
) => BuildMutationIntent
type CreateJobMutationIntent = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  immutableImage: string,
  expectedEnv: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
  window: CorrelationWindow,
) => JobMutationIntent
type CreateLocalPushMutationIntent = (
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
) => LocalPushMutationIntent
type RegisterMutationIntent = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  intent: MutationIntent,
) => void
type MarkMutationPossiblyAccepted = (
  owned: OwnedResources,
  kind: MutationIntent['kind'],
) => void

const createPendingOwnership: CreatePendingOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CreatePendingOwnership>('createPendingOwnership')(...args)
const cleanupOwnedResources: CleanupOwnedResources = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CleanupOwnedResources>('cleanupOwnedResources')(...args)
const cleanupWithIndependentBudget: CleanupOwnedResources = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CleanupOwnedResources>(
    'cleanupWithIndependentBudget',
  )(...args)
const acquireSnapshotOwnership: AcquireSnapshotOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireSnapshotOwnership>('acquireSnapshotOwnership')(...args)
const acquireSourceOwnership: AcquireSourceOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireSourceOwnership>('acquireSourceOwnership')(...args)
const acquireBuildOwnership: AcquireBuildOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireBuildOwnership>('acquireBuildOwnership')(...args)
const acquireBuildAndImageOwnership: AcquireBuildAndImageOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireBuildAndImageOwnership>(
    'acquireBuildAndImageOwnership',
  )(...args)
const acquireJobOwnership: AcquireJobOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireJobOwnership>('acquireJobOwnership')(...args)
const acquireExecutionOwnership: AcquireExecutionOwnership = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireExecutionOwnership>('acquireExecutionOwnership')(...args)
const acquireTerminalExecution: AcquireTerminalExecution = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<AcquireTerminalExecution>('acquireTerminalExecution')(...args)
const emitOwnershipEvent: EmitOwnershipEvent = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<EmitOwnershipEvent>('emitOwnershipEvent')(...args)
const emitTerminalReceipt: EmitTerminalReceipt = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<EmitTerminalReceipt>('emitTerminalReceipt')(...args)
const parseOwnershipReceipt: ParseOwnershipReceipt = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<ParseOwnershipReceipt>('parseOwnershipReceipt')(...args)
const createSourceMutationIntent: CreateSourceMutationIntent = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CreateSourceMutationIntent>(
    'createSourceMutationIntent',
  )(...args)
const createBuildMutationIntent: CreateBuildMutationIntent = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CreateBuildMutationIntent>(
    'createBuildMutationIntent',
  )(...args)
const createJobMutationIntent: CreateJobMutationIntent = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CreateJobMutationIntent>(
    'createJobMutationIntent',
  )(...args)
const createLocalPushMutationIntent: CreateLocalPushMutationIntent = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<CreateLocalPushMutationIntent>(
    'createLocalPushMutationIntent',
  )(...args)
const registerMutationIntent: RegisterMutationIntent = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<RegisterMutationIntent>(
    'registerMutationIntent',
  )(...args)
const markMutationPossiblyAccepted: MarkMutationPossiblyAccepted = (...args) =>
  requiredLifecycleFunction<MarkMutationPossiblyAccepted>(
    'markMutationPossiblyAccepted',
  )(...args)

async function cleanupOwnedFixture(options: {
  build?: boolean
  image?: boolean
  job?: boolean
  execution?: boolean
} = {}) {
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.runId)
  const tempRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), `freshell-clean-${IDENTITY.runId}-`))
  const sourceDir = join(tempRoot, 'source')
  const dockerConfigDir = join(tempRoot, 'docker-config')
  await mkdir(sourceDir, { mode: 0o700 })
  await mkdir(dockerConfigDir, { mode: 0o700 })
  await mkdir(join(sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run'), {
    recursive: true,
    mode: 0o700,
  })
  await writeFile(
    join(sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run/cloudbuild.yaml'),
    TEMPLATE_TEXT,
    { mode: 0o600 },
  )
  const snapshot = {
    branch: 'feature', upstreamRemote: 'origin', upstreamMergeRef: 'refs/heads/feature',
    head: 'a'.repeat(40), tempRoot, sourceArchive: join(tempRoot, 'source.tar.gz'),
    sourceDir, buildTemplatePath: join(sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run/cloudbuild.yaml'),
    dockerConfigDir,
  }
  acquireSnapshotOwnership(owned, snapshot, IDENTITY)
  acquireSourceOwnership(owned, SOURCE)
  const image = {
    taggedImage: `${IMAGE_PATH}:${IDENTITY.imageTag}`,
    tag: IDENTITY.imageTag,
    tagResourceName: TEST_TAG_RESOURCE,
    digest: `sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
    immutableImage: `${IMAGE_PATH}@sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
    digestVersionResourceName:
      `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e/packages/freshell-vitest/versions/sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
    localTags: [`freshell-vitest:${IDENTITY.runId}`],
  }
  if (options.build !== false) {
    const template = await readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath)
    registerMutationIntent(owned, createBuildMutationIntent(
      BASE_INPUT,
      BUILD_AUTHORITY,
      snapshot,
      IDENTITY,
      SOURCE,
      template,
      TEST_WINDOW,
    ))
    markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'build')
    acquireBuildOwnership(owned, terminalBuildFixture())
  }
  if (options.image !== false) {
    owned.image = image
    owned.receipt.image = { ...image, state: 'owned' }
  }
  if (options.job !== false) acquireJobOwnership(owned, VERIFIED_JOB)
  if (options.execution !== false) {
    acquireExecutionOwnership(owned, {
      operationName: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/run-op',
      resourceName: `${VERIFIED_JOB.name}/executions/execution-owned`,
      name: 'execution-owned',
      uid: 'execution-uid-1',
    })
    acquireTerminalExecution(owned, canonicalizeExecution(
      fixtures.executionSuccessOmittedZeros,
    ))
  }
  return owned
}

it('ledgers exact source Build and Job intent before possibly accepted state', async () => {
  const write = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write').mockImplementation(() => true)
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  const template = await readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath)
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.runId)
  acquireSnapshotOwnership(owned, snapshot, IDENTITY)

  const sourceIntent = await createSourceMutationIntent(
    BASE_INPUT,
    snapshot,
    IDENTITY,
  )
  registerMutationIntent(owned, sourceIntent)
  markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'source')

  const buildIntent = createBuildMutationIntent(
    BASE_INPUT,
    BUILD_AUTHORITY,
    snapshot,
    IDENTITY,
    SOURCE,
    template,
    TEST_WINDOW,
  )
  registerMutationIntent(owned, buildIntent)
  markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'build')
  expect(owned.build).toBeUndefined()
  expect(owned).toMatchObject({
    buildTerminal: false,
    buildMayReadSource: true,
    buildIntent: { state: 'possibly-accepted' },
  })

  const jobIntent = createJobMutationIntent(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, taskCount: 1 },
    IDENTITY,
    VERIFIED_JOB.template.template.containers[0].image,
    {
      FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND: 'vitest',
      FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID: IDENTITY.runId,
    },
    TEST_WINDOW,
  )
  registerMutationIntent(owned, jobIntent)
  markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'job')

  expect(owned.sourceIntent).toMatchObject({
    state: 'possibly-accepted',
    bucket: BASE_INPUT.sourceBucket,
    object: IDENTITY.sourceObject,
    expectedMetadata: { runId: IDENTITY.runId, head: snapshot.head },
  })
  expect(owned.buildIntent).toMatchObject({
    state: 'possibly-accepted',
    correlationTag: IDENTITY.buildCorrelationTag,
    source: SOURCE,
    taggedImage: `${IMAGE_PATH}:${IDENTITY.imageTag}`,
    window: TEST_WINDOW,
  })
  expect(owned.jobIntent).toMatchObject({
    state: 'possibly-accepted',
    jobName: IDENTITY.jobName,
    immutableImage: VERIFIED_JOB.template.template.containers[0].image,
    taskTimeout: '1800s',
    window: TEST_WINDOW,
  })
  const events = write.mock.calls.map(([line]) => String(line))
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP '))
    .map((line) => JSON.parse(line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP '.length)))
  expect(events.map(({ phase }) => phase)).toEqual([
    'acquired',
    'intent', 'possibly-accepted',
    'intent', 'possibly-accepted',
    'intent', 'possibly-accepted',
  ])
  expect(JSON.stringify(events)).not.toMatch(/short-lived-token|authorization|stdin/i)
  write.mockRestore()
})

it('registers both local tags and the remote push before local mutation', async () => {
  const snapshot = await makeSnapshot()
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, buildMode: 'local' },
    IDENTITY.runId,
  )
  acquireSnapshotOwnership(owned, snapshot, IDENTITY)
  const intent = createLocalPushMutationIntent(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, buildMode: 'local' },
    BUILD_AUTHORITY,
    snapshot,
    IDENTITY,
  )
  registerMutationIntent(owned, intent)
  markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'local-push')
  expect(owned.localPushIntent).toMatchObject({
    state: 'possibly-accepted',
    correlationTag: IDENTITY.buildCorrelationTag,
    authority: BUILD_AUTHORITY,
    runId: IDENTITY.runId,
    head: snapshot.head,
    remoteTag: `${IMAGE_PATH}:${IDENTITY.imageTag}`,
    localTags: [
      `freshell-vitest:${IDENTITY.runId}`,
      `${IMAGE_PATH}:${IDENTITY.imageTag}`,
    ],
  })
  expect(owned.localPushIntent).not.toHaveProperty('recoveryArgv')
  expect(owned.receipt.image).toMatchObject({
    state: 'possibly-accepted',
    taggedImage: `${IMAGE_PATH}:${IDENTITY.imageTag}`,
  })
})

it('attempts every owned cleanup and preserves every independent failure', async () => {
  const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture()
  owned.terminalExecution = undefined
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockRejectedValue(new Error('provider unavailable'))
  const errors = await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
  expect(errors.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5)
  expect(new Set(owned.receipt.residuals.map(({ kind }) => kind))).toEqual(new Set([
    'execution', 'job', 'build', 'source', 'tag',
  ]))
  expect(owned.receipt.residuals.every((residual) =>
    typeof residual.kind === 'string' &&
    typeof residual.identity === 'string' &&
    typeof residual.reason === 'string' &&
    !Object.hasOwn(residual, 'recoveryArgv'),
  )).toBe(true)
})

it('records terminal, absent, deleted, and retained dispositions exactly', async () => {
  const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture()
  const rows = new Map<string, unknown[]>([
    ['storage-list', [[{
      bucket: SOURCE.bucket, name: SOURCE.object, generation: SOURCE.generation,
      size: SOURCE.size,
      custom_fields: {
        'freshell-run-id': IDENTITY.runId,
        'freshell-head': SOURCE.commit,
      },
    }], []]],
    ['tag-list', [[{
      image: IMAGE_PATH,
      tag: TEST_TAG_RESOURCE,
      version: TEST_VERSION_RESOURCE,
    }], []]],
  ])
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (text.includes('artifacts versions list')) {
      return commandOk([{ name: owned.image!.digestVersionResourceName }])
    }
    if (text.includes('storage objects list')) return commandOk(rows.get('storage-list')!.shift())
    if (text.includes('storage rm')) {
      expect(args).toContain(`--if-generation-match=${SOURCE.generation}`)
      return commandOk()
    }
    if (text.includes('artifacts docker tags list')) return commandOk(rows.get('tag-list')!.shift())
    if (text.includes('artifacts docker tags delete')) return commandOk()
    if (text.includes('docker image rm')) {
      expect(options.env?.DOCKER_CONFIG).toBe(owned.snapshot!.dockerConfigDir)
      return commandOk()
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected cleanup command ${text}`)
  })
  owned.job = undefined
  const errors = await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
  expect(errors).toEqual([])
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'execution', outcome: 'terminal' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'build', outcome: 'terminal' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'source', outcome: 'deleted' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'tag', outcome: 'deleted' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'local-image', outcome: 'deleted' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'docker-config', outcome: 'deleted' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'temp-root', outcome: 'deleted' }),
  ]))
  await expect(stat(owned.snapshot!.tempRoot)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' })
})

it('records a local rebuild terminally without reading Cloud Build', async () => {
  const input = { ...BASE_INPUT, buildMode: 'local' as const }
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(input, IDENTITY.runId)
  acquireBuildOwnership(owned, {
    ...terminalBuildFixture(),
    id: `local-build-${IDENTITY.runId}`,
    resourceName: `local-builds/${IDENTITY.runId}`,
  })
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
  expect(await cleanupOwnedResources(input, owned, runner)).toEqual([])
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'build', outcome: 'terminal',
  }))
  expect(runner).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
})

it.each(['INTERNAL_ERROR', 'EXPIRED'] as const)(
  'treats documented Build status %s as failed-but-terminal during cleanup',
  async (status) => {
    const owned = await activeCleanupFixture()
    const row = { ...buildRow(), status }
    acquireBuildOwnership(owned, {
      ...terminalBuildFixture(),
      status,
      normalizedConfig: row,
    })
    const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (_command, args) =>
      args.includes('artifacts') ? commandOk([]) : commandOk([row]))
    expect(await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)).toEqual([])
    expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
      kind: 'build', outcome: 'terminal',
    }))
    expect(commandTranscript(runner)).not.toContain('builds cancel')
  },
)

it('records pre-absent source, tag, and Job without claiming deletion', async () => {
  const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture({
    build: false,
    job: true,
    execution: false,
  })
  owned.execution = undefined
  owned.terminalExecution = undefined
  owned.image!.localTags = []
  const token = { ...commandOk(), stdout: 'short-lived-token\n' }
  const runner = fixedRunTransportRunner([
    token,
    runTransportResult(404, {
      error: { code: 404, status: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'absent' },
    }),
  ])
  const mixed = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (command === process.execPath || text.includes('print-access-token')) {
      return runner(command, args, options)
    }
    if (text.includes('storage objects list')) return commandOk([])
    if (text.includes('tags list')) return commandOk([])
    if (text.includes('versions list')) {
      return commandOk([{ name: owned.image!.digestVersionResourceName }])
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected absent command ${text}`)
  })
  await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, mixed)
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'job', outcome: 'absent' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'source', outcome: 'absent' }),
    expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'tag', outcome: 'absent' }),
  ]))
  expect(runner.mock.calls.map(([command, args]) => [
    command,
    args.slice(0, 3),
  ])).toEqual([
    ['gcloud', [
      'auth', 'print-access-token', `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    ]],
    [process.execPath, ['--input-type=module', '--eval', expect.any(String)]],
  ])
  expect(runner.mock.calls[1][2].stdin).toContain('short-lived-token')
})

it('deletes the source when no Build mutation could have started', async () => {
  const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture({
    build: false,
    image: false,
    job: false,
    execution: false,
  })
  const generations: unknown[] = [[{
    bucket: SOURCE.bucket, name: SOURCE.object, generation: SOURCE.generation,
    size: SOURCE.size,
    custom_fields: {
      'freshell-run-id': IDENTITY.runId,
      'freshell-head': SOURCE.commit,
    },
  }], []]
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (_command, args) => {
    if (args.includes('list')) return commandOk(generations.shift())
    if (args.includes('rm')) return commandOk()
    throw new Error(`unexpected pre-Build cleanup ${args.join(' ')}`)
  })
  expect(await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)).toEqual([])
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'source', outcome: 'deleted',
  }))
})

it('retains an acknowledged source generation while Build identity is unresolved', async () => {
  const owned = await activeCleanupFixture()
  expect(owned.build).toBeUndefined()
  expect(owned).toMatchObject({
    buildMayReadSource: true,
    buildTerminal: false,
    buildIntent: { state: 'possibly-accepted' },
  })
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
  const errors = await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
  expect(errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'source', outcome: 'retained',
  }))
  const residual = owned.receipt.residuals.find(({ kind }) => kind === 'source')!
  expect(residual).toMatchObject({
    identity: `gs://${SOURCE.bucket}/${SOURCE.object}#${SOURCE.generation}`,
    reason: 'Source generation retained because Build may still read it',
  })
  expect(residual).not.toHaveProperty('recoveryArgv')
  expect(commandTranscript(runner)).not.toMatch(/storage rm/)
})

it.each(['pre-replaced', 'post-unreadable', 'post-retargeted'] as const)(
  'retains/reports a tag for %s state and never deletes a digest',
  async (fault) => {
    const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture()
    owned.execution = undefined
    owned.terminalExecution = undefined
    owned.job = undefined
    owned.source = undefined
    owned.image!.localTags = []
    let tagReads = 0
    const ownedRow = {
      image: IMAGE_PATH,
      tag: owned.image!.tagResourceName,
      version: owned.image!.digestVersionResourceName,
    }
    const retargetedRow = {
      ...ownedRow,
      version:
        `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e` +
        `/packages/freshell-vitest/versions/sha256:${'e'.repeat(64)}`,
    }
    const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (_command, args) => {
      const text = args.join(' ')
      if (text.includes('tags list')) {
        tagReads += 1
        if (fault === 'pre-replaced') return commandOk([retargetedRow])
        if (tagReads === 1) return commandOk([ownedRow])
        if (fault === 'post-unreadable') {
          throw new Error('post-delete read unavailable')
        }
        return commandOk([retargetedRow])
      }
      if (text.includes('tags delete')) return commandOk()
      if (text.includes('versions list')) {
        return commandOk([{ name: owned.image!.digestVersionResourceName }])
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected tag-race command ${text}`)
    })
    const errors = await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
    expect(errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
      kind: 'tag', outcome: 'retained',
    }))
    expect(tagReads).toBe(fault === 'pre-replaced' ? 1 : 2)
    expect(commandTranscript(runner).match(/artifacts docker tags delete/g) ?? [])
      .toHaveLength(fault === 'pre-replaced' ? 0 : 1)
    expect(commandTranscript(runner)).not.toMatch(/versions delete|images delete/)
  },
)

it('uses an independent cleanup signal after the overall signal is aborted', async () => {
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.runId)
  const controller = new AbortController()
  controller.abort(new Error('overall stopped'))
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
  const errors = await cleanupWithIndependentBudget(
    { ...BASE_INPUT, signal: controller.signal }, owned, runner,
  )
  expect(errors).toEqual([])
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toEqual([])
})

it('emits redacted progressive ownership and one schema-valid terminal receipt', () => {
  const lines: string[] = []
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.runId)
  emitOwnershipEvent((line) => lines.push(line), 'intent', owned.receipt)
  emitTerminalReceipt((line) => lines.push(line), owned.receipt)
  const terminals = lines.filter((line) => line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '))
  expect(terminals).toHaveLength(1)
  expect(parseOwnershipReceipt(JSON.parse(
    terminals[0].slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length),
  ))).toMatchObject({ runId: IDENTITY.runId })
  expect(lines.join('\n')).not.toMatch(/authorization|password|short-lived-token/i)
})

it('keeps manual recovery symbols and commands out of production source', async () => {
  const source = await readFile('scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts', 'utf8')
  expect(source).not.toMatch(/\brunRecoveryCli\b/)
  expect(source).not.toMatch(/\brecoveryArgv\b/)
  expect(source).not.toMatch(
    /['"]recover-(?:job|source|tag|local-image|path)['"]/,
  )
})

it('allows Build-risk writes only in the sanctioned transition and ownership helper', async () => {
  const source = await readFile('scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts', 'utf8')
  const testSource = await readFile(
    'test/unit/server/testing/cloud-run-lifecycle.test.ts',
    'utf8',
  )
  const writes = [...source.matchAll(/owned\.buildMayReadSource\s*=/g)]
    .map(({ index }) => index!)
  const terminalWrites = [...source.matchAll(/owned\.buildTerminal\s*=/g)]
    .map(({ index }) => index!)
  const buildWrites = [...source.matchAll(/owned\.build\s*=/g)]
    .map(({ index }) => index!)
  const sanctionedStart = source.indexOf(
    'export function markMutationPossiblyAccepted(',
  )
  const sanctionedEnd = source.indexOf(
    '\nfunction assignOnce',
    sanctionedStart,
  )
  const ownershipStart = source.indexOf(
    'export function acquireBuildOwnership(',
  )
  const ownershipEnd = source.indexOf(
    '\nexport function acquireBuildAndImageOwnership(',
    ownershipStart,
  )
  expect(writes).toHaveLength(2)
  expect(writes.every((index) =>
    (index > sanctionedStart && index < sanctionedEnd) ||
    (index > ownershipStart && index < ownershipEnd),
  )).toBe(true)
  expect(terminalWrites).toHaveLength(1)
  expect(terminalWrites[0]).toBeGreaterThan(ownershipStart)
  expect(terminalWrites[0]).toBeLessThan(ownershipEnd)
  expect(buildWrites).toHaveLength(2)
  expect(buildWrites.every((index) =>
    index > ownershipStart && index < ownershipEnd,
  )).toBe(true)
  expect(testSource).not.toMatch(/\bowned\.build\s*=/)
  expect(testSource).not.toMatch(/\bowned\.buildMayReadSource\s*=/)
  expect(testSource).not.toMatch(/\bowned\.buildTerminal\s*=/)
})

it.each([
  'recover-job',
  'recover-source',
  'recover-tag',
  'recover-local-image',
  'recover-path',
] as const)('rejects removed manual command %s before mutation', async (command) => {
  const stubRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'removed-recovery-'))
  roots.push(stubRoot)
  const toolLog = join(stubRoot, 'tool.log')
  const sentinel = join(stubRoot, 'filesystem-sentinel')
  await writeFile(sentinel, 'must survive\n', { mode: 0o600 })
  for (const tool of ['gcloud', 'docker']) {
    await writeFile(
      join(stubRoot, tool),
      `#!/bin/sh\nprintf '%s\\n' ${tool} >>${toolLog}\nexit 99\n`,
      { mode: 0o700 },
    )
  }
  const argvByCommand: Record<typeof command, readonly string[]> = {
    'recover-job': [
      command,
      '--account=caller@example.com',
      '--project=test-project',
      '--region=us-west1',
      '--name=projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/foreign',
      '--uid=foreign-uid',
      '--generation=7',
    ],
    'recover-source': [
      command,
      '--account=caller@example.com',
      '--project=test-project',
      '--project-number=123456789',
      '--bucket=foreign-bucket',
      '--object=source/foreign.tar.gz',
      '--generation=7',
      '--build-id=foreign-build',
    ],
    'recover-tag': [
      command,
      '--account=caller@example.com',
      '--project=test-project',
      '--region=us-west1',
      '--repository=freshell-e2e',
      '--image=freshell-vitest',
      '--tag=foreign',
      `--digest=sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      '--version=projects/test/versions/foreign',
    ],
    'recover-local-image': [
      command,
      `--docker-config=${stubRoot}`,
      '--tag=foreign:latest',
    ],
    'recover-path': [command, `--path=${stubRoot}`],
  }
  const result = await execFileAsync(
    process.execPath,
    [
      '--import',
      'tsx',
      'scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts',
      ...argvByCommand[command],
    ],
    {
      cwd: process.cwd(),
      env: { ...process.env, PATH: `${stubRoot}:${process.env.PATH}` },
    },
  ).then(
    ({ stdout, stderr }) => ({ status: 0, stdout, stderr }),
    (error: { code?: number; stdout?: string; stderr?: string }) => ({
      status: error.code ?? 1,
      stdout: error.stdout ?? '',
      stderr: error.stderr ?? '',
    }),
  )
  expect(result.status).toBe(2)
  expect(result.stderr).toContain('manual lifecycle recovery commands are not supported')
  await expect(readFile(toolLog, 'utf8')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' })
  await expect(readFile(sentinel, 'utf8')).resolves.toBe('must survive\n')
})

it('keeps residuals diagnostic and structurally non-executable', async () => {
  const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture()
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockRejectedValue(new Error('provider unavailable'))
  await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
  expect(owned.receipt.residuals.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  for (const residual of owned.receipt.residuals) {
    expect(Object.keys(residual).sort()).toEqual(['identity', 'kind', 'reason'])
    expect(JSON.stringify(residual)).not.toMatch(/recover-|(?:delete|cancel|rm)\b/)
  }
})
```

Add the active-Build cleanup regressions to the same lifecycle test file before
the sandbox cases. They use the existing one-shot `sequenceRunner`, real
ownership helpers, and complete provider-shaped Build rows; no test assigns
Build identity or derived risk booleans directly:

```ts
async function activeCleanupFixture(acquire = false) {
  const owned = await cleanupOwnedFixture({
    build: false,
    image: false,
    job: false,
    execution: false,
  })
  const template = await readStrictBuildTemplate(owned.snapshot!.buildTemplatePath)
  registerMutationIntent(owned, createBuildMutationIntent(
    BASE_INPUT,
    BUILD_AUTHORITY,
    owned.snapshot!,
    IDENTITY,
    SOURCE,
    template,
    TEST_WINDOW,
  ))
  markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'build')
  if (acquire) acquireBuildOwnership(owned, activeBuildFixture())
  return owned
}

function exactBuildReadArgv(id: string): readonly string[] {
  return [
    'builds', 'list', '--region=global',
    `--filter=id="${id}"`,
    '--limit=2',
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ]
}

function exactBuildCancelArgv(id: string): readonly string[] {
  return [
    'builds', 'cancel', id,
    '--region=global',
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ]
}

function exactSourceDeleteArgv(source: SourceIdentity): readonly string[] {
  return [
    'storage', 'rm', `gs://${source.bucket}/${source.object}#${source.generation}`,
    `--if-generation-match=${source.generation}`,
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
  ]
}

function exactGcloudCallIndex(
  runner: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>>,
  expectedArgs: readonly string[],
  after = -1,
): number {
  return runner.mock.calls.findIndex(([command, args], index) =>
    index > after &&
    command === 'gcloud' &&
    JSON.stringify(args) === JSON.stringify(expectedArgs))
}

it.each([
  ['identity replacement', () => {
    const original = activeBuildFixture()
    return [original, {
      ...original,
      id: 'build-foreign',
      resourceName:
        `projects/${BUILD_AUTHORITY.projectNumber}/locations/global/builds/build-foreign`,
    }] as const
  }],
  ['frozen request drift', () => {
    const original = activeBuildFixture()
    const normalizedConfig = structuredClone(
      original.normalizedConfig,
    ) as ReturnType<typeof buildRow>
    normalizedConfig.source.storageSource.generation = '999'
    return [original, { ...original, normalizedConfig }] as const
  }],
  ['terminal to active regression', () => {
    const original = terminalBuildFixture()
    return [original, activeBuildFixture()] as const
  }],
  ['conflicting terminal status', () => {
    const original = terminalBuildFixture()
    return [original, { ...original, status: 'CANCELLED' as const }] as const
  }],
  ['conflicting terminal finish time', () => {
    const original = terminalBuildFixture()
    return [original, { ...original, finishTime: testIso(61_000) }] as const
  }],
] as const)(
  'rejects non-monotonic Build ownership: %s',
  (_name, makeBuilds) => {
    const [original, conflicting] = makeBuilds()
    const owned = createPendingOwnership(BASE_INPUT, IDENTITY.runId)
    acquireBuildOwnership(owned, original)
    const receiptBefore = structuredClone(owned.receipt.build)
    const riskBefore = {
      buildTerminal: owned.buildTerminal,
      buildMayReadSource: owned.buildMayReadSource,
    }
    expect(() => acquireBuildOwnership(owned, conflicting)).toThrow()
    expect(owned.build).toBe(original)
    expect(owned.receipt.build).toEqual(receiptBefore)
    expect(owned).toMatchObject(riskBefore)
  },
)

function cancelledBuildRow() {
  const row = buildRow()
  row.status = 'CANCELLED'
  row.finishTime = testIso(61_000)
  return row
}

function sourceRows() {
  return [{
    bucket: SOURCE.bucket,
    name: SOURCE.object,
    generation: SOURCE.generation,
    size: SOURCE.size,
    custom_fields: {
      'freshell-run-id': IDENTITY.runId,
      'freshell-head': SOURCE.commit,
    },
  }]
}

it('rejects immutable Build drift before cancel and performs no Storage operation', async () => {
  const owned = await activeCleanupFixture(true)
  const original = owned.build
  const { finishTime: _finishTime, ...terminalFields } = buildRow()
  const drifted = structuredClone({
    ...terminalFields,
    status: 'WORKING' as const,
  })
  drifted.source.storageSource.generation = '999'
  let callCount = 0
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args) => {
    callCount += 1
    if (callCount === 1) {
      expect(command).toBe('gcloud')
      expect(args).toEqual(exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'))
      return commandOk([drifted])
    }
    throw new Error(
      `forbidden command after immutable drift: ${command} ${args.join(' ')}`,
    )
  })
  const boundedInput = {
    ...BASE_INPUT,
    budgets: {
      ...BASE_INPUT.budgets,
      buildMs: 50,
      commandMs: 50,
      cleanupMs: 250,
    },
  }
  const started = Date.now()
  let guard: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  let failures: unknown[] = []
  try {
    failures = await Promise.race([
      cleanupOwnedResources(boundedInput, owned, runner),
      new Promise<never>((_resolve, reject) => {
        guard = setTimeout(() => reject(
          new Error('drift-before-cancel cleanup exceeded 1000ms'),
        ), 1_000)
      }),
    ])
  } finally {
    if (guard) clearTimeout(guard)
  }
  expect(Date.now() - started).toBeLessThan(1_000)
  const failureText = failures.map(String).join('\n')
  expect(failureText).toContain(
    'Build accepted config differs from frozen request',
  )
  expect(failureText).not.toMatch(
    /forbidden command after immutable drift|drift-before-cancel cleanup exceeded/,
  )
  expect(owned.build).toBe(original)
  expect(owned).toMatchObject({
    buildTerminal: false,
    buildMayReadSource: true,
    receipt: {
      build: { id: 'build-1', status: 'WORKING', state: 'active' },
      cleanup: expect.arrayContaining([
        expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'build', outcome: 'failed' }),
        expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'source', outcome: 'retained' }),
      ]),
    },
  })
  expect(callCount).toBe(1)
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  expect(runner.mock.calls[0][1]).toEqual(exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'))
  expect(commandTranscript(runner)).not.toMatch(
    /builds cancel|storage objects list|storage rm/,
  )
})

it('acquires a late-correlated active Build and cancels it without a reconciliation wait', async () => {
  const owned = await activeCleanupFixture()
  const { finishTime: _finishTime, ...terminalFields } = buildRow()
  const active = { ...terminalFields, status: 'WORKING' as const }
  const runner = sequenceRunner([
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'builds', 'list', IDENTITY.buildCorrelationTag,
    ], result: commandOk([active]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'builds', 'list', 'build-1',
    ], exactArgs: exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'), result: commandOk([active]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'builds', 'cancel', 'build-1',
    ], exactArgs: exactBuildCancelArgv('build-1'), result: commandOk({}) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'builds', 'list', 'build-1',
    ], exactArgs: exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'),
      result: commandOk([cancelledBuildRow()]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list',
    ], result: commandOk([]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'storage', 'objects', 'list',
    ], result: commandOk(sourceRows()) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'storage', 'rm', `--if-generation-match=${SOURCE.generation}`,
    ], exactArgs: exactSourceDeleteArgv(SOURCE), result: commandOk() },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: [
      'storage', 'objects', 'list',
    ], result: commandOk([]) },
  ])
  await expect(cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)).resolves.toEqual([])
  const typedRunner = runner as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>>
  const cancelIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactBuildCancelArgv('build-1'),
  )
  const terminalReadIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'), cancelIndex,
  )
  const sourceDeleteIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactSourceDeleteArgv(SOURCE), terminalReadIndex,
  )
  expect(cancelIndex).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
  expect(terminalReadIndex).toBeGreaterThan(cancelIndex)
  expect(sourceDeleteIndex).toBeGreaterThan(terminalReadIndex)
  expect(owned.receipt.build).toMatchObject({
    id: 'build-1', status: 'CANCELLED', state: 'terminal',
  })
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'source', outcome: 'deleted',
  }))
})

it('preserves a cancel failure when the exact Build nevertheless settles before source deletion', async () => {
  const owned = await activeCleanupFixture(true)
  const { finishTime: _finishTime, ...terminalFields } = buildRow()
  const active = { ...terminalFields, status: 'WORKING' as const }
  const runner = sequenceRunner([
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'list', 'build-1'],
      exactArgs: exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'), result: commandOk([active]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'cancel', 'build-1'],
      exactArgs: exactBuildCancelArgv('build-1'),
      error: new Error('cancel response lost') },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'list', 'build-1'],
      exactArgs: exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'),
      result: commandOk([cancelledBuildRow()]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list'],
      result: commandOk([]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['storage', 'objects', 'list'],
      result: commandOk(sourceRows()) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['storage', 'rm'],
      exactArgs: exactSourceDeleteArgv(SOURCE), result: commandOk() },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['storage', 'objects', 'list'],
      result: commandOk([]) },
  ])
  const failures = await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
  expect(failures.map(String).join('\n')).toContain('cancel response lost')
  const typedRunner = runner as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>>
  const cancelIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactBuildCancelArgv('build-1'),
  )
  const terminalReadIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'), cancelIndex,
  )
  const sourceDeleteIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactSourceDeleteArgv(SOURCE), terminalReadIndex,
  )
  expect(cancelIndex).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
  expect(terminalReadIndex).toBeGreaterThan(cancelIndex)
  expect(sourceDeleteIndex).toBeGreaterThan(terminalReadIndex)
  expect(owned).toMatchObject({
    buildTerminal: true,
    buildMayReadSource: false,
    receipt: {
      build: { status: 'CANCELLED', state: 'terminal' },
      cleanup: expect.arrayContaining([
        expect.objectContaining({
          kind: 'build', outcome: 'terminal',
          error: expect.stringContaining('cancel response lost'),
        }),
        expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'source', outcome: 'deleted' }),
      ]),
    },
  })
})

it('retains source and preserves cancel plus settlement failures when active Build terminality is unknown', async () => {
  const owned = await activeCleanupFixture(true)
  const { finishTime: _finishTime, ...terminalFields } = buildRow()
  const active = { ...terminalFields, status: 'WORKING' as const }
  const runner = sequenceRunner([
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'list', 'build-1'],
      exactArgs: exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'), result: commandOk([active]) },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'cancel', 'build-1'],
      exactArgs: exactBuildCancelArgv('build-1'),
      error: new Error('cancel failed') },
    { command: 'gcloud', includes: ['builds', 'list', 'build-1'],
      exactArgs: exactBuildReadArgv('build-1'),
      error: new Error('Build settlement unavailable') },
  ])
  const failures = await cleanupOwnedResources(BASE_INPUT, owned, runner)
  const aggregate = failures.find((error) => error instanceof AggregateError)
  expect(aggregate).toBeInstanceOf(AggregateError)
  expect((aggregate as AggregateError).errors.map((error) =>
    error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error))).toEqual([
    'cancel failed',
    'Build settlement unavailable',
  ])
  const typedRunner = runner as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>>
  const cancelIndex = exactGcloudCallIndex(
    typedRunner, exactBuildCancelArgv('build-1'),
  )
  expect(cancelIndex).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
  expect(typedRunner.mock.calls.slice(cancelIndex + 1).some(([, args]) =>
    args[0] === 'storage' &&
    (args[1] === 'rm' || (args[1] === 'objects' && args[2] === 'list')),
  )).toBe(false)
  expect(owned).toMatchObject({
    buildTerminal: false,
    buildMayReadSource: true,
    receipt: {
      cleanup: expect.arrayContaining([
        expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'build', outcome: 'failed' }),
        expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'source', outcome: 'retained' }),
      ]),
      residuals: expect.arrayContaining([expect.objectContaining({
        kind: 'source',
        reason: 'Source generation retained because Build may still read it',
      })]),
    },
  })
})
```

Append these self-contained cases inside the existing Docker-gated
`final owned POSIX process groups` describe in
`test/sandbox/cloud-run-process-groups.test.ts`. They use only Task 1's local
`fixture`, `expectGone`, and `defaultRunCommand`; every Task 6 dependency is
loaded explicitly. The first proves the honest abrupt-loss boundary. The second
forces a cleanup-owned command and its descendant to ignore TERM, so the cleanup
deadline can settle only after Task 1's process-group KILL escalation:

```ts
const SANDBOX_RUN_ID = '1'.repeat(32)
const sandboxLifecycleInput = {
  origin: { surface: 'vitest-build', argv: [] },
  kind: 'vitest',
  repoRoot: process.cwd(),
  account: 'caller@example.com',
  projectId: 'test-project',
  iamAnalysisScope: 'organizations/123456789',
  buildRegion: 'global',
  runRegion: 'us-west1',
  sourceBucket: 'test-source-bucket',
  repository: 'freshell-e2e',
  imageName: 'freshell-vitest',
  jobPrefix: 'freshell-vitest',
  buildServiceAccount: 'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  runtimeServiceAccount: 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  buildMode: 'cloud',
  operation: 'build',
  taskCount: 1,
  taskTimeout: '60s',
  memory: '1Gi',
  cpu: 1,
  workloadEnv: {},
  expectedParts: [],
  budgets: {
    overallMs: 10_000,
    provenanceMs: 1_000,
    sourceMs: 1_000,
    buildMs: 5_000,
    imageMs: 1_000,
    jobMs: 1_000,
    executionMs: 1_000,
    resultReadMs: 1_000,
    cleanupMs: 300,
    commandMs: 5_000,
    terminateGraceMs: 75,
    settlementMs: 1_000,
  },
} as const

async function task6LifecycleModule() {
  return await import('../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js') as {
    createPendingOwnership: (input: typeof sandboxLifecycleInput, runId: string) => {
      build?: import('../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js').CorrelatedBuildIdentity
      buildTerminal: boolean
      buildMayReadSource: boolean
      receipt: {
        build: Record<string, unknown>
        cleanup: { kind: string; outcome: string }[]
        residuals: { kind: string; identity: string; reason: string }[]
      }
    }
    acquireBuildOwnership: (
      owned: Awaited<ReturnType<
        Awaited<ReturnType<typeof task6LifecycleModule>>['createPendingOwnership']
      >>,
      build: import('../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js').CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
    ) => void
    cleanupWithIndependentBudget: (
      input: typeof sandboxLifecycleInput,
      owned: Awaited<ReturnType<
        Awaited<ReturnType<typeof task6LifecycleModule>>['createPendingOwnership']
      >>,
      runCommand: TestRunCommand,
    ) => Promise<unknown[]>
    emitOwnershipEvent: (
      write: (line: string) => unknown,
      phase: 'intent' | 'possibly-accepted' | 'acquired' | 'reconciled' | 'residual',
      value: unknown,
    ) => void
  }
}

it('prints acquired ownership before SIGKILL but no impossible terminal receipt', async () => {
  const source = `
    import { writeSync } from 'node:fs'
    import { createPendingOwnership, emitOwnershipEvent } from './scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts'
    const input = ${JSON.stringify(sandboxLifecycleInput)}
    const owned = createPendingOwnership(input, '${SANDBOX_RUN_ID}')
    emitOwnershipEvent((line) => {
      writeSync(2, line)
      return true
    }, 'acquired', owned.receipt)
    process.kill(process.pid, 'SIGKILL')
  `
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['--import', 'tsx', '--eval', source], {
    cwd: process.cwd(),
    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
  })
  let stderr = ''
  child.stderr!.setEncoding('utf8').on('data', (chunk) => { stderr += chunk })
  const termination = await new Promise<{
    code: number | null
    signal: NodeJS.Signals | null
  }>((resolve) => child.once('close', (code, signal) => resolve({ code, signal })))
  expect(termination).toEqual({ code: null, signal: 'SIGKILL' })
  expect(stderr).toContain('FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP ')
  expect(stderr).not.toContain('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ')
})

it('settles the hard cleanup deadline after TERM-resistant owned-group escalation', async () => {
  const { script, pidFile } = await fixture(`
trap '' TERM
( trap '' TERM; while :; do sleep 1; done ) &
child=$!
echo "$child" >"${pidFile}"
wait`)
  const lifecycle = await task6LifecycleModule()
  const owned = lifecycle.createPendingOwnership(
    sandboxLifecycleInput,
    SANDBOX_RUN_ID,
  )
  lifecycle.acquireBuildOwnership(owned, {
    correlationTag: `freshell-${SANDBOX_RUN_ID}`,
    id: `build-${SANDBOX_RUN_ID}`,
    resourceName:
      `projects/123456789/locations/global/builds/build-${SANDBOX_RUN_ID}`,
    projectId: sandboxLifecycleInput.projectId,
    projectNumber: '123456789',
    createTime: new Date().toISOString(),
    startTime: new Date().toISOString(),
    serviceAccount: sandboxLifecycleInput.buildServiceAccount,
    status: 'WORKING',
    normalizedConfig: {},
  })
  const blockingOwnedCommand: TestRunCommand = async (_command, _args, options) =>
    defaultRunCommand(script, [], options)
  const started = Date.now()
  const failures = await lifecycle.cleanupWithIndependentBudget(
    sandboxLifecycleInput,
    owned,
    blockingOwnedCommand,
  )
  expect(Date.now() - started).toBeLessThan(2_000)
  expect(failures.map(String).join('\n')).toContain('Cleanup exceeded 300ms')
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'build',
    outcome: 'failed',
  }))
  expect(owned.receipt.residuals).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'build',
    identity: expect.any(String),
    reason: expect.any(String),
  }))
  expectGone(Number((await readFile(pidFile, 'utf8')).trim()))
})
```
