Replace `lifecycle.ts` imports with the cumulative Task 6 block (Task 5 adds no
imports). Then add the complete ownership, automatic cleanup, receipt, and residual bodies
below before Task 7 consumes them:

```ts
import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from 'node:child_process'
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
import {
  mkdir,
  mkdtemp,
  readFile,
  rm,
  stat,
  writeFile,
} from 'node:fs/promises'
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'
import { parse as parseYaml } from 'yaml'
import { z } from 'zod'
```

```ts
export type OwnedResources = {
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt
  identity?: InvocationIdentity
  snapshot?: SourceSnapshot
  sourceIntent?: SourceMutationIntent
  buildIntent?: BuildMutationIntent
  localPushIntent?: LocalPushMutationIntent
  jobIntent?: JobMutationIntent
  source?: SourceIdentity
  build?: CorrelatedBuildIdentity
  image?: ImageIdentity
  job?: VerifiedRunJob
  execution?: ExecutionIdentity
  terminalExecution?: CanonicalExecution
  buildTerminal: boolean
  buildMayReadSource: boolean
}

export type MutationIntentState =
  | 'intent'
  | 'possibly-accepted'
  | 'acquired'
  | 'unresolved'

export type SourceMutationIntent = {
  kind: 'source'
  state: MutationIntentState
  bucket: string
  object: string
  expectedSize: string
  expectedMetadata: {
    runId: string
    head: string
  }
}

export type BuildMutationIntent = {
  kind: 'build'
  state: MutationIntentState
  correlationTag: string
  runId: string
  authority: BuildAuthority
  source: SourceIdentity
  taggedImage: string
  serviceAccount: string
  expectedRequest: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>
  window: CorrelationWindow
}

export type LocalPushMutationIntent = {
  kind: 'local-push'
  state: MutationIntentState
  correlationTag: string
  authority: BuildAuthority
  runId: string
  head: string
  localTags: readonly [string, string]
  remoteTag: string
}

export type JobMutationIntent = {
  kind: 'job'
  state: MutationIntentState
  jobName: string
  runId: string
  immutableImage: string
  serviceAccount: string
  taskCount: number
  // Canonical positive whole-second protobuf duration.
  taskTimeout: string
  memory: string
  cpu: number
  expectedEnv: Readonly<Record<string, string>>
  window: CorrelationWindow
}

export type MutationIntent =
  | SourceMutationIntent
  | BuildMutationIntent
  | LocalPushMutationIntent
  | JobMutationIntent

type CleanupSuccess = {
  outcome: 'deleted' | 'absent' | 'terminal' | 'retained'
  precondition?: string
  preservedFailures?: readonly unknown[]
  residualReason?: string
}

const cleanupDispositionSchema = z.object({
  kind: z.enum([
    'execution', 'job', 'build', 'source', 'tag', 'local-image',
    'docker-config', 'temp-root',
  ]),
  identity: z.string(),
  outcome: z.enum(['deleted', 'absent', 'terminal', 'retained', 'failed']),
  precondition: z.string().optional(),
  error: z.string().optional(),
}).strict()
const residualResourceSchema = z.object({
  kind: cleanupDispositionSchema.shape.kind,
  identity: z.string(),
  reason: z.string(),
}).strict()

const ownershipReceiptSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  status: z.enum(['succeeded', 'failed']),
  origin: z.object({ surface: z.string(), argv: z.array(z.string()) }).strict(),
  kind: z.enum(['vitest', 'e2e']),
  operation: z.enum(['run', 'build']),
  buildMode: z.enum(['cloud', 'local']),
  runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
  branch: z.string().optional(),
  upstreamRemote: z.string().optional(),
  upstreamMergeRef: z.string().optional(),
  head: z.string().regex(FULL_OBJECT_ID).optional(),
  account: z.string(),
  projectId: z.string(),
  iamAnalysisScope: z.string().regex(/^organizations\/[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  projectNumber: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+$/).optional(),
  buildRegion: z.literal('global'),
  runRegion: z.string(),
  source: z.object({ state: z.string() }).passthrough(),
  build: z.object({ correlationTag: z.string(), state: z.string() }).passthrough(),
  image: z.object({ state: z.string() }).passthrough(),
  job: z.object({
    name: z.string(), resourceName: z.string().optional(), uid: z.string().optional(),
    etag: z.string().optional(),
    generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/).optional(),
    serviceAccount: z.string(), state: z.string(),
  }).strict(),
  execution: z.object({
    operationName: z.string().optional(), resourceName: z.string().optional(),
    name: z.string().optional(), uid: z.string().optional(),
    serviceAccount: z.string().optional(),
    counters: z.object({
      succeededCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
      failedCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
      cancelledCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
      runningCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
    }).strict().optional(),
    omittedZeroCounters: z.array(z.enum(counterKeys)).optional(),
    startTime: z.string().optional(), completionTime: z.string().optional(),
    state: z.string(),
  }).strict(),
  temp: z.object({
    root: z.string().optional(), sourceDir: z.string().optional(),
    dockerConfig: z.string().optional(), rootRemoved: z.boolean(),
    dockerConfigRemoved: z.boolean(),
  }).strict(),
  parts: z.array(cloudPartResultSchema),
  cleanup: z.array(cleanupDispositionSchema),
  residuals: z.array(residualResourceSchema),
  failure: z.object({
    primary: z.string().optional(),
    causes: z.array(z.string()),
    cleanup: z.array(z.string()),
  }).strict().optional(),
}).strict()

export function parseOwnershipReceipt(value: unknown): OwnershipReceipt {
  return ownershipReceiptSchema.parse(value) as OwnershipReceipt
}

function redactOwnership(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(redactOwnership)
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
      .filter(([key]) => !/(?:authorization|password|token|stdin)/i.test(key))
      .map(([key, child]) => [key, redactOwnership(child)]))
  }
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.length > 8192
    ? `${value.slice(0, 8192)}…`
    : value
}

type LineWriter = (line: string) => unknown

export function emitOwnershipEvent(
  write: LineWriter,
  phase: 'intent' | 'possibly-accepted' | 'acquired' | 'reconciled' | 'residual',
  value: unknown,
): void {
  write(`FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP ${JSON.stringify({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    phase,
    value: redactOwnership(value),
  })}\n`)
}

export function emitTerminalReceipt(write: LineWriter, receipt: OwnershipReceipt): void {
  const parsed = parseOwnershipReceipt(receipt)
  write(`FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(parsed)}\n`)
}

export class CloudRunLifecycleError extends AggregateError {
  readonly primary: unknown | undefined
  readonly cleanupErrors: readonly unknown[]
  readonly receipt: OwnershipReceipt

  constructor(
    primary: unknown | undefined,
    cleanupErrors: readonly unknown[],
    receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  ) {
    super(
      [primary, ...cleanupErrors].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
      'Cloud Run lifecycle failed',
    )
    this.name = 'CloudRunLifecycleError'
    this.primary = primary
    this.cleanupErrors = [...cleanupErrors]
    this.receipt = receipt
  }
}

export function createPendingOwnership(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runId: string,
): OwnedResources {
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(runId)) throw new Error('Pending run ID must be 128-bit hex')
  return {
    buildTerminal: false,
    buildMayReadSource: false,
    receipt: {
      schemaVersion: 1,
      status: 'failed',
      origin: input.origin,
      kind: input.kind,
      operation: input.operation,
      buildMode: input.buildMode,
      runId,
      account: input.account,
      projectId: input.projectId,
      iamAnalysisScope: input.iamAnalysisScope,
      buildRegion: 'global',
      runRegion: input.runRegion,
      source: { state: 'pending' },
      build: { correlationTag: `freshell-${runId}`, state: 'pending' },
      image: { state: 'pending' },
      job: {
        name: '', serviceAccount: input.runtimeServiceAccount, state: 'pending',
      },
      execution: { state: 'pending' },
      temp: { rootRemoved: false, dockerConfigRemoved: false },
      parts: [],
      cleanup: [],
      residuals: [],
    },
  }
}

function mutationIntentEventValue(intent: MutationIntent): unknown {
  switch (intent.kind) {
    case 'source':
      return intent
    case 'build':
      return {
        kind: intent.kind,
        state: intent.state,
        correlationTag: intent.correlationTag,
        runId: intent.runId,
        source: intent.source,
        taggedImage: intent.taggedImage,
        serviceAccount: intent.serviceAccount,
        window: intent.window,
      }
    case 'local-push':
      return {
        kind: intent.kind,
        state: intent.state,
        correlationTag: intent.correlationTag,
        authority: intent.authority,
        runId: intent.runId,
        head: intent.head,
        localTags: intent.localTags,
        remoteTag: intent.remoteTag,
      }
    case 'job':
      return {
        kind: intent.kind,
        state: intent.state,
        jobName: intent.jobName,
        runId: intent.runId,
        immutableImage: intent.immutableImage,
        serviceAccount: intent.serviceAccount,
        taskCount: intent.taskCount,
        taskTimeout: intent.taskTimeout,
        memory: intent.memory,
        cpu: intent.cpu,
        expectedEnvNames: Object.keys(intent.expectedEnv).sort(),
        window: intent.window,
      }
  }
}

export async function createSourceMutationIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
): Promise<SourceMutationIntent> {
  const details = await stat(snapshot.sourceArchive)
  if (!details.isFile() || details.size < 1) {
    throw new Error('Source intent requires one non-empty immutable archive')
  }
  return {
    kind: 'source',
    state: 'intent',
    bucket: input.sourceBucket,
    object: identity.sourceObject,
    expectedSize: String(details.size),
    expectedMetadata: { runId: identity.runId, head: snapshot.head },
  }
}

export function createBuildMutationIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  source: SourceIdentity,
  template: Readonly<BuildTemplate>,
  window: CorrelationWindow,
): BuildMutationIntent {
  return {
    kind: 'build',
    state: 'intent',
    correlationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    runId: identity.runId,
    authority,
    source,
    taggedImage: `${imagePath(input)}:${identity.imageTag}`,
    serviceAccount: input.buildServiceAccount,
    expectedRequest: generateBuildRequest(input, snapshot, identity, source, template),
    window,
  }
}

export function createLocalPushMutationIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  authority: BuildAuthority,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
): LocalPushMutationIntent {
  const remoteTag = `${imagePath(input)}:${identity.imageTag}`
  const localTag = `freshell-${input.kind}:${identity.runId}`
  return {
    kind: 'local-push',
    state: 'intent',
    correlationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    authority,
    runId: identity.runId,
    head: snapshot.head,
    localTags: [localTag, remoteTag],
    remoteTag,
  }
}

export function createJobMutationIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
  immutableImage: string,
  expectedEnv: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
  window: CorrelationWindow,
): JobMutationIntent {
  return {
    kind: 'job',
    state: 'intent',
    jobName: identity.jobName,
    runId: identity.runId,
    immutableImage,
    serviceAccount: input.runtimeServiceAccount,
    taskCount: input.taskCount,
    taskTimeout: input.taskTimeout,
    memory: input.memory,
    cpu: input.cpu,
    expectedEnv: { ...expectedEnv },
    window,
  }
}

export function registerMutationIntent(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  intent: MutationIntent,
): void {
  switch (intent.kind) {
    case 'source':
      owned.sourceIntent = assignOnce(owned.sourceIntent, intent, 'source intent')
      owned.receipt.source = {
        state: 'intent',
        bucket: intent.bucket,
        object: intent.object,
        size: intent.expectedSize,
        commit: intent.expectedMetadata.head,
      }
      break
    case 'build':
      owned.buildIntent = assignOnce(owned.buildIntent, intent, 'Build intent')
      owned.receipt.build = {
        correlationTag: intent.correlationTag,
        projectId: intent.authority.projectId,
        projectNumber: intent.authority.projectNumber,
        expectedTag: intent.taggedImage,
        source: intent.source,
        serviceAccount: intent.serviceAccount,
        state: 'intent',
      }
      owned.receipt.image = {
        tag: intent.taggedImage.split(':').at(-1),
        taggedImage: intent.taggedImage,
        state: 'intent',
      }
      break
    case 'local-push':
      owned.localPushIntent = assignOnce(
        owned.localPushIntent,
        intent,
        'local push intent',
      )
      owned.receipt.build = {
        correlationTag: intent.correlationTag,
        projectId: intent.authority.projectId,
        projectNumber: intent.authority.projectNumber,
        source: { localExtractedCommit: intent.head },
        state: 'intent',
      }
      owned.receipt.image = {
        taggedImage: intent.remoteTag,
        tag: intent.remoteTag.split(':').at(-1),
        localTags: [...intent.localTags],
        state: 'intent',
      }
      break
    case 'job':
      owned.jobIntent = assignOnce(owned.jobIntent, intent, 'Job intent')
      owned.receipt.job = {
        name: intent.jobName,
        serviceAccount: intent.serviceAccount,
        state: 'intent',
      }
      break
  }
  emitOwnershipEvent(
    process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr),
    'intent',
    mutationIntentEventValue(intent),
  )
}

export function markMutationPossiblyAccepted(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  kind: MutationIntent['kind'],
): void {
  const intent = kind === 'source'
    ? owned.sourceIntent
    : kind === 'build'
      ? owned.buildIntent
      : kind === 'local-push'
        ? owned.localPushIntent
        : owned.jobIntent
  if (!intent || intent.state !== 'intent') {
    throw new Error(`${kind} intent must be registered exactly once before mutation`)
  }
  if (kind === 'build') {
    if (owned.build) {
      throw new Error('Build mutation risk can begin only before ownership acquisition')
    }
    // This is the sole pre-identity Build-risk transition. Every caller invokes
    // it immediately before the one possibly accepted cloud Build mutation.
    owned.buildMayReadSource = true
  }
  intent.state = 'possibly-accepted'
  if (kind === 'source') owned.receipt.source.state = 'possibly-accepted'
  if (kind === 'build') {
    owned.receipt.build.state = 'possibly-accepted'
    owned.receipt.image.state = 'possibly-accepted'
  }
  if (kind === 'local-push') {
    owned.receipt.build.state = 'possibly-accepted'
    owned.receipt.image.state = 'possibly-accepted'
  }
  if (kind === 'job') owned.receipt.job.state = 'possibly-accepted'
  emitOwnershipEvent(
    process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr),
    'possibly-accepted',
    mutationIntentEventValue(intent),
  )
}

function assignOnce<T>(current: T | undefined, next: T, label: string): T {
  if (current !== undefined && JSON.stringify(current) !== JSON.stringify(next)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} ownership cannot change after acquisition`)
  }
  return current ?? next
}

export function acquireSnapshotOwnership(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  identity: InvocationIdentity,
): void {
  owned.snapshot = assignOnce(owned.snapshot, snapshot, 'snapshot')
  owned.identity = assignOnce(owned.identity, identity, 'identity')
  Object.assign(owned.receipt, {
    branch: snapshot.branch,
    upstreamRemote: snapshot.upstreamRemote,
    upstreamMergeRef: snapshot.upstreamMergeRef,
    head: snapshot.head,
  })
  owned.receipt.temp = {
    root: snapshot.tempRoot,
    sourceDir: snapshot.sourceDir,
    dockerConfig: snapshot.dockerConfigDir,
    rootRemoved: false,
    dockerConfigRemoved: false,
  }
  owned.receipt.build.correlationTag = identity.buildCorrelationTag
  owned.receipt.job.name = identity.jobName
  emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'acquired', {
    runId: identity.runId,
    tempRoot: snapshot.tempRoot,
    sourceObject: identity.sourceObject,
    imageTag: identity.imageTag,
    buildCorrelationTag: identity.buildCorrelationTag,
    jobName: identity.jobName,
  })
}

export function acquireSourceOwnership(owned: OwnedResources, source: SourceIdentity): void {
  owned.source = assignOnce(owned.source, source, 'source')
  owned.receipt.source = { ...source, state: 'owned' }
  if (owned.sourceIntent) owned.sourceIntent.state = 'acquired'
  emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'acquired', owned.receipt.source)
}

export function acquireBuildOwnership(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  build: CorrelatedBuildIdentity,
): void {
  if (owned.build) {
    if (!sameImmutableBuildOwnership(owned.build, build)) {
      throw new Error('Build ownership identity or frozen request cannot change')
    }
    const currentTerminal = !buildIsActive(owned.build.status)
    const nextTerminal = !buildIsActive(build.status)
    if (currentTerminal && !nextTerminal) {
      throw new Error('Build ownership cannot move from terminal to active')
    }
    if (currentTerminal && nextTerminal &&
        (owned.build.status !== build.status ||
         owned.build.finishTime !== build.finishTime)) {
      throw new Error('Build ownership cannot change terminal state')
    }
  }
  owned.build = owned.build ?? build
  if (owned.build !== build && buildIsActive(owned.build.status) &&
      !buildIsActive(build.status)) {
    owned.build = build
  }
  owned.buildTerminal = !buildIsActive(owned.build.status)
  owned.buildMayReadSource = !owned.buildTerminal
  owned.receipt.build = {
    ...owned.build,
    state: owned.buildTerminal ? 'terminal' : 'active',
  }
  if (owned.buildIntent) owned.buildIntent.state = 'acquired'
  emitOwnershipEvent(
    process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr),
    'acquired',
    owned.receipt.build,
  )
}

export function acquireBuildAndImageOwnership(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  built: BuiltImage,
): void {
  acquireBuildOwnership(owned, built.build)
  owned.image = assignOnce(owned.image, built.image, 'image')
  owned.receipt.image = { ...built.image, state: 'owned' }
  if (owned.localPushIntent) {
    owned.localPushIntent.state = 'acquired'
    emitOwnershipEvent(
      process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr),
      'acquired',
      mutationIntentEventValue(owned.localPushIntent),
    )
  }
  emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'acquired', {
    build: owned.receipt.build,
    image: owned.receipt.image,
  })
}

export function acquireJobOwnership(owned: OwnedResources, job: VerifiedRunJob): void {
  owned.job = assignOnce(owned.job, job, 'job')
  owned.receipt.job = {
    name: job.name.split('/').at(-1)!,
    resourceName: job.name,
    uid: job.uid,
    etag: job.etag,
    generation: job.generation,
    serviceAccount: job.template.template.serviceAccount,
    state: 'owned',
  }
  if (owned.jobIntent) owned.jobIntent.state = 'acquired'
  emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'acquired', owned.receipt.job)
}

export function acquireExecutionOwnership(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  execution: ExecutionIdentity,
): void {
  owned.execution = assignOnce(owned.execution, execution, 'execution')
  owned.receipt.execution = {
    ...execution,
    serviceAccount: owned.receipt.job.serviceAccount,
    state: 'active',
  }
  emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'acquired', owned.receipt.execution)
}

export function acquireTerminalExecution(
  owned: OwnedResources,
  terminal: CanonicalExecution,
): void {
  owned.terminalExecution = assignOnce(owned.terminalExecution, terminal, 'terminal execution')
  owned.receipt.execution = {
    ...owned.receipt.execution,
    counters: terminal.counters,
    omittedZeroCounters: terminal.omittedZeroCounters,
    startTime: terminal.execution.status.startTime,
    completionTime: terminal.execution.status.completionTime,
    state: 'terminal',
  }
  emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'reconciled', owned.receipt.execution)
}

async function attemptCleanup(
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  failures: unknown[],
  kind: CleanupDisposition['kind'],
  identity: string,
  action: () => Promise<CleanupSuccess>,
): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const result = await action()
    const preserved = [...(result.preservedFailures ?? [])]
    failures.push(...preserved)
    const error = preserved.length === 0
      ? undefined
      : preserved.flatMap(errorChain).join('; ')
    receipt.cleanup.push(cleanupDispositionSchema.parse({
      kind,
      identity,
      outcome: result.outcome,
      ...(result.precondition === undefined ? {} : { precondition: result.precondition }),
      ...(error === undefined ? {} : { error }),
    }) as CleanupDisposition)
    if (result.outcome === 'retained' || result.residualReason !== undefined) {
      const reason = result.residualReason ?? `${kind} intentionally retained`
      const residual = residualResourceSchema.parse({
        kind, identity, reason,
      }) as ResidualResource
      receipt.residuals.push(residual)
      if (preserved.length === 0) failures.push(new Error(reason))
      emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'residual', residual)
    }
  } catch (error) {
    failures.push(error)
    const message = errorChain(error).join('; ')
    receipt.cleanup.push({ kind, identity, outcome: 'failed', error: message })
    const residual = { kind, identity, reason: message }
    receipt.residuals.push(residual)
    emitOwnershipEvent(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), 'residual', residual)
  }
}

async function cleanupExecution(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  if (!owned.execution) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  if (owned.terminalExecution) return { outcome: 'terminal' }
  if (!owned.receipt.projectNumber) throw new Error('Cannot reconcile Execution without project number')
  let cancelFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
      'run', 'jobs', 'executions', 'cancel', owned.execution.name,
      '--quiet',
      `--region=${input.runRegion}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`, '--format=json',
    ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.commandMs))
  } catch (error) {
    cancelFailure = error
  }
  const terminal = await waitForExecutionTerminal(
    input, owned.execution, owned.receipt.projectNumber, runCommand,
  )
  acquireTerminalExecution(owned, terminal)
  return {
    outcome: 'terminal',
    preservedFailures: cancelFailure === undefined ? [] : [cancelFailure],
  }
}

async function cleanupJob(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  if (!owned.job) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  try {
    const deleted = await deleteExactRunJob(input, owned.job, runCommand)
    return {
      outcome: deleted.outcome,
      preservedFailures: deleted.preservedFailures,
    }
  } catch (deleteFailure) {
    const fresh = await getExactRunJob(
      input, owned.job.name.split('/').at(-1)!, runCommand,
    )
    if (!fresh) {
      return { outcome: 'absent', preservedFailures: [deleteFailure] }
    }
    throw new AggregateError([deleteFailure], 'Job delete failed and Job remains')
  }
}

async function cleanupBuild(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  if (!owned.build) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  const ownedBuild = owned.build
  if (input.buildMode === 'local') {
    if (!owned.buildTerminal || !ownedBuild.id.startsWith('local-')) {
      throw new Error('Local build receipt is not exact terminal local ownership')
    }
    return { outcome: 'terminal' }
  }
  const authority = {
    projectId: ownedBuild.projectId,
    projectNumber: ownedBuild.projectNumber,
  }
  const current = await readExactBuild(
    input, authority, ownedBuild.id, runCommand,
  )
  if (!owned.identity) {
    throw new Error('Owned Build cannot be revalidated without invocation identity')
  }
  const invocationIdentity = owned.identity
  const expectedRequest = acceptedBuildRequest(
    buildRowSchema.parse(ownedBuild.normalizedConfig),
  )
  const currentIdentity = correlatedBuildIdentity(
    current,
    input,
    authority,
    invocationIdentity,
    expectedRequest,
  )
  if (!sameImmutableBuildOwnership(ownedBuild, currentIdentity)) {
    throw new Error('Exact Build read drifted from immutable owned request')
  }
  let cancelFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  if (buildIsActive(currentIdentity.status)) {
    try {
      await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
        'builds', 'cancel', ownedBuild.id,
        '--region=global', `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
        '--format=json',
      ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.commandMs))
    } catch (error) {
      cancelFailure = error
    }
  }
  let terminalIdentity = currentIdentity
  try {
    if (buildIsActive(currentIdentity.status)) {
      const terminal = await waitForBuildTerminal(
        input, authority, ownedBuild.id, runCommand,
      )
      terminalIdentity = correlatedBuildIdentity(
        terminal,
        input,
        authority,
        invocationIdentity,
        expectedRequest,
      )
      if (!sameImmutableBuildOwnership(ownedBuild, terminalIdentity)) {
        throw new Error('Terminal Build read drifted from immutable owned request')
      }
    }
  } catch (settlementFailure) {
    throw new AggregateError(
      [cancelFailure, settlementFailure].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
      'Build cancel/settlement did not establish terminality',
    )
  }
  if (ownedBuild.status !== terminalIdentity.status ||
      ownedBuild.finishTime !== terminalIdentity.finishTime) {
    acquireBuildOwnership(owned, terminalIdentity)
  }
  const productFailures: unknown[] = []
  if (!owned.image || terminalIdentity.status !== 'SUCCESS') {
    await reconcileTerminalBuildProduct(input, owned, productFailures, runCommand)
  }
  return {
    outcome: 'terminal',
    preservedFailures: [
      ...(cancelFailure === undefined ? [] : [cancelFailure]),
      ...productFailures,
    ],
  }
}

async function listSourceGenerations(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  source: SourceIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<readonly z.infer<typeof storageObjectSchema>[]> {
  return listExactSourceObjects(
    input, source.bucket, source.object, runCommand,
  )
}

async function cleanupSource(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  const source = owned.source
  if (!source) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  if (input.buildMode === 'cloud' && owned.buildMayReadSource && !owned.buildTerminal) return {
    outcome: 'retained',
    precondition: owned.build
      ? `Build ${owned.build.id} terminality not established`
      : `Build correlation ${owned.identity?.buildCorrelationTag ?? 'unknown'} unresolved`,
    residualReason: 'Source generation retained because Build may still read it',
  }
  const before = await listSourceGenerations(input, source, runCommand)
  const matches = before.filter((row) => row.bucket === source.bucket &&
    row.name === source.object && row.generation === source.generation)
  if (matches.length === 0) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  if (matches.length !== 1) throw new Error('Source generation ownership is ambiguous')
  let deletionFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
      'storage', 'rm', sourceGenerationUri(source),
      `--if-generation-match=${source.generation}`,
      `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.commandMs))
  } catch (error) {
    deletionFailure = error
  }
  const after = await listSourceGenerations(input, source, runCommand)
  if (after.some((row) => row.bucket === source.bucket && row.name === source.object &&
      row.generation === source.generation)) {
    throw new AggregateError(
      deletionFailure === undefined ? [] : [deletionFailure],
      'Exact source generation remains after delete',
    )
  }
  return {
    outcome: 'deleted',
    precondition: `generation=${source.generation}`,
    preservedFailures: deletionFailure === undefined ? [] : [deletionFailure],
  }
}

export async function listExactTag(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  image: ImageIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<readonly z.infer<typeof artifactTagRowSchema>[]> {
  requireCoherentImageIdentity(input, image)
  const rows = await gcloudJson(z.array(artifactTagRowSchema).max(2), [
    'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list', imagePath(input),
    exactTagFilter(input, image.tag), '--limit=2',
    `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    '--format=json(image,tag,version)',
  ], input, runCommand)
  if (rows.length === 1 &&
      (rows[0].image !== imagePath(input) ||
       rows[0].tag !== image.tagResourceName)) {
    throw new Error('Exact tag list returned a foreign row')
  }
  if (rows.length > 1) throw new Error('Exact tag list is ambiguous')
  return rows
}

async function requireDigestRetained(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  image: ImageIdentity,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<void> {
  const rows = await gcloudJson(artifactVersionRowsSchema, [
    'artifacts', 'versions', 'list',
    `--package=${input.imageName}`,
    `--filter=name="${image.digestVersionResourceName}"`, '--limit=2',
    `--location=${input.runRegion}`,
    `--repository=${input.repository}`, `--account=${input.account}`,
    `--project=${input.projectId}`, '--format=json(name)',
  ], input, runCommand)
  const exact = rows.filter(({ name }) =>
    name === image.digestVersionResourceName)
  if (exact.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(
      `Retained digest exact list returned ${exact.length} rows`,
    )
  }
}

async function cleanupTag(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  const image = owned.image
  if (!image) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  requireCoherentImageIdentity(input, image)
  if (owned.build && !owned.buildTerminal) return {
    outcome: 'retained',
    precondition: 'Build terminality not established',
    residualReason: 'Tag retained because Build may still write it',
  }
  const before = await listExactTag(input, image, runCommand)
  if (before.length === 0) {
    await requireDigestRetained(input, image, runCommand)
    return { outcome: 'absent' }
  }
  if (before.length !== 1 ||
      before[0].image !== imagePath(input) ||
      before[0].tag !== image.tagResourceName ||
      before[0].version !== image.digestVersionResourceName ||
      normalizeDigest(before[0].version) !== image.digest) {
    return {
      outcome: 'retained',
      precondition: `expected digest ${image.digest}`,
      residualReason: 'Tag replacement window is ambiguous; tag was not deleted',
    }
  }
  let deletionFailure: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'gcloud', [
      'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'delete', image.taggedImage,
      '--quiet',
      `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
    ], phaseOptions(input, input.budgets.commandMs))
  } catch (error) {
    deletionFailure = error
  }
  let after: readonly z.infer<typeof artifactTagRowSchema>[]
  try {
    after = await listExactTag(input, image, runCommand)
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      outcome: 'retained',
      preservedFailures: [deletionFailure, error].filter((value) => value !== undefined),
      residualReason: 'Tag post-delete state is unreadable',
    }
  }
  if (after.length !== 0) {
    return {
      outcome: 'retained',
      preservedFailures: deletionFailure === undefined ? [] : [deletionFailure],
      residualReason: 'Exact invocation tag remains after delete',
    }
  }
  await requireDigestRetained(input, image, runCommand)
  return {
    outcome: 'deleted',
    precondition: `digest=${image.digest}`,
    preservedFailures: deletionFailure === undefined ? [] : [deletionFailure],
  }
}

async function dockerImagePresent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  tag: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const result = await runCommand('docker', ['image', 'inspect', tag],
    dockerOptions(input, snapshot, input.budgets.commandMs))
  if (result.exitCode === 0 && result.signal === null && !result.timedOut) return true
  if (result.exitCode !== 0 && /No such (?:image|object)/i.test(result.stderr)) return false
  throw new Error(`Docker image state unreadable for ${tag}`)
}

async function cleanupLocalImage(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  snapshot: SourceSnapshot,
  tag: string,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  if (!await dockerImagePresent(input, snapshot, tag, runCommand)) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  await checkedCommand(runCommand, 'docker', ['image', 'rm', tag],
    dockerOptions(input, snapshot, input.budgets.commandMs))
  if (await dockerImagePresent(input, snapshot, tag, runCommand)) {
    throw new Error(`Local image ${tag} remains after removal`)
  }
  return { outcome: 'deleted' }
}

async function pathPresent(path: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try { await stat(path); return true }
  catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') return false
    throw error
  }
}

async function cleanupPath(path: string): Promise<CleanupSuccess> {
  if (!await pathPresent(path)) return { outcome: 'absent' }
  await rm(path, { recursive: true, force: false })
  if (await pathPresent(path)) throw new Error(`Path remains after cleanup: ${path}`)
  return { outcome: 'deleted' }
}

function recordUnresolvedIntent(
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  failures: unknown[],
  kind: 'source' | 'build' | 'tag' | 'job',
  identity: string,
  reason: string,
  causes: readonly unknown[] = [],
): void {
  failures.push(...causes)
  const residualFailure = new Error(reason)
  failures.push(residualFailure)
  receipt.cleanup.push({
    kind,
    identity,
    outcome: 'retained',
    error: [...causes.flatMap(errorChain), reason].join('; '),
  })
  const residual = {
    kind,
    identity,
    reason,
  }
  receipt.residuals.push(residual)
  emitOwnershipEvent(
    process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr),
    'residual',
    residual,
  )
}

async function reconcileTerminalBuildProduct(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  failures: unknown[],
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!owned.build || !owned.buildTerminal || !owned.identity || !owned.buildIntent) {
    throw new Error('Terminal Build product reconciliation requires exact ownership')
  }
  const build = terminalBuildIdentity(owned.build)
  if (build.status === 'SUCCESS') {
    try {
      const image = await resolveProducedImage(
        mutationReconciliationInput(input),
        owned.identity,
        build,
        runCommand,
      )
      owned.image = assignOnce(owned.image, image, 'reconciled image intent')
      owned.receipt.image = { ...image, state: 'owned' }
    } catch (error) {
      recordUnresolvedIntent(
        owned.receipt,
        failures,
        'tag',
        owned.buildIntent.taggedImage,
        'Terminal successful Build tag/digest is unreadable',
        [error],
      )
    }
    return
  }
  try {
    const rows = await gcloudJson(z.array(artifactTagRowSchema).max(2), [
      'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list', imagePath(input),
      exactTagFilter(input, owned.identity.imageTag), '--limit=2',
      `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
      '--format=json(image,tag,version)',
    ], input, runCommand)
    if (rows.length === 0) {
      owned.receipt.image = {
        ...owned.receipt.image,
        state: 'absent-after-terminal-build',
      }
      return
    }
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'tag',
      owned.buildIntent.taggedImage,
      `Terminal ${build.status} Build left a tag that cannot be bound to a trusted digest`,
    )
  } catch (error) {
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'tag',
      owned.buildIntent.taggedImage,
      `Terminal ${build.status} Build tag state is unreadable`,
      [error],
    )
  }
}

async function reconcileSourceIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  failures: unknown[],
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<void> {
  const intent = owned.sourceIntent
  if (!intent || intent.state === 'acquired') return
  try {
    const rows = await listExactSourceObjects(
      input, intent.bucket, intent.object, runCommand,
    )
    const exact = rows.filter((row) =>
      row.bucket === intent.bucket && row.name === intent.object)
    if (exact.length === 0) {
      intent.state = 'unresolved'
      recordUnresolvedIntent(
        owned.receipt,
        failures,
        'source',
        `gs://${intent.bucket}/${intent.object}`,
        'Possibly accepted source upload is not observable; absence is not ownership proof',
      )
      return
    }
    if (exact.length !== 1) {
      throw new Error(
        `Source intent exact list returned ${exact.length} rows`,
      )
    }
    const row = exact[0]
    if (row.bucket !== intent.bucket || row.name !== intent.object ||
        row.size !== intent.expectedSize ||
        row.custom_fields['freshell-run-id'] !== intent.expectedMetadata.runId ||
        row.custom_fields['freshell-head'] !== intent.expectedMetadata.head) {
      throw new Error('Source intent exact list returned foreign metadata')
    }
    acquireSourceOwnership(owned, {
      bucket: row.bucket,
      object: row.name,
      generation: row.generation,
      size: row.size,
      commit: intent.expectedMetadata.head,
    })
  } catch (error) {
    intent.state = 'unresolved'
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'source',
      `gs://${intent.bucket}/${intent.object}`,
      'Possibly accepted source upload remains unreadable or ambiguous',
      [error],
    )
  }
}

async function reconcileBuildIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  failures: unknown[],
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<void> {
  const intent = owned.buildIntent
  if (!intent) return
  if (owned.build && buildIsActive(owned.build.status)) {
    return
  }
  if (owned.build && owned.buildTerminal) {
    if (!owned.image) {
      await reconcileTerminalBuildProduct(input, owned, failures, runCommand)
    }
    return
  }
  try {
    if (!owned.identity) throw new Error('Build intent lacks invocation identity')
    const rows = await gcloudJson(z.array(buildRowSchema).max(2), [
      'builds', 'list', '--region=global', '--limit=2',
      `--filter=tags=${intent.correlationTag}`,
      `--account=${input.account}`,
      `--project=${input.projectId}`,
      '--format=json',
    ], input, runCommand)
    const matches = rows.filter((row) => buildMatchesCorrelation(
      row,
      input,
      intent.authority,
      owned.identity!,
      intent.source,
      intent.taggedImage,
      intent.window,
    ))
    if (matches.length !== 1) {
      throw new Error(`Build intent exact correlation count is ${matches.length}`)
    }
    const build = correlatedBuildIdentity(
      matches[0],
      input,
      intent.authority,
      owned.identity,
      intent.expectedRequest,
    )
    acquireBuildOwnership(owned, build)
    if (buildIsActive(build.status)) return
    await reconcileTerminalBuildProduct(input, owned, failures, runCommand)
  } catch (error) {
    intent.state = 'unresolved'
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'build',
      intent.correlationTag,
      'Possibly accepted Build remains absent, unreadable, or ambiguously correlated',
      [error],
    )
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'tag',
      intent.taggedImage,
      'Possible Build-owned tag cannot be deleted without an exact terminal Build digest',
    )
  }
}

async function reconcileLocalPushIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  failures: unknown[],
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<void> {
  const intent = owned.localPushIntent
  if (!intent || intent.state === 'acquired') {
    return
  }
  try {
    const leafTag = intent.remoteTag.split(':').at(-1)!
    const rows = await gcloudJson(z.array(artifactTagRowSchema).max(2), [
      'artifacts', 'docker', 'tags', 'list', imagePath(input),
      exactTagFilter(input, leafTag), '--limit=2',
      `--account=${input.account}`, `--project=${input.projectId}`,
      '--format=json(image,tag,version)',
    ], input, runCommand, input.budgets.imageMs)
    const exactTagResource = artifactTagResourceName(input, leafTag)
    const exactTags = rows.filter(({ image, tag }) =>
      image === imagePath(input) && tag === exactTagResource)
    if (exactTags.length !== 1) {
      throw new Error(
        `Local push exact remote tag list returned ${exactTags.length} rows`,
      )
    }
    const digest = normalizeDigest(exactTags[0].version)
    const versionResource = artifactVersionResourceName(input, digest)
    if (exactTags[0].version !== versionResource) {
      throw new Error('Local push tag returned a foreign version resource')
    }
    const versions = await gcloudJson(
      artifactVersionRowsSchema,
      [
        'artifacts', 'versions', 'list',
        `--package=${input.imageName}`,
        `--filter=name="${versionResource}"`,
        '--limit=2',
        `--location=${input.runRegion}`,
        `--repository=${input.repository}`,
        `--account=${input.account}`,
        `--project=${input.projectId}`,
        '--format=json(name)',
      ],
      input,
      runCommand,
      input.budgets.imageMs,
    )
    if (versions.filter(({ name }) => name === versionResource).length !== 1) {
      throw new Error('Local push digest version is absent or ambiguous')
    }
    const completedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    acquireBuildAndImageOwnership(owned, {
      build: {
        correlationTag: intent.correlationTag,
        id: `local-build-${intent.runId}`,
        resourceName: `local-builds/${intent.runId}`,
        projectId: intent.authority.projectId,
        projectNumber: intent.authority.projectNumber,
        createTime: completedAt,
        startTime: completedAt,
        finishTime: completedAt,
        serviceAccount: input.buildServiceAccount,
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        normalizedConfig: deepFreeze({
          source: {
            localExtractedCommit: intent.head,
            context: owned.snapshot?.sourceDir,
          },
          results: {
            images: [{ name: intent.remoteTag, digest }],
          },
        }),
      },
      image: {
        taggedImage: intent.remoteTag,
        tag: intent.remoteTag.split(':').at(-1)!,
        tagResourceName: exactTags[0].tag,
        digest,
        immutableImage: `${imagePath(input)}@${digest}`,
        digestVersionResourceName: versionResource,
        localTags: [...intent.localTags],
      },
    })
  } catch (error) {
    intent.state = 'unresolved'
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'tag',
      intent.remoteTag,
      'Possibly accepted local push remains absent, unreadable, or fails exact tag/digest proof',
      [error],
    )
  }
}

async function reconcileJobIntent(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  failures: unknown[],
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<void> {
  const intent = owned.jobIntent
  if (!intent || intent.state === 'acquired') return
  try {
    if (!owned.identity) throw new Error('Job intent lacks invocation identity')
    const job = await getExactRunJob(input, intent.jobName, runCommand)
    if (!job) {
      intent.state = 'unresolved'
      recordUnresolvedIntent(
        owned.receipt,
        failures,
        'job',
        intent.jobName,
        'Possibly accepted Job is not observable; absence is not ownership proof',
      )
      return
    }
    const created = Date.parse(job.createTime)
    if (!Number.isFinite(created) ||
        created < intent.window.notBeforeMs ||
        created > intent.window.notAfterMs) {
      throw new Error('Job intent create time is outside exact bounds')
    }
    assertExactJobConfiguration(
      {
        ...input,
        taskCount: intent.taskCount,
        taskTimeout: intent.taskTimeout,
      },
      owned.identity,
      job,
      intent.immutableImage,
      intent.expectedEnv,
    )
    acquireJobOwnership(owned, job)
  } catch (error) {
    intent.state = 'unresolved'
    recordUnresolvedIntent(
      owned.receipt,
      failures,
      'job',
      intent.jobName,
      'Possibly accepted Job remains unreadable or fails full ownership proof',
      [error],
    )
  }
}

async function reconcileUnresolvedMutationIntents(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<unknown[]> {
  const failures: unknown[] = []
  await reconcileJobIntent(input, owned, failures, runCommand)
  await reconcileLocalPushIntent(input, owned, failures, runCommand)
  await reconcileBuildIntent(input, owned, failures, runCommand)
  await reconcileSourceIntent(input, owned, failures, runCommand)
  return failures
}

export async function cleanupOwnedResources(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<unknown[]> {
  const failures = await reconcileUnresolvedMutationIntents(
    input,
    owned,
    runCommand,
  )
  if (owned.execution) await attemptCleanup(
    owned.receipt, failures, 'execution', owned.execution.resourceName,
    () => cleanupExecution(input, owned, runCommand),
  )
  if (owned.job) await attemptCleanup(
    owned.receipt, failures, 'job', owned.job.name,
    () => cleanupJob(input, owned, runCommand),
  )
  if (owned.build) await attemptCleanup(
    owned.receipt, failures, 'build', owned.build.id,
    () => cleanupBuild(input, owned, runCommand),
  )
  if (owned.source) await attemptCleanup(
    owned.receipt, failures, 'source', sourceGenerationUri(owned.source),
    () => cleanupSource(input, owned, runCommand),
  )
  if (owned.image) await attemptCleanup(
    owned.receipt, failures, 'tag', owned.image.taggedImage,
    () => cleanupTag(input, owned, runCommand),
  )
  if (owned.snapshot) {
    const localTags = new Set([
      ...(owned.localPushIntent?.localTags ?? []),
      ...(owned.image?.localTags ?? []),
    ])
    for (const tag of localTags) await attemptCleanup(
      owned.receipt, failures, 'local-image', tag,
      () => cleanupLocalImage(input, owned.snapshot!, tag, runCommand),
    )
  }
  if (owned.snapshot) {
    await attemptCleanup(
      owned.receipt, failures, 'docker-config', owned.snapshot.dockerConfigDir,
      async () => {
        const result = await cleanupPath(owned.snapshot!.dockerConfigDir)
        owned.receipt.temp.dockerConfigRemoved = result.outcome !== 'retained'
        return result
      },
    )
    await attemptCleanup(
      owned.receipt, failures, 'temp-root', owned.snapshot.tempRoot,
      async () => {
        const result = await cleanupPath(owned.snapshot!.tempRoot)
        owned.receipt.temp.rootRemoved = result.outcome !== 'retained'
        return result
      },
    )
  }
  return failures
}

export async function cleanupWithIndependentBudget(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<unknown[]> {
  const cleanupController = new AbortController()
  const timer = setTimeout(() => cleanupController.abort(
    new Error(`Cleanup exceeded ${input.budgets.cleanupMs}ms`),
  ), input.budgets.cleanupMs)
  try {
    const cleanupInput = { ...input, signal: cleanupController.signal }
    const failures = await cleanupOwnedResources(cleanupInput, owned, runCommand)
    if (cleanupController.signal.aborted) {
      failures.push(cleanupController.signal.reason ?? new Error('Cleanup aborted'))
    }
    return failures
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer)
  }
}
```

Post-loss output is diagnostic only. Do not add a recovery parser, inspection
dispatch, caller-authored resource tuple, delete/cancel/rm argv, durable
ownership capability, or janitor. Keep only this direct-execution guard after
the automatic cleanup implementation:

```ts
if (process.argv[1] &&
    import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href &&
    process.argv.length > 2) {
  process.stderr.write('manual lifecycle recovery commands are not supported\n')
  process.exitCode = 2
}
```

### Task 6 nested RGR microcycles before the single commit

The complete bodies above are the final destination, but install them through
the following uncommitted sequence so FE15 and FE16 each reach the production
seam they control. No intermediate state is committed.

#### Base cleanup state machine — uncommitted GREEN

Transcribe every Task 6 GREEN body except the two final deltas below. In

`input.taskTimeout` pins the predecessor to the later caller value:

In `cleanupExecution`, install this complete consumed `checkedCommand`

Keep the canonical `JobMutationIntent.taskTimeout` field and its `1800s` storage

```ts
  expect(owned.receipt.residuals).not.toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
```

```ts
function exactExecutionCancelArgv(name: string): readonly string[] {
  return [
    'run', 'jobs', 'executions', 'cancel', name,
    '--quiet',
    `--region=${BASE_INPUT.runRegion}`,
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ]
}

function exactExecutionDescribeArgv(name: string): readonly string[] {
  return [
    'run', 'jobs', 'executions', 'describe', name,
    `--region=${BASE_INPUT.runRegion}`,
    `--account=${BASE_INPUT.account}`,
    `--project=${BASE_INPUT.projectId}`,
    '--format=json',
  ]
}

it('cancels an active Execution noninteractively before terminal reconciliation', async () => {
  const input: CloudRunLifecycleInput = {
    ...BASE_INPUT,
    budgets: {
      ...BASE_INPUT.budgets,
      executionMs: 100,
      commandMs: 100,
      cleanupMs: 500,
    },
  }
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(input, IDENTITY.runId)
  owned.receipt.projectNumber = '123456789'
  const execution: ExecutionIdentity = {
    operationName:
      'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/run-op',
    resourceName:
      `${VERIFIED_JOB.name}/executions/execution-owned`,
    name: 'execution-owned',
    uid: 'execution-uid-1',
  }
  acquireExecutionOwnership(owned, execution)

  const cancelArgv = exactExecutionCancelArgv(execution.name)
  const describeArgv = exactExecutionDescribeArgv(execution.name)
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    expect(command).toBe('gcloud')
    if (args[3] === 'cancel') {
      expect(args).toEqual(cancelArgv)
      // --quiet, not a synthetic affirmative answer, owns prompt policy.
      expect(options.stdin).toBeUndefined()
      return commandOk()
    }
    expect(args).toEqual(describeArgv)
    return commandOk(fixtures.executionSuccessOmittedZeros)
  })

  let guard: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  let failures: unknown[] = []
  try {
    failures = await Promise.race([
      cleanupWithIndependentBudget(input, owned, runner),
      new Promise<never>((_resolve, reject) => {
        guard = setTimeout(() => reject(
          new Error('noninteractive Execution cleanup exceeded 1000ms'),
        ), 1_000)
      }),
    ])
  } finally {
    if (guard) clearTimeout(guard)
  }

  expect(failures).toEqual([])
  expect(runner.mock.calls.map(([command, args]) => [command, args]))
    .toEqual([
      ['gcloud', cancelArgv],
      ['gcloud', describeArgv],
    ])
  expect(owned.receipt.execution).toMatchObject({
    state: 'terminal',
    counters: {
      succeededCount: 4,
      failedCount: 0,
      cancelledCount: 0,
      runningCount: 0,
    },
  })
  expect(owned.receipt.cleanup).toContainEqual(expect.objectContaining({
    kind: 'execution',
    outcome: 'terminal',
  }))
})
```

Create the shared deterministic provider in
`test/unit/server/testing/cloud-run-test-helpers.ts`; this is the single concrete
fixture used by Task 7's lifecycle tests (earlier task-local fixtures remain
focused on their own primitive):

```ts
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { expect, vi } from 'vitest'
import type {
  Clock,
  CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  CommandResult,
  RunCommand,
  RunCommandOptions,
} from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'

export type LifecycleFault =
  | 'none'
  | 'malformed-template'
  | 'source-response-loss'
  | 'build-failed'
  | 'run-ambiguous'
  | 'result-malformed'
  | 'cleanup-source'
  | 'local-push-thrown'
  | 'local-push-returned'
  | 'local-push-post-read'
  | 'local-push-recovered'

const result = (value: unknown = {}): CommandResult => ({
  stdout: `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`, stderr: '', exitCode: 0,
  signal: null, timedOut: false,
})
export function successfulLifecycleRunner(
  lane: string,
  fault: LifecycleFault = 'none',
  clock: Clock = Date.now,
): ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>> {
  const now = clock()
  const iso = (offsetMs: number) => new Date(now + offsetMs).toISOString()
  const head = lane.padEnd(40, 'a').slice(0, 40).replace(/[^0-9a-f]/g, 'a')
  let runId = ''
  let jobName = ''
  let sourceObject = ''
  let sourcePresent = true
  let tagPresent = true
  let jobPresent = true
  let buildRequest: Record<string, unknown> | undefined
  let env: Record<string, string> = {}
  let localPushTagReads = 0
  const localImages = new Set<string>()
  const buildId = () => `build-${runId}`
  const executionName = () => `execution-${runId}`
  const taggedImage = () =>
    `us-west1-docker.pkg.dev/test-project/freshell-e2e/freshell-vitest:` +
    `sbnj-${head.slice(0, 12)}-${runId}`
  const digest = `sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`
  const gateBuild = 'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com'
  const gateRuntime = 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com'
  const caller = 'user:caller@example.com'

  const buildRow = (status = fault === 'build-failed' ? 'FAILURE' : 'SUCCESS') => ({
    id: buildId(),
    name: `projects/123456789/locations/global/builds/${buildId()}`,
    projectId: 'test-project',
    status,
    createTime: iso(0),
    startTime: iso(1_000),
    finishTime: iso(60_000),
    ...(buildRequest ?? {}),
    results: { images: [{ name: taggedImage(), digest }] },
  })
  const job = () => ({
    name: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/${jobName}`,
    uid: `job-uid-${runId}`,
    etag: `job-etag-${runId}`,
    generation: '7',
    createTime: iso(2_000),
    labels: { 'freshell-run-id': runId },
    template: {
      taskCount: 4,
      template: {
        serviceAccount: gateRuntime,
        containers: [{
          image: taggedImage().replace(/:[^:]+$/, `@${digest}`),
          env: Object.entries(env).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value })),
          resources: { limits: { cpu: '4', memory: '4Gi' } },
        }],
        maxRetries: 0,
        timeout: '1800s',
      },
    },
  })
  const execution = () => ({
    metadata: {
      name: executionName(), namespace: '123456789', uid: `execution-uid-${runId}`,
    },
    spec: {
      taskCount: 4,
      template: { spec: { serviceAccountName: gateRuntime } },
    },
    status: {
      conditions: [{ type: 'Completed', status: 'True' }],
      succeededCount: 4,
      startTime: iso(3_000),
      completionTime: iso(4_000),
    },
  })

  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (
    command: string,
    args: readonly string[],
    options: RunCommandOptions,
  ) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (command === 'git') {
      if (args.includes('symbolic-ref')) return { ...result(), stdout: 'feature\n' }
      if (args.includes('status')) return { ...result(), stdout: '' }
      if (args.includes('rev-parse')) return { ...result(), stdout: `${head}\n` }
      if (args.includes('--get-all') && text.includes('.remote')) {
        return { ...result(), stdout: 'origin\0' }
      }
      if (args.includes('--get-all') && text.includes('.merge')) {
        return { ...result(), stdout: 'refs/heads/feature\0' }
      }
      if (args.includes('ls-remote')) {
        return { ...result(), stdout: `${head}\trefs/heads/feature\n` }
      }
      if (args.includes('archive')) {
        const output = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--output='))!.slice(9)
        await writeFile(output, 'abc')
        return result()
      }
    }
    if (command === 'tar') {
      const sourceDir = args[args.indexOf('-C') + 1]
      await mkdir(join(sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run'), { recursive: true })
      const template = fault === 'malformed-template'
        ? 'steps: []\n'
        : `steps:\n` +
          `  - name: gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker@sha256:${'6'.repeat(64)}\n` +
          `    args: [build, --no-cache, --file, docker/cloud-run/Dockerfile, --tag, '\${_IMAGE}', .]\n` +
          `  - name: gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker@sha256:${'6'.repeat(64)}\n` +
          `    args: [push, '\${_IMAGE}']\n` +
          `images: ['\${_IMAGE}']\n` +
          `options:\n  machineType: E2_HIGHCPU_32\n  diskSizeGb: 200\n` +
          `  logging: CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY\ntimeout: 3600s\n`
      await writeFile(join(sourceDir, 'docker/cloud-run/cloudbuild.yaml'), template)
      return result()
    }
    if (command === 'docker') {
      if (args[0] === 'build') {
        for (let index = 0; index < args.length; index += 1) {
          if (args[index] === '--tag') localImages.add(String(args[index + 1]))
        }
        const local = [...localImages].find((tag) =>
          /^freshell-(?:vitest|e2e):[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(tag))
        if (!local) throw new Error('local build omitted invocation tag')
        runId = local.split(':').at(-1)!
        return result()
      }
      if (args[0] === 'login') {
        expect(String(options.stdin)).toBe('short-lived-token\n')
        return result()
      }
      if (args[0] === 'push') {
        tagPresent = true
        if (fault === 'local-push-thrown') {
          throw Object.assign(new Error('docker push response lost'), {
            code: 'ECONNRESET',
          })
        }
        if (fault === 'local-push-returned') {
          return {
            stdout: '',
            stderr: 'docker push connection closed after upload',
            exitCode: 1,
            signal: null,
            timedOut: false,
          }
        }
        return result()
      }
      if (args[0] === 'image' && args[1] === 'inspect') {
        const inspectedTag = String(args[2])
        expect([
          `freshell-vitest:${runId}`,
          taggedImage(),
        ]).toContain(inspectedTag)
        return localImages.has(inspectedTag)
          ? result({})
          : {
              ...result(),
              exitCode: 1,
              stdout: '',
              stderr: `Error response from daemon: No such image: ${inspectedTag}\n`,
            }
      }
      if (args[0] === 'image' && args[1] === 'rm') {
        localImages.delete(String(args[2]))
        return result()
      }
    }

    if (command === process.execPath) {
      const envelope = JSON.parse(String(options.stdin)) as {
        command: { kind: string; jobName?: string; operationId?: string }
      }
      const kind = envelope.command.kind
      if (kind === 'get-job') {
        return result(jobPresent
          ? { httpStatus: 200, body: job() }
          : { httpStatus: 404, body: {
              error: { code: 404, status: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'absent' },
            } })
      }
      if (kind === 'run-job') {
        if (fault === 'run-ambiguous') throw Object.assign(
          new Error('run response lost'), { code: 'ECONNRESET' },
        )
        return result({
          httpStatus: 200,
          body: { name: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/run-op' },
        })
      }
      if (kind === 'delete-job') {
        jobPresent = false
        return result({
          httpStatus: 200,
          body: { name: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/delete-op' },
        })
      }
      if (kind === 'get-operation') {
        const deleting = envelope.command.operationId === 'delete-op'
        return result({
          httpStatus: 200,
          body: deleting
            ? {
                name: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/delete-op',
                done: true,
              }
            : {
                name: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/operations/run-op',
                done: true,
                response: {
                  name: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/${jobName}` +
                    `/executions/${executionName()}`,
                  uid: `execution-uid-${runId}`,
                },
              },
        })
      }
    }

    if (command === 'gcloud' && args.includes('print-access-token')) {
      return { ...result(), stdout: 'short-lived-token\n' }
    }
    if (text.includes('projects describe')) {
      return result({ projectId: 'test-project', projectNumber: '123456789' })
    }
    if (text.includes('projects get-ancestors')) {
      return result([
        { id: 'test-project', type: 'project' },
        { id: '123456789', type: 'organization' },
      ])
    }
    if (text.includes('iam service-accounts describe')) {
      const email = String(args[3])
      return result({
        name: `projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${email}`, email, disabled: false,
      })
    }
    if (text.includes('asset analyze-iam-policy')) {
      const identity = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--identity='))!.slice(11)
      const permission = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--permissions='))
        ?.slice('--permissions='.length)
      const resources = permission === 'iam.serviceAccounts.actAs'
        ? [
            `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${gateBuild}`,
            `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${gateRuntime}`,
          ]
        : permission === 'iam.serviceAccounts.getAccessToken'
          ? [`//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${gateBuild}`]
          : identity.includes('freshell-build')
            ? [
                '//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/test-project',
                '//storage.googleapis.com/projects/_/buckets/test-source-bucket',
                '//artifactregistry.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e',
              ]
            : []
      const roles = permission === 'iam.serviceAccounts.actAs'
        ? resources.map(() => 'roles/iam.serviceAccountUser')
        : permission === 'iam.serviceAccounts.getAccessToken'
          ? resources.map(() => 'roles/iam.serviceAccountTokenCreator')
          : [
              'roles/logging.logWriter',
              'roles/storage.objectViewer',
              'roles/artifactregistry.writer',
            ]
      return result({
        fullyExplored: true,
        mainAnalysis: {
          analysisQuery: {
            scope: 'organizations/123456789',
            identitySelector: { identity },
            options: { expandGroups: true, expandRoles: true },
            ...(permission
              ? { accessSelector: { permissions: [permission] } }
              : {}),
          },
          ...(resources.length === 0 ? {} : {
            analysisResults: resources.map((resource, index) => ({
              accessControlLists: [{
                accesses: permission
                  ? [{ permission }]
                  : [{ role: roles[index] }],
                resources: [{ fullResourceName: resource }],
              }],
              attachedResourceFullName: resource,
              fullyExplored: true,
              iamBinding: {
                members: [identity],
                role: roles[index],
              },
              identityList: { identities: [{ name: identity }] },
            })),
          }),
          fullyExplored: true,
        },
      })
    }
    if (text.includes('storage buckets list')) {
      return result([{ name: 'test-source-bucket', location: 'US' }])
    }
    if (text.includes('artifacts repositories list')) return result([{
      name: 'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e',
      format: 'DOCKER',
    }])
    if (text.includes(
      'beta quotas info describe OngoingBuildsPerProject ' +
      '--service=cloudbuild.googleapis.com --project=test-project ' +
      '--account=caller@example.com ' +
      '--format=json(metric,quotaId,dimensions,dimensionsInfos)',
    )) return result({
      metric: 'cloudbuild.googleapis.com/ongoing_builds',
      quotaId: 'OngoingBuildsPerProject',
      dimensionsInfos: [{
        applicableLocations: ['global'],
        details: { value: '10' },
      }],
    })
    if (text.includes('storage cp')) {
      if (fault === 'source-response-loss') {
        sourcePresent = false
        return {
          stdout: '',
          stderr: 'ERROR: gcloud transport ended without structured status',
          exitCode: 1,
          signal: null,
          timedOut: false,
        }
      }
      const uri = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('gs://'))!
      sourceObject = uri.split('/').slice(3).join('/')
      runId = /([0-9a-f]{32})\.tar\.gz$/.exec(sourceObject)![1]
      return result()
    }
    if (text.includes('storage objects list')) return result(sourcePresent ? [{
      bucket: 'test-source-bucket', name: sourceObject, generation: `1${runId.slice(0, 8)}`,
      size: '3',
      custom_fields: { 'freshell-run-id': runId, 'freshell-head': head },
    }] : [])
    if (text.includes('builds submit')) {
      const configPath = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--config='))!.slice(9)
      buildRequest = JSON.parse(await readFile(configPath, 'utf8'))
      return result(buildRow())
    }
    if (text.includes('builds list')) return result([buildRow()])
    if (text.includes('artifacts docker tags list')) {
      localPushTagReads += 1
      if (fault === 'local-push-recovered' && localPushTagReads === 1) {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('first post-push tag read lost'), {
          code: 'ECONNRESET',
        })
      }
      if (fault === 'local-push-thrown' ||
          fault === 'local-push-returned' ||
          fault === 'local-push-post-read') {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('post-push tag read lost'), {
          code: 'ECONNRESET',
        })
      }
      return result(tagPresent ? [{
        image: taggedImage().replace(/:[^:]+$/, ''),
        tag: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e` +
          `/packages/freshell-vitest/tags/${taggedImage().split(':').at(-1)}`,
        version:
          `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e` +
          `/packages/freshell-vitest/versions/${digest}`,
      }] : [])
    }
    if (text.includes('artifacts versions list')) return result([{
      name: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e` +
        `/packages/freshell-vitest/versions/${digest}`,
    }])
    if (text.includes('run jobs create')) {
      jobName = String(args[3])
      runId = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--labels='))!.match(/[0-9a-f]{32}/)![0]
      const envFile = args.find((arg) => arg.startsWith('--env-vars-file='))!.slice(16)
      expect(args).toEqual([
        'run', 'jobs', 'create', jobName,
        `--image=${taggedImage().replace(/:[^:]+$/, `@${digest}`)}`,
        `--service-account=${gateRuntime}`,
        '--tasks=4',
        '--task-timeout=1800s',
        '--max-retries=0',
        '--memory=4Gi',
        '--cpu=4',
        `--labels=freshell-run-id=${runId}`,
        `--env-vars-file=${envFile}`,
        '--region=us-west1',
        '--account=caller@example.com',
        '--project=test-project',
        '--format=json',
      ])
      env = JSON.parse(await readFile(envFile, 'utf8'))
      return result({
        apiVersion: 'run.googleapis.com/v1',
        kind: 'Job',
        metadata: {
          name: jobName,
          namespace: 'test-project',
          uid: `v1-ack-${runId}`,
          creationTimestamp: iso(2_000),
        },
        status: { conditions: [{ type: 'Ready', status: 'True' }] },
      })
    }
    if (text.includes('executions describe')) return result(execution())
    if (text.includes('logging read')) {
      const parts = ['client:1/4']
      return result(parts.map((partId) => ({
        resource: { type: 'cloud_run_job', labels: {
          project_id: 'test-project', location: 'us-west1', job_name: jobName,
        } },
        labels: { 'run.googleapis.com/execution_name': executionName() },
        jsonPayload: fault === 'result-malformed'
          ? { marker: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT', partId: 7 }
          : {
              marker: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT', schemaVersion: 1, kind: 'vitest',
              runId, execution: executionName(), partId, taskIndex: 0, taskCount: 4,
              taskAttempt: 0, status: 'passed', exitCode: 0, testCount: 3,
            },
      })))
    }
    if (text.includes('storage objects list')) return result(sourcePresent ? [{
      bucket: 'test-source-bucket', name: sourceObject, generation: `1${runId.slice(0, 8)}`,
    }] : [])
    if (text.includes('storage rm')) {
      if (fault === 'cleanup-source') throw new Error('source delete failed')
      sourcePresent = false
      return result()
    }
    if (text.includes('artifacts docker tags delete')) {
      tagPresent = false
      return result()
    }
    throw new Error(`unhandled lifecycle command: ${text}`)
  })
}

export function activeBuildLifecycleRunner(
  mode:
    | 'interrupt-lost-cancel'
    | 'interrupt-cancel-settlement-fail'
    | 'late-active-recovery',
  controller: AbortController,
): ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>> {
  const base = successfulLifecycleRunner(`active-${mode}`)
  let buildState: 'WORKING' | 'CANCELLED' = 'WORKING'
  let correlationLists = 0
  let cancelCalls = 0
  const asResult = (value: unknown): CommandResult => ({
    stdout: `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`,
    stderr: '',
    exitCode: 0,
    signal: null,
    timedOut: false,
  })
  const activeRow = async (
    command: string,
    args: readonly string[],
    options: RunCommandOptions,
  ) => {
    const original = await base(command, args, options)
    const decoded = JSON.parse(original.stdout) as
      | Record<string, unknown>
      | Record<string, unknown>[]
    const row = Array.isArray(decoded) ? decoded[0] : decoded
    row.status = buildState
    if (buildState === 'WORKING') delete row.finishTime
    return row
  }
  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    if (text.includes('builds submit')) {
      const row = await activeRow(command, args, options)
      if (mode === 'late-active-recovery') {
        throw Object.assign(new Error('accepted Build response lost'), {
          code: 'ECONNRESET',
        })
      }
      controller.abort(new Error('caught SIGTERM'))
      return asResult(row)
    }
    if (text.includes('builds list')) {
      const byCorrelation = args.some((arg) =>
        arg.startsWith('--filter=tags='))
      if (byCorrelation) {
        correlationLists += 1
        if (mode === 'late-active-recovery' && correlationLists === 1) {
          throw new Error('initial Build correlation unavailable')
        }
      } else if (options.signal?.aborted && cancelCalls === 0) {
        throw options.signal.reason
      } else if (
        mode === 'interrupt-cancel-settlement-fail' &&
        cancelCalls === 1
      ) {
        throw new Error('Build settlement unavailable')
      }
      return asResult([await activeRow(command, args, {
        ...options,
        signal: undefined,
      })])
    }
    if (text.includes('builds cancel')) {
      cancelCalls += 1
      expect(cancelCalls).toBe(1)
      if (mode === 'interrupt-cancel-settlement-fail') {
        throw new Error('cancel failed')
      }
      buildState = 'CANCELLED'
      if (mode === 'interrupt-lost-cancel') {
        throw new Error('cancel response lost')
      }
      return asResult({})
    }
    if (buildState === 'CANCELLED' &&
        text.includes('artifacts docker tags list')) {
      return asResult([])
    }
    return base(command, args, options)
  })
}

export function unresolvedMutationRunner(
  kind: 'source' | 'build' | 'job',
  loss: 'thrown' | 'returned',
): ReturnType<typeof vi.fn<RunCommand>> {
  const base = successfulLifecycleRunner(
    kind === 'source' ? 'a' : kind === 'build' ? 'b' : 'c',
  )
  let mutationAttempted = false
  const loseResponse = (): CommandResult => {
    if (loss === 'thrown') {
      throw Object.assign(new Error(`${kind} response lost`), {
        code: 'ECONNRESET',
      })
    }
    return {
      stdout: '',
      stderr: '',
      exitCode: 1,
      signal: null,
      timedOut: true,
    }
  }
  return vi.fn<RunCommand>(async (command, args, options) => {
    const text = `${command} ${args.join(' ')}`
    const isMutation = kind === 'source'
      ? text.includes('storage cp')
      : kind === 'build'
        ? text.includes('builds submit')
        : text.includes('run jobs create')
    if (isMutation) {
      expect(mutationAttempted).toBe(false)
      mutationAttempted = true
      // Mutate the deterministic provider first: this models accepted request
      // plus lost response for both thrown and returned transport outcomes.
      await base(command, args, options)
      return loseResponse()
    }
    if (mutationAttempted && kind === 'source' &&
        text.includes('storage objects list')) {
      throw new Error('source reconciliation unavailable')
    }
    if (mutationAttempted && kind === 'build' &&
        text.includes('builds list')) {
      throw new Error('Build reconciliation unavailable')
    }
    if (mutationAttempted && kind === 'job' && command === process.execPath) {
      const envelope = JSON.parse(String(options.stdin)) as {
        command: { kind: string }
      }
      if (envelope.command.kind === 'get-job') {
        throw new Error('Job reconciliation unavailable')
      }
    }
    return base(command, args, options)
  })
}
```
