```ts
export function lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout(taskTimeout: string): LifecycleBudgets {
  const taskMs = parseTaskTimeoutMilliseconds(taskTimeout)
  const budgets: LifecycleBudgets = {
    provenanceMs: 60_000,
    sourceMs: 5 * 60_000,
    buildMs: 75 * 60_000,
    imageMs: 5 * 60_000,
    jobMs: 5 * 60_000,
    executionMs: taskMs + 5 * 60_000,
    resultReadMs: 2 * 60_000,
    cleanupMs: 5 * 60_000,
    commandMs: 60_000,
    terminateGraceMs: 5_000,
    settlementMs: 30_000,
    overallMs: 0,
  }
  budgets.overallMs = budgets.provenanceMs + budgets.sourceMs + budgets.buildMs +
    budgets.imageMs + budgets.jobMs + budgets.executionMs + budgets.resultReadMs +
    10 * 60_000
  return budgets
}

function throwIfLifecycleAborted(
  signal: AbortSignal,
  afterPhase: string,
): void {
  if (!signal.aborted) return
  throw new Error(`Cloud lifecycle aborted after ${afterPhase}`, {
    cause: signal.reason,
  })
}

export async function runCloudRunLifecycle(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<OwnershipReceipt> {
  if (input.signal?.aborted) {
    throw new Error('Cloud lifecycle aborted before start')
  }
  assertCanonicalTaskTimeout(input.taskTimeout)
  const overall = new AbortController()
  const forward = () => overall.abort(input.signal?.reason)
  if (input.signal?.aborted) forward()
  else input.signal?.addEventListener('abort', forward, { once: true })
  const timer = setTimeout(() => {
    overall.abort(new Error(`Lifecycle exceeded ${input.budgets.overallMs}ms`))
  }, input.budgets.overallMs)
  const operationInput = { ...input, signal: overall.signal }
  const runId = createRunId()
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(input, runId)
  let primary: unknown | undefined = undefined
  try {
    const snapshot = await prepareSourceSnapshot(
      input.repoRoot,
      runId,
      overall.signal,
      runCommand,
    )
    const identity = createResourceIdentity(input.jobPrefix, snapshot.head, runId)
    acquireSnapshotOwnership(owned, snapshot, identity)
    throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'source snapshot acquisition')

    // Freeze and validate the captured config before the first mutation. The
    // immutable object, not a later path reread, feeds request generation.
    const buildTemplate = await readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath)
    const prerequisites = await verifyCloudPrerequisites(operationInput, runCommand)
    owned.receipt.projectNumber = prerequisites.projectNumber
    const buildAuthority: BuildAuthority = {
      projectId: input.projectId,
      projectNumber: prerequisites.projectNumber,
    }

    let source: SourceIdentity | undefined
    if (input.buildMode === 'cloud') {
      const sourceIntent = await createSourceMutationIntent(
        operationInput,
        snapshot,
        identity,
      )
      registerMutationIntent(owned, sourceIntent)
      markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'source')
      try {
        source = await uploadOwnedSource(
          operationInput,
          snapshot,
          identity,
          runCommand,
        )
      } catch (error) { throw error }
      acquireSourceOwnership(owned, source)
    }
    throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'source upload reconciliation')

    let built: BuiltImage
    if (input.buildMode === 'cloud') {
      const buildWindow = correlationWindow()
      registerMutationIntent(owned, createBuildMutationIntent(
        operationInput,
        buildAuthority,
        snapshot,
        identity,
        source!,
        buildTemplate,
        buildWindow,
      ))
      markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'build')
      const correlated = await startBuildFromGeneration(
        operationInput,
        buildAuthority,
        snapshot,
        identity,
        source!,
        buildTemplate,
        runCommand,
        buildWindow,
      )
      acquireBuildOwnership(owned, correlated)
      built = await finishBuildFromGeneration(
        operationInput,
        identity,
        correlated,
        runCommand,
      )
    } else {
      owned.receipt.source = {
        state: 'not-created',
        commit: snapshot.head,
      }
      const localPushIntent = createLocalPushMutationIntent(
        operationInput,
        buildAuthority,
        snapshot,
        identity,
      )
      registerMutationIntent(owned, localPushIntent)
      markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'local-push')
      built = await buildAndPushLocally(
        operationInput,
        buildAuthority,
        snapshot,
        identity,
        runCommand,
      )
    }
    acquireBuildAndImageOwnership(owned, built)
    throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'Build and image reconciliation')

    if (input.operation === 'run') {
      const expectedEnv = {
        ...input.workloadEnv,
        FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND: input.kind,
        FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID: identity.runId,
      }
      const jobWindow = correlationWindow()
      registerMutationIntent(owned, createJobMutationIntent(
        operationInput,
        identity,
        built.image.immutableImage,
        expectedEnv,
        jobWindow,
      ))
      markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'job')
      let job: VerifiedRunJob
      try {
        job = await createAndVerifyJob(
          operationInput,
          identity,
          snapshot.tempRoot,
          built.image.immutableImage,
          expectedEnv,
          runCommand,
          jobWindow,
        )
      } catch (error) { throw error }
      acquireJobOwnership(owned, job)
      throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'Job create reconciliation')
      const execution = await runExactJobOnce(operationInput, job, runCommand)
      acquireExecutionOwnership(owned, execution)
      throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'Execution identity acquisition')
      const terminal = await waitForExecutionTerminal(
        operationInput,
        execution,
        prerequisites.projectNumber,
        runCommand,
      )
      acquireTerminalExecution(owned, terminal)
      throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'Execution terminal reconciliation')
      if (!executionSucceeded(terminal)) throw new Error('Exact Execution failed')
      owned.receipt.parts = await collectExactPartResults({
        input: operationInput,
        runId: identity.runId,
        jobName: identity.jobName,
        executionName: execution.name,
        runCommand,
      })
    }
    owned.receipt.status = 'succeeded'
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof TerminalBuildError) {
      acquireBuildOwnership(owned, error.build)
    }
    primary = error
    owned.receipt.status = 'failed'
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer)
    input.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', forward)
  }

  const cleanupErrors = await cleanupWithIndependentBudget(input, owned, runCommand)
  if (primary !== undefined || cleanupErrors.length > 0) {
    owned.receipt.status = 'failed'
    owned.receipt.failure = {
      ...(primary === undefined ? {} : { primary: errorMessage(primary) }),
      causes: primary === undefined ? [] : errorChain(primary),
      cleanup: cleanupErrors.flatMap(errorChain),
    }
  }
  emitTerminalReceipt(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), owned.receipt)
  if (primary !== undefined || cleanupErrors.length > 0) {
    throw new CloudRunLifecycleError(primary, cleanupErrors, owned.receipt)
  }
  return owned.receipt
}
```

`runCloudRunLifecycle` calls `createRunId()` itself before any work, records that
same 128-bit value in the pending receipt and temp/source/Build/Job intents, and
passes it only to private lifecycle helpers. After provenance supplies the full
SHA, `createResourceIdentity` completes the image identity without changing the
reserved suffix. No adapter or lifecycle input can supply the value.
Ownership-acquisition helpers enforce that identities never change. All provider/command phases use
their phase budget capped by the overall signal; cleanup uses its independent
signal. Every success and failure emits one terminal strict receipt after
cleanup.

```bash
run_case() {
  local name="$1"; shift
  set +e
  PATH="$FAKE_BIN:$PATH" "$@" >"$CASE_ROOT/$name.out" 2>"$CASE_ROOT/$name.err"
  printf '%s\n' "$?" >"$CASE_ROOT/$name.status"
  set -e
}
assert_exit() {
  test "$(cat "$CASE_ROOT/$1.status")" -eq "$2"
}
assert_raw_result_count() {
  node - "$CASE_ROOT/$1.out" "$2" <<'NODE'
const [path, expected] = process.argv.slice(2)
const lines = require('node:fs').readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split('\n').filter(Boolean)
const records = lines.flatMap((line) => {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(line)
    return value?.marker === 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT' ? [value] : []
  } catch { return [] }
})
if (records.length !== Number(expected)) process.exit(1)
NODE
}
assert_child_line_prefixed() {
  grep -Fqx "[cloud-child stdout] $2" "$CASE_ROOT/$1.out"
}
assert_result_field() {
  node - "$CASE_ROOT/$1.out" "$2" "$3" <<'NODE'
const [path, field, expected] = process.argv.slice(2)
const lines = require('node:fs').readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split('\n').filter(Boolean)
const values = lines.flatMap((line) => {
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(line)
    return value?.marker === 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT' ? [value] : []
  } catch { return [] }
})
if (values.length !== 1 || String(values[0][field]) !== expected) process.exit(1)
NODE
}
assert_result_absent() {
  node - "$CASE_ROOT/$1.out" "$2" <<'NODE'
const [path, field] = process.argv.slice(2)
const values = require('node:fs').readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split('\n')
  .flatMap((line) => {
    try {
      const value = JSON.parse(line)
      return value?.marker === 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT' ? [value] : []
    } catch { return [] }
  })
if (values.length !== 1 || Object.hasOwn(values[0], field)) process.exit(1)
NODE
}
```

report. Normal `e2e-single` proves the Playwright test command ran before its
one authoritative `e2e:1/1` record is accepted. `e2e-failure` proves ordinary
nonzero Playwright execution emits exactly one failed authoritative part,
preserves the exit code, and exits nonzero. Direct one- and two-task dry-runs
are shard-planning diagnostics only: they print "no tests executed," emit zero
result records, and the sharded case invokes only Playwright `--list`. A failed
list exits with its original status after exactly one call, and a successful
list with zero parseable spec rows exits 86; neither can execute a broad second
Playwright command. The non-dry two-task `e2e-empty` case still proves that an
individual shard may pass empty after a positive global inventory.

- [ ] **GREEN — apply focused final wrapper helpers and context exceptions.**

Replace `docker/cloud-run/entrypoint.sh` with this complete final wrapper. Child
streams are always prefixed; the sole authoritative line is bare serialized
JSON so Cloud Logging stores it under `jsonPayload`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

KIND="${FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND:-}"
RUN_ID="${FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID:-}"
EXECUTION="${CLOUD_RUN_EXECUTION:-}"
TASK_INDEX="${CLOUD_RUN_TASK_INDEX:-}"
TASK_COUNT="${CLOUD_RUN_TASK_COUNT:-}"
case "$KIND" in vitest|e2e) ;; *) echo 'invalid FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND' >&2; exit 2 ;; esac
[[ "$RUN_ID" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{32}$ ]] || { echo 'invalid FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID' >&2; exit 2; }
[[ "$EXECUTION" =~ ^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,62}$ ]] || { echo 'invalid CLOUD_RUN_EXECUTION' >&2; exit 2; }
[[ "$TASK_INDEX" =~ ^[0-9]+$ && "$TASK_COUNT" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || {
  echo 'invalid Cloud Run task index/count' >&2; exit 2;
}
(( TASK_COUNT >= 1 && TASK_COUNT <= 32 && TASK_INDEX < TASK_COUNT )) || {
  echo 'Cloud Run task index/count outside 1..32' >&2; exit 2;
}

json_to_nul() {
  node -e '
const value = JSON.parse(process.argv[1])
if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.some((item) => typeof item !== "string")) process.exit(2)
for (const item of value) process.stdout.write(item + "\0")
' "$1"
}

load_json_array() {
  local json="$1" target="$2" file
  file="$(mktemp)"
  if ! json_to_nul "$json" >"$file"; then rm -f "$file"; return 2; fi
  local -n output="$target"
  mapfile -d '' -t output <"$file"
  rm -f "$file"
}

load_suites() {
  local json="$1" target="$2" file
  file="$(mktemp)"
  if ! node -e '
const value = JSON.parse(process.argv[1])
if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length < 1 || value.length > 3) process.exit(2)
const names = new Set()
for (const item of value) {
  if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item) ||
      JSON.stringify(Object.keys(item).sort()) !== JSON.stringify(["configPath","name"]) ||
      !["client","server","electron"].includes(item.name) || names.has(item.name) ||
      typeof item.configPath !== "string" || !item.configPath.startsWith("config/vitest/")) process.exit(2)
  names.add(item.name)
  process.stdout.write(item.name + "\0" + item.configPath + "\0")
}
' "$json" >"$file"; then rm -f "$file"; return 2; fi
  local -n output="$target"
  mapfile -d '' -t output <"$file"
  rm -f "$file"
}

run_mediated() {
  "$@" \
    > >(sed -u 's/^/[cloud-child stdout] /') \
    2> >(sed -u 's/^/[cloud-child stderr] /' >&2)
}

part_id() {
  local kind="$1" suite="${2:-}"
  if [ "$kind" = vitest ]; then
    printf '%s:%s/%s\n' "$suite" "$((TASK_INDEX + 1))" "$TASK_COUNT"
  else
    printf 'e2e:%s/%s\n' "$((TASK_INDEX + 1))" "$TASK_COUNT"
  fi
}

emit_result() {
  local id="$1" status="$2" exit_code="$3" test_count="${4:-}"
  node - "$KIND" "$RUN_ID" "$EXECUTION" "$id" "$TASK_INDEX" \
    "$TASK_COUNT" "$status" "$exit_code" "$test_count" <<'NODE'
const [kind, runId, execution, partId, taskIndex, taskCount, status, exitCode, count] =
  process.argv.slice(2)
const value = {
  marker: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT',
  schemaVersion: 1,
  kind,
  runId,
  execution,
  partId,
  taskIndex: Number(taskIndex),
  taskCount: Number(taskCount),
  taskAttempt: 0,
  status,
  exitCode: Number(exitCode),
  ...(kind === 'vitest' ? { testCount: Number(count) } : {}),
}
process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`)
NODE
}

run_vitest() {
  local -a suite_fields=() extra_args=() shard_arg=()
  load_suites "${VITEST_SUITES_JSON:-}" suite_fields || exit 2
  load_json_array "${VITEST_ARGS_JSON:-[]}" extra_args || exit 2
  if (( TASK_COUNT > 1 )); then shard_arg=("--shard=$((TASK_INDEX + 1))/$TASK_COUNT"); fi
  local overall=0 index name config report code count status
  for ((index = 0; index < ${#suite_fields[@]}; index += 2)); do
    name="${suite_fields[index]}"
    config="${suite_fields[index + 1]}"
    report="$(mktemp)"
    set +e
    run_mediated npx vitest run --config "$config" "${shard_arg[@]}" \
      --reporter=json --outputFile="$report" "${extra_args[@]}"
    code=$?
    set -e
    count="$(node -e '
const fs = require("node:fs")
let value
try { value = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[1], "utf8")) } catch { process.exit(86) }
if (!Number.isInteger(value.numTotalTests) || value.numTotalTests <= 0) process.exit(86)
process.stdout.write(String(value.numTotalTests))
' "$report")" || code=86
    rm -f "$report"
    if (( code == 0 )); then status=passed; else status=failed; overall=$code; fi
    emit_result "$(part_id vitest "$name")" "$status" "$code" "${count:-0}"
  done
  exit "$overall"
}

finish_e2e() {
  local code="$1" status=passed
  (( code == 0 )) || status=failed
  emit_result "$(part_id e2e)" "$status" "$code"
  exit "$code"
}

playwright_list() {
  local output code
  set +e
  output="$(npx playwright test --config test/e2e-browser/playwright.cloud.config.ts \
    --list "$@" 2> >(sed -u 's/^/[cloud-child stderr] /' >&2))"
  code=$?
  set -e
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    printf '[cloud-child stdout] %s\n' "$line" >&2
  done <<<"$output"
  (( code == 0 )) || return "$code"
  printf '%s' "$output"
}

run_e2e() {
  local -a args=() flags=() specs=()
  load_json_array "${PLAYWRIGHT_ARGS_JSON:-[]}" args || exit 2
  local dry=false arg
  for arg in "${args[@]}"; do
    case "$arg" in
      --dry-run) dry=true ;;
      --shard|--shard=*) echo 'wrapper owns Playwright sharding' >&2; exit 2 ;;
      -*) flags+=("$arg") ;;
      *) specs+=("$arg") ;;
    esac
  done
  if (( TASK_COUNT == 1 )); then
    if $dry; then
      printf 'Dry-run: single task (no sharding); no tests executed\n'
      printf 'Would run all tests with flags:'
      printf ' %q' "${flags[@]}" "${specs[@]}"
      printf '\n'
      exit 0
    fi
    set +e
    run_mediated npx playwright test \
      --config test/e2e-browser/playwright.cloud.config.ts "${flags[@]}" "${specs[@]}"
    local code=$?
    set -e
    finish_e2e "$code"
  fi

  local listed='' list_code
  listed="$(playwright_list "${flags[@]}" "${specs[@]}")" || {
    list_code=$?
    printf 'Playwright inventory failed (exit %s); refusing to widen scope\n' \
      "$list_code" >&2
    if $dry; then exit "$list_code"; else finish_e2e "$list_code"; fi
  }
  local -a names=()
  mapfile -t names < <(printf '%s\n' "$listed" |
    sed -n 's/.*› \([^:]*\.spec\.ts\):.*/\1/p' | LC_ALL=C sort -u)
  if (( ${#names[@]} == 0 )); then
    printf 'Playwright inventory matched zero spec files; refusing to widen scope\n' >&2
    if $dry; then exit 86; else finish_e2e 86; fi
  fi
  local duration_file=docker/cloud-run/test-durations.txt default_duration=30
  declare -A durations=()
  if [ -f "$duration_file" ]; then
    local duration_name duration_seconds
    while IFS=: read -r duration_name duration_seconds; do
      duration_name="${duration_name//[[:space:]]/}"
      duration_seconds="${duration_seconds//[[:space:]]/}"
      [[ "$duration_name" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\.spec\.ts$ &&
         "$duration_seconds" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || continue
      durations["$duration_name"]="$duration_seconds"
    done <"$duration_file"
  fi
  local -a totals=() assigned=()
  local i
  for ((i = 0; i < TASK_COUNT; i += 1)); do totals[i]=0; assigned[i]=''; done
  local pairs='' name seconds target minimum
  for name in "${names[@]}"; do
    seconds="${durations[$name]:-$default_duration}"
    pairs+="$seconds $name"$'\n'
  done
  while read -r seconds name; do
    [ -n "$name" ] || continue
    target=0; minimum="${totals[0]}"
    for ((i = 1; i < TASK_COUNT; i += 1)); do
      if (( totals[i] < minimum )); then target=$i; minimum="${totals[i]}"; fi
    done
    totals[target]=$((minimum + seconds))
    assigned[target]+="$name"$'\n'
  done < <(printf '%s' "$pairs" | LC_ALL=C sort -rn)
  if $dry; then
    for ((i = 0; i < TASK_COUNT; i += 1)); do
      printf 'Shard %s/%s:\n' "$((i + 1))" "$TASK_COUNT"
      printf '%s' "${assigned[i]}"
    done
    printf 'Dry-run complete; no tests executed\n'
    exit 0
  fi
  local -a selected=()
  while IFS= read -r name; do
    [ -n "$name" ] && selected+=("test/e2e-browser/specs/$name")
  done <<<"${assigned[TASK_INDEX]}"
  (( ${#selected[@]} > 0 )) || finish_e2e 0
  set +e
  run_mediated npx playwright test \
    --config test/e2e-browser/playwright.cloud.config.ts "${flags[@]}" "${selected[@]}"
  local code=$?
  set -e
  finish_e2e "$code"
}

if [ "$KIND" = vitest ]; then run_vitest; else run_e2e; fi
```

The cumulative Task 9 test import/fixture block is complete and reuses the one
concrete provider from Task 7:

```ts
import { beforeAll, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { z } from 'zod'

import type {
  CloudInvocationOrigin,
  CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  CommandResult,
  RunCommand,
} from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
import type {
  ParsedVitestArgs,
} from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/vitest.js'
import {
  classifyCommand,
  type CloudEligibility,
  type CloudPrePhase,
  type CommandDisposition,
  type CommandKey,
} from '../../../../scripts/testing/coordinator-command-matrix.js'
import * as matrixModule from '../../../../scripts/testing/coordinator-command-matrix.js'
import { successfulLifecycleRunner } from './cloud-run-test-helpers.js'

type ParseVitestArgs = (
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
) => ParsedVitestArgs
type CreateVitestLifecycleInput = (
  parsed: ParsedVitestArgs,
  repoRoot: string,
  origin: CloudInvocationOrigin,
) => CloudRunLifecycleInput
type RunVitest = (
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  runCommand?: RunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  originOverride?: CloudInvocationOrigin,
) => Promise<number>
type RunCloudPrePhases = (
  phases: readonly CloudPrePhase[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
) => Promise<CloudPrePhase[]>
type RunCoordinatorCloudVitest = (
  parsed: { commandKey: CommandKey; forwardedArgs: readonly string[] },
  eligibility: Extract<CloudEligibility, { kind: 'cloud' }>,
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  runCommand?: RunCommand,
) => Promise<number>
type ClassifyCloudEligibility = (input: {
  commandKey: CommandKey
  argv: readonly string[]
  disposition: Exclude<CommandDisposition, { kind: 'rejected' }>
}) => CloudEligibility
type PrePhasesForCloudCommand = (
  commandKey: 'test' | 'test:all' | 'check' | 'verify',
) => readonly CloudPrePhase[]

function requiredMatrixFunction<T extends (...args: never[]) => unknown>(
  name: string,
): T {
  const candidate = (matrixModule as Record<string, unknown>)[name]
  expect(candidate, `${name} classifier behavior must be installed`).toBeTypeOf('function')
  return candidate as T
}

const classifyCloudEligibility: ClassifyCloudEligibility = (...args) =>
  requiredMatrixFunction<ClassifyCloudEligibility>('classifyCloudEligibility')(...args)
const prePhasesForCloudCommand: PrePhasesForCloudCommand = (...args) =>
  requiredMatrixFunction<PrePhasesForCloudCommand>('prePhasesForCloudCommand')(...args)

let parseVitestArgs: ParseVitestArgs
let createVitestLifecycleInput: CreateVitestLifecycleInput
let runVitest: RunVitest
let runCloudPrePhases: RunCloudPrePhases
let runCoordinatorCloudVitest: RunCoordinatorCloudVitest
beforeAll(async () => {
  const modulePath = '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/vitest.js'
  const module = await import(modulePath).catch(() => ({})) as Record<string, unknown>
  for (const name of [
    'parseVitestArgs',
    'createVitestLifecycleInput',
    'runVitest',
    'runCloudPrePhases',
    'runCoordinatorCloudVitest',
  ] as const) {
    expect(module[name], `${name} public behavior must be installed`).toBeTypeOf('function')
  }
  parseVitestArgs = module.parseVitestArgs as ParseVitestArgs
  createVitestLifecycleInput = module.createVitestLifecycleInput as CreateVitestLifecycleInput
  runVitest = module.runVitest as RunVitest
  runCloudPrePhases = module.runCloudPrePhases as RunCloudPrePhases
  runCoordinatorCloudVitest = module.runCoordinatorCloudVitest as RunCoordinatorCloudVitest
})

const ok = (): CommandResult => ({
  stdout: '', stderr: '', exitCode: 0, signal: null, timedOut: false,
})
const CLOUD_ENV: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {
  FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND: 'cloud',
  FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT: 'caller@example.com',
  FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT: 'test-project',
  FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE: 'organizations/123456789',
  FRESHELL_GCP_SOURCE_BUCKET: 'test-source-bucket',
  FRESHELL_GCP_REPO: 'freshell-e2e',
  FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
    'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
    'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
}
const FULL_HEAD = `e${'a'.repeat(39)}`
const cloudPreflightReceiptSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  head: z.string(),
  iamAnalysisScope: z.string().regex(/^organizations\/[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  buildRegion: z.literal('global'),
  runRegion: z.string(),
  sourceBucket: z.string(),
  buildServiceAccount: z.string(),
  runtimeServiceAccount: z.string(),
  buildTemplate: z.object({
    options: z.object({
      machineType: z.literal('E2_HIGHCPU_32'),
      logging: z.literal('CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY'),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).passthrough(),
  tempRemoved: z.literal(true),
}).passthrough()

function commandTranscript(runCommand: RunCommand): string {
  return (runCommand as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls
    .map(([command, argv]) => `${command} ${argv.join(' ')}`)
    .join('\n')
}

function cloudEligibilityFor(
  command: 'test' | 'test:all' | 'check' | 'verify',
): Extract<CloudEligibility, { kind: 'cloud' }> {
  const surface: CloudInvocationOrigin['surface'] = command === 'test:all'
    ? 'coordinator-test-all'
    : `coordinator-${command}` as CloudInvocationOrigin['surface']
  return { kind: 'cloud', origin: { surface, argv: [] } }
}

function onePrefixedJsonFromMock<T>(
  spy: { mock: { calls: readonly (readonly unknown[])[] } },
  prefix: string,
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
): T {
  const lines = spy.mock.calls.flatMap(([value]) => String(value).split('\n'))
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith(prefix))
  if (lines.length !== 1) throw new Error(`Expected one ${prefix.trim()} line`)
  return schema.parse(JSON.parse(lines[0].slice(prefix.length)))
}

function preflightRunner(): RunCommand {
  return successfulLifecycleRunner('e')
}

it.each([
  ['test', ['--help'], 'passthrough'],
  ['test', ['--version'], 'passthrough'],
  ['test', ['--config=x'], 'rejected'],
  ['test', ['--reporter=dot'], 'rejected'],
  ['test', ['-t', 'focused'], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test', ['test/unit/a.test.ts'], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test', ['test/unit/a.test.ts', 'test/server/b.test.ts'], 'rejected'],
  ['test', ['--watch'], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test', ['--ui'], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test', ['--changed', 'origin/main'], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test:server', [], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test:server', ['--run'], 'cloud-ineligible'],
  ['test', [], 'cloud'],
  ['test:all', [], 'cloud'],
  ['check', [], 'cloud'],
  ['verify', [], 'cloud'],
] as const)('%s %j preserves classifier then yields %s', (commandKey, argv, route) => {
  const disposition = classifyCommand({ commandKey, forwardedArgs: [...argv] })
  const actual = disposition.kind === 'rejected'
    ? 'rejected'
    : classifyCloudEligibility({ commandKey, argv, disposition }).kind
  expect(actual).toBe(route)
})

it('runs exact check and verify pre-phases through inherited RunCommand', async () => {
  expect(prePhasesForCloudCommand('check')).toEqual(['typecheck'])
  expect(prePhasesForCloudCommand('verify')).toEqual(['typecheck', 'build'])
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockResolvedValue(ok())
  await runCloudPrePhases(['typecheck', 'build'], process.env, new AbortController().signal, runner)
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'npm', ['run', 'typecheck'],
    expect.objectContaining({ output: { kind: 'inherit' } }))
  expect(runner).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, 'npm', ['run', 'build'],
    expect.objectContaining({ output: { kind: 'inherit' } }))
})

it('prints an exact origin-aware local command and never executes it', async () => {
  const runner = vi.fn<RunCommand>().mockRejectedValue(new Error('cloud failed'))
  const stderr = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write').mockImplementation(() => true)
  expect(await runCoordinatorCloudVitest(
    { commandKey: 'check', forwardedArgs: [] },
    cloudEligibilityFor('check'),
    CLOUD_ENV,
    runner,
  )).toBe(1)
  expect(stderr).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
    'Cloud Vitest failed. Run locally with: FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=local npm run check\n',
  )
  expect(commandTranscript(runner)).not.toMatch(/vitest|test:balanced|electron/)
})

it('runs preflight from the extracted captured tree and performs no mutation', async () => {
  const runner = preflightRunner()
  const stdout = vi.spyOn(process.stdout, 'write').mockImplementation(() => true)
  await expect(runVitest([
    'run', '--cloud', '--preflight-only',
  ], CLOUD_ENV, runner)).resolves.toBe(0)
  const record = onePrefixedJsonFromMock(
    stdout,
    'FRESHELL_CLOUD_PREFLIGHT ',
    cloudPreflightReceiptSchema,
  )
  expect(record).toMatchObject({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    head: FULL_HEAD,
    iamAnalysisScope: CLOUD_ENV.FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE,
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: 'us-west1',
    sourceBucket: 'test-source-bucket',
    buildServiceAccount: CLOUD_ENV.FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT,
    runtimeServiceAccount: CLOUD_ENV.FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT,
    tempRemoved: true,
  })
  expect(record.buildTemplate.options).toMatchObject({
    machineType: 'E2_HIGHCPU_32', logging: 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY',
  })
  for (const invalidScope of [
    undefined,
    'organizations/0',
    'projects/123',
    'not-a-scope',
  ] as const) {
    const invalidRecord: Record<string, unknown> = { ...record }
    if (invalidScope === undefined) delete invalidRecord.iamAnalysisScope
    else invalidRecord.iamAnalysisScope = invalidScope
    expect(() => cloudPreflightReceiptSchema.parse(invalidRecord)).toThrow()
  }
  expect(commandTranscript(runner)).not.toMatch(
    /storage cp|builds submit|docker push|run jobs create|"kind":"run-job"/,
  )
  stdout.mockRestore()
})
```

Append the integration case to the existing coordinator fixture suite. Its
cumulative imports and every local helper consumed by the new test remain the
following concrete code (unchanged except formatting to the repository style):

```ts
import { execFile, spawn, type ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams } from 'node:child_process'
import { once } from 'node:events'
import fs from 'node:fs'
import fsp from 'node:fs/promises'
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'
import os from 'node:os'
import path from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import { promisify } from 'node:util'

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import {
  buildCoordinatorEndpoint,
  tryListen,
} from '../../../scripts/testing/coordinator-endpoint.js'
import {
  buildReusableSuccessKey,
  type HolderRecord,
  type LatestRunRecord,
  type ReusableSuccessRecord,
} from '../../../scripts/testing/coordinator-schema.js'
import {
  getCoordinatorStoreDir,
  readCommandRuns,
  readHolder,
  readReusableSuccesses,
  readSuiteRuns,
  recordCommandResult,
  recordReusableSuccess,
  recordSuiteResult,
} from '../../../scripts/testing/coordinator-store.js'

const execFileAsync = promisify(execFile)
const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename)
const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../..')
const require = createRequire(import.meta.url)
const TSX_CLI = require.resolve('tsx/cli')
const COORDINATOR_SCRIPT = path.join(
  REPO_ROOT, 'scripts', 'testing', 'test-coordinator.ts',
)
const FAKE_UPSTREAM = path.join(
  REPO_ROOT, 'test', 'fixtures', 'testing', 'fake-coordinated-workload.mjs',
)
const itRequiresObservableForeignListener = process.platform === 'win32'
  ? it.skip
  : it

type CoordinatorHandle = {
  child: ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams
  stdout: string
  stderr: string
  output: string
}

type RepoFixture = {
  baseDir: string
  repoRoot: string
  checkoutRoot: string
  worktreePath: string
  commonDir: string
  storeDir: string
  headCommit: string
  branch: string
  cleanup: () => Promise<void>
  markDirty: () => Promise<void>
  cleanWorktree: () => Promise<void>
}

const activeChildren: CoordinatorHandle[] = []
const activeFixtureDirs: string[] = []

function delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms))
}

async function signalChildAndWait(
  handle: CoordinatorHandle,
  signal: NodeJS.Signals,
  timeoutMs: number,
): Promise<void> {
  if (handle.child.exitCode !== null) return
  const exited = once(handle.child, 'exit').catch(() => undefined)
  handle.child.kill(signal)
  await Promise.race([exited, delay(timeoutMs)])
}

async function stopChild(handle: CoordinatorHandle): Promise<void> {
  const index = activeChildren.indexOf(handle)
  if (index >= 0) activeChildren.splice(index, 1)
  if (handle.child.exitCode !== null) return
  await signalChildAndWait(handle, 'SIGTERM', 3_000)
  if (handle.child.exitCode === null) {
    await signalChildAndWait(handle, 'SIGKILL', 3_000)
  }
}

beforeEach(() => {
  activeChildren.length = 0
  activeFixtureDirs.length = 0
})

afterEach(async () => {
  await Promise.all(activeChildren.map((handle) => stopChild(handle)))
  await Promise.all(activeFixtureDirs.map((dir) =>
    fsp.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => undefined)))
})

function createChildEnv(
  cwd: string,
  overrides: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {},
): NodeJS.ProcessEnv {
  const env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = { ...process.env }
  for (const key of [
    'FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_ACTIVE',
    'VITEST',
    'VITEST_POOL_ID',
    'VITEST_WORKER_ID',
    'PW_TEST_LOGS_DIR',
  ]) delete env[key]
  return {
    ...env,
    PWD: cwd,
    INIT_CWD: cwd,
    FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_FAKE_UPSTREAM: FAKE_UPSTREAM,
    FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_REPO_ROOT: REPO_ROOT,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

function spawnCoordinator(
  cwd: string,
  commandKey: string,
  forwardedArgs: string[] = [],
  envOverrides: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {},
): CoordinatorHandle {
  const args = [TSX_CLI, COORDINATOR_SCRIPT, 'run', commandKey]
  if (forwardedArgs.length > 0) args.push('--', ...forwardedArgs)
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, args, {
    cwd,
    env: createChildEnv(cwd, envOverrides),
    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
  })
  const handle: CoordinatorHandle = { child, stdout: '', stderr: '', output: '' }
  child.stdout.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
    handle.stdout += chunk.toString()
    handle.output = `${handle.stdout}${handle.stderr}`
  })
  child.stderr.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
    handle.stderr += chunk.toString()
    handle.output = `${handle.stdout}${handle.stderr}`
  })
  activeChildren.push(handle)
  return handle
}

async function waitForOutput(
  handle: CoordinatorHandle,
  pattern: RegExp,
  timeoutMs = 5_000,
): Promise<string> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    if (pattern.test(handle.output)) return handle.output
    if (handle.child.exitCode !== null) break
    await delay(50)
  }
  throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for output ${pattern}. Output:\n${handle.output}`)
}

async function waitForExit(
  handle: CoordinatorHandle,
  timeoutMs = 10_000,
): Promise<{ code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null; output: string }> {
  if (handle.child.exitCode !== null) {
    return {
      code: handle.child.exitCode,
      signal: handle.child.signalCode,
      output: handle.output,
    }
  }
  const result = await Promise.race([
    once(handle.child, 'exit').then(([code, signal]) => ({
      code: code as number | null,
      signal: signal as NodeJS.Signals | null,
    })),
    delay(timeoutMs).then(() => {
      throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for process exit. Output:\n${handle.output}`)
    }),
  ])
  return { ...result, output: handle.output }
}

async function runGit(args: string[], cwd: string): Promise<void> {
  await execFileAsync('git', args, { cwd })
}

async function initRepo(repoDir: string, branchName: string): Promise<void> {
  await fsp.mkdir(repoDir, { recursive: true })
  await runGit(['init'], repoDir)
  await runGit(['config', 'user.email', 'test@example.com'], repoDir)
  await runGit(['config', 'user.name', 'Freshell Test'], repoDir)
  await fsp.writeFile(path.join(repoDir, 'README.md'), '# test\n')
  await runGit(['add', 'README.md'], repoDir)
  await runGit(['commit', '-m', 'init'], repoDir)
  await runGit(['checkout', '-B', branchName], repoDir)
}

async function createRepoFixture(
  options: { linkedWorktree?: boolean; dirty?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<RepoFixture> {
  const linkedWorktree = options.linkedWorktree ?? true
  const baseDir = fs.realpathSync(await fsp.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'fcf-')))
  activeFixtureDirs.push(baseDir)
  const repoRoot = path.join(baseDir, 'repo')
  const worktreePath = linkedWorktree ? path.join(baseDir, 'repo-feature') : repoRoot
  const dirtyFile = path.join(worktreePath, 'dirty.txt')
  await initRepo(repoRoot, 'main')
  if (linkedWorktree) {
    await runGit(['worktree', 'add', '-b', 'feature/worktree', worktreePath], repoRoot)
  }
  if (options.dirty) await fsp.writeFile(dirtyFile, 'dirty\n')
  const headCommit = (
    await execFileAsync('git', ['-C', worktreePath, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'])
  ).stdout.trim()
  const branch = (
    await execFileAsync('git', ['-C', worktreePath, 'rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])
  ).stdout.trim()
  const commonDir = path.join(repoRoot, '.git')
  return {
    baseDir,
    repoRoot,
    checkoutRoot: worktreePath,
    worktreePath,
    commonDir,
    storeDir: getCoordinatorStoreDir(commonDir),
    headCommit,
    branch,
    cleanup: () => fsp.rm(baseDir, { recursive: true, force: true }),
    markDirty: () => fsp.writeFile(dirtyFile, 'dirty\n'),
    cleanWorktree: async () => {
      await execFileAsync('git', ['-C', worktreePath, 'reset', '--hard', 'HEAD'])
      await execFileAsync('git', ['-C', worktreePath, 'clean', '-fd'])
    },
  }
}
```

```ts
it('routes cloud before a held coordinator endpoint while local broad work queues', async () => {
  const fixture = await createRepoFixture()
  const holder = spawnCoordinator(fixture.worktreePath, 'test', [], {
    FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND: 'local',
    FRESHELL_TEST_SUMMARY: 'held local broad run',
    FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_FAKE_BEHAVIOR: JSON.stringify({
      default: { holdMs: 30_000, exitCode: 0 },
    }),
  })
  await waitForOutput(holder, /Starting coordinated test run/i, 15_000)
  const before = await readHolder(fixture.storeDir)
  expect(before?.summary).toContain('held local broad run')

  const cloud = spawnCoordinator(fixture.worktreePath, 'test', [], {
    FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND: 'cloud',
    FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT: 'caller@example.com',
    FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT: 'test-project',
    FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE: 'organizations/123456789',
    FRESHELL_GCP_SOURCE_BUCKET: 'test-source-bucket',

    FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
      'same-worker@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
    FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
      'same-worker@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  })
  const cloudExit = await waitForExit(cloud, 15_000)
  expect(cloudExit.code).not.toBe(0)
  expect(cloudExit.output).toContain(
    'FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=local npm test',
  )
  expect(cloudExit.output).not.toMatch(/queued intentionally|test:balanced|vitest\.mjs/)
  expect((await readHolder(fixture.storeDir))?.runId).toBe(before?.runId)

  const queued = spawnCoordinator(fixture.worktreePath, 'test', [], {
    FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND: 'local',
    FRESHELL_TEST_SUMMARY: 'second local broad run',
  })
  await waitForOutput(queued, /queued intentionally/i, 15_000)
})
```

```ts
export function shellQuote(value: string): string {
  if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_./:@%+=,-]+$/.test(value)) return value
  return `'${value.replaceAll("'", `'"'"'`)}'`
}

export function shellJoin(argv: readonly string[]): string {
  return argv.map(shellQuote).join(' ')
}

export const MIN_CLOUD_TASK_COUNT = 1
export const MAX_CLOUD_TASK_COUNT = 32

export function parseCloudTaskCount(value: string, label = 'task count'): number {
  if (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(value)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} must be an integer from 1 through 32`)
  }
  const count = Number(value)
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(count) || count < MIN_CLOUD_TASK_COUNT ||
      count > MAX_CLOUD_TASK_COUNT) {
    throw new Error(`${label} must be an integer from 1 through 32`)
  }
  return count
}
```

Create `scripts/cloud-run/vitest.ts` with this cumulative import block and the
complete parser/defaults. No caller-supplied run ID or shard/result ownership is
accepted:

```ts
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'

import {
  canonicalizeTaskTimeout,
  defaultRunCommand,
  lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout,
  readExactExecutionLogs,
  runCloudPreflight,
  runCloudRunLifecycle,
  superviseCatchableSignals,
  type CloudInvocationOrigin,
  type CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  type RunCommand,
} from './lifecycle.js'
import { parseCloudTaskCount, shellJoin } from './argv.js'
import {
  STANDARD_VITEST_SUITES,
  selectStandardVitestSuites,
  type StandardVitestSuite,
  type StandardVitestSuiteSelector,
} from '../testing/vitest-suites.js'
import {
  prePhasesForCloudCommand,
  renderCoordinatorLocalCommand,
  type CloudEligibility,
  type CloudPrePhase,
  type CommandKey,
} from '../testing/coordinator-command-matrix.js'

export const VITEST_DEFAULT_TASK_COUNT = 4

export function resolveVitestBackend(value: string | undefined): 'local' | 'cloud' {
  if (value === undefined || value === '' || value === 'local') return 'local'
  if (value === 'cloud') return 'cloud'
  throw new Error(
    `Invalid FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND ${JSON.stringify(value)}; expected local or cloud`,
  )
}

export type ParsedVitestArgs = {
  subcommand: 'run' | 'build' | 'push' | 'logs' | 'help'
  backend: 'local' | 'cloud'
  operation: 'run' | 'build'
  buildMode: 'cloud' | 'local'
  selector: StandardVitestSuiteSelector
  taskCount: number
  taskTimeout: string
  account?: string
  projectId?: string
  runRegion?: string
  repository?: string
  jobPrefix?: string
  logJobName?: string
  logExecutionName?: string
  preflightOnly: boolean
  forwardedArgs: string[]
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv
}

const vitestSubcommands = new Set(['run', 'build', 'push', 'logs', 'help'])
const selectorValues = new Set(['default', 'server', 'electron', 'all'])

function optionValue(argv: readonly string[], index: number, name: string): {
  value: string
  consumed: number
} | undefined {
  const token = argv[index]
  if (token.startsWith(`${name}=`)) return {
    value: token.slice(name.length + 1), consumed: 1,
  }
  if (token === name) {
    const value = argv[index + 1]
    if (value === undefined) throw new Error(`Missing value for ${name}`)
    return { value, consumed: 2 }
  }
  return undefined
}

export function parseVitestArgs(
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
): ParsedVitestArgs {
  const tokens = [...argv]
  let subcommand: ParsedVitestArgs['subcommand'] = 'run'
  if (tokens[0] && vitestSubcommands.has(tokens[0])) {
    subcommand = tokens.shift() as ParsedVitestArgs['subcommand']
  } else if (tokens[0] === '--help' || tokens[0] === '-h') {
    subcommand = 'help'
    tokens.shift()
  }
  let backend = resolveVitestBackend(env.FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND)
  let explicitBackend: 'local' | 'cloud' | undefined
  let selector: StandardVitestSuiteSelector = 'all'
  let taskCount = VITEST_DEFAULT_TASK_COUNT
  let taskTimeout = canonicalizeTaskTimeout('30m')
  let account: string | undefined
  let projectId: string | undefined
  let runRegion: string | undefined
  let repository: string | undefined
  let jobPrefix: string | undefined
  let logJobName: string | undefined
  let logExecutionName: string | undefined
  let preflightOnly = false
  let buildMode: 'cloud' | 'local' = subcommand === 'push' ? 'local' : 'cloud'
  const forwardedArgs: string[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < tokens.length;) {
    const token = tokens[index]
    if (token === '--local' || token === '--cloud') {
      const next = token === '--local' ? 'local' : 'cloud'
      if (explicitBackend && explicitBackend !== next) {
        throw new Error('--local and --cloud are mutually exclusive')
      }
      explicitBackend = next
      backend = next
      index += 1
      continue
    }
    if (token === '--preflight-only') {
      preflightOnly = true
      backend = 'cloud'
      index += 1
      continue
    }
    if (token === '--local-build') {
      buildMode = 'local'
      index += 1
      continue
    }
    const options = [
      ['--config', (value: string) => {
        if (!selectorValues.has(value)) throw new Error('Invalid Vitest suite selector')
        selector = value as StandardVitestSuiteSelector
      }],
      ['--shards', (value: string) => { taskCount = parseCloudTaskCount(value, 'Vitest shards') }],
      ['--timeout', (value: string) => {
        taskTimeout = canonicalizeTaskTimeout(value)
      }],
      ['--account', (value: string) => { account = value }],
      ['--project-id', (value: string) => { projectId = value }],
      ['--region', (value: string) => { runRegion = value }],
      ['--repository', (value: string) => { repository = value }],
      ['--job-prefix', (value: string) => { jobPrefix = value }],
      ['--job', (value: string) => { logJobName = value }],
      ['--execution', (value: string) => { logExecutionName = value }],
    ] as const
    let matched = false
    for (const [name, assign] of options) {
      const parsed = optionValue(tokens, index, name)
      if (!parsed) continue
      assign(parsed.value)
      index += parsed.consumed
      matched = true
      break
    }
    if (matched) continue
    forwardedArgs.push(token)
    index += 1
  }

  if (['build', 'push', 'logs'].includes(subcommand)) backend = 'cloud'
  if (subcommand === 'logs' && (!logJobName || !logExecutionName)) {
    throw new Error('logs requires exact --job and --execution')
  }
  if (subcommand !== 'logs' && (logJobName || logExecutionName)) {
    throw new Error('--job/--execution are valid only for logs')
  }
  if (preflightOnly && subcommand !== 'run') {
    throw new Error('--preflight-only is valid only for run')
  }
  if (backend === 'cloud') {
    const forbidden = forwardedArgs.find((token) =>
      token === '--passWithNoTests' || token === '--shard' ||
      token.startsWith('--shard=') || token === '--reporter' ||
      token.startsWith('--reporter=') || token === '--outputFile' ||
      token.startsWith('--outputFile='))
    if (forbidden) throw new Error(`Cloud wrapper owns ${forbidden}`)
  }
  const operation = subcommand === 'run' ? 'run' : 'build'
  return {
    subcommand,
    backend,
    operation,
    buildMode,
    selector,
    taskCount,
    taskTimeout,
    account,
    projectId,
    runRegion,
    repository,
    jobPrefix,
    logJobName,
    logExecutionName,
    preflightOnly,
    forwardedArgs,
    env: { ...env },
  }
}

function required(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv, key: string): string {
  const value = env[key]
  if (!value) throw new Error(`Missing required cloud environment ${key}`)
  return value
}

export function expectedVitestParts(
  suites: readonly StandardVitestSuite[],
  taskCount: number,
): string[] {
  return suites.flatMap((suite) =>
    Array.from(
      { length: taskCount },
      (_, taskIndex) => `${suite.name}:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`,
    ),
  )
}

export function createVitestLifecycleInput(
  parsed: ParsedVitestArgs,
  repoRoot: string,
  origin: CloudInvocationOrigin,
): CloudRunLifecycleInput {
  const suites = selectStandardVitestSuites(parsed.selector)
  return {
    origin,
    kind: 'vitest',
    repoRoot,
    account: parsed.account ?? required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT'),
    projectId: parsed.projectId ?? required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT'),
    iamAnalysisScope: required(
      parsed.env,
      'FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE',
    ),
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: parsed.runRegion ?? parsed.env.FRESHELL_GCP_REGION ?? 'us-west1',
    sourceBucket: required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_SOURCE_BUCKET'),
    repository: parsed.repository ?? required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_REPO'),
    imageName: 'freshell-vitest',
    jobPrefix: parsed.jobPrefix ?? parsed.env.FRESHELL_GCP_VITEST_JOB ?? 'freshell-vitest',
    buildServiceAccount: required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT'),
    runtimeServiceAccount: required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT'),
    buildMode: parsed.buildMode,
    operation: parsed.operation,
    taskCount: parsed.taskCount,
    taskTimeout: parsed.taskTimeout,
    memory: '4Gi',
    cpu: 4,
    workloadEnv: {
      VITEST_SUITES_JSON: JSON.stringify(suites.map(({ name, configPath }) => ({ name, configPath }))),
      VITEST_ARGS_JSON: JSON.stringify(parsed.forwardedArgs),
    },
    expectedParts: expectedVitestParts(suites, parsed.taskCount),
    budgets: lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout(parsed.taskTimeout),
  }
}

function vitestSurface(
  subcommand: 'run' | 'build' | 'push',
): Extract<CloudInvocationOrigin['surface'], `vitest-${string}`> {
  if (subcommand === 'run') return 'vitest-run'
  if (subcommand === 'build') return 'vitest-build'
  return 'vitest-push'
}

function directLocalFallback(parsed: ParsedVitestArgs): string {
  return shellJoin([
    'FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=local',
    'bash', 'scripts/vitest-cloud.sh', 'run', '--local',
    `--config=${parsed.selector}`,
    ...parsed.forwardedArgs,
  ])
}

async function runLocalVitest(
  parsed: ParsedVitestArgs,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  let exitCode = 0
  for (const suite of selectStandardVitestSuites(parsed.selector)) {
    const result = await runCommand('npx', [
      'vitest', 'run', '--passWithNoTests',
      '--config', suite.configPath, ...parsed.forwardedArgs,
    ], {
      cwd: process.cwd(),
      env: parsed.env,
      signal,
      output: { kind: 'inherit' },
      timeoutMs: parsed.env.CI ? 60 * 60_000 : 24 * 60 * 60_000,
      terminateGraceMs: 5_000,
      settlementTimeoutMs: 30_000,
    })
    if (result.exitCode !== 0 || result.signal !== null || result.timedOut) exitCode = 1
  }
  return exitCode
}

async function runVitestLogs(
  parsed: ParsedVitestArgs,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  const suites = selectStandardVitestSuites(parsed.selector)
  const rows = await readExactExecutionLogs({
    account: parsed.account ?? required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT'),
    projectId: parsed.projectId ?? required(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT'),
    runRegion: parsed.runRegion ?? parsed.env.FRESHELL_GCP_REGION ?? 'us-west1',
    jobName: parsed.logJobName!,
    executionName: parsed.logExecutionName!,
    limit: expectedVitestParts(suites, parsed.taskCount).length + 1,
    budgets: lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout(parsed.taskTimeout),
    signal,
  }, runCommand)
  for (const row of rows) process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(row)}\n`)
  return 0
}

function printVitestHelp(): void {
  process.stdout.write(
    'Usage: scripts/vitest-cloud.sh [run|build|push|logs|help] ' +
    '[--local|--cloud] [--config=default|server|electron|all] ' +
    '[--shards=1..32] [--timeout=30m] [vitest args...]\n',
  )
}

export async function runVitest(
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
  originOverride?: CloudInvocationOrigin,
): Promise<number> {
  const parsed = parseVitestArgs(argv, env)
  if (parsed.subcommand === 'help') { printVitestHelp(); return 0 }
  if (parsed.subcommand === 'logs') return runVitestLogs(parsed, runCommand, signal)
  if (parsed.backend === 'local') {
    if (parsed.subcommand !== 'run') throw new Error('Build and push require cloud configuration')
    return runLocalVitest(parsed, runCommand, signal)
  }
  const origin = originOverride ?? {
    surface: vitestSurface(parsed.subcommand),
    argv: [...parsed.forwardedArgs],
  }
  const input = {
    ...createVitestLifecycleInput(parsed, process.cwd(), origin),
    signal,
  }
  try {
    if (parsed.preflightOnly) {
      const receipt = await runCloudPreflight(input, runCommand)
      process.stdout.write(`FRESHELL_CLOUD_PREFLIGHT ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`)
      return 0
    }
    await runCloudRunLifecycle(input, runCommand)
    return 0
  } catch (error) {
    process.stderr.write(
      `Cloud Vitest failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n` +
      `Run locally with: ${directLocalFallback(parsed)}\n`,
    )
    return 1
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  superviseCatchableSignals((signal) =>
    runVitest(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, defaultRunCommand, signal),
  ).then((code) => { process.exitCode = code }, (error) => {
    process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.stack : String(error)}\n`)
    process.exitCode = 1
  })
}
```

Add the final read-only preflight to `lifecycle.ts` and dispatch it before any
lifecycle mutation when `parsed.preflightOnly` is true:

```ts
export type CloudPreflightReceipt = {
  schemaVersion: 1
  head: string
  projectId: string
  projectNumber: string
  iamAnalysisScope: string
  buildRegion: 'global'
  runRegion: string
  sourceBucket: string
  repositoryResource: string
  buildServiceAccount: string
  runtimeServiceAccount: string
  globalOngoingBuildLimit: number
  effectiveIam: EffectiveIamEvidence
  quota: QuotaEvidence
  buildTemplate: Readonly<BuildTemplate>
  tempRemoved: true
}

export async function runCloudPreflight(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<CloudPreflightReceipt> {
  const runId = createRunId()
  const snapshot = await prepareSourceSnapshot(
    input.repoRoot,
    runId,
    input.signal,
    runCommand,
  )
  let values: Omit<CloudPreflightReceipt, 'tempRemoved'> | undefined = undefined
  try {
    const buildTemplate = await readStrictBuildTemplate(snapshot.buildTemplatePath)
    const prerequisites = await verifyCloudPrerequisites(input, runCommand)
    values = {
      schemaVersion: 1,
      head: snapshot.head,
      projectId: prerequisites.projectId,
      projectNumber: prerequisites.projectNumber,
      iamAnalysisScope: prerequisites.iamAnalysisScope,
      buildRegion: 'global',
      runRegion: input.runRegion,
      sourceBucket: prerequisites.sourceBucket,
      repositoryResource: prerequisites.repositoryResource,
      buildServiceAccount: prerequisites.buildServiceAccount,
      runtimeServiceAccount: prerequisites.runtimeServiceAccount,
      globalOngoingBuildLimit: prerequisites.globalOngoingBuildLimit,
      effectiveIam: prerequisites.effectiveIam,
      quota: prerequisites.quota,
      buildTemplate,
    }
  } finally {
    await rm(snapshot.tempRoot, { recursive: true, force: true })
    if (await pathPresent(snapshot.tempRoot)) {
      throw new Error('Preflight temp root remains after cleanup')
    }
  }
  if (!values) throw new Error('Preflight completed without validated values')
  return { ...values, tempRemoved: true }
}
```

this preflight,
emits the success record only after exact temp absence, and otherwise returns
nonzero without source/Build/registry/Job mutation.

Cloud eligibility is finite and consumes the existing classifier result. Add
this sole cumulative import to `coordinator-command-matrix.ts`, then append the
bodies shown below to its existing classifier implementation:

```ts
import { shellJoin } from '../cloud-run/argv.js'
import type { CloudInvocationOrigin } from '../cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
```

```ts
export type CloudEligibility =
  | { kind: 'cloud'; origin: CloudInvocationOrigin }
  | { kind: 'cloud-ineligible'; reason: string; localCommand: string }
  | { kind: 'passthrough'; phases: readonly UpstreamPhase[] }

type CloudCoordinatorCommandKey = 'test' | 'test:all' | 'check' | 'verify'

function isCloudCoordinatorCommandKey(
  commandKey: CommandKey,
): commandKey is CloudCoordinatorCommandKey {
  switch (commandKey) {
    case 'test':
    case 'test:all':
    case 'check':
    case 'verify':
      return true
    default:
      return false
  }
}

function surfaceForCoordinatorCommand(
  commandKey: CloudCoordinatorCommandKey,
): Extract<CloudInvocationOrigin, { surface: `coordinator-${string}` }>['surface'] {
  switch (commandKey) {
    case 'test': return 'coordinator-test'
    case 'test:all': return 'coordinator-test-all'
    case 'check': return 'coordinator-check'
    case 'verify': return 'coordinator-verify'
  }
}

export function classifyCloudEligibility(input: {
  commandKey: CommandKey
  argv: readonly string[]
  disposition: Exclude<CommandDisposition, { kind: 'rejected' }>
}): CloudEligibility {
  if (input.disposition.kind === 'passthrough') {
    return { kind: 'passthrough', phases: input.disposition.phases }
  }
  if (
    input.disposition.kind === 'coordinated'
    && isCloudCoordinatorCommandKey(input.commandKey)
    && input.argv.length === 0
  ) {
    return {
      kind: 'cloud',
      origin: {
        surface: surfaceForCoordinatorCommand(input.commandKey),
        argv: [],
      },
    }
  }
  return cloudIneligible(input.commandKey, input.argv)
}

export function renderCoordinatorLocalCommand(
  commandKey: CommandKey,
  argv: readonly string[],
): string {
  const npm = commandKey === 'test'
    ? ['npm', 'test']
    : ['npm', 'run', commandKey]
  return shellJoin([
    'FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=local',
    ...npm,
    ...(argv.length === 0 ? [] : ['--', ...argv]),
  ])
}

function cloudIneligible(
  commandKey: CommandKey,
  argv: readonly string[],
  reason = 'This classified workload is not eligible for cloud execution',
): Extract<CloudEligibility, { kind: 'cloud-ineligible' }> {
  return {
    kind: 'cloud-ineligible',
    reason,
    localCommand: renderCoordinatorLocalCommand(commandKey, argv),
  }
}
```

`prePhasesForCloudCommand` is data-only:

```ts
const cloudPrePhases = {
  test: [],
  'test:all': [],
  check: ['typecheck'],
  verify: ['typecheck', 'build'],
} as const

export type CloudPrePhase = 'typecheck' | 'build'

export function prePhasesForCloudCommand(
  commandKey: CloudCoordinatorCommandKey,
): readonly CloudPrePhase[] {
  return [...cloudPrePhases[commandKey]]
}
```

`runCoordinatorCloudVitest` runs those phases under one
`superviseCatchableSignals`, records each completion in memory, and emits
exactly one structured route event after all required pre-phases and before
lifecycle dispatch:

```ts
export type CloudRouteReceipt = {
  schemaVersion: 1
  commandKey: 'test' | 'test:all' | 'check' | 'verify'
  surface: CloudInvocationOrigin['surface']
  coordinatorEffects: false
  plannedPrePhases: readonly ('typecheck' | 'build')[]
  completedPrePhases: readonly ('typecheck' | 'build')[]
}

function emitCloudRouteReceipt(receipt: CloudRouteReceipt): void {
  process.stderr.write(`FRESHELL_CLOUD_ROUTE ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`)
}

export async function runCloudPrePhases(
  phases: readonly CloudPrePhase[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<CloudPrePhase[]> {
  const completed: CloudPrePhase[] = []
  for (const phase of phases) {
    const result = await runCommand('npm', ['run', phase], {
      cwd: process.cwd(),
      env,
      signal,
      output: { kind: 'inherit' },
      timeoutMs: phase === 'build' ? 30 * 60_000 : 10 * 60_000,
      terminateGraceMs: 5_000,
      settlementTimeoutMs: 30_000,
    })
    if (result.exitCode !== 0 || result.signal !== null || result.timedOut) {
      throw new Error(`Cloud pre-phase npm run ${phase} failed`)
    }
    completed.push(phase)
  }
  return completed
}

export async function runCoordinatorCloudVitest(
  parsed: { commandKey: CommandKey; forwardedArgs: readonly string[] },
  eligibility: Extract<CloudEligibility, { kind: 'cloud' }>,
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<number> {
  const commandKey = parsed.commandKey
  if (!['test', 'test:all', 'check', 'verify'].includes(commandKey)) {
    throw new Error(`Unsupported coordinator cloud command ${commandKey}`)
  }
  const localCommand = renderCoordinatorLocalCommand(commandKey, parsed.forwardedArgs)
  try {
    return await superviseCatchableSignals(async (signal) => {
      const planned = prePhasesForCloudCommand(
        commandKey as 'test' | 'test:all' | 'check' | 'verify',
      )
      const completed = await runCloudPrePhases(planned, env, signal, runCommand)
      emitCloudRouteReceipt({
        schemaVersion: 1,
        commandKey: commandKey as 'test' | 'test:all' | 'check' | 'verify',
        surface: eligibility.origin.surface,
        coordinatorEffects: false,
        plannedPrePhases: planned,
        completedPrePhases: completed,
      })
      const direct = parseVitestArgs(['run', '--cloud'], env)
      await runCloudRunLifecycle({
        ...createVitestLifecycleInput(direct, process.cwd(), eligibility.origin),
        signal,
      }, runCommand)
      return 0
    })
  } catch (error) {
    process.stderr.write(
      `Cloud Vitest failed. Run locally with: ${localCommand}\n`,
    )
    return 1
  }
}
```

Replace `coordinator-upstream.ts`'s direct-spawn implementation with this
complete cumulative import block and final bodies. The three command-resolution
bodies are included literally rather than treated as ambient existing behavior:

```ts
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'
import path from 'node:path'

import {
  defaultRunCommand,
  type RunCommand,
} from '../cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
import type { UpstreamPhase } from './coordinator-command-matrix.js'

const ACTIVE_ENV_KEY = 'FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_ACTIVE'
const FAKE_UPSTREAM_ENV_KEY = 'FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_FAKE_UPSTREAM'
const REPO_ROOT_ENV_KEY = 'FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_REPO_ROOT'

export function assertNoCoordinatorRecursion(
  envVars: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): void {
  if (envVars[ACTIVE_ENV_KEY] === '1') {
    throw new Error(
      'Recursive coordinator entry is not allowed while '
      + 'FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_ACTIVE=1.',
    )
  }
}

export function resolveVitestCommand(
  repoRoot: string,
): { command: string; args: string[] } {
  const require = createRequire(path.join(repoRoot, 'package.json'))
  return {
    command: process.execPath,
    args: [require.resolve('vitest/vitest.mjs')],
  }
}

export function resolveNpmCommand(
  args: string[],
  envVars: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
): { command: string; args: string[] } {
  const npmExecPath = envVars.npm_execpath
  if (npmExecPath?.endsWith('.js')) {
    return { command: process.execPath, args: [npmExecPath, ...args] }
  }
  return {
    command: process.platform === 'win32' ? 'npm.cmd' : 'npm',
    args,
  }
}

function resolveSpawnSpec(
  phase: UpstreamPhase,
  envVars: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
): { command: string; args: string[]; selector: string } {
  if (phase.runner === 'npm') {
    const forwardedArgs = phase.args.length > 0 ? ['--', ...phase.args] : []
    const npm = resolveNpmCommand(
      ['run', phase.script, ...forwardedArgs], envVars,
    )
    return {
      command: npm.command,
      args: npm.args,
      selector: `npm:${phase.script}${
        phase.args.length > 0 ? ` ${phase.args.join(' ')}` : ''
      }`,
    }
  }
  const repoRoot = envVars[REPO_ROOT_ENV_KEY] ?? process.cwd()
  const vitest = resolveVitestCommand(repoRoot)
  return {
    command: vitest.command,
    args: [...vitest.args, ...phase.args],
    selector: `vitest:${phase.config}:${phase.args.join(' ')}`.trimEnd(),
  }
}

export async function runUpstreamPhase(
  phase: UpstreamPhase,
  envVars: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  const childEnv: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {
    ...envVars,
    FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_ACTIVE: '1',
  }
  const spec = resolveSpawnSpec(phase, childEnv)
  const fake = childEnv.FRESHELL_TEST_COORDINATOR_FAKE_UPSTREAM
  const command = fake ? process.execPath : spec.command
  const args = fake
    ? [fake, JSON.stringify({
        selector: spec.selector,
        command: spec.command,
        args: spec.args,
      })]
    : spec.args
  const result = await runCommand(command, args, {
    env: childEnv,
    signal,
    output: { kind: 'inherit' },
    timeoutMs: 24 * 60 * 60_000,
    terminateGraceMs: 5_000,
    settlementTimeoutMs: 30_000,
  })
  return result.exitCode === 0 && result.signal === null && !result.timedOut
    ? 0
    : result.exitCode || 1
}
```

Delete `runFakePhase`, `runRealPhase`, and `spawnAndWait`; every coordinator
child now uses the final owned POSIX process-group primitive. Replace `test-coordinator.ts`'s `runPhases` with:

```ts
async function runPhases(
  phases: readonly UpstreamPhase[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  for (const phase of phases) {
    const exitCode = await runUpstreamPhase(phase, env, runCommand, signal)
    if (exitCode !== 0) return exitCode
  }
  return 0
}
```

```ts
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process'
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { promisify } from 'node:util'
import { beforeAll, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import type {
  CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  CommandResult,
  RunCommand,
} from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.js'
import type { ParsedE2EArgs } from '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/e2e.js'

type ParseE2EArgs = (
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
) => ParsedE2EArgs
type CreateE2ELifecycleInput = (
  parsed: ParsedE2EArgs,
  repoRoot: string,
) => CloudRunLifecycleInput
type RunE2E = (
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  runCommand?: RunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
) => Promise<number>

let parseE2EArgs: ParseE2EArgs
let createE2ELifecycleInput: CreateE2ELifecycleInput
let runE2E: RunE2E
beforeAll(async () => {
  const modulePath = '../../../../scripts/cloud-run/e2e.js'
  const module = await import(modulePath).catch(() => ({})) as Record<string, unknown>
  for (const name of [
    'parseE2EArgs', 'createE2ELifecycleInput', 'runE2E',
  ] as const) {
    expect(module[name], `${name} public behavior must be installed`).toBeTypeOf('function')
  }
  parseE2EArgs = module.parseE2EArgs as ParseE2EArgs
  createE2ELifecycleInput = module.createE2ELifecycleInput as CreateE2ELifecycleInput
  runE2E = module.runE2E as RunE2E
})

const execFileAsync = promisify(execFile)
const CLOUD_ENV: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {
  FRESHELL_E2E_BACKEND: 'cloud',
  FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT: 'caller@example.com',
  FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT: 'test-project',
  FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE: 'organizations/123456789',
  FRESHELL_GCP_SOURCE_BUCKET: 'test-source-bucket',
  FRESHELL_GCP_REPO: 'freshell-e2e',
  FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
    'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
  FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
    'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
}
const ok = (): CommandResult => ({
  stdout: '', stderr: '', exitCode: 0, signal: null, timedOut: false,
})
const okJson = (value: unknown): CommandResult => ({
  ...ok(), stdout: `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`,
})
function commandTranscript(runCommand: RunCommand): string {
  return (runCommand as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>).mock.calls
    .map(([command, argv]) => `${command} ${argv.join(' ')}`)
    .join('\n')
}

  ['--global-timeout', '60000'], ['--max-failures', '1'],

  ['--test-list', 'test list.txt'], ['--test-list-invert', 'skip list.txt'],
```

Create `scripts/cloud-run/e2e.ts` with this complete cumulative import block,
constants, and parser. The only cloud calls imported are the shared lifecycle
and exact log reader:

```ts
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'

import {
  canonicalizeTaskTimeout,
  defaultRunCommand,
  lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout,
  readExactExecutionLogs,
  runCloudRunLifecycle,
  superviseCatchableSignals,
  type CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  type RunCommand,
} from './lifecycle.js'
import { parseCloudTaskCount, shellJoin } from './argv.js'

export const E2E_DEFAULT_TASK_COUNT = 1

export type ParsedE2EArgs = {
  subcommand: 'run' | 'build' | 'push' | 'logs' | 'help'
  backend: 'local' | 'cloud'
  operation: 'run' | 'build'
  buildMode: 'cloud' | 'local'
  taskCount: number
  taskTimeout: string
  account?: string
  projectId?: string
  runRegion?: string
  repository?: string
  jobPrefix?: string
  logJobName?: string
  logExecutionName?: string
  playwrightArgs: string[]
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv
}

const e2eSubcommands = new Set(['run', 'build', 'push', 'logs', 'help'])
const playwrightValueFlags = new Set([
  '--browser', '--grep', '-g', '--grep-invert', '--project', '--reporter',
  '--retries', '--workers', '-j', '--timeout', '--global-timeout',
  '--max-failures', '--repeat-each', '--output', '--test-list',
  '--test-list-invert', '--trace', '--tsconfig', '--ui-host', '--ui-port',
])

function resolveE2EBackend(value: string | undefined): 'local' | 'cloud' {
  if (value === undefined || value === '' || value === 'local') return 'local'
  if (value === 'cloud') return 'cloud'
  throw new Error(
    `Invalid FRESHELL_E2E_BACKEND ${JSON.stringify(value)}; expected local or cloud`,
  )
}

function takeE2EOption(
  tokens: readonly string[],
  index: number,
  name: string,
): { value: string; consumed: number } | undefined {
  const token = tokens[index]
  if (token.startsWith(`${name}=`)) {
    return { value: token.slice(name.length + 1), consumed: 1 }
  }
  if (token === name) {
    const value = tokens[index + 1]
    if (value === undefined) throw new Error(`Missing value for ${name}`)
    return { value, consumed: 2 }
  }
  return undefined
}

export function parseE2EArgs(
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
): ParsedE2EArgs {
  const tokens = [...argv]
  let subcommand: ParsedE2EArgs['subcommand'] = 'run'
  if (tokens[0] && e2eSubcommands.has(tokens[0])) {
    subcommand = tokens.shift() as ParsedE2EArgs['subcommand']
  } else if (tokens[0] === '--help' || tokens[0] === '-h') {
    subcommand = 'help'
    tokens.shift()
  }
  let backend = resolveE2EBackend(env.FRESHELL_E2E_BACKEND)
  let explicitBackend: 'local' | 'cloud' | undefined
  let buildMode: 'cloud' | 'local' = subcommand === 'push' ? 'local' : 'cloud'
  let taskCount = E2E_DEFAULT_TASK_COUNT
  let taskTimeout = canonicalizeTaskTimeout('60m')
  let account: string | undefined
  let projectId: string | undefined
  let runRegion: string | undefined
  let repository: string | undefined
  let jobPrefix: string | undefined
  let logJobName: string | undefined
  let logExecutionName: string | undefined
  const playwrightArgs: string[] = []

  for (let index = 0; index < tokens.length;) {
    const token = tokens[index]
    if (token === '--dry-run') {
      throw new Error(
        '--dry-run is a container-only shard-assignment diagnostic; ' +
        'it cannot be used as an E2E test run',
      )
    }
    if (token === '--local' || token === '--cloud') {
      const next = token === '--local' ? 'local' : 'cloud'
      if (explicitBackend && explicitBackend !== next) {
        throw new Error('--local and --cloud are mutually exclusive')
      }
      explicitBackend = next
      backend = next
      index += 1
      continue
    }
    if (token === '--local-build') { buildMode = 'local'; index += 1; continue }
    const adapterOptions = [
      ['--shards', (value: string) => { taskCount = parseCloudTaskCount(value, 'E2E shards') }],
      ['--task-timeout', (value: string) => {
        taskTimeout = canonicalizeTaskTimeout(value)
      }],
      ['--account', (value: string) => { account = value }],
      ['--project-id', (value: string) => { projectId = value }],
      ['--region', (value: string) => { runRegion = value }],
      ['--repository', (value: string) => { repository = value }],
      ['--job-prefix', (value: string) => { jobPrefix = value }],
      ['--job', (value: string) => { logJobName = value }],
      ['--execution', (value: string) => { logExecutionName = value }],
    ] as const
    let matched = false
    for (const [name, assign] of adapterOptions) {
      const parsed = takeE2EOption(tokens, index, name)
      if (!parsed) continue
      assign(parsed.value)
      index += parsed.consumed
      matched = true
      break
    }
    if (matched) continue
    if (token.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const value = token.slice('--timeout='.length)
      if (!/^[0-9]+$/.test(value)) {
        taskTimeout = canonicalizeTaskTimeout(value)
        index += 1
        continue
      }
    }
    const valueFlag = [...playwrightValueFlags].find((name) => token === name)
    if (valueFlag) {
      const value = tokens[index + 1]
      if (value === undefined) throw new Error(`Missing Playwright value for ${token}`)
      playwrightArgs.push(`${token}=${value}`)
      index += 2
      continue
    }
    playwrightArgs.push(token)
    index += 1
  }

  if (['build', 'push', 'logs'].includes(subcommand)) backend = 'cloud'
  if (subcommand === 'logs' && (!logJobName || !logExecutionName)) {
    throw new Error('logs requires exact --job and --execution')
  }
  if (subcommand !== 'logs' && (logJobName || logExecutionName)) {
    throw new Error('--job/--execution are valid only for logs')
  }
  if (backend === 'cloud') {
    const forbidden = playwrightArgs.find((token) =>
      token === '--config' || token.startsWith('--config=') ||
      token === '--shard' || token.startsWith('--shard=') ||
      token === '--update-snapshots' || token.startsWith('--update-snapshots='))
    if (forbidden) throw new Error(`Cloud E2E wrapper owns ${forbidden}`)
  }
  return {
    subcommand,
    backend,
    operation: subcommand === 'run' ? 'run' : 'build',
    buildMode,
    taskCount,
    taskTimeout,
    account,
    projectId,
    runRegion,
    repository,
    jobPrefix,
    logJobName,
    logExecutionName,
    playwrightArgs,
    env: { ...env },
  }
}

function requireE2EEnv(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv, key: string): string {
  const value = env[key]
  if (!value) throw new Error(`Missing required cloud environment ${key}`)
  return value
}

export function expectedE2EParts(taskCount: number): string[] {
  return Array.from(
    { length: taskCount },
    (_, taskIndex) => `e2e:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`,
  )
}

export function createE2ELifecycleInput(
  parsed: ParsedE2EArgs,
  repoRoot: string,
): CloudRunLifecycleInput {
  const surface = (() => {
    switch (parsed.subcommand) {
      case 'run': return 'e2e-run'
      case 'build': return 'e2e-build'
      case 'push': return 'e2e-push'
      case 'logs':
      case 'help':
        throw new Error(`${parsed.subcommand} does not create a lifecycle input`)
    }
  })()
  return {
    origin: { surface, argv: [...parsed.playwrightArgs] },
    kind: 'e2e',
    repoRoot,
    account: parsed.account ?? requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT'),
    projectId: parsed.projectId ?? requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT'),
    iamAnalysisScope: requireE2EEnv(
      parsed.env,
      'FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE',
    ),
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: parsed.runRegion ?? parsed.env.FRESHELL_GCP_REGION ?? 'us-west1',
    sourceBucket: requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_SOURCE_BUCKET'),
    repository: parsed.repository ?? requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_REPO'),
    imageName: 'freshell-e2e',
    jobPrefix: parsed.jobPrefix ?? parsed.env.FRESHELL_GCP_JOB ?? 'freshell-e2e',
    buildServiceAccount: requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT'),
    runtimeServiceAccount: requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT'),
    buildMode: parsed.buildMode,
    operation: parsed.operation,
    taskCount: parsed.taskCount,
    taskTimeout: parsed.taskTimeout,
    memory: '4Gi',
    cpu: 4,
    workloadEnv: { PLAYWRIGHT_ARGS_JSON: JSON.stringify(parsed.playwrightArgs) },
    expectedParts: expectedE2EParts(parsed.taskCount),
    budgets: lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout(parsed.taskTimeout),
  }
}

function e2eLocalFallback(parsed: ParsedE2EArgs): string {
  return shellJoin([
    'FRESHELL_E2E_BACKEND=local',
    'npm', 'run', 'test:e2e:local', '--',
    ...parsed.playwrightArgs,
  ])
}

async function runLocalE2E(
  parsed: ParsedE2EArgs,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  const result = await runCommand('npx', [
    'playwright', 'test', '--config', 'test/e2e-browser/playwright.config.ts',
    ...parsed.playwrightArgs,
  ], {
    cwd: process.cwd(),
    env: parsed.env,
    signal,
    output: { kind: 'inherit' },
    timeoutMs: 24 * 60 * 60_000,
    terminateGraceMs: 5_000,
    settlementTimeoutMs: 30_000,
  })
  return result.exitCode === 0 && result.signal === null && !result.timedOut ? 0 : 1
}

async function runE2ELogs(
  parsed: ParsedE2EArgs,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await readExactExecutionLogs({
    account: parsed.account ?? requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT'),
    projectId: parsed.projectId ?? requireE2EEnv(parsed.env, 'FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT'),
    runRegion: parsed.runRegion ?? parsed.env.FRESHELL_GCP_REGION ?? 'us-west1',
    jobName: parsed.logJobName!,
    executionName: parsed.logExecutionName!,
    limit: expectedE2EParts(parsed.taskCount).length + 1,
    budgets: lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout(parsed.taskTimeout),
    signal,
  }, runCommand)
  for (const row of rows) process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(row)}\n`)
  return 0
}

function printE2EHelp(): void {
  process.stdout.write(
    'Usage: scripts/e2e-cloud.sh [run|build|push|logs|help] ' +
    '[--local|--cloud] [--shards=1..32] [--task-timeout=60m] ' +
    '[playwright args...]\n' +
    '  --timeout=<s|m|h duration> remains the Cloud Run timeout alias; ' +
    'unitless --timeout is forwarded to Playwright.\n',
  )
}

export async function runE2E(
  argv: readonly string[],
  env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<number> {
  const parsed = parseE2EArgs(argv, env)
  if (parsed.subcommand === 'help') { printE2EHelp(); return 0 }
  if (parsed.subcommand === 'logs') return runE2ELogs(parsed, runCommand, signal)
  if (parsed.backend === 'local') {
    if (parsed.subcommand !== 'run') throw new Error('E2E build/push require cloud configuration')
    return runLocalE2E(parsed, runCommand, signal)
  }
  try {
    await runCloudRunLifecycle({
      ...createE2ELifecycleInput(parsed, process.cwd()),
      signal,
    }, runCommand)
    return 0
  } catch (error) {
    process.stderr.write(
      `Cloud E2E failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n` +
      `Run locally with: ${e2eLocalFallback(parsed)}\n`,
    )
    return 1
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  superviseCatchableSignals((signal) =>
    runE2E(process.argv.slice(2), process.env, defaultRunCommand, signal),
  ).then((code) => { process.exitCode = code }, (error) => {
    process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.stack : String(error)}\n`)
    process.exitCode = 1
  })
}
```

```ts
function normalizedPlaywrightValue(flag: string, value: string): string {
  if (!playwrightValueFlags.has(flag) || value.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid Playwright value pair ${flag}`)
  }
  return `${flag}=${value}`
}
```

```ts
) => void

  capture: ProviderContractCapture | undefined,
  write?: (line: string) => unknown,
) => void
type DiscardProviderContractCapture = (
  capture: ProviderContractCapture | undefined,
) => void
type SemanticProviderRecordLimit = (expectedPartCount: number) => number
type ReadBoundedRegularFile = (
  path: string,
  options: {
    label: string
    maxBytes: number
    afterInitialStatForTest?: () => Promise<void>

) => Promise<string>

    maxLineBytes: number
    maxLines: number
    maxSelectedRecords: number
    maxSelectedBytes: number

) => Promise<{

}>
type CharacterizeContractLogs = (

) => CharacterizeContractsResult

function straceExecve(argv: readonly string[]): ParsedTrace {
  return {
    execs: [{
      executable: `/usr/bin/${argv[0]}`,
      argv: [...argv],
      line: `execve("/usr/bin/${argv[0]}", ${JSON.stringify(argv)}, 0x0) = 0`,
    }],
    syscalls: [],
  }
}

const testPublicArgv: Record<(typeof requiredPublicCommands)[number], string[]> = {
  'coordinator-test': ['npm', 'test'],
  'coordinator-check': ['npm', 'run', 'check'],
  'coordinator-verify': ['npm', 'run', 'verify'],
  'coordinator-test-all': ['npm', 'run', 'test:all'],
  'vitest-run': ['npm', 'run', 'test:cloud'],
  'vitest-build': ['npm', 'run', 'test:cloud:build'],
  'vitest-push': ['bash', 'scripts/vitest-cloud.sh', 'push'],
  'vitest-logs': ['bash', 'scripts/vitest-cloud.sh', 'logs'],

  'e2e-build': ['npm', 'run', 'test:e2e:cloud:build'],
```
