```ts
function fixtureReceipt(
  index: number,
  command: string,
  status: 'succeeded' | 'failed' = 'succeeded',
  options: {
    runId?: string
    sourceGeneration?: string
    taskCount?: number
    suites?: readonly ('client' | 'server' | 'electron')[]
  } = {},
): OwnershipReceipt {
  const runId = options.runId ?? index.toString(16).padStart(32, '0')
  const e2e = command.startsWith('e2e-')
  const buildOnly = /-(?:build|push)$/.test(command)
  const localBuild = command.endsWith('-push')
  const kind = e2e ? 'e2e' : 'vitest'
  const taskCount = options.taskCount ?? (e2e ? 1 : 4)
  const suites = options.suites ?? ['client', 'server', 'electron']
  const expectedParts = buildOnly || status === 'failed' ? [] : e2e
    ? Array.from({ length: taskCount }, (_, taskIndex) =>
        `e2e:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`)
    : suites.flatMap((suite) =>
        Array.from({ length: taskCount }, (_, taskIndex) =>
          `${suite}:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`))
  const head = 'a'.repeat(40)
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    status,
    origin: {
      surface: command === 'coordinator-test-all'
        ? 'coordinator-test-all'
        : command === 'coordinator-check'
          ? 'coordinator-check'
          : command === 'coordinator-verify'
            ? 'coordinator-verify'
            : command === 'coordinator-test'
              ? 'coordinator-test'
              : command.replace(/-logs$/, '-run') as OwnershipReceipt['origin']['surface'],
      argv: [],
    },
    kind,
    operation: buildOnly ? 'build' : 'run',
    buildMode: localBuild ? 'local' : 'cloud',
    runId,
    branch: 'feature',
    upstreamRemote: 'origin',
    upstreamMergeRef: 'refs/heads/feature',
    head,
    account: 'caller@example.com',
    projectId: 'test-project',
    iamAnalysisScope: 'organizations/123456789',
    projectNumber: '123456789',
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: 'us-west1',
    source: localBuild ? { state: 'not-created' } : {
      state: 'deleted', bucket: 'test-source-bucket', object: `source/${runId}.tar.gz`,
      generation: options.sourceGeneration ?? `${index + 100}`, size: '3', commit: head,
    },
    build: {
      state: 'terminal', correlationTag: `freshell-${runId}`,
      id: localBuild ? `local-build-${runId}` : `build-${runId}`,
      resourceName: localBuild
        ? `local-builds/${runId}`
        : `projects/123456789/locations/global/builds/build-${runId}`,
      projectId: 'test-project',
      projectNumber: '123456789',
      createTime: `2026-08-16T00:00:${String(index).padStart(2, '0')}.000Z`,
      startTime: '2026-08-16T00:01:00.000Z',
      finishTime: '2026-08-16T00:05:00.000Z',
      serviceAccount: 'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
      status: 'SUCCESS', normalizedConfig: {},
    },
    image: {
      state: 'tag-deleted',
      taggedImage: `us-west1-docker.pkg.dev/test-project/freshell-e2e/freshell-${kind}:${runId}`,
      tag: runId,
      tagResourceName:
        `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e` +
        `/packages/freshell-${kind}/tags/${runId}`,
      digest: `sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      immutableImage: `us-west1-docker.pkg.dev/test-project/freshell-e2e/freshell-${kind}@sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      digestVersionResourceName:
        `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e` +
        `/packages/freshell-${kind}/versions/sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      localTags: localBuild
        ? [`freshell-${kind}:${runId}`, `remote-${kind}:${runId}`]
        : [],
    },
    job: buildOnly ? {
      name: '', serviceAccount: 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
      state: 'not-created',
    } : {
      name: `job-${runId}`, resourceName: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/job-${runId}`,
      uid: `job-uid-${runId}`, etag: `etag-${runId}`,
      generation: `${index + 7}`,
      serviceAccount: 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
      state: 'deleted',
    },
    execution: buildOnly ? { state: 'not-created' } : {
      operationName: `operations/run-${runId}`,
      resourceName: `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/job-${runId}/executions/execution-${runId}`,
      name: `execution-${runId}`, uid: `execution-uid-${runId}`,
      serviceAccount: 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
      counters: status === 'succeeded'
        ? { succeededCount: taskCount, failedCount: 0, cancelledCount: 0, runningCount: 0 }
        : { succeededCount: 0, failedCount: taskCount, cancelledCount: 0, runningCount: 0 },
      omittedZeroCounters: status === 'succeeded'
        ? ['cancelledCount', 'runningCount']
        : ['succeededCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount'],
      startTime: '2026-08-16T00:06:00.000Z',
      completionTime: '2026-08-16T00:10:00.000Z', state: 'terminal',
    },
    temp: {
      root: `/tmp/freshell-cloud-${runId}-x`, sourceDir: `/tmp/source-${runId}`,
      dockerConfig: `/tmp/docker-${runId}`, rootRemoved: true, dockerConfigRemoved: true,
    },
    parts: expectedParts.map((partId) => ({
      marker: 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT', schemaVersion: 1, kind, runId,
      execution: `execution-${runId}`,
      partId,
      taskIndex: Number(/:([1-9][0-9]*)\//.exec(partId)![1]) - 1,
      taskCount,
      taskAttempt: 0, status: 'passed', exitCode: 0,
      ...(kind === 'vitest' ? { testCount: 3 } : {}),
    })),
    cleanup: [
      {
        kind: 'build',
        identity: localBuild ? `local-build-${runId}` : `build-${runId}`,
        outcome: 'terminal',
      },
      ...(localBuild ? [] : [{
        kind: 'source' as const,
        identity: `source/${runId}.tar.gz#${options.sourceGeneration ?? index + 100}`,
        outcome: 'deleted' as const,
      }]),
      {
        kind: 'tag',
        identity:
          `us-west1-docker.pkg.dev/test-project/freshell-e2e/freshell-${kind}:${runId}`,
        outcome: 'deleted',
        precondition: `digest=sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
      },
      ...(localBuild ? [
        { kind: 'local-image' as const, identity: `freshell-${kind}:${runId}`, outcome: 'deleted' as const },
        { kind: 'local-image' as const, identity: `remote-${kind}:${runId}`, outcome: 'deleted' as const },
      ] : []),
      { kind: 'docker-config', identity: `/tmp/docker-${runId}`, outcome: 'deleted' },
      { kind: 'temp-root', identity: `/tmp/freshell-cloud-${runId}-x`, outcome: 'deleted' },
      ...(buildOnly ? [] : [
        { kind: 'execution' as const, identity: `execution-${runId}`, outcome: 'terminal' as const },
        { kind: 'job' as const, identity: `job-${runId}`, outcome: 'deleted' as const },
      ]),
    ],
    residuals: [],
    ...(status === 'failed' ? {
      failure: {
        primary: 'intentional failure',
        causes: ['intentional failure'],
        cleanup: [],
      },
    } : {}),
  }
}

it('extracts every required run/resource identity and rejects partial receipts', () => {
  const receipt = fixtureReceipt(7, 'vitest-run')
  expect(extractReceiptIdentity(receipt, 'vitest')).toEqual({
    kind: 'vitest',
    runId: receipt.runId,
    head: receipt.head,
    sourceBucket: receipt.source.bucket,
    sourceObject: receipt.source.object,
    sourceGeneration: receipt.source.generation,
    buildCorrelationTag: receipt.build.correlationTag,
    buildId: receipt.build.id,
    buildResourceName: receipt.build.resourceName,
    buildProjectId: receipt.build.projectId,
    buildProjectNumber: receipt.build.projectNumber,
    imageTag: receipt.image.tag,
    digest: receipt.image.digest,
    job: receipt.job.name,
    jobResourceName: receipt.job.resourceName,
    jobUid: receipt.job.uid,
    jobGeneration: receipt.job.generation,
    execution: receipt.execution.name,
    executionResourceName: receipt.execution.resourceName,
    executionUid: receipt.execution.uid,
  })
  expect(() => extractReceiptIdentity({
    ...receipt,
    source: { ...receipt.source, generation: undefined },
  } as unknown as OwnershipReceipt, 'vitest')).toThrow()
})

async function testBoundedText(
  path: string,
  label: string,
  maxBytes = 1024 * 1024,
): Promise<string> {
  return readBoundedRegularFile(path, { label, maxBytes })
}

async function testPrefixedText(
  path: string,
  label: string,
  prefixes: readonly string[],
  maxSelectedRecords: number,
): Promise<string> {
  const scanned = await readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    label,
    maxBytes: 16 * 1024 * 1024,
    maxLineBytes: 1024 * 1024,
    maxLines: 100_000,
    maxSelectedRecords,
    maxSelectedBytes: maxSelectedRecords * 1024 * 1024,
    select: (line) =>
      prefixes.some((prefix) => line.startsWith(prefix)),
  })
  return `${scanned.records.join('\n')}\n`
}

async function completeAcceptanceManifest(): Promise<AcceptanceManifest> {
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'cloud-acceptance-'))
  const acceptedSha = 'a'.repeat(40)
  const committedContractFixturePath = join(
    process.cwd(),
    'test/fixtures/cloud-run/live-contracts.json',
  )
  const committedContractFixture = JSON.parse(await testBoundedText(
    committedContractFixturePath,
    'committed contract fixture',
  ))
  const commands = [
    ...requiredPublicCommands,
    'coordinator-test',
    'contract-characterization',
    'contract-characterization',
  ] as const
  const artifacts: AcceptanceManifest['artifacts'] = commands.map(
    (command, index) => {
    const id = command === 'coordinator-test' && index >= requiredPublicCommands.length
      ? 'coordinator-test-b'
      : command === 'contract-characterization'
        ? `contract-${index === commands.length - 1 ? 'failure' : 'success'}`
        : `${command}-a`
    const actualCommand = command === 'contract-characterization'
      ? [
          'npm', 'run', 'test:cloud', '--', '--config=server', '--shards=1',
          'test/unit/server/testing/vitest-suites.test.ts',
        ]
      : testPublicArgv[command]
    const receipt = fixtureReceipt(
      index + 1,
      command === 'contract-characterization' ? 'vitest-run' : command,
      id.endsWith('failure') ? 'failed' : 'succeeded',
      command === 'contract-characterization'
        ? {
            runId: id.endsWith('failure') ? '2'.repeat(32) : '1'.repeat(32),
            sourceGeneration: id.endsWith('failure')
              ? '2786766419256749'
              : '1786766419256749',
            taskCount: 1,
            suites: ['server'],
          }
        : {},
    )
    const requiresExtractedResources = [
      'vitest-run', 'vitest-logs', 'e2e-run', 'e2e-logs',
    ].includes(command)
    const extracted = requiresExtractedResources
      ? extractReceiptIdentity(receipt, receipt.kind)
      : undefined
    const expectedResources = extracted === undefined
      ? undefined
      : (({ kind: _kind, ...value }) => value)(extracted)
    const commandLogPath = join(root, `${id}.log`)
    const receiptLogPath = command === 'contract-characterization'
      ? commandLogPath
      : join(root, `${id}.receipt.log`)
    const tracePrefix = join(root, `${id}.trace`)
    const providerStatePath = join(root, `${id}.state.json`)
    const route = command.startsWith('coordinator-')
      ? `FRESHELL_CLOUD_ROUTE ${JSON.stringify({
          schemaVersion: 1,
          commandKey: command.replace('coordinator-', '').replace('test-all', 'test:all'),
          surface: command,
          coordinatorEffects: false,
          plannedPrePhases: expectedPrePhases(command),
          completedPrePhases: expectedPrePhases(command),
        })}\n`
      : ''
    const contractEvent = (kind: string, value: unknown) =>
      `FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ${JSON.stringify({
        schemaVersion: 1,
        runId: receipt.runId,
        kind,
        value,
      })}\n`
    const localPushEvents = command.endsWith('-push')
      ? (['intent', 'possibly-accepted', 'acquired'] as const)
          .map((phase) =>
            `FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP ${JSON.stringify({
              schemaVersion: 1,
              phase,
              value: {
                kind: 'local-push',
                state: phase,
                authority: {
                  projectId: receipt.projectId,
                  projectNumber: receipt.projectNumber,
                },
                correlationTag: receipt.build.correlationTag,
                runId: receipt.runId,
                head: receipt.head,
                localTags: receipt.image.localTags,
                remoteTag: receipt.image.taggedImage,
              },
            })}\n`)
          .join('')
      : ''
    const logOutput = command === 'contract-characterization'
      ? id.endsWith('failure')
        ? [
            contractEvent('execution', committedContractFixture.executionFailure),
            contractEvent('logEntry', committedContractFixture.logEntryFailure),
            `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`,
          ].join('')
        : [
            contractEvent('build', committedContractFixture.build),
            contractEvent('job', committedContractFixture.job),
            contractEvent('operation', committedContractFixture.operation),
            contractEvent('execution', committedContractFixture.executionSuccess),
            contractEvent('logEntry', committedContractFixture.logEntrySuccess),
            `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`,
          ].join('')
      : command.endsWith('-logs')
      ? receipt.parts.map((part) => JSON.stringify({
          resource: {
            type: 'cloud_run_job',
            labels: {
              project_id: receipt.projectId,
              location: receipt.runRegion,
              job_name: receipt.job.name,
            },
          },
          labels: {
            'run.googleapis.com/execution_name': receipt.execution.name,
          },
          jsonPayload: part,
        })).join('\n') + '\n'
      : `${route}${localPushEvents}command complete\n`
    writeFileSync(commandLogPath, logOutput)
    if (receiptLogPath !== commandLogPath) {
      writeFileSync(
        receiptLogPath,
        `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`,
      )
    }
    const pre = expectedPrePhases(command).map((phase) =>
      `execve("/usr/bin/npm", ["npm","run","${phase}"], 0x0) = 0`).join('\n')
    const providerTrace = command.endsWith('-logs')
      ? 'execve("/usr/bin/gcloud", ["gcloud","logging","read"], 0x0) = 0'
      : command.endsWith('-push')
        ? [
            'execve("/usr/bin/docker", ["docker","build"], 0x0) = 0',
            'execve("/usr/bin/docker", ["docker","push"], 0x0) = 0',
          ].join('\n')
        : 'execve("/usr/bin/gcloud", ["gcloud","storage","cp"], 0x0) = 0'
    writeFileSync(`${tracePrefix}.1`, [
      `execve("/usr/bin/${actualCommand[0]}", ${JSON.stringify(actualCommand)}, 0x0) = 0`,
      pre,
      providerTrace,
    ].filter(Boolean).join('\n'))
    writeFileSync(providerStatePath, JSON.stringify({
      schemaVersion: 1,
      job: receipt.operation === 'run' ? 'absent' : 'not-created',
      sourceGeneration: 'absent', tag: 'absent', digest: 'retained',
      tempRoot: 'absent', dockerConfig: 'absent', localImages: 'absent',
    }))
    return {
      id, command, expectedArgv: actualCommand, commandLogPath, receiptLogPath,
      tracePrefix, providerStatePath,
      expectedOrigin: receipt.origin.surface,
      expectedStatus: receipt.status,
      expectedKind: receipt.kind,
      expectedBuildMode: receipt.buildMode,
      ...(expectedResources === undefined ? {} : { expectedResources }),
      expectedPrePhases: [...expectedPrePhases(command)],
      acceptedSha,
    }
    },
  )
  const buildServiceAccount = 'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com'
  const runtimeServiceAccount = 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com'
  const effectiveIam = {
    analysisScope: 'organizations/123456789',
    caller: 'user:caller@example.com',
    buildEffectiveGrantKeys: [
      '//artifactregistry.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e\0roles/artifactregistry.writer',
      '//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/test-project\0roles/logging.logWriter',
      '//storage.googleapis.com/projects/_/buckets/test-source-bucket\0roles/storage.objectViewer',
    ],
    runtimeEffectiveGrantKeys: [],
    actAsResources: [
      `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${buildServiceAccount}`,
      `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${runtimeServiceAccount}`,
    ],
    tokenMintResources: [
      `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/test-project/serviceAccounts/${buildServiceAccount}`,
    ],
  }
  const quota = {
    metric: 'cloudbuild.googleapis.com/ongoing_builds', dimensions: {}, limit: 10,
  }
  const buildTemplate = {
    steps: [
      {
        name: `gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker@sha256:${'6'.repeat(64)}`,
        args: [
          'build', '--no-cache', '--file', 'docker/cloud-run/Dockerfile',
          '--tag', '${_IMAGE}', '.',
        ],
      },
      {
        name: `gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker@sha256:${'6'.repeat(64)}`,
        args: ['push', '${_IMAGE}'],
      },
    ],
    images: ['${_IMAGE}'],
    options: {
      machineType: 'E2_HIGHCPU_32', diskSizeGb: 200,
      logging: 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY',
    },
    timeout: '3600s',
  }
  writeFileSync(join(root, 'preflight.json'), JSON.stringify({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    head: acceptedSha,
    projectId: 'test-project',
    projectNumber: '123456789',
    iamAnalysisScope: 'organizations/123456789',
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: 'us-west1',
    sourceBucket: 'test-source-bucket',
    repositoryResource:
      'projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/repositories/freshell-e2e',
    buildServiceAccount,
    runtimeServiceAccount,
    globalOngoingBuildLimit: 10,
    effectiveIam,
    quota,
    buildTemplate,
    tempRemoved: true,
  }))
  writeFileSync(join(root, 'effective-iam.json'), JSON.stringify({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    acceptedSha,
    buildServiceAccount,
    runtimeServiceAccount,
    ...effectiveIam,
  }))
  writeFileSync(join(root, 'quota.json'), JSON.stringify({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    acceptedSha,
    buildRegion: 'global',
    machineType: 'E2_HIGHCPU_32',
    logging: 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY',
    ...quota,
  }))
  const contractSuccess = artifacts.find(({ id }) => id === 'contract-success')!
  const contractFailure = artifacts.find(({ id }) => id === 'contract-failure')!
  const characterized = characterizeContractLogs({
    acceptedSha,
    providerVersion: 'Google Cloud SDK 576.0.0',
    successLog: await testPrefixedText(
      contractSuccess.commandLogPath,
      'synthetic success contract log',
      ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ', 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
      semanticProviderRecordLimit(1) + 1,
    ),
    failureLog: await testPrefixedText(
      contractFailure.commandLogPath,
      'synthetic failure contract log',
      ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ', 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
      semanticProviderRecordLimit(0) + 1,
    ),
    committedFixture: committedContractFixture,
  })
  const sanitizedContractsPath = join(root, 'live-contracts.json')
  const contractReplayPath = join(root, 'contract-replay.json')
  writeFileSync(
    sanitizedContractsPath,
    `${JSON.stringify(characterized.sanitized, null, 2)}\n`,
  )
  writeFileSync(
    contractReplayPath,
    `${JSON.stringify({
      schemaVersion: 1,
      replayed: [characterized.replay],
    }, null, 2)}\n`,
  )
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    acceptedSha,
    repoRoot: process.cwd(),
    evidenceRoot: root,
    buildServiceAccount: 'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
    runtimeServiceAccount: 'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
    preflightPath: join(root, 'preflight.json'),
    effectiveIamPath: join(root, 'effective-iam.json'),
    quotaPath: join(root, 'quota.json'),
    contracts: {
      sanitizedPath: sanitizedContractsPath,
      replayPath: contractReplayPath,
      committedFixturePath: committedContractFixturePath,
    },
    artifacts,
  }
}

it('requires every retained public cloud command and exact pre-phases', async () => {
  const manifest = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)).resolves.toBeDefined()
  for (const command of requiredPublicCommands) {
    await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest({
      ...manifest,
      artifacts: manifest.artifacts.filter((entry) => entry.command !== command),
    })).rejects.toThrow(`missing public command ${command}`)
  }
  expect(expectedPrePhases('coordinator-check')).toEqual(['typecheck'])
  expect(expectedPrePhases('coordinator-verify')).toEqual(['typecheck', 'build'])
  expect(expectedPrePhases('coordinator-test-all')).toEqual([])
})

it.each([
  'buildResourceName',

  'jobGeneration',
] as const)('rejects expectedResources missing %s', async (field) => {

  delete (run.expectedResources as Partial<
    NonNullable<typeof run.expectedResources>
  >)[field]
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)).rejects.toThrow()
})

it('keeps the Task 21.7 manifest generator valid plain JavaScript', () => {
  const plan = readFileSync(
    'docs/plans/2026-08-15-kata-sbnj.md',
    'utf8',
  )
  const heading = '### 21.7 ' + 'Validate one exact manifest'
  expect(plan.split(heading)).toHaveLength(2)
  const section = plan.split(heading, 2)[1]
  const start = "node <<'NODE'\n"
  expect(section.split(start)).toHaveLength(2)
  const body = section.split(start, 2)[1].split('\nNODE\n', 1)[0]
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'manifest-generator-'))
  try {
    const script = join(root, 'manifest-generator.cjs')
    const evidence = join(root, 'evidence')
    writeFileSync(script, `${body}\n`)
    const checked = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['--check', script], {
      encoding: 'utf8',
    })
    expect(checked.status, checked.stderr).toBe(0)
    const head = 'a'.repeat(40)
    const env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {
      ...process.env,
      EVIDENCE: evidence,
      REPO_ROOT: process.cwd(),
      ACCEPTED_SHA: head,
      FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
        'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
      FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT:
        'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com',
    }
    for (const [prefix, digit, generation] of [
      ['VITEST', '1', '101'],
      ['E2E', '2', '102'],
    ] as const) {
      const runId = digit.repeat(32)
      const job = `job-${runId}`
      const execution = `execution-${runId}`
      Object.assign(env, {
        [`${prefix}_RUN_ID`]: runId,
        [`${prefix}_HEAD`]: head,
        [`${prefix}_SOURCE_BUCKET`]: 'test-source-bucket',
        [`${prefix}_SOURCE_OBJECT`]: `source/${runId}.tar.gz`,
        [`${prefix}_SOURCE_GENERATION`]: generation,
        [`${prefix}_BUILD_CORRELATION`]: `freshell-${runId}`,
        [`${prefix}_BUILD_ID`]: `build-${runId}`,
        [`${prefix}_BUILD_RESOURCE`]:
          `projects/123456789/locations/global/builds/build-${runId}`,
        [`${prefix}_BUILD_PROJECT_ID`]: 'test-project',
        [`${prefix}_BUILD_PROJECT_NUMBER`]: '123456789',
        [`${prefix}_IMAGE_TAG`]: runId,
        [`${prefix}_DIGEST`]: `sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`,
        [`${prefix}_JOB`]: job,
        [`${prefix}_JOB_RESOURCE`]:
          `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/${job}`,
        [`${prefix}_JOB_UID`]: `job-uid-${runId}`,
        [`${prefix}_JOB_GENERATION`]: '7',
        [`${prefix}_EXECUTION`]: execution,
        [`${prefix}_EXECUTION_RESOURCE`]:
          `projects/test-project/locations/us-west1/jobs/${job}/executions/${execution}`,
        [`${prefix}_EXECUTION_UID`]: `execution-uid-${runId}`,
      })
    }
    mkdirSync(evidence, { mode: 0o700 })
    const executed = spawnSync(process.execPath, [script], {
      env,
      encoding: 'utf8',
    })
    expect(executed.status, executed.stderr).toBe(0)
    const manifest = acceptanceManifestSchema.parse(JSON.parse(
      readFileSync(join(evidence, 'manifest.json'), 'utf8'),
    ))
    expect(manifest.artifacts).toHaveLength(15)
  } finally {
    rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

it('fails real-push evidence with a duplicate push or missing intent phase', async () => {
  const duplicate = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  const duplicateArtifact = duplicate.artifacts.find(
    ({ command }) => command === 'vitest-push',
  )!
  appendFileSync(
    `${duplicateArtifact.tracePrefix}.1`,
    '\nexecve("/usr/bin/docker", ["docker","push","duplicate"], 0x0) = 0\n',
  )
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(duplicate)).rejects.toThrow(
    'one build/one push',
  )

  const missingEvent = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  const missingArtifact = missingEvent.artifacts.find(
    ({ command }) => command === 'e2e-push',
  )!
  const commandLog = (await testPrefixedText(
    missingArtifact.commandLogPath,
    'local-push event mutation',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP '],
    3,
  )).split('\n')
    .filter((line) =>
      !line.includes('"kind":"local-push"') ||
      !line.includes('"phase":"possibly-accepted"'))
    .join('\n')
  writeFileSync(missingArtifact.commandLogPath, commandLog)
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(missingEvent)).rejects.toThrow(
    'intent/possibly-accepted/acquired',
  )
})

  for (const argv of [
    ['node', '/repo/node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs', 'run'],
    ['node', 'scripts/run-standard-tests.ts'],
    ['node', 'test-coordinator.ts', 'holder'],
    ['gcloud', 'run', 'jobs', 'update', 'shared'],
    ['gcloud', 'run', 'jobs', 'executions', 'list'],
    ['docker', 'buildx', 'build', '--cache-from=x'],
    ['gcloud', 'artifacts', 'versions', 'delete', 'sha256:deadbeef'],
  ]) {
    expect(() => assertSafeTrace(straceExecve(argv), true)).toThrow()
  }
  for (const syscall of [
    'openat(AT_FDCWD, "/repo/.git/freshell-test-coordinator/holder.json", O_WRONLY)',
    'connect(9, {sa_family=AF_UNIX, sun_path="/run/user/1000/frt-123.sock"}, 110)',
    'bind(9, {sa_family=AF_UNIX, sun_path="/tmp/f-123.sock"}, 110)',
    'listen(9, 511)',
  ]) {
    expect(() => assertNoCoordinatorEffects([syscall])).toThrow()
  }
})
```

The remaining REDs mutate one exact artifact at a time and prove every
fail-closed validator branch:

```ts
async function rewriteArtifactReceipt(
  artifact: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>['artifacts'][number],
  mutate: (receipt: OwnershipReceipt) => void,
): Promise<void> {
  const line = (await testPrefixedText(
    artifact.receiptLogPath,
    'receipt rewrite input',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )).trimEnd()
  const receipt = JSON.parse(
    line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length),
  ) as OwnershipReceipt
  mutate(receipt)
  writeFileSync(
    artifact.receiptLogPath,
    `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`,
  )
}

it.each([
  ['unreadable log', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    manifest.artifacts[0].receiptLogPath = join(manifest.evidenceRoot, 'missing.log')
  }],
  ['stale SHA', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    manifest.artifacts[0].acceptedSha = 'b'.repeat(40)
  }],
  ['wrong prephases', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    manifest.artifacts.find(({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-check')!
      .expectedPrePhases = []
  }],
  ['missing suite', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const target = manifest.artifacts.find(
      ({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test',
    )!
    await rewriteArtifactReceipt(target, (receipt) => {
      receipt.parts = receipt.parts.filter(({ partId }) =>
        !partId.startsWith('electron:'))
    })
  }],
  ['missing shard', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const target = manifest.artifacts.find(
      ({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test',
    )!
    await rewriteArtifactReceipt(target, (receipt) => {
      receipt.parts = receipt.parts.filter(({ partId }) => partId !== 'server:4/4')
    })
  }],
  ['wrong task count', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const target = manifest.artifacts.find(
      ({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test',
    )!
    await rewriteArtifactReceipt(target, (receipt) => {
      receipt.execution.counters = {
        succeededCount: 3,
        failedCount: 0,
        cancelledCount: 0,
        runningCount: 0,
      }
    })
  }],
  ['forged manifest expectation', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const target = manifest.artifacts.find(
      ({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test',
    )!
    ;(target as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).expectedParts = ['client:1/4']
  }],
  ['wrong extracted resource', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const run = manifest.artifacts.find(({ command }) => command === 'vitest-run')!
    const { kind: _kind, ...expected } = extractReceiptIdentity(
      fixtureReceipt(2, 'vitest-run'),
      'vitest',
    )
    run.expectedResources = { ...expected, runId: 'f'.repeat(32) }
  }],
  ['cleanup ambiguity', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const target = manifest.artifacts[0]
    writeFileSync(target.providerStatePath, JSON.stringify({
      schemaVersion: 1, job: 'unknown', sourceGeneration: 'absent', tag: 'absent',
      digest: 'retained', tempRoot: 'absent', dockerConfig: 'absent',
      localImages: 'absent',
    }))
  }],
  ['wrong cleanup disposition', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const target = manifest.artifacts[0]
    const line = (await testPrefixedText(
      target.receiptLogPath,
      'cleanup receipt mutation',
      ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
      1,
    )).trimEnd()
    const receipt = JSON.parse(line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length))
    const source = receipt.cleanup.find(({ kind }: { kind: string }) => kind === 'source')
    source.outcome = 'absent'
    writeFileSync(
      target.receiptLogPath,
      `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`,
    )
  }],
] as const)('rejects %s evidence', async (_name, mutate) => {
  const manifest = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  await mutate(manifest)
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)).rejects.toThrow()
})

it('rejects cross-run identity reuse and non-overlapping required intervals', async () => {
  const manifest = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  const runs = manifest.artifacts.filter(({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test')
  const first = await testPrefixedText(
    runs[0].receiptLogPath,
    'first concurrent receipt',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )
  writeFileSync(runs[1].receiptLogPath, first)
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)).rejects.toThrow(/distinct|identity/i)

  const fresh = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  const second = fresh.artifacts.filter(({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test')[1]
  const receiptText = await testPrefixedText(
    second.receiptLogPath,
    'second concurrent receipt',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )
  const receipt = JSON.parse(
    receiptText.trimEnd().slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length),
  )
  receipt.build.startTime = '2026-08-16T01:00:00.000Z'
  receipt.build.finishTime = '2026-08-16T01:05:00.000Z'
  receipt.execution.startTime = '2026-08-16T01:06:00.000Z'
  receipt.execution.completionTime = '2026-08-16T01:10:00.000Z'
  writeFileSync(second.receiptLogPath, `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}\n`)
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(fresh)).rejects.toThrow(/overlap/i)
})

it('rejects cleanup that names any identity from the concurrent peer', async () => {
  const manifest = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  const runs = manifest.artifacts.filter(({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test')
  const leftLine = (await testPrefixedText(
    runs[0].receiptLogPath,
    'left concurrent receipt',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )).trimEnd()
  const rightLine = (await testPrefixedText(
    runs[1].receiptLogPath,
    'right concurrent receipt',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )).trimEnd()
  const left = JSON.parse(leftLine.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length))
  const right = JSON.parse(rightLine.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length))
  left.cleanup[0].identity = right.build.id
  writeFileSync(
    runs[0].receiptLogPath,
    `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(left)}\n`,
  )
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)).rejects.toThrow('Cross-run')
})

it('parses pointer-form strace and rejects truncated or malformed argv', async () => {
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'trace-parse-'))
  const prefix = join(root, 'run.trace')
  writeFileSync(`${prefix}.7`, [
    '123 execve("/usr/bin/npm", ["npm", "run", "check"], 0x7ffd) = 0',
    '123 openat(AT_FDCWD, "/tmp/value", O_RDONLY) = 3',
  ].join('\n'))
  expect((await parseTraceFiles(prefix)).execs[0].argv).toEqual(['npm', 'run', 'check'])
  writeFileSync(`${prefix}.7`, 'execve("/usr/bin/npm", ["npm", ...], 0x0) = 0')
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(prefix)).rejects.toThrow('truncated')
})

function traceLimits(patch: Partial<TraceLimits>): TraceLimits {
  return { ...defaultTraceLimits, ...patch }
}

it('rejects oversized regular files and files growing after initial stat', async () => {
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'bounded-evidence-'))
  const oversized = join(root, 'oversized.json')
  writeFileSync(oversized, '12345')
  await expect(readBoundedRegularFile(oversized, {
    label: 'oversized regular evidence',
    maxBytes: 4,
  })).rejects.toThrow('exceeds 4')

  const growing = join(root, 'growing.log')
  writeFileSync(growing, 'a')
  await expect(readBoundedRegularFile(growing, {
    label: 'growing evidence',
    maxBytes: 64,
    afterInitialStatForTest: async () => {
      appendFileSync(growing, 'b')
    },
  })).rejects.toThrow('changed while it was read')
})

it('bounds prefix record count, selected bytes, total lines, and line bytes before parse', async () => {
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'bounded-prefix-'))
  const path = join(root, 'records.log')
  writeFileSync(path, 'KEEP one\nKEEP two\nKEEP three\n')
  const base = {
    label: 'prefix evidence',
    maxBytes: 1024,
    maxLineBytes: 64,
    maxLines: 16,
    maxSelectedRecords: 8,
    maxSelectedBytes: 1024,
    select: (line: string) => line.startsWith('KEEP '),
  }
  await expect(readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    ...base,
    maxSelectedRecords: 2,
  })).rejects.toThrow('selected records exceed 2')
  await expect(readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    ...base,
    maxSelectedBytes: 7,
  })).rejects.toThrow('selected bytes exceed 7')
  await expect(readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    ...base,
    maxLines: 2,
  })).rejects.toThrow('line count exceeds 2')
  await expect(readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    ...base,
    maxLineBytes: 7,
  })).rejects.toThrow('line exceeds 7 bytes')
})

it('bounds trace files, per-file/aggregate bytes, lines, execs, and syscalls', async () => {
  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'bounded-trace-'))
  const tooMany = join(root, 'many.trace')
  for (let index = 0; index < 3; index += 1) {
    writeFileSync(`${tooMany}.${index}`, 'openat(AT_FDCWD, "/tmp/x", O_RDONLY) = 3\n')
  }
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(tooMany, traceLimits({
    maxFiles: 2,
  }))).rejects.toThrow('file count exceeds 2')

  const perFile = join(root, 'per-file.trace')
  writeFileSync(`${perFile}.1`, 'x'.repeat(65))
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(perFile, traceLimits({
    maxFileBytes: 64,
  }))).rejects.toThrow('exceeds 64')

  const aggregate = join(root, 'aggregate.trace')
  writeFileSync(`${aggregate}.1`, 'openat(AT_FDCWD, "/tmp/one", O_RDONLY) = 3\n')
  writeFileSync(`${aggregate}.2`, 'openat(AT_FDCWD, "/tmp/two", O_RDONLY) = 3\n')
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(aggregate, traceLimits({
    maxFileBytes: 128,
    maxAggregateBytes: 64,
  }))).rejects.toThrow('aggregate bytes exceed 64')

  const lines = join(root, 'lines.trace')
  writeFileSync(`${lines}.1`, 'noise one\nnoise two\n')
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(lines, traceLimits({
    maxLines: 1,
  }))).rejects.toThrow('line count exceeds 1')

  const execs = join(root, 'execs.trace')
  writeFileSync(`${execs}.1`, [
    'execve("/usr/bin/npm", ["npm"], 0x0) = 0',
    'execve("/usr/bin/node", ["node"], 0x0) = 0',
  ].join('\n'))
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(execs, traceLimits({
    maxExecs: 1,
    maxSyscalls: 1,
  }))).rejects.toThrow('execs exceed 1')

  const syscalls = join(root, 'syscalls.trace')
  writeFileSync(`${syscalls}.1`, [
    'openat(AT_FDCWD, "/tmp/one", O_RDONLY) = 3',
    'openat(AT_FDCWD, "/tmp/two", O_RDONLY) = 3',
  ].join('\n'))
  await expect(parseTraceFiles(syscalls, traceLimits({
    maxExecs: 1,
    maxSyscalls: 1,
  }))).rejects.toThrow('syscalls exceed 1')
})

it('inspects exact provider state and rejects arbitrary nonzero absence', async () => {

it('captures a bounded redacted invocation-local set only under explicit characterization', () => {
  const lines: string[] = []
  try {
    vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '')
    const disabled = createProviderContractCapture([], '0'.repeat(32))
    expect(disabled).toBeUndefined()
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(
      disabled,
      'job',
      { name: 'not-emitted' },
    )
    flushProviderContractCapture(disabled, (line) => lines.push(line))
    expect(lines).toEqual([])

    vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
    const capture = createProviderContractCapture([], '1'.repeat(32))
    if (!capture) throw new Error('Expected characterized capture')
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'job', {
      name: 'projects/test/locations/us-west1/jobs/job-owned',
      uid: 'uid-owned',
      authorization: 'must-not-appear',
      nested: { accessToken: 'must-not-appear', generation: '7' },
    })
    flushProviderContractCapture(capture, (line) => lines.push(line))
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1)
    const line = lines[0]
    expect(line).toMatch(/^FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT /)
    const record = JSON.parse(
      line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT '.length),
    )
    expect(record).toEqual({
      schemaVersion: 1,
      runId: '1'.repeat(32),
      kind: 'job',
      value: {
        name: 'projects/test/locations/us-west1/jobs/job-owned',
        uid: 'uid-owned',
        nested: { generation: '7' },
      },
    })
    expect(Buffer.byteLength(line, 'utf8')).toBeLessThanOrEqual(256 * 1024)
    expect(capture.status).toBe('flushed')
    expect(capture.records.size).toBe(0)

    vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', 'yes')
    expect(() => createProviderContractCapture([], '2'.repeat(32)))
      .toThrow('must be 1 or unset')
  } finally {
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  }
})

it('discards scoped records after a flush writer failure without affecting a later capture', () => {
  vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
  try {
    const failed = createProviderContractCapture([], '3'.repeat(32))
    if (!failed) throw new Error('Expected characterized capture')
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(failed, 'job', {
      name: 'projects/test/locations/us-west1/jobs/job-failed-flush',
    })
    expect(() => flushProviderContractCapture(failed, () => {
      throw new Error('capture writer failed')
    })).toThrow('capture writer failed')
    expect(failed.status).toBe('discarded')
    expect(failed.records.size).toBe(0)

    const later = createProviderContractCapture([], '4'.repeat(32))
    if (!later) throw new Error('Expected later characterized capture')
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(later, 'job', {
      name: 'projects/test/locations/us-west1/jobs/job-later',
    })
    const lines: string[] = []
    flushProviderContractCapture(later, (line) => lines.push(line))
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1)
    discardProviderContractCapture(later)
    expect(later.status).toBe('flushed')
  } finally {
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  }
})

it('collapses 75m/35m repeated polls to bounded semantic transitions', () => {
  vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
  const lines: string[] = []
  try {
    const capture = createProviderContractCapture(
      ['server:1/1'],
      '5'.repeat(32),
    )
    if (!capture) throw new Error('Expected characterized capture')
    for (let poll = 0; poll < 4_500; poll += 1) {
      captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'build', {
        id: 'build-owned',
        status: 'PENDING',
        poll,
      })
    }
    for (let poll = 0; poll < 2_100; poll += 1) {
      captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'operation', {
        name: 'operations/run-owned',
        done: false,
        poll,
      })
      captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'execution', {
        metadata: { name: 'execution-owned' },
        status: { conditions: [], poll },
      })
    }
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'build', {
      id: 'build-owned',
      status: 'SUCCESS',
    })
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'operation', {
      name: 'operations/run-owned',
      done: true,
      response: { name: 'execution-owned' },
    })
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'execution', {
      metadata: { name: 'execution-owned' },
      status: {
        conditions: [{ type: 'Completed', status: 'True' }],
      },
    })
    captureProviderContractForAcceptance(capture, 'logEntry', {
      jsonPayload: {
        partId: 'server:1/1',
        status: 'passed',
      },
    })
    flushProviderContractCapture(capture, (line) => lines.push(line))
    expect(lines.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(semanticProviderRecordLimit(1))
    const records = lines.map((line) => JSON.parse(
      line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT '.length),
    ))
    const pendingBuild = records.find(({ kind, value }) =>
      kind === 'build' && value.status === 'PENDING')
    const pendingOperation = records.find(({ kind, value }) =>
      kind === 'operation' && value.done === false)
    const pendingExecution = records.find(({ kind, value }) =>
      kind === 'execution' && value.status.conditions.length === 0)
    if (!pendingBuild || !pendingOperation || !pendingExecution) {
      throw new Error('Slow-poll semantic transitions were not retained')
    }
    expect(pendingBuild.value.poll).toBe(4_499)
    expect(pendingOperation.value.poll).toBe(2_099)
    expect(pendingExecution.value.status.poll).toBe(2_099)
  } finally {
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  }
})

it('sanitizes raw success/failure events, validates them, and binds both receipts', async () => {
  const fixture = JSON.parse(await testBoundedText(
    'test/fixtures/cloud-run/live-contracts.json',
    'literal live contract fixture',
  ))
  const success = fixtureReceipt(1, 'vitest-run', 'succeeded', {
    runId: '1'.repeat(32),
    sourceGeneration: '1786766419256749',
    taskCount: 1,
    suites: ['server'],
  })
  const failure = fixtureReceipt(2, 'vitest-run', 'failed', {
    runId: '2'.repeat(32),
    sourceGeneration: '2786766419256749',
    taskCount: 1,
    suites: ['server'],
  })
  const event = (runId: string, kind: string, value: unknown) =>
    `FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ${JSON.stringify({
      schemaVersion: 1,
      runId,
      kind,
      value,
    })}\n`
  const successLog = [
    event(success.runId, 'build', fixture.build),
    event(success.runId, 'job', fixture.job),
    event(success.runId, 'operation', fixture.operation),
    event(success.runId, 'execution', fixture.executionSuccess),
    event(success.runId, 'logEntry', fixture.logEntrySuccess),
    `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(success)}\n`,
  ].join('')
  const failureLog = [
    event(failure.runId, 'execution', fixture.executionFailure),
    event(failure.runId, 'logEntry', fixture.logEntryFailure),
    `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(failure)}\n`,
  ].join('')
  const characterized = characterizeContractLogs({
    acceptedSha: 'a'.repeat(40),
    providerVersion: 'Google Cloud SDK 576.0.0',
    successLog,
    failureLog,
    committedFixture: fixture,
  })
  expect(characterized.replay).toMatchObject({
    failureCounters: {
      succeededCount: 0,
      failedCount: 1,
      cancelledCount: 0,
      runningCount: 0,
    },
    failureOmittedCounters: [
      'succeededCount', 'cancelledCount', 'runningCount',
    ],
  })
  expect(characterized.bindings).toEqual({
    acceptedSha: 'a'.repeat(40),
    providerVersion: 'Google Cloud SDK 576.0.0',
    success: {
      runId: '1'.repeat(32),
      buildId: `build-${'1'.repeat(32)}`,
      jobName: `job-${'1'.repeat(32)}`,
      jobUid: `job-uid-${'1'.repeat(32)}`,
      jobGeneration: String(success.job.generation),
      executionName: `execution-${'1'.repeat(32)}`,
      executionUid: `execution-uid-${'1'.repeat(32)}`,
    },
    failure: {
      runId: '2'.repeat(32),
      jobName: `job-${'2'.repeat(32)}`,
      jobGeneration: String(failure.job.generation),
      executionName: `execution-${'2'.repeat(32)}`,
      executionUid: `execution-uid-${'2'.repeat(32)}`,
    },
  })
  expect(JSON.stringify(characterized.sanitized))
    .not.toMatch(/authorization|accessToken|password|secret/i)
  const foreignEnvelope = successLog.replace(
    `"runId":"${success.runId}"`,
    `"runId":"${'9'.repeat(32)}"`,
  )
  expect(() => characterizeContractLogs({
    acceptedSha: 'a'.repeat(40),
    providerVersion: 'Google Cloud SDK 576.0.0',
    successLog: foreignEnvelope,
    failureLog,
    committedFixture: fixture,
  })).toThrow('provider record run ID differs from receipt')
  for (const invalidTimeout of ['30m', '0s', undefined] as const) {
    const invalidJob = structuredClone(fixture.job)
    if (invalidTimeout === undefined) delete invalidJob.template.template.timeout
    else invalidJob.template.template.timeout = invalidTimeout
    const invalidSuccessLog = [
      event(success.runId, 'build', fixture.build),
      event(success.runId, 'job', invalidJob),
      event(success.runId, 'operation', fixture.operation),
      event(success.runId, 'execution', fixture.executionSuccess),
      event(success.runId, 'logEntry', fixture.logEntrySuccess),
      `FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ${JSON.stringify(success)}\n`,
    ].join('')
    expect(() => characterizeContractLogs({
      acceptedSha: 'a'.repeat(40),
      providerVersion: 'Google Cloud SDK 576.0.0',
      successLog: invalidSuccessLog,
      failureLog,
      committedFixture: fixture,
    })).toThrow('success Job: expected one distinct exact job; got 0')
  }
})

it.each([

    const value = JSON.parse(await testBoundedText(
      manifest.contracts.sanitizedPath,
      'sanitized contract mutation',
    ))
    value.executionFailure.status.succeededCount = 0
    writeFileSync(
      manifest.contracts.sanitizedPath,
      `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`,
    )
  }],
  ['stale raw replay', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const value = JSON.parse(await testBoundedText(
      manifest.contracts.replayPath,
      'contract replay mutation',
    ))
    value.replayed[0].acceptedSha = 'b'.repeat(40)
    writeFileSync(manifest.contracts.replayPath, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`)
  }],
  ['committed provider-shape drift', async (manifest: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof completeAcceptanceManifest>>) => {
    const copy = join(manifest.evidenceRoot, 'committed-shape-drift.json')
    const value = JSON.parse(await testBoundedText(
      manifest.contracts.committedFixturePath,
      'committed contract mutation',

    value.job.providerUnexpectedField = true
    writeFileSync(copy, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`)
    manifest.contracts.committedFixturePath = copy
    manifest.repoRoot = manifest.evidenceRoot
  }],
] as const)('fails closed for %s', async (_name, mutate) => {
  const manifest = await completeAcceptanceManifest()
  await mutate(manifest)
  await expect(validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)).rejects.toThrow()
})
```

Also append these production-path regressions to the already-cumulative
`cloud-run-lifecycle.test.ts`. They invoke `runCloudRunLifecycle` itself; the
direct capture-object tests above do not stand in for them:

```ts
type CapturedProviderRecord = {
  schemaVersion: 1
  runId: string
  kind: 'build' | 'job' | 'operation' | 'execution' | 'logEntry'
  value: unknown
}

function capturedLifecycleStderrLines(
  calls: readonly (readonly unknown[])[],
): string[] {
  return calls.flatMap(([value]) => String(value).split('\n'))
    .filter((line) => line.length > 0)
}

function capturedProviderRecords(
  lines: readonly string[],
): CapturedProviderRecord[] {
  return lines
    .filter((line) =>
      line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT '))
    .map((line) => JSON.parse(
      line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT '.length),
    ) as CapturedProviderRecord)
}

function capturedTerminalReceipts(
  lines: readonly string[],
): OwnershipReceipt[] {
  return lines
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '))
    .map((line) => parseOwnershipReceipt(JSON.parse(
      line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '.length),
    )))
}

it('does not activate capture for pre-aborted input and characterizes the next run', async () => {
  vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
  const write = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write')
    .mockImplementation(() => true)
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    controller.abort(new Error('stop before characterization'))
    const abortedRunner = vi.fn<RunCommand>()
    await expect(runCloudRunLifecycle({
      ...BASE_INPUT,
      signal: controller.signal,
    }, abortedRunner)).rejects.toThrow('aborted before start')
    expect(abortedRunner).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(write).not.toHaveBeenCalled()

    const receipt = await runCloudRunLifecycle(
      BASE_INPUT,
      successfulLifecycleRunner('a'),
    )
    const lines = capturedLifecycleStderrLines(write.mock.calls)
    const records = capturedProviderRecords(lines)
    expect(records.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(records.every(({ runId }) => runId === receipt.runId)).toBe(true)
    expect(capturedTerminalReceipts(lines)).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ runId: receipt.runId, status: 'succeeded' }),
    ])
  } finally {
    write.mockRestore()
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  }
})

it('isolates two concurrent characterized lifecycle captures without cross-attribution', async () => {
  vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
  const write = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write')
    .mockImplementation(() => true)
  try {
    const receipts = await Promise.all([
      runCloudRunLifecycle(BASE_INPUT, successfulLifecycleRunner('b')),
      runCloudRunLifecycle(BASE_INPUT, successfulLifecycleRunner('c')),
    ])
    const lines = capturedLifecycleStderrLines(write.mock.calls)
    const records = capturedProviderRecords(lines)
    const emittedReceipts = capturedTerminalReceipts(lines)
    expect(emittedReceipts).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(new Set(records.map(({ runId }) => runId))).toEqual(
      new Set(receipts.map(({ runId }) => runId)),
    )
    for (const receipt of receipts) {
      const own = records.filter(({ runId }) => runId === receipt.runId)
      const peer = receipts.find(({ runId }) => runId !== receipt.runId)!
      expect(own.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
      expect(own.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(
        lifecycleModule.semanticProviderRecordLimit(receipt.parts.length),
      )
      const ownValues = JSON.stringify(own.map(({ value }) => value))
      expect(ownValues).toContain(receipt.runId)
      expect(ownValues).not.toContain(peer.runId)
    }
  } finally {
    write.mockRestore()
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  }
})

it.each([
  ['malformed-template', 'setup'],
  ['cleanup-source', 'cleanup'],
] as const)(
  '%s failure after capture activation releases scope before the next %s run',
  async (fault, _phase) => {
    vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
    const write = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write')
      .mockImplementation(() => true)
    try {
      await expect(runCloudRunLifecycle(
        BASE_INPUT,
        successfulLifecycleRunner('d', fault),
      )).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: 'CloudRunLifecycleError' })
      const later = await runCloudRunLifecycle(
        BASE_INPUT,
        successfulLifecycleRunner('e'),
      )
      const lines = capturedLifecycleStderrLines(write.mock.calls)
      expect(capturedTerminalReceipts(lines)).toEqual([
        expect.objectContaining({ status: 'failed' }),
        expect.objectContaining({
          runId: later.runId,
          status: 'succeeded',
        }),
      ])
      expect(capturedProviderRecords(lines).every(({ runId }) =>
        capturedTerminalReceipts(lines).some((receipt) =>
          receipt.runId === runId))).toBe(true)
    } finally {
      write.mockRestore()
      vi.unstubAllEnvs()
    }
  },
)

it('flushes one bounded receipt-bound provider set before the terminal receipt', async () => {
  vi.stubEnv('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS', '1')
  const write = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write')
    .mockImplementation(() => true)
  try {
    const receipt = await runCloudRunLifecycle(
      BASE_INPUT,
      successfulLifecycleRunner('f'),
    )
    const lines = capturedLifecycleStderrLines(write.mock.calls)
    const records = capturedProviderRecords(lines)
      .filter(({ runId }) => runId === receipt.runId)
    expect(records.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(records.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(
      lifecycleModule.semanticProviderRecordLimit(receipt.parts.length),
    )
    expect(new Set(records.map(({ kind }) => kind))).toEqual(new Set([
      'build', 'job', 'operation', 'execution', 'logEntry',
    ]))
    const operationNames = records
      .filter(({ kind }) => kind === 'operation')
      .map(({ value }) => String(
        (value as { name?: unknown }).name ?? '',
      ))
    expect(operationNames.some((name) => name.endsWith('/run-op'))).toBe(true)
    expect(operationNames.some((name) => name.endsWith('/delete-op'))).toBe(true)
    const receiptIndex = lines.findIndex((line) =>
      line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ') &&
      line.includes(receipt.runId))
    const recordIndexes = lines.flatMap((line, index) =>
      line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ') &&
      line.includes(`"runId":"${receipt.runId}"`)
        ? [index]
        : [])
    expect(receiptIndex).toBeGreaterThan(-1)
    expect(recordIndexes.every((index) => index < receiptIndex)).toBe(true)
  } finally {
    write.mockRestore()
    vi.unstubAllEnvs()
  }
})
```

Create `scripts/cloud-run/evidence-io.ts` first. Every Task 11 evidence reader
uses this one open-file stream primitive. It verifies a regular file and its
declared size before allocating content, limits bytes while reading, and checks
the same open file again after EOF. The optional callback is only deterministic
test synchronization for the grow-after-stat RED; production callers omit it:

```ts
import { open, type FileHandle } from 'node:fs/promises'

export type BoundedRegularFileOptions = {
  label: string
  maxBytes: number
  afterInitialStatForTest?: () => Promise<void>
}

export type BoundedLineOptions = BoundedRegularFileOptions & {
  maxLineBytes: number
  maxLines: number
  maxSelectedRecords: number
  maxSelectedBytes: number
  select: (line: string) => boolean
}

export type BoundedSelectedLines = {
  records: string[]
  totalLines: number
  totalBytes: number
}

type OpenRegularFile = {
  handle: FileHandle
  initialSize: number
  chunks: AsyncGenerator<Buffer, void, void>
}

async function openBoundedRegularFile(
  path: string,
  options: BoundedRegularFileOptions,
): Promise<OpenRegularFile> {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(options.maxBytes) || options.maxBytes < 1) {
    throw new Error(`${options.label}: maxBytes must be a positive safe integer`)
  }
  const handle = await open(path, 'r')
  try {
    const initial = await handle.stat()
    if (!initial.isFile()) {
      throw new Error(`${options.label}: evidence path is not a regular file`)
    }
    if (initial.size > options.maxBytes) {
      throw new Error(
        `${options.label}: ${initial.size} bytes exceeds ${options.maxBytes}`,
      )
    }
    await options.afterInitialStatForTest?.()
    async function* chunks(): AsyncGenerator<Buffer, void, void> {
      const scratch = Buffer.allocUnsafe(
        Math.min(64 * 1024, options.maxBytes + 1),
      )
      let actualBytes = 0
      while (true) {
        const { bytesRead } = await handle.read(
          scratch,
          0,
          scratch.byteLength,
          null,
        )
        if (bytesRead === 0) break
        actualBytes += bytesRead
        if (actualBytes > options.maxBytes) {
          throw new Error(
            `${options.label}: streamed bytes exceed ${options.maxBytes}`,
          )
        }
        yield Buffer.from(scratch.subarray(0, bytesRead))
      }
      const final = await handle.stat()
      if (!final.isFile() ||
          final.size !== initial.size ||
          final.size !== actualBytes) {
        throw new Error(
          `${options.label}: regular file changed while it was read`,
        )
      }
    }
    return { handle, initialSize: initial.size, chunks: chunks() }
  } catch (error) {
    await handle.close()
    throw error
  }
}

export async function readBoundedRegularFile(
  path: string,
  options: BoundedRegularFileOptions,
): Promise<string> {
  const opened = await openBoundedRegularFile(path, options)
  const chunks: Buffer[] = []
  let actualBytes = 0
  try {
    for await (const chunk of opened.chunks) {
      actualBytes += chunk.byteLength
      chunks.push(chunk)
    }
    const text = Buffer.concat(chunks, actualBytes).toString('utf8')
    if (Buffer.byteLength(text, 'utf8') !== actualBytes ||
        actualBytes !== opened.initialSize) {
      throw new Error(`${options.label}: decoded byte count changed`)
    }
    return text
  } finally {
    await opened.handle.close()
  }
}

export async function readBoundedSelectedLines(
  path: string,
  options: BoundedLineOptions,
): Promise<BoundedSelectedLines> {
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({
    maxLineBytes: options.maxLineBytes,
    maxLines: options.maxLines,
    maxSelectedRecords: options.maxSelectedRecords,
    maxSelectedBytes: options.maxSelectedBytes,
  })) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 1) {
      throw new Error(`${options.label}: ${name} must be positive`)
    }
  }
  const opened = await openBoundedRegularFile(path, options)
  const selected: string[] = []
  let pending: Buffer = Buffer.alloc(0)
  let lineCount = 0
  let selectedBytes = 0
  const consume = (lineBytes: Buffer): void => {
    lineCount += 1
    if (lineCount > options.maxLines) {
      throw new Error(`${options.label}: line count exceeds ${options.maxLines}`)
    }
    if (lineBytes.byteLength > options.maxLineBytes) {
      throw new Error(
        `${options.label}: line exceeds ${options.maxLineBytes} bytes`,
      )
    }
    const line = lineBytes.toString('utf8')
    if (!options.select(line)) return
    selectedBytes += lineBytes.byteLength
    if (selected.length + 1 > options.maxSelectedRecords) {
      throw new Error(
        `${options.label}: selected records exceed ${options.maxSelectedRecords}`,
      )
    }
    if (selectedBytes > options.maxSelectedBytes) {
      throw new Error(
        `${options.label}: selected bytes exceed ${options.maxSelectedBytes}`,
      )
    }
    selected.push(line)
  }
  try {
    for await (const chunk of opened.chunks) {
      pending = pending.length === 0
        ? chunk
        : Buffer.concat([pending, chunk], pending.length + chunk.length)
      if (pending.length > options.maxLineBytes &&
          !pending.includes(0x0a)) {
        throw new Error(
          `${options.label}: unterminated line exceeds ${options.maxLineBytes} bytes`,
        )
      }
      let newline = pending.indexOf(0x0a)
      while (newline >= 0) {
        const end = newline > 0 && pending[newline - 1] === 0x0d
          ? newline - 1
          : newline
        consume(pending.subarray(0, end))
        pending = pending.subarray(newline + 1)
        newline = pending.indexOf(0x0a)
      }
    }
    if (pending.length > 0) consume(pending)
    return {
      records: selected,
      totalLines: lineCount,
      totalBytes: opened.initialSize,
    }
  } finally {
    await opened.handle.close()
  }
}
```

Replace the opt-in acceptance capture in `lifecycle.ts` with this scoped
semantic object. It is not a provider seam or run history: it cannot issue a
command, accepts no URL/method, belongs to exactly one lifecycle invocation,
is disabled unless the exact value `1` is present, and flushes a bounded
semantic set. There is no module-global active slot or mutable capture flag.
The first relevant Build, Operation, and Execution state is represented
immediately; transitions and terminal states get distinct keys, while repeated
polls replace the same semantic-state record in memory instead of appending
another event:

```ts
type ProviderContractKind =
  | 'build'
  | 'job'
  | 'operation'
  | 'execution'
  | 'logEntry'

type ProviderContractRecord = {
  schemaVersion: 1
  runId: string
  kind: ProviderContractKind
  value: unknown
}

export type ProviderContractCapture = {
  readonly runId: string
  status: 'active' | 'flushed' | 'discarded'
  expectedParts: Set<string>
  records: Map<string, { line: string; bytes: number }>
  identities: {
    build: Set<string>
    job: Set<string>
    operation: Set<string>
    execution: Set<string>
  }
  unknownResultParts: Set<string>
  maxRecords: number
  maxBytes: number
  bytes: number
}

const BUILD_SEMANTIC_STATES = 10
const JOB_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES = 1
const OPERATION_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES = 2
const OPERATION_STATES_PER_IDENTITY = 3
const EXECUTION_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES = 1
const EXECUTION_STATES_PER_IDENTITY = 4
const FIXED_SEMANTIC_PROVIDER_RECORDS =
  BUILD_SEMANTIC_STATES +
  JOB_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES +
  OPERATION_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES * OPERATION_STATES_PER_IDENTITY +
  EXECUTION_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES * EXECUTION_STATES_PER_IDENTITY
const FIXED_PROVIDER_RECORD_BYTES = 256 * 1024
const RESULT_PROVIDER_RECORD_BYTES = 64 * 1024

function providerContractCaptureEnabled(): boolean {
  const value = process.env.FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS
  if (value === undefined || value === '') return false
  if (value === '1') return true
  throw new Error('FRESHELL_CLOUD_CAPTURE_CONTRACTS must be 1 or unset')
}

function redactProviderContract(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(redactProviderContract)
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
        .filter(([key]) =>
          !/(?:authorization|access[_-]?token|identity[_-]?token|password|secret|stdin)/i
            .test(key))
        .map(([key, child]) => [key, redactProviderContract(child)]),
    )
  }
  return value
}

export function semanticProviderRecordLimit(
  expectedPartCount: number,
): number {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expectedPartCount) ||
      expectedPartCount < 0 ||
      expectedPartCount > 96) {
    throw new Error('Semantic provider expected-part count is outside 0..96')
  }
  return FIXED_SEMANTIC_PROVIDER_RECORDS + expectedPartCount + 1
}

export function semanticProviderByteLimit(expectedPartCount: number): number {
  semanticProviderRecordLimit(expectedPartCount)
  return FIXED_SEMANTIC_PROVIDER_RECORDS * FIXED_PROVIDER_RECORD_BYTES +
    (expectedPartCount + 1) * RESULT_PROVIDER_RECORD_BYTES
}

export function createProviderContractCapture(
  expectedParts: readonly string[],
  runId: string,
): ProviderContractCapture | undefined {
  if (!providerContractCaptureEnabled()) return undefined
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(runId)) {
    throw new Error('Provider contract capture requires the invocation run ID')
  }
  if (new Set(expectedParts).size !== expectedParts.length) {
    throw new Error('Semantic provider capture expected parts are duplicated')
  }
  return {
    runId,
    status: 'active',
    expectedParts: new Set(expectedParts),
    records: new Map(),
    identities: {
      build: new Set(),
      job: new Set(),
      operation: new Set(),
      execution: new Set(),
    },
    unknownResultParts: new Set(),
    maxRecords: semanticProviderRecordLimit(expectedParts.length),
    maxBytes: semanticProviderByteLimit(expectedParts.length),
    bytes: 0,
  }
}

function providerObject(value: unknown, label: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} provider contract is not an object`)
  }
  return value as Record<string, unknown>
}

function addProviderIdentity(
  state: ProviderContractCapture,
  kind: keyof ProviderContractCapture['identities'],
  identity: string,
  maximum: number,
): void {
  const identities = state.identities[kind]
  if (!identities.has(identity) && identities.size >= maximum) {
    throw new Error(
      `Semantic provider capture has too many ${kind} identities`,
    )
  }
  identities.add(identity)
}

function semanticProviderKey(
  state: ProviderContractCapture,
  kind: ProviderContractKind,
  value: unknown,
): string {
  const object = providerObject(value, kind)
  if (kind === 'build') {
    const id = String(object.id ?? '')
    const status = String(object.status ?? '')
    addProviderIdentity(state, 'build', id, 1)
    return `build:${id}:${status}`
  }
  if (kind === 'job') {
    const name = String(object.name ?? '')
    addProviderIdentity(state, 'job', name, JOB_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES)
    return `job:${name}`
  }
  if (kind === 'operation') {
    const name = String(object.name ?? '')
    addProviderIdentity(
      state,
      'operation',
      name,
      OPERATION_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES,
    )
    const phase = object.done !== true
      ? 'pending'
      : object.error === undefined
        ? 'done-success'
        : 'done-error'
    return `operation:${name}:${phase}`
  }
  if (kind === 'execution') {
    const metadata = providerObject(object.metadata, 'Execution metadata')
    const status = providerObject(object.status, 'Execution status')
    const name = String(metadata.name ?? '')
    addProviderIdentity(
      state,
      'execution',
      name,
      EXECUTION_SEMANTIC_IDENTITIES,
    )
    const conditions = Array.isArray(status.conditions)
      ? status.conditions
      : []
    const completed = conditions.find((candidate) =>
      providerObject(candidate, 'Execution condition').type === 'Completed')
    const phase = completed === undefined
      ? 'pending'
      : String(providerObject(completed, 'Completed condition').status)
    if (!['pending', 'True', 'False', 'Unknown'].includes(phase)) {
      throw new Error(`Unsupported Execution semantic state ${phase}`)
    }
    return `execution:${name}:${phase}`
  }
  const payload = providerObject(object.jsonPayload, 'LogEntry jsonPayload')
  const partId = String(payload.partId ?? '')
  if (!state.expectedParts.has(partId)) {
    if (!state.unknownResultParts.has(partId) &&
        state.unknownResultParts.size >= 1) {
      throw new Error('Semantic provider capture has more than one unknown result')
    }
    state.unknownResultParts.add(partId)
  }
  return `logEntry:${partId}:${String(payload.status ?? '')}`
}

export function captureProviderContractForAcceptance(
  state: ProviderContractCapture | undefined,
  kind: ProviderContractKind,
  value: unknown,
): void {
  if (!state) return
  if (state.status !== 'active') {
    throw new Error(`Provider contract capture is ${state.status}`)
  }
  const record: ProviderContractRecord = {
    schemaVersion: 1 as const,
    runId: state.runId,
    kind,
    value: redactProviderContract(value),
  }
  const line =
    `FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ${JSON.stringify(record)}\n`
  const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(line, 'utf8')
  const perRecordLimit = kind === 'logEntry'
    ? RESULT_PROVIDER_RECORD_BYTES
    : FIXED_PROVIDER_RECORD_BYTES
  if (bytes > perRecordLimit) {
    throw new Error(
      `Raw ${kind} provider contract exceeds ${perRecordLimit} bytes`,
    )
  }
  const key = semanticProviderKey(state, kind, record.value)
  const previous = state.records.get(key)
  const nextBytes = state.bytes - (previous?.bytes ?? 0) + bytes
  if (previous === undefined &&
      state.records.size >= state.maxRecords) {
    throw new Error(
      `Semantic provider records exceed ${state.maxRecords}`,
    )
  }
  if (nextBytes > state.maxBytes) {
    throw new Error(`Semantic provider bytes exceed ${state.maxBytes}`)
  }
  state.records.set(key, { line, bytes })
  state.bytes = nextBytes
}

function releaseProviderContractCapture(
  capture: ProviderContractCapture,
  status: 'flushed' | 'discarded',
): void {
  capture.records.clear()
  capture.expectedParts.clear()
  for (const identities of Object.values(capture.identities)) identities.clear()
  capture.unknownResultParts.clear()
  capture.bytes = 0
  capture.status = status
}

export function flushProviderContractCapture(
  capture: ProviderContractCapture | undefined,
  write: (line: string) => unknown = (line) => process.stderr.write(line),
): void {
  if (!capture) return
  if (capture.status !== 'active') {
    throw new Error(`Provider contract capture is ${capture.status}`)
  }
  let completed = false
  try {
    for (const [, { line }] of [...capture.records].sort(([left], [right]) =>
      left.localeCompare(right))) {
      write(line)
    }
    completed = true
  } finally {
    releaseProviderContractCapture(
      capture,
      completed ? 'flushed' : 'discarded',
    )
  }
}

export function discardProviderContractCapture(
  capture: ProviderContractCapture | undefined,
): void {
  if (capture?.status === 'active') {
    releaseProviderContractCapture(capture, 'discarded')
  }
}
```

Thread one optional `providerCapture?: ProviderContractCapture` argument
explicitly through the invocation's provider path; never put it on
`CloudRunLifecycleInput`, in `OwnedResources`, or in a module-global slot.
Add it as the final parameter to `waitForBuildTerminal`,
`startBuildFromGeneration`, `finishBuildFromGeneration`,
`reconcileBuildIntent`, `cleanupBuild`, `getExactRunJob`, `createAndVerifyJob`,
`getExactRunOperation`, `waitForRunOperation`, `deleteExactRunJob`,
`containAmbiguousRun`, `runExactJobOnce`, `waitForExecutionTerminal`,
`readExactExecutionLogs`, `cleanupJob`, `cleanupOwnedResources`, and
`cleanupWithIndependentBudget`. For `readExactBuild`, add it after the existing
`timeoutMs`; add `providerCapture?: ProviderContractCapture` to
`ResultCollectionInput`. Every intermediate caller passes the same object
unchanged, including ambiguous-run containment and normal Job cleanup.
The final parameter order is binding:

```text
readExactBuild(input, authority, id, runCommand, timeoutMs = input.budgets.commandMs, providerCapture?)
waitForBuildTerminal(input, authority, id, runCommand, providerCapture?)
startBuildFromGeneration(input, authority, snapshot, identity, source, template, runCommand, window = correlationWindow(), providerCapture?)
finishBuildFromGeneration(input, identity, correlatedBuild, runCommand, providerCapture?)
reconcileBuildIntent(input, owned, failures, runCommand, providerCapture?)
cleanupBuild(input, owned, runCommand, providerCapture?)
getExactRunJob(input, jobName, runCommand, providerCapture?)
createAndVerifyJob(input, identity, tempRoot, immutableImage, expectedEnv, runCommand, window = correlationWindow(), providerCapture?)
getExactRunOperation(input, operationId, runCommand, providerCapture?)
waitForRunOperation(input, name, runCommand, providerCapture?)
deleteExactRunJob(input, acknowledged, runCommand, providerCapture?)
containAmbiguousRun(input, acknowledged, runCommand, providerCapture?)
runExactJobOnce(input, job, runCommand, providerCapture?)
waitForExecutionTerminal(input, execution, projectNumber, runCommand, providerCapture?)
readExactExecutionLogs(input, runCommand = defaultRunCommand, providerCapture?)
cleanupJob(input, owned, runCommand, providerCapture?)
cleanupOwnedResources(input, owned, runCommand, providerCapture?)
cleanupWithIndependentBudget(input, owned, runCommand, providerCapture?)
```
