These declarations and every intermediate call remain in
`scripts/cloud-run/lifecycle.ts`, which is included by `tsconfig.scripts.json`;
the complete Task 11 `npm run typecheck:scripts` therefore rejects any omitted,
misordered, or incompatible capture argument without exporting private helper
functions solely for a test.

Apply these exact recording additions. Capture occurs only *after* each
existing production schema/identity check. Execution capture occurs after its
exact identity/configuration check on every poll, so the pending/Unknown state
in the bounded semantic test is a real production path. Polls in the same
semantic state replace their prior record; transitions and terminal states
remain distinct:

```ts
// readExactBuild, immediately before `return row`
captureProviderContractForAcceptance(providerCapture, 'build', row)

// startBuildFromGeneration, after full correlation/config validation and
// immediately before returning the correlated identity
const correlated = correlatedBuildIdentity(
  row, input, authority, identity, request,
)
captureProviderContractForAcceptance(providerCapture, 'build', row)
return correlated

// getExactRunJob, after runJobSchema.parse and expected-name validation
captureProviderContractForAcceptance(providerCapture, 'job', job)

// getExactRunOperation, after operationSchema.parse and exact-ID validation
captureProviderContractForAcceptance(providerCapture, 'operation', operation)

// waitForExecutionTerminal, after exact identity/config validation on every poll
captureProviderContractForAcceptance(providerCapture, 'execution', raw)

// readExactExecutionLogs, replace its final parse/return with:
const rows = z.array(loggingEntrySchema).max(input.limit).parse(decoded)
for (const row of rows) {
  captureProviderContractForAcceptance(providerCapture, 'logEntry', row)
}
return rows
```

A failed Execution needs its raw LogEntry too, so add this final bounded
collector and invoke it after terminal reconciliation but before throwing
`Exact Execution failed`. It uses the already-created invocation-local object;
it never rereads the environment or activates capture:

```ts
async function captureFailedResultContract(
  collection: ResultCollectionInput,
): Promise<void> {
  const providerCapture = collection.providerCapture
  if (!providerCapture) return
  const { input, jobName, executionName, runCommand } = collection
  const expected = [...input.expectedParts].sort()
  const deadline = Date.now() + input.budgets.resultReadMs
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    const rows = await readExactExecutionLogs({
      account: input.account,
      projectId: input.projectId,
      runRegion: input.runRegion,
      jobName,
      executionName,
      limit: expected.length + 1,
      budgets: input.budgets,
      signal: input.signal,
    }, runCommand, providerCapture)
    const failed = rows.map((entry) => {
      if (entry.jsonPayload === undefined) return undefined
      const part = cloudPartResultSchema.parse(entry.jsonPayload)
      if (entry.resource.labels.project_id !== input.projectId ||
          entry.resource.labels.location !== input.runRegion ||
          entry.resource.labels.job_name !== jobName ||
          entry.labels['run.googleapis.com/execution_name'] !== executionName ||
          part.kind !== input.kind ||
          part.runId !== collection.runId ||
          part.execution !== executionName ||
          part.taskCount !== input.taskCount ||
          part.taskIndex >= input.taskCount ||
          part.taskAttempt !== 0 ||
          part.status !== 'failed' ||
          part.exitCode === 0) {
        throw new Error(`Foreign or non-failed contract result ${part.partId}`)
      }
      return part
    }).filter((part): part is CloudPartResult => part !== undefined)
    const actual = failed.map(({ partId }) => partId).sort()
    if (new Set(actual).size !== actual.length) {
      throw new Error('Duplicate failed contract result')
    }
    if (JSON.stringify(actual) === JSON.stringify(expected)) return
    if (actual.some((part) => !expected.includes(part))) {
      throw new Error('Unknown failed contract result')
    }
    await waitForRetry(
      Math.min(2_000, Math.max(1, deadline - Date.now())),
      input.signal,
    )
  }
  throw new Error('Failed result contract did not arrive within result-read budget')
}

// runCloudRunLifecycle, after acquireTerminalExecution:
if (!executionSucceeded(terminal)) {
  await captureFailedResultContract({
    input: operationInput,
    runId: identity.runId,
    jobName: identity.jobName,
    executionName: execution.name,
    runCommand,
    providerCapture,
  })
  throw new Error('Exact Execution failed')
}
```

The full replacement lifecycle body below contains the corresponding
Build/Job/run/result calls. Pass `providerCapture` through both Build phases,
`readExactBuild`/`waitForBuildTerminal`, `reconcileBuildIntent`,
`cleanupBuild`, and terminal-product reconciliation. In `createAndVerifyJob`,
pass it to `getExactRunJob`. In
`runExactJobOnce`, pass it through `waitForRunOperation`,
`containAmbiguousRun`, and every nested `getExactRunJob` /
`deleteExactRunJob`; those functions pass it to every exact Operation and Job
read. In `collectExactPartResults`, pass
`collection.providerCapture` to `readExactExecutionLogs`. The cleanup chain
passes it from `cleanupWithIndependentBudget` through `cleanupOwnedResources`
and `cleanupJob` to the same exact Job/Operation functions. Calls outside a
lifecycle cleanup and acceptance inspection omit the optional argument and
therefore cannot record into another invocation.

Task 11 preserves Task 6's cleanup ordering exactly: `cleanupBuild` exact-reads
with `providerCapture`, reconstructs and compares immutable ownership before
the one exact cancel, then requires a post-cancel exact-ID terminal read before
source cleanup. Provider capture does not authorize cancellation and may not
move that immutable comparison after the mutation. The cumulative Task 6/7
drift, full-argv, monotonic-rejection, and structured-index tests remain in the
Task 11 GREEN command and must pass unchanged. The active-Execution exact-argv
regression also remains unchanged: cancellation includes `--quiet`, supplies no
synthetic stdin, occurs once before exact terminal describe, and retains its
bounded one-second guard. Provider capture may be threaded through the waiter
but must not reorder or replace cancellation policy.

Add this finalizer. It catches an unexpected cleanup rejection, flushes either
the bounded success or bounded failure artifact before the terminal receipt,
and records a flush failure with the cleanup failures. `flush` itself releases
all Maps/Sets even when its writer throws:

```ts
async function finalizeCloudRunLifecycle(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  owned: OwnedResources,
  primary: unknown | undefined,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
  providerCapture: ProviderContractCapture | undefined,
): Promise<OwnershipReceipt> {
  const cleanupErrors: unknown[] = []
  try {
    cleanupErrors.push(...await cleanupWithIndependentBudget(
      input,
      owned,
      runCommand,
      providerCapture,
    ))
  } catch (error) {
    cleanupErrors.push(error)
  }
  try {
    flushProviderContractCapture(providerCapture)
  } catch (error) {
    cleanupErrors.push(error)
  }
  if (primary !== undefined || cleanupErrors.length > 0) {
    owned.receipt.status = 'failed'
    owned.receipt.failure = {
      ...(primary === undefined ? {} : { primary: errorMessage(primary) }),
      causes: primary === undefined ? [] : errorChain(primary),
      cleanup: cleanupErrors.flatMap(errorChain),
    }
  }
  emitTerminalReceipt(process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr), owned.receipt)
  if (primary !== undefined || cleanupErrors.length > 0) {
    throw new CloudRunLifecycleError(primary, cleanupErrors, owned.receipt)
  }
  return owned.receipt
}
```

Finally, restructure `runCloudRunLifecycle` in this exact order:

1. Keep the already-aborted guard as the first executable statement. It throws
   before run ID allocation, capture creation, command execution, ownership
   output, or terminal receipt.
2. Assert `assertCanonicalTaskTimeout(input.taskTimeout)` as the second
   executable action, before run ID allocation, ownership, provider capture,
   listener/timer setup, or any command.
3. Create `runId`, `owned`, the unarmed overall controller/forward callback,
   and unassigned timer/primary locals. Then call
   `createProviderContractCapture(input.expectedParts, runId)` as the last
   statement before the outer `try`. This is the only capture creation site.
4. Immediately enter that one outer `try/finally`. Attach the input abort listener
   and lifecycle timer inside that `try`; the existing phase body and its
   `TerminalBuildError`/primary catch also stay inside it.
5. After the phase catch, call `finalizeCloudRunLifecycle` and return its
   result.
6. In the outer `finally`, clear the timer if assigned, remove the abort
   listener, and call `discardProviderContractCapture(providerCapture)`.
   Discard is idempotent after a successful/failed flush and releases an active
   object after any setup, finalizer, receipt-write, or other exception.

Replace Task 7's lifecycle function with this exact final body:

```ts
export async function runCloudRunLifecycle(
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<OwnershipReceipt> {
  if (input.signal?.aborted) {
    throw new Error('Cloud lifecycle aborted before start')
  }
  assertCanonicalTaskTimeout(input.taskTimeout)
  const runId = createRunId()
  const owned = createPendingOwnership(input, runId)
  const overall = new AbortController()
  const forward = () => overall.abort(input.signal?.reason)
  let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined
  let primary: unknown | undefined
  const providerCapture = createProviderContractCapture(
    input.expectedParts,
    runId,
  )
  try {
    if (input.signal?.aborted) forward()
    else input.signal?.addEventListener('abort', forward, { once: true })
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      overall.abort(new Error(
        `Lifecycle exceeded ${input.budgets.overallMs}ms`,
      ))
    }, input.budgets.overallMs)
    const operationInput = { ...input, signal: overall.signal }
    try {
      const snapshot = await prepareSourceSnapshot(
        input.repoRoot,
        runId,
        overall.signal,
        runCommand,
      )
      const identity = createResourceIdentity(
        input.jobPrefix,
        snapshot.head,
        runId,
      )
      acquireSnapshotOwnership(owned, snapshot, identity)
      throwIfLifecycleAborted(overall.signal, 'source snapshot acquisition')

      const buildTemplate = await readStrictBuildTemplate(
        snapshot.buildTemplatePath,
      )
      const prerequisites = await verifyCloudPrerequisites(
        operationInput,
        runCommand,
      )
      owned.receipt.projectNumber = prerequisites.projectNumber
      const buildAuthority: BuildAuthority = {
        projectId: input.projectId,
        projectNumber: prerequisites.projectNumber,
      }

      let source: SourceIdentity | undefined
      if (input.buildMode === 'cloud') {
        const sourceIntent = await createSourceMutationIntent(
          operationInput,
          snapshot,
          identity,
        )
        registerMutationIntent(owned, sourceIntent)
        markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'source')
        source = await uploadOwnedSource(
          operationInput,
          snapshot,
          identity,
          runCommand,
        )
        acquireSourceOwnership(owned, source)
      }
      throwIfLifecycleAborted(
        overall.signal,
        'source upload reconciliation',
      )

      let built: BuiltImage
      if (input.buildMode === 'cloud') {
        const buildWindow = correlationWindow()
        registerMutationIntent(owned, createBuildMutationIntent(
          operationInput,
          buildAuthority,
          snapshot,
          identity,
          source!,
          buildTemplate,
          buildWindow,
        ))
        markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'build')
        const correlated = await startBuildFromGeneration(
          operationInput,
          buildAuthority,
          snapshot,
          identity,
          source!,
          buildTemplate,
          runCommand,
          buildWindow,
          providerCapture,
        )
        acquireBuildOwnership(owned, correlated)
        built = await finishBuildFromGeneration(
          operationInput,
          identity,
          correlated,
          runCommand,
          providerCapture,
        )
      } else {
        owned.receipt.source = {
          state: 'not-created',
          commit: snapshot.head,
        }
        const localPushIntent = createLocalPushMutationIntent(
          operationInput,
          buildAuthority,
          snapshot,
          identity,
        )
        registerMutationIntent(owned, localPushIntent)
        markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'local-push')
        built = await buildAndPushLocally(
          operationInput,
          buildAuthority,
          snapshot,
          identity,
          runCommand,
        )
      }
      acquireBuildAndImageOwnership(owned, built)
      throwIfLifecycleAborted(
        overall.signal,
        'Build and image reconciliation',
      )

      if (input.operation === 'run') {
        const expectedEnv = expectedWorkloadEnv(input, identity.runId)
        const jobWindow = correlationWindow()
        registerMutationIntent(owned, createJobMutationIntent(
          operationInput,
          identity,
          built.image.immutableImage,
          expectedEnv,
          jobWindow,
        ))
        markMutationPossiblyAccepted(owned, 'job')
        const job = await createAndVerifyJob(
          operationInput,
          identity,
          snapshot.tempRoot,
          built.image.immutableImage,
          expectedEnv,
          runCommand,
          jobWindow,
          providerCapture,
        )
        acquireJobOwnership(owned, job)
        throwIfLifecycleAborted(
          overall.signal,
          'Job create reconciliation',
        )
        const execution = await runExactJobOnce(
          operationInput,
          job,
          runCommand,
          providerCapture,
        )
        acquireExecutionOwnership(owned, execution)
        throwIfLifecycleAborted(
          overall.signal,
          'Execution identity acquisition',
        )
        const terminal = await waitForExecutionTerminal(
          operationInput,
          execution,
          prerequisites.projectNumber,
          runCommand,
          providerCapture,
        )
        acquireTerminalExecution(owned, terminal)
        throwIfLifecycleAborted(
          overall.signal,
          'Execution terminal reconciliation',
        )
        if (!executionSucceeded(terminal)) {
          await captureFailedResultContract({
            input: operationInput,
            runId: identity.runId,
            jobName: identity.jobName,
            executionName: execution.name,
            runCommand,
            providerCapture,
          })
          throw new Error('Exact Execution failed')
        }
        owned.receipt.parts = await collectExactPartResults({
          input: operationInput,
          runId: identity.runId,
          jobName: identity.jobName,
          executionName: execution.name,
          runCommand,
          providerCapture,
        })
      }
      owned.receipt.status = 'succeeded'
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof TerminalBuildError) {
        acquireBuildOwnership(owned, error.build)
      }
      primary = error
      owned.receipt.status = 'failed'
    }
    return await finalizeCloudRunLifecycle(
      input,
      owned,
      primary,
      runCommand,
      providerCapture,
    )
  } finally {
    if (timer !== undefined) clearTimeout(timer)
    input.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', forward)
    discardProviderContractCapture(providerCapture)
  }
}
```

This replaces Task 7's old setup/timer `finally` and old post-cleanup receipt
tail. There is exactly one cleanup, one capture flush, one terminal receipt,
and one release path. Pre-aborted input exits before capture creation; every
path after activation is enclosed by the outer `finally`.

```ts
export type TraceExec = { executable: string; argv: readonly string[]; line: string }
```

Add the concrete trace/record parsers and negative assertions:

```ts
function onePrefixedJson<T>(
  lines: readonly string[],
  prefix: string,
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
): T {
  const rows = lines.filter((line) => line.startsWith(prefix))
  if (rows.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(`Expected exactly one ${prefix.trim()} record; got ${rows.length}`)
  }
  let decoded: unknown
  try { decoded = JSON.parse(rows[0].slice(prefix.length)) }
  catch (error) { throw new Error(`${prefix.trim()} is malformed JSON: ${String(error)}`) }
  return schema.parse(decoded)
}

function decodeStraceArgv(raw: string): string[] {
  if (raw.includes('...')) throw new Error('strace argv is truncated')
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(raw)
    return z.array(z.string()).min(1).parse(value)
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(`strace argv is malformed: ${String(error)}`)
  }
}

export async function parseTraceFiles(
  prefix: string,
  limits: TraceLimits = DEFAULT_TRACE_LIMITS,
): Promise<ParsedTrace> {
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(limits)) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 1) {
      throw new Error(`Trace ${name} must be a positive safe integer`)
    }
  }
  const directory = dirname(prefix)
  const stem = basename(prefix)
  const names: string[] = []
  const entries = await opendir(directory)
  for await (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.name === stem || entry.name.startsWith(`${stem}.`)) {
      names.push(entry.name)
      if (names.length > limits.maxFiles) {
        throw new Error(`Trace file count exceeds ${limits.maxFiles}`)
      }
    }
  }
  names.sort()
  if (names.length === 0) throw new Error(`No trace files for ${prefix}`)
  const parsed: ParsedTrace = { execs: [], syscalls: [] }
  let aggregateBytes = 0
  let aggregateLines = 0
  for (const name of names) {
    const tracePath = resolve(directory, name)
    const before = await stat(tracePath)
    if (!before.isFile()) {
      throw new Error(`Trace ${name} is not a regular file`)
    }
    if (before.size > limits.maxFileBytes) {
      throw new Error(
        `Trace ${name} ${before.size} bytes exceeds ${limits.maxFileBytes}`,
      )
    }
    if (aggregateBytes + before.size > limits.maxAggregateBytes) {
      throw new Error(
        `Trace aggregate bytes exceed ${limits.maxAggregateBytes}`,
      )
    }
    const remainingAggregateBytes =
      limits.maxAggregateBytes - aggregateBytes
    const currentFileByteLimit = Math.min(
      limits.maxFileBytes,
      remainingAggregateBytes,
    )
    const remainingLines = limits.maxLines - aggregateLines
    const remainingRecords =
      limits.maxExecs - parsed.execs.length +
      limits.maxSyscalls - parsed.syscalls.length
    if (remainingLines < 1 || remainingRecords < 1) {
      throw new Error('Trace aggregate line or record budget exhausted')
    }
    const scanned = await readBoundedSelectedLines(
      tracePath,
      {
        label: `trace ${name}`,
        maxBytes: currentFileByteLimit,
        maxLineBytes: limits.maxLineBytes,
        maxLines: remainingLines,
        maxSelectedRecords: remainingRecords,
        maxSelectedBytes: currentFileByteLimit,
        select: (line) =>
          /\b(?:execve|openat|connect|bind|listen)\(/.test(line),
      },
    )
    aggregateBytes += scanned.totalBytes
    aggregateLines += scanned.totalLines
    for (const line of scanned.records) {
      const match = /execve\("((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)",\s*(\[(?:[^\]]|\\\])*)\],\s*(?:0x[0-9a-f]+|NULL)/.exec(line)
      if (match) {
        const executable = JSON.parse(`"${match[1]}"`) as string
        parsed.execs.push({ executable, argv: decodeStraceArgv(`${match[2]}]`), line })
        if (parsed.execs.length > limits.maxExecs) {
          throw new Error(`Trace execs exceed ${limits.maxExecs}`)
        }
      } else if (/\b(?:execve|openat|connect|bind|listen)\(/.test(line)) {
        if (/\bexecve\(/.test(line)) throw new Error(`Unparseable execve line: ${line}`)
        parsed.syscalls.push(line)
        if (parsed.syscalls.length > limits.maxSyscalls) {
          throw new Error(`Trace syscalls exceed ${limits.maxSyscalls}`)
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return parsed
}

function argvContains(argv: readonly string[], sequence: readonly string[]): boolean {
  return sequence.length <= argv.length && sequence.every((value, index) => argv[index] === value)
}

export function assertNoCoordinatorEffects(lines: readonly string[]): void {
  const forbidden = lines.find((line) =>
    /(?:openat|rename|unlink).*freshell-test-coordinator\/holder\.json/.test(line) ||
    /(?:connect|bind).*\/(?:frt|f)-[^"}]*\.sock/.test(line) ||
    /\blisten\(/.test(line))
  if (forbidden) throw new Error(`Coordinator side effect in cloud trace: ${forbidden}`)
}

export function assertSafeTrace(trace: ParsedTrace, cloudCoordinator: boolean): void {
  for (const { argv } of trace.execs) {
    const text = argv.join(' ')
    const forbidden =
      /node_modules\/vitest\/.*vitest\.mjs|scripts\/run-standard-tests\.ts|vitest\.electron|test:balanced/.test(text) ||
      /test-coordinator\.ts.*(?:holder|wait)/.test(text) ||
      /gcloud run jobs (?:update|execute)\b/.test(text) ||
      /gcloud run jobs executions (?:list|logs)\b/.test(text) ||
      /latestCreatedExecution|startExecutionToken|runExecutionToken|:latest\b/.test(text) ||
      /docker (?:buildx|pull)|--cache-(?:from|to)/.test(text) ||
      /gcloud artifacts (?:versions|docker images) delete/.test(text)
    if (forbidden) throw new Error(`Banned command in trace: ${text}`)
    if (/gcloud builds submit/.test(text) && !argv.includes('--no-source')) {
      throw new Error(`Build submit lacks --no-source: ${text}`)
    }
  }
  if (cloudCoordinator) assertNoCoordinatorEffects(trace.syscalls)
}

function assertSurfaceTracePolicy(
  command: AcceptanceArtifact['command'],
  trace: ParsedTrace,
): void {
  const text = trace.execs.map(({ argv }) => argv.join(' ')).join('\n')
  const mutations = /gcloud storage cp|gcloud builds submit|docker push|gcloud run jobs create|run\.googleapis\.com\/v2/
  if (command.endsWith('-logs')) {
    if (!/gcloud logging read/.test(text) || mutations.test(text)) {
      throw new Error(`${command}: logs trace is not an exact read-only Logging path`)
    }
    return
  }
  if (command.endsWith('-push')) {
    const builds = trace.execs.filter(({ argv }) =>
      argv[0] === 'docker' && argv[1] === 'build')
    const pushes = trace.execs.filter(({ argv }) =>
      argv[0] === 'docker' && argv[1] === 'push')
    if (builds.length !== 1 || pushes.length !== 1 ||
        /gcloud storage cp|gcloud builds submit|gcloud run jobs create|run\.googleapis\.com\/v2/.test(text)) {
      throw new Error(
        `${command}: local push requires one build/one push and no other provider lifecycle`,
      )
    }
    return
  }
  if (command.endsWith('-build') &&
      /gcloud run jobs create|run\.googleapis\.com\/v2/.test(text)) {
    throw new Error(`${command}: build-only path created or ran a Job`)
  }
}

function firstExecIndex(trace: ParsedTrace, prefix: readonly string[]): number {
  return trace.execs.findIndex(({ argv }) => argvContains(argv, prefix))
}

function assertPrePhaseOrder(
  trace: ParsedTrace,
  phases: readonly ('typecheck' | 'build')[],
): void {
  let previous = -1
  for (const phase of phases) {
    const index = trace.execs.findIndex(({ argv }, candidate) =>
      candidate > previous && argvContains(argv, ['npm', 'run', phase]))
    if (index < 0) throw new Error(`Missing ordered pre-phase ${phase}`)
    previous = index
  }
  const mutation = trace.execs.findIndex(({ argv }) => {
    const text = argv.join(' ')
    return /gcloud storage cp|gcloud builds submit|docker push|gcloud run jobs create/.test(text)
  })
  if (mutation >= 0 && previous >= mutation) {
    throw new Error('Cloud mutation occurred before required pre-phases')
  }
}

export function classifyRunJobRead(value: unknown): 'found' | 'absent' {
  const response = z.object({
    httpStatus: z.number().int(), body: z.unknown(),
  }).strict().parse(value)
  if (response.httpStatus === 200) return 'found'
  if (response.httpStatus === 404) {
    const error = z.object({ error: z.object({
      code: z.literal(404), status: z.literal('NOT_FOUND'), message: z.string(),
    }).passthrough() }).passthrough().parse(response.body)
    if (error.error.status === 'NOT_FOUND') return 'absent'
  }
  if (response.httpStatus === 0) throw new Error('network failure is not absence')
  const provider = z.object({ error: z.object({
    code: z.number(), status: z.string(), message: z.string(),
  }).passthrough() }).passthrough().safeParse(response.body)
  throw new Error(provider.success ? provider.data.error.status : 'malformed provider response')
}
```

Add the exact live resource inspector. Artifact Registry and Storage remain
dedicated structured `gcloud` reads; only Job absence crosses the closed Run-v2
child:

```ts
function receiptInput(receipt: OwnershipReceipt): CloudRunLifecycleInput {
  const tagged = String(receipt.image.taggedImage ?? '')
  const match = /^([a-z0-9-]+)-docker\.pkg\.dev\/([^/]+)\/([^/]+)\/([^:]+):(.+)$/.exec(tagged)
  if (!match || match[2] !== receipt.projectId) throw new Error('Receipt image identity is malformed')
  const counters = receipt.execution.counters
  const taskCount = counters
    ? counters.succeededCount + counters.failedCount + counters.cancelledCount
    : 1
  return {
    origin: receipt.origin,
    kind: receipt.kind,
    repoRoot: process.cwd(),
    account: receipt.account,
    projectId: receipt.projectId,
    iamAnalysisScope: receipt.iamAnalysisScope,
    buildRegion: 'global',
    runRegion: receipt.runRegion,
    sourceBucket: String(receipt.source.bucket ?? ''),
    repository: match[3],
    imageName: match[4],
    jobPrefix: receipt.job.name || 'not-created',
    buildServiceAccount: String(receipt.build.serviceAccount ?? ''),
    runtimeServiceAccount: receipt.job.serviceAccount,
    buildMode: receipt.buildMode,
    operation: receipt.operation,
    taskCount: Math.max(1, taskCount),
    taskTimeout: '300s', memory: '4Gi', cpu: 4,
    workloadEnv: {},
    expectedParts: receipt.parts.map(
      ({ partId }: CloudPartResult) => partId,
    ),
    budgets: lifecycleBudgetsForTaskTimeout('300s'),
  }
}

async function pathIsAbsent(path: string | undefined): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!path) return true
  try { await stat(path); return false }
  catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') return true
    throw error
  }
}

async function inspectLocalImages(
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  input: CloudRunLifecycleInput,
  runCommand: RunCommand,
): Promise<'absent'> {
  const tags = (receipt.image.localTags ?? []) as readonly string[]
  const dockerConfig = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'freshell-inspect-docker-'))
  try {
    for (const tag of tags) {
      const result = await runCommand('docker', ['image', 'inspect', tag], {
        env: { ...process.env, DOCKER_CONFIG: dockerConfig },
        output: { kind: 'capture', maxStdoutBytes: 64 * 1024, maxStderrBytes: 64 * 1024 },
        timeoutMs: input.budgets.commandMs,
        terminateGraceMs: input.budgets.terminateGraceMs,
        settlementTimeoutMs: input.budgets.settlementMs,
      })
      if (result.exitCode === 0) throw new Error(`Local image remains: ${tag}`)
      if (!/No such (?:image|object)/i.test(result.stderr)) {
        throw new Error(`Local image state unreadable for ${tag}`)
      }
    }
  } finally {
    await rm(dockerConfig, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
  return 'absent'
}

export async function inspectReceiptResources(
  receiptValue: OwnershipReceipt,
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<z.infer<typeof providerStateSchema>> {
  const receipt = parseOwnershipReceipt(receiptValue)
  const input = receiptInput(receipt)
  let job: 'absent' | 'not-created' = 'not-created'
  if (receipt.operation === 'run') {
    const live = await getExactRunJob(input, receipt.job.name, runCommand)
    if (live) throw new Error(`Owned Job still exists: ${live.name}`)
    job = 'absent'
  }

  if (receipt.source.bucket && receipt.source.object && receipt.source.generation) {
    const sourceRows = await gcloudJson(z.array(z.object({
      bucket: z.string(), name: z.string(), generation: z.string(),
    }).strict()).max(2), [
      'storage', 'objects', 'list', `gs://${receipt.source.bucket}`,
      `--filter=name="${receipt.source.object}"`, '--limit=2',
      `--account=${receipt.account}`, `--project=${receipt.projectId}`,
      '--format=json(bucket,name,generation)',
    ], input, runCommand)
    if (sourceRows.some((row) =>
        row.bucket === receipt.source.bucket && row.name === receipt.source.object &&
        row.generation === receipt.source.generation)) {
      throw new Error('Owned source generation remains live')
    }
  } else if (receipt.buildMode !== 'local') {
    throw new Error('Cloud Build receipt omits source generation identity')
  }

  const image = requireCoherentImageIdentity(input, imageIdentitySchema.parse(receipt.image))
  const tagRows = await listExactTag(input, image, runCommand)
  if (tagRows.length !== 0) {
    throw new Error('Owned Artifact Registry tag remains')
  }
  const digestRows = await gcloudJson(
    z.array(z.object({ name: z.string() }).strict()).max(2),
    [
    'artifacts', 'versions', 'list',
    `--package=${input.imageName}`,
    `--filter=name="${receipt.image.digestVersionResourceName}"`, '--limit=2',
    `--location=${receipt.runRegion}`,
    `--repository=${input.repository}`, `--account=${receipt.account}`,
    `--project=${receipt.projectId}`, '--format=json(name)',
  ], input, runCommand)
  const exactDigest = digestRows.filter(({ name }) =>
    name === receipt.image.digestVersionResourceName)
  if (exactDigest.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error('Retained digest resource is absent or changed')
  }
  if (!await pathIsAbsent(receipt.temp.root) || !await pathIsAbsent(receipt.temp.dockerConfig)) {
    throw new Error('Invocation temp or Docker config remains')
  }
  const state = {
    schemaVersion: 1 as const,
    job,
    sourceGeneration: 'absent' as const,
    tag: 'absent' as const,
    digest: 'retained' as const,
    tempRoot: 'absent' as const,
    dockerConfig: 'absent' as const,
    localImages: await inspectLocalImages(receipt, input, runCommand),
  }
  return providerStateSchema.parse(state)
}
```

Add the complete acceptance validator. Every file is required to be an absolute
regular file under the declared evidence root; unreadable, malformed, stale, or
ambiguous evidence fails:

```ts
const requiredPublicCommandKinds = [
  'coordinator-test', 'coordinator-check', 'coordinator-verify',
  'coordinator-test-all', 'vitest-run', 'vitest-build', 'vitest-push',
  'vitest-logs', 'e2e-run', 'e2e-build', 'e2e-push', 'e2e-logs',
] as const

async function readJsonFile<T>(
  path: string,
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
  label = 'JSON evidence',
): Promise<T> {
  let raw: string
  try {
    raw = await readBoundedRegularFile(path, {
      label,
      maxBytes: JSON_EVIDENCE_MAX_BYTES,
    })
  }
  catch (error) { throw new Error(`Unreadable evidence file ${path}: ${String(error)}`) }
  let value: unknown
  try { value = JSON.parse(raw) }
  catch (error) { throw new Error(`Malformed JSON evidence ${path}: ${String(error)}`) }
  return schema.parse(value)
}

async function readEvidenceRecords(
  path: string,
  label: string,
  select: (line: string) => boolean,
  maxSelectedRecords: number,
  maxSelectedBytes = maxSelectedRecords * PREFIX_RECORD_MAX_BYTES,
): Promise<string[]> {
  const scanned = await readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    label,
    maxBytes: COMMAND_LOG_MAX_BYTES,
    maxLineBytes: COMMAND_LOG_MAX_LINE_BYTES,
    maxLines: COMMAND_LOG_MAX_LINES,
    maxSelectedRecords,
    maxSelectedBytes,
    select,
  })
  return scanned.records
}

async function readPrefixedEvidenceRecords(
  path: string,
  label: string,
  prefixes: readonly string[],
  maxSelectedRecords: number,
  maxSelectedBytes?: number,
): Promise<string[]> {
  return readEvidenceRecords(
    path,
    label,
    (line) => prefixes.some((prefix) => line.startsWith(prefix)),
    maxSelectedRecords,
    maxSelectedBytes,
  )
}

async function exactEvidenceFile(root: string, path: string): Promise<string> {
  if (!isAbsolute(root) || !isAbsolute(path)) throw new Error('Evidence paths must be absolute')
  const rootPath = resolve(root)
  const target = resolve(path)
  const inside = relative(rootPath, target)
  if (inside.startsWith('..') || isAbsolute(inside)) {
    throw new Error(`Evidence path escapes root: ${path}`)
  }
  const details = await stat(target)
  if (!details.isFile()) throw new Error(`Evidence path is not a regular file: ${path}`)
  return target
}

function exactStringSet(actual: readonly string[], expected: readonly string[], label: string): void {
  const left = [...actual].sort()
  const right = [...expected].sort()
  if (new Set(left).size !== left.length || JSON.stringify(left) !== JSON.stringify(right)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} mismatch: ${JSON.stringify(left)}`)
  }
}

function exactStringMultiset(
  actual: readonly string[],
  expected: readonly string[],
  label: string,
): void {
  const left = [...actual].sort()
  const right = [...expected].sort()
  if (JSON.stringify(left) !== JSON.stringify(right)) {
    throw new Error(`${label} mismatch: ${JSON.stringify(left)}`)
  }
}

function sameArgv(actual: readonly string[], expected: readonly string[]): boolean {
  return actual.length === expected.length && actual.every((value, index) => value === expected[index])
}

type DerivedAcceptanceExpectation = {
  taskCount: number
  parts: readonly string[]
}

function exactFlagValue(
  argv: readonly string[],
  name: '--config' | '--shards',
): string | undefined {
  const values: string[] = []
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {
    const token = argv[index]
    if (token.startsWith(`${name}=`)) {
      values.push(token.slice(name.length + 1))
    } else if (token === name) {
      const value = argv[index + 1]
      if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) {
        throw new Error(`Missing value for ${name}`)
      }
      values.push(value)
      index += 1
    }
  }
  if (values.length > 1) throw new Error(`Duplicate ${name}`)
  return values[0]
}

function derivedVitestParts(
  argv: readonly string[],
  status: 'succeeded' | 'failed',
): DerivedAcceptanceExpectation {
  const selector = exactFlagValue(argv, '--config') ?? 'all'
  const taskCount = parseCloudTaskCount(
    exactFlagValue(argv, '--shards') ?? '4',
    'Vitest acceptance shards',
  )
  const suites = selectStandardVitestSuites(selector)
  const parts = status === 'failed' ? [] : suites.flatMap(({ name }) =>
    Array.from({ length: taskCount }, (_, taskIndex) =>
      `${name}:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`))
  return { taskCount, parts }
}

function derivedE2EParts(
  argv: readonly string[],
  status: 'succeeded' | 'failed',
): DerivedAcceptanceExpectation {
  const taskCount = parseCloudTaskCount(
    exactFlagValue(argv, '--shards') ?? '1',
    'E2E acceptance shards',
  )
  return {
    taskCount,
    parts: status === 'failed'
      ? []
      : Array.from({ length: taskCount }, (_, taskIndex) =>
          `e2e:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`),
  }
}

export function deriveAcceptanceExpectation(
  artifact: AcceptanceArtifact,
): DerivedAcceptanceExpectation {
  if ([
    'vitest-build', 'vitest-push', 'e2e-build', 'e2e-push',
  ].includes(artifact.command)) {
    return { taskCount: 0, parts: [] }
  }
  if (artifact.command.startsWith('coordinator-')) {
    const taskCount = 4
    return {
      taskCount,
      parts: STANDARD_VITEST_SUITES.flatMap(({ name }) =>
        Array.from({ length: taskCount }, (_, taskIndex) =>
          `${name}:${taskIndex + 1}/${taskCount}`)),
    }
  }
  if (artifact.command.startsWith('e2e-')) {
    return derivedE2EParts(artifact.expectedArgv, artifact.expectedStatus)
  }
  return derivedVitestParts(artifact.expectedArgv, artifact.expectedStatus)
}

function derivedCleanup(receipt: OwnershipReceipt): string[] {
  return [
    ...(receipt.operation === 'run'
      ? ['execution:terminal', 'job:deleted']
      : []),
    'build:terminal',
    ...(receipt.buildMode === 'cloud' ? ['source:deleted'] : []),
    'tag:deleted',
    ...(receipt.buildMode === 'local'
      ? ['local-image:deleted', 'local-image:deleted']
      : []),
    'docker-config:deleted',
    'temp-root:deleted',
  ]
}

function taskCountFromReceipt(receipt: OwnershipReceipt): number {
  if (receipt.operation === 'build') return 0
  const counters = receipt.execution.counters
  if (!counters) throw new Error('Run receipt omits terminal Execution counters')
  return counters.succeededCount + counters.failedCount +
    counters.cancelledCount + counters.runningCount
}

function receiptIdentityValues(receipt: OwnershipReceipt): string[] {
  const sourceObject = String(receipt.source.object ?? '')
  const generation = String(receipt.source.generation ?? '')
  return [
    receipt.runId,
    receipt.temp.root ?? '',
    receipt.temp.sourceDir ?? '',
    receipt.temp.dockerConfig ?? '',
    sourceObject && generation ? `${sourceObject}#${generation}` : '',
    sourceObject && generation && receipt.source.bucket
      ? `gs://${receipt.source.bucket}/${sourceObject}#${generation}`
      : '',
    String(receipt.image.tag ?? ''),
    String(receipt.image.taggedImage ?? ''),
    String(receipt.build.id ?? ''),
    receipt.job.name,
    receipt.job.resourceName ?? '',
    receipt.job.uid ?? '',
    receipt.execution.name ?? '',
    receipt.execution.resourceName ?? '',
    receipt.execution.uid ?? '',
  ].filter((value) => value.length > 0)
}

function assertReceiptMatchesArtifact(
  artifact: AcceptanceArtifact,
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  manifest: AcceptanceManifest,
  expectation: DerivedAcceptanceExpectation,
): void {
  if (receipt.head !== manifest.acceptedSha || artifact.acceptedSha !== manifest.acceptedSha) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: stale accepted SHA`)
  }
  if (receipt.origin.surface !== artifact.expectedOrigin ||
      receipt.status !== artifact.expectedStatus ||
      receipt.kind !== artifact.expectedKind ||
      receipt.buildMode !== artifact.expectedBuildMode) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: receipt identity/status mismatch`)
  }
  if (String(receipt.build.serviceAccount) !== manifest.buildServiceAccount ||
      receipt.job.serviceAccount !== manifest.runtimeServiceAccount) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: worker identity mismatch`)
  }
  if (artifact.expectedResources) {
    const actual = {
      runId: receipt.runId,
      head: String(receipt.head),
      sourceBucket: String(receipt.source.bucket),
      sourceObject: String(receipt.source.object),
      sourceGeneration: String(receipt.source.generation),
      buildCorrelationTag: String(receipt.build.correlationTag),
      buildId: String(receipt.build.id),
      buildResourceName: String(receipt.build.resourceName),
      buildProjectId: String(receipt.build.projectId),
      buildProjectNumber: String(receipt.build.projectNumber),
      imageTag: String(receipt.image.tag),
      digest: String(receipt.image.digest),
      job: receipt.job.name,
      jobResourceName: String(receipt.job.resourceName),
      jobUid: String(receipt.job.uid),
      jobGeneration: String(receipt.job.generation),
      execution: String(receipt.execution.name),
      executionResourceName: String(receipt.execution.resourceName),
      executionUid: String(receipt.execution.uid),
    }
    if (JSON.stringify(actual) !== JSON.stringify(artifact.expectedResources)) {
      throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: exact resource identities differ from extraction`)
    }
  }
  if (taskCountFromReceipt(receipt) !== expectation.taskCount) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: task count mismatch`)
  }
  exactStringSet(
    receipt.parts.map(({ partId }: CloudPartResult) => partId),
    expectation.parts,
    `${artifact.id}: result parts`,
  )
  for (const part of receipt.parts) {
    if (part.runId !== receipt.runId || part.kind !== receipt.kind ||
        part.execution !== receipt.execution.name || part.taskAttempt !== 0 ||
        part.status !== 'passed' || part.exitCode !== 0 ||
        part.taskCount !== expectation.taskCount ||
        part.taskIndex >= expectation.taskCount) {
      throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: result self-attribution mismatch`)
    }
  }
  exactStringMultiset(
    receipt.cleanup.map(
      ({ kind, outcome }: OwnershipReceipt['cleanup'][number]) =>
        `${kind}:${outcome}`,
    ),
    derivedCleanup(receipt),
    `${artifact.id}: cleanup dispositions`,
  )
  if (receipt.residuals.length !== 0 ||
      receipt.cleanup.some(({ outcome }) => outcome === 'failed' || outcome === 'retained')) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: cleanup is ambiguous or incomplete`)
  }
  if (receipt.status === 'failed' && receipt.failure?.cleanup.length !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: failed run has cleanup failures`)
  }
}

function validateExactLogOutput(
  artifact: AcceptanceArtifact,
  lines: readonly string[],
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  expectation: DerivedAcceptanceExpectation,
): void {
  if (!artifact.command.endsWith('-logs')) return
  if (lines.length > expectation.parts.length + 1) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: log output exceeded expectedParts + 1 bound`)
  }
  const entries = lines.map((line, index) => {
    let decoded: unknown
    try { decoded = JSON.parse(line) }
    catch (error) {
      throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: unreadable log row ${index}: ${String(error)}`)
    }
    return acceptanceLogEntrySchema.parse(decoded)
  })
  const parts = entries.map((entry) => {
    const part = entry.jsonPayload
    if (entry.resource.labels.project_id !== receipt.projectId ||
        entry.resource.labels.location !== receipt.runRegion ||
        entry.resource.labels.job_name !== receipt.job.name ||
        entry.labels['run.googleapis.com/execution_name'] !== receipt.execution.name ||
        part.runId !== receipt.runId || part.kind !== receipt.kind ||
        part.execution !== receipt.execution.name ||
        part.taskCount !== expectation.taskCount ||
        part.taskIndex >= expectation.taskCount ||
        (part.kind === 'vitest' ? part.testCount === undefined : part.testCount !== undefined)) {
      throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: log row is not exact self-attribution`)
    }
    return part
  })
  exactStringSet(
    parts.map(({ partId }: CloudPartResult) => partId),
    expectation.parts,
    `${artifact.id}: exact logged parts`)
}

function coordinatorCommandKey(command: AcceptanceArtifact['command']): string | undefined {
  if (command === 'coordinator-test') return 'test'
  if (command === 'coordinator-test-all') return 'test:all'
  if (command === 'coordinator-check') return 'check'
  if (command === 'coordinator-verify') return 'verify'
  return undefined
}

const ownershipEventSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  phase: z.enum([
    'intent', 'possibly-accepted', 'acquired', 'reconciled', 'residual',
  ]),
  value: z.unknown(),
}).strict()

function assertLocalPushOwnershipEvents(
  commandRecords: readonly string[],
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
): void {
  const events = commandRecords
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP '))
    .map((line) => ownershipEventSchema.parse(JSON.parse(
      line.slice('FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP '.length),
    )))
    .filter(({ value }) =>
      value !== null &&
      typeof value === 'object' &&
      (value as { kind?: unknown }).kind === 'local-push')
  if (events.length !== 3 ||
      events[0].phase !== 'intent' ||
      events[1].phase !== 'possibly-accepted' ||
      events[2].phase !== 'acquired') {
    throw new Error(
      'Local push evidence requires exact intent/possibly-accepted/acquired order',
    )
  }
  const expected = {
    correlationTag: receipt.build.correlationTag,
    runId: receipt.runId,
    head: receipt.head,
    remoteTag: receipt.image.taggedImage,
    localTags: receipt.image.localTags,
  }
  for (const { value } of events) {
    const parsed = z.object({
      kind: z.literal('local-push'),
      correlationTag: z.string(),
      runId: z.string(),
      head: z.string(),
      remoteTag: z.string(),
      localTags: z.array(z.string()).length(2),
    }).passthrough().parse(value)
    if (JSON.stringify({
      correlationTag: parsed.correlationTag,
      runId: parsed.runId,
      head: parsed.head,
      remoteTag: parsed.remoteTag,
      localTags: parsed.localTags,
    }) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
      throw new Error('Local push ownership event differs from terminal receipt')
    }
  }
}

async function validateArtifact(
  manifest: AcceptanceManifest,
  artifact: AcceptanceArtifact,
): Promise<OwnershipReceipt> {
  const commandLogPath = await exactEvidenceFile(manifest.evidenceRoot, artifact.commandLogPath)
  const receiptLogPath = await exactEvidenceFile(manifest.evidenceRoot, artifact.receiptLogPath)
  const providerStatePath = await exactEvidenceFile(manifest.evidenceRoot, artifact.providerStatePath)
  if (!isAbsolute(artifact.tracePrefix)) throw new Error('Trace prefix must be absolute')
  const traceInside = relative(resolve(manifest.evidenceRoot), resolve(artifact.tracePrefix))
  if (traceInside.startsWith('..') || isAbsolute(traceInside)) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: trace prefix escapes evidence root`)
  }
  const trace = await parseTraceFiles(artifact.tracePrefix)
  assertSafeTrace(trace, artifact.command.startsWith('coordinator-'))
  assertSurfaceTracePolicy(artifact.command, trace)
  if (!trace.execs.some(({ argv }) => sameArgv(argv, artifact.expectedArgv))) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: trace is not bound to exact public argv`)
  }
  const canonical = artifact.command === 'contract-characterization'
    ? artifact.expectedArgv
    : publicCommandPrefix[artifact.command]
  if (['vitest-run', 'vitest-logs', 'e2e-run', 'e2e-logs'].includes(
    artifact.command,
  ) && artifact.expectedResources === undefined) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: exact extracted resource identities are required`)
  }
  if (!argvContains(artifact.expectedArgv, canonical)) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: manifest argv is not the retained public command`)
  }

  const expectation = deriveAcceptanceExpectation(artifact)
  const commandRecordLimit = artifact.command.endsWith('-logs')
    ? expectation.parts.length + 1
    : artifact.command === 'contract-characterization'
      ? semanticProviderRecordLimit(expectation.parts.length) + 1
      : artifact.command.endsWith('-push')
        ? 3
        : 1
  const commandScan = await readBoundedSelectedLines(commandLogPath, {
    label: `${artifact.id} command log`,
    maxBytes: COMMAND_LOG_MAX_BYTES,
    maxLineBytes: COMMAND_LOG_MAX_LINE_BYTES,
    maxLines: COMMAND_LOG_MAX_LINES,
    maxSelectedRecords: commandRecordLimit,
    maxSelectedBytes: commandRecordLimit * PREFIX_RECORD_MAX_BYTES,
    select: artifact.command.endsWith('-logs')
      ? (line) => line.length > 0
      : artifact.command === 'contract-characterization'
        ? (line) =>
            line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ') ||
            line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ')
        : artifact.command.endsWith('-push')
          ? (line) => line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP ')
          : artifact.command.startsWith('coordinator-')
            ? (line) => line.startsWith('FRESHELL_CLOUD_ROUTE ')
            : () => false,
  })
  const commandRecords = commandScan.records
  const key = coordinatorCommandKey(artifact.command)
  if (key) {
    const route = onePrefixedJson(
      commandRecords,
      'FRESHELL_CLOUD_ROUTE ',
      cloudRouteSchema,
    )
    const expected = expectedPrePhases(artifact.command)
    if (route.commandKey !== key || route.surface !== artifact.expectedOrigin ||
        JSON.stringify(route.plannedPrePhases) !== JSON.stringify(expected) ||
        JSON.stringify(route.completedPrePhases) !== JSON.stringify(expected) ||
        JSON.stringify(artifact.expectedPrePhases) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
      throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: route/pre-phase receipt mismatch`)
    }
    assertPrePhaseOrder(trace, expected)
  } else if (artifact.expectedPrePhases.length !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`${artifact.id}: non-coordinator command declares pre-phases`)
  }

  const receiptRecords = await readPrefixedEvidenceRecords(
    receiptLogPath,
    `${artifact.id} receipt log`,
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )
  const receipt = onePrefixedJson(
    receiptRecords,
    'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ',
    z.custom<OwnershipReceipt>((value) => {
      try { parseOwnershipReceipt(value); return true } catch { return false }
    }).transform((value) => parseOwnershipReceipt(value)),
  )
  assertReceiptMatchesArtifact(artifact, receipt, manifest, expectation)
  if (artifact.command.endsWith('-push')) {
    assertLocalPushOwnershipEvents(commandRecords, receipt)
  }
  validateExactLogOutput(artifact, commandRecords, receipt, expectation)
  await readJsonFile(providerStatePath, providerStateSchema)
  return receipt
}

function overlaps(startA: string, endA: string, startB: string, endB: string): boolean {
  const values = [startA, endA, startB, endB].map(Date.parse)
  if (values.some((value) => !Number.isFinite(value))) throw new Error('Malformed interval timestamp')
  return values[0] < values[3] && values[2] < values[1]
}

function assertConcurrentRuns(receipts: readonly OwnershipReceipt[]): void {
  if (receipts.length !== 2) throw new Error('Exactly two coordinator-test runs are required')
  const [left, right] = receipts
  const leftValues = receiptIdentityValues(left)
  const rightValues = new Set(receiptIdentityValues(right))
  const reused = leftValues.find((value) => rightValues.has(value))
  if (reused) throw new Error(`Concurrent identity is not distinct: ${reused}`)
  if (!overlaps(
    String(left.build.startTime), String(left.build.finishTime),
    String(right.build.startTime), String(right.build.finishTime),
  )) throw new Error('Concurrent global Build intervals do not overlap')
  if (!overlaps(
    String(left.execution.startTime), String(left.execution.completionTime),
    String(right.execution.startTime), String(right.execution.completionTime),
  )) throw new Error('Concurrent Job Execution intervals do not overlap')
  for (const receipt of receipts) {
    const other = receipt === left ? right : left
    const cleanupText = JSON.stringify(receipt.cleanup)
    const crossed = receiptIdentityValues(other).find((identity) =>
      cleanupText.includes(identity))
    if (crossed !== undefined) {
      throw new Error(`Cross-run cleanup identity detected: ${crossed}`)
    }
  }
}

async function exactRepoFile(repoRoot: string, path: string): Promise<string> {
  if (!isAbsolute(repoRoot) || !isAbsolute(path)) {
    throw new Error('Repository evidence paths must be absolute')
  }
  const root = resolve(repoRoot)
  const target = resolve(path)
  const inside = relative(root, target)
  if (inside.startsWith('..') || isAbsolute(inside)) {
    throw new Error(`Committed fixture escapes repository root: ${path}`)
  }
  const details = await stat(target)
  if (!details.isFile()) throw new Error(`Committed fixture is not a file: ${path}`)
  return target
}

async function validateContractEvidence(
  manifest: AcceptanceManifest,
  receipts: ReadonlyMap<string, OwnershipReceipt>,
): Promise<void> {
  const sanitizedPath = await exactEvidenceFile(
    manifest.evidenceRoot,
    manifest.contracts.sanitizedPath,
  )
  const replayPath = await exactEvidenceFile(
    manifest.evidenceRoot,
    manifest.contracts.replayPath,
  )
  const committedPath = await exactRepoFile(
    manifest.repoRoot,
    manifest.contracts.committedFixturePath,
  )
  const [sanitizedText, committedText] = await Promise.all([
    readBoundedRegularFile(sanitizedPath, {
      label: 'sanitized provider contracts',
      maxBytes: JSON_EVIDENCE_MAX_BYTES,
    }),
    readBoundedRegularFile(committedPath, {
      label: 'committed provider contracts',
      maxBytes: JSON_EVIDENCE_MAX_BYTES,
    }),
  ])
  let sanitizedValue: unknown
  let committedValue: unknown
  try {
    sanitizedValue = JSON.parse(sanitizedText)
    committedValue = JSON.parse(committedText)
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error(`Contract evidence is malformed JSON: ${String(error)}`)
  }
  const replay = replayRawLiveContracts(sanitizedValue)
  if (replay.acceptedSha !== manifest.acceptedSha) {
    throw new Error('Raw contract characterization SHA is stale')
  }
  const replayEvidence = await readJsonFile(replayPath, contractReplayEvidenceSchema)
  if (JSON.stringify(replayEvidence.replayed) !== JSON.stringify([replay])) {
    throw new Error('Stored raw-contract replay differs from production replay')
  }
  if (JSON.stringify(contractShape(sanitizedValue)) !==
      JSON.stringify(contractShape(committedValue))) {
    throw new Error('Sanitized provider contract shape differs from committed fixture')
  }

  const characterizations = manifest.artifacts.filter(
    ({ command }) => command === 'contract-characterization',
  )
  const successArtifact = characterizations.find(
    ({ expectedStatus }) => expectedStatus === 'succeeded',
  )
  const failureArtifact = characterizations.find(
    ({ expectedStatus }) => expectedStatus === 'failed',
  )
  if (!successArtifact || !failureArtifact) {
    throw new Error('Raw contracts require success and failure artifacts')
  }
  const successReceipt = receipts.get(successArtifact.id)
  const failureReceipt = receipts.get(failureArtifact.id)
  if (!successReceipt || !failureReceipt) {
    throw new Error('Raw contracts reference missing characterization receipts')
  }
  const contractPrefixes = [
    'FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT ',
    'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ',
  ] as const
  const successRecords = await readPrefixedEvidenceRecords(
    await exactEvidenceFile(
      manifest.evidenceRoot,
      successArtifact.commandLogPath,
    ),
    'success characterization records',
    contractPrefixes,
    semanticProviderRecordLimit(successReceipt.parts.length) + 1,
  )
  const failureRecords = await readPrefixedEvidenceRecords(
    await exactEvidenceFile(
      manifest.evidenceRoot,
      failureArtifact.commandLogPath,
    ),
    'failure characterization records',
    contractPrefixes,
    semanticProviderRecordLimit(failureReceipt.parts.length) + 1,
  )
  const successLog = `${successRecords.join('\n')}\n`
  const failureLog = `${failureRecords.join('\n')}\n`
  const characterized = characterizeContractLogs({
    acceptedSha: manifest.acceptedSha,
    providerVersion: replay.providerVersion,
    successLog,
    failureLog,
    committedFixture: committedValue,
  })
  if (JSON.stringify(characterized.sanitized) !==
      JSON.stringify(sanitizedValue) ||
      JSON.stringify(characterized.replay) !== JSON.stringify(replay)) {
    throw new Error(
      'Raw selected envelopes do not directly replay to the stored sanitized evidence',
    )
  }
  const expectedBindings = {
    acceptedSha: manifest.acceptedSha,
    providerVersion: replay.providerVersion,
    success: {
      runId: successReceipt.runId,
      buildId: String(successReceipt.build.id),
      jobName: successReceipt.job.name,
      jobUid: String(successReceipt.job.uid),
      jobGeneration: String(successReceipt.job.generation),
      executionName: String(successReceipt.execution.name),
      executionUid: String(successReceipt.execution.uid),
    },
    failure: {
      runId: failureReceipt.runId,
      jobName: failureReceipt.job.name,
      jobGeneration: String(failureReceipt.job.generation),
      executionName: String(failureReceipt.execution.name),
      executionUid: String(failureReceipt.execution.uid),
    },
  }
  if (JSON.stringify(characterized.bindings) !==
      JSON.stringify(expectedBindings)) {
    throw new Error(
      'Raw selected provider identities are not directly bound to exact receipts',
    )
  }
}

async function validateGateEvidence(manifest: AcceptanceManifest): Promise<void> {
  const preflight = await readJsonFile(
    await exactEvidenceFile(manifest.evidenceRoot, manifest.preflightPath),
    preflightEvidenceSchema,
  )
  const iam = await readJsonFile(
    await exactEvidenceFile(manifest.evidenceRoot, manifest.effectiveIamPath),
    effectiveIamEvidenceSchema,
  )
  const quota = await readJsonFile(
    await exactEvidenceFile(manifest.evidenceRoot, manifest.quotaPath),
    quotaEvidenceSchema,
  )
  if (preflight.head !== manifest.acceptedSha || iam.acceptedSha !== manifest.acceptedSha ||
      quota.acceptedSha !== manifest.acceptedSha) {
    throw new Error('Gate evidence is stale')
  }
  if (preflight.buildServiceAccount !== manifest.buildServiceAccount ||
      preflight.runtimeServiceAccount !== manifest.runtimeServiceAccount ||
      iam.buildServiceAccount !== manifest.buildServiceAccount ||
      iam.runtimeServiceAccount !== manifest.runtimeServiceAccount) {
    throw new Error('Gate evidence worker identities differ from manifest')
  }
  const expectedBuildGrants = [
    `//artifactregistry.googleapis.com/projects/${preflight.projectId}/locations/${preflight.runRegion}/repositories/${preflight.repositoryResource.split('/').at(-1)}\0roles/artifactregistry.writer`,
    `//cloudresourcemanager.googleapis.com/projects/${preflight.projectId}\0roles/logging.logWriter`,
    `//storage.googleapis.com/projects/_/buckets/${preflight.sourceBucket}\0roles/storage.objectViewer`,
  ]
  exactStringSet(iam.buildEffectiveGrantKeys, expectedBuildGrants, 'Build effective grants')
  exactStringSet(iam.runtimeEffectiveGrantKeys, [], 'Runtime effective grants')
  if (preflight.iamAnalysisScope !== iam.analysisScope ||
      preflight.effectiveIam.analysisScope !== iam.analysisScope) {
    throw new Error('IAM analysis authority scope differs')
  }
  const buildResource =
    `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/${preflight.projectId}/serviceAccounts/${manifest.buildServiceAccount}`
  const runtimeResource =
    `//iam.googleapis.com/projects/${preflight.projectId}/serviceAccounts/${manifest.runtimeServiceAccount}`
  exactStringSet(
    iam.actAsResources,
    [buildResource, runtimeResource],
    'actAs resources',
  )
  exactStringSet(iam.tokenMintResources, [buildResource], 'Token mint resources')
  if (quota.limit !== preflight.globalOngoingBuildLimit ||
      quota.limit !== preflight.quota.limit) {
    throw new Error('Global Build quota evidence differs')
  }
}

export async function validateAcceptanceManifest(
  value: unknown,
): Promise<AcceptanceManifest> {
  const manifest = acceptanceManifestSchema.parse(value)
  await validateGateEvidence(manifest)
  for (const command of requiredPublicCommandKinds) {
    const count = manifest.artifacts.filter((artifact) => artifact.command === command).length
    const expected = command === 'coordinator-test' ? 2 : 1
    if (count !== expected) throw new Error(`missing public command ${command}: expected ${expected}, got ${count}`)
  }
  const characterizations = manifest.artifacts.filter(
    ({ command }) => command === 'contract-characterization',
  )
  if (characterizations.length !== 2 ||
      characterizations.filter(({ expectedStatus }) => expectedStatus === 'succeeded').length !== 1 ||
      characterizations.filter(({ expectedStatus }) => expectedStatus === 'failed').length !== 1) {
    throw new Error('Exactly one success and one failure characterization are required')
  }
  const ids = manifest.artifacts.map(({ id }) => id)
  if (new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) throw new Error('Artifact IDs must be unique')
  const receipts = new Map<string, OwnershipReceipt>()
  for (const artifact of manifest.artifacts) {
    receipts.set(artifact.id, await validateArtifact(manifest, artifact))
  }
  await validateContractEvidence(manifest, receipts)
  assertConcurrentRuns(
    manifest.artifacts
      .filter(({ command }) => command === 'coordinator-test')
      .map(({ id }) => receipts.get(id)!),
  )
  return manifest
}
```

Add concrete extract/inspect/preflight and validator CLI modes. They emit no
credential/token data and use exact cardinality, so Task 21 never assigns a
manual Job/Execution identity:

```ts
function parseExactFlags(argv: readonly string[]): {
  command: string
  flags: Readonly<Record<string, string>>
} {
  const [command, ...tokens] = argv
  if (!command) throw new Error('Missing acceptance command')
  const flags: Record<string, string> = {}
  for (const token of tokens) {
    const match = /^--([a-z-]+)=(.+)$/.exec(token)
    if (!match || flags[match[1]] !== undefined) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid or duplicate acceptance flag ${JSON.stringify(token)}`)
    }
    flags[match[1]] = match[2]
  }
  return { command, flags }
}

function requireFlagNames(flags: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, expected: readonly string[]): void {
  exactStringSet(Object.keys(flags), expected, 'acceptance CLI flags')
}

async function receiptFromLog(path: string): Promise<OwnershipReceipt> {
  const records = await readPrefixedEvidenceRecords(
    path,
    'terminal receipt log',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '],
    1,
  )
  return onePrefixedJson(
    records,
    'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN ',
    z.custom<OwnershipReceipt>((value) => {
      try { parseOwnershipReceipt(value); return true } catch { return false }
    }).transform((value) => parseOwnershipReceipt(value)),
  )
}

export type ExtractedReceiptIdentity = {
  kind: 'vitest' | 'e2e'
  runId: string
  head: string
  sourceBucket: string
  sourceObject: string
  sourceGeneration: string
  buildCorrelationTag: string
  buildId: string
  buildResourceName: string
  buildProjectId: string
  buildProjectNumber: string
  imageTag: string
  digest: string
  job: string
  jobResourceName: string
  jobUid: string
  jobGeneration: string
  execution: string
  executionResourceName: string
  executionUid: string
}

const identityValueSchema = z.string()
  .min(1)
  .max(4096)
  .refine((value) => !/[\r\n]/.test(value), 'identity value contains newline')
const extractedReceiptIdentitySchema: z.ZodType<ExtractedReceiptIdentity> =
  z.object({
    kind: z.enum(['vitest', 'e2e']),
    runId: identityValueSchema,
    head: identityValueSchema,
    sourceBucket: identityValueSchema,
    sourceObject: identityValueSchema,
    sourceGeneration: identityValueSchema,
    buildCorrelationTag: identityValueSchema,
    buildId: identityValueSchema,
    buildResourceName: identityValueSchema,
    buildProjectId: identityValueSchema,
    buildProjectNumber: identityValueSchema,
    imageTag: identityValueSchema,
    digest: identityValueSchema,
    job: identityValueSchema,
    jobResourceName: identityValueSchema,
    jobUid: identityValueSchema,
    jobGeneration: identityValueSchema,
    execution: identityValueSchema,
    executionResourceName: identityValueSchema,
    executionUid: identityValueSchema,
  }).strict()
const extractedReceiptIdentityOrder = [
  'kind', 'runId', 'head', 'sourceBucket', 'sourceObject', 'sourceGeneration',
  'buildCorrelationTag', 'buildId', 'buildResourceName', 'buildProjectId',
  'buildProjectNumber', 'imageTag', 'digest', 'job', 'jobResourceName',
  'jobUid', 'jobGeneration', 'execution', 'executionResourceName',
  'executionUid',
] as const satisfies readonly (keyof ExtractedReceiptIdentity)[]

export function extractReceiptIdentity(
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  kind: 'vitest' | 'e2e',
): ExtractedReceiptIdentity {
  const value = z.object({
    status: z.literal('succeeded'),
    kind: z.literal(kind),
    operation: z.literal('run'),
    runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
    head: z.string().regex(FULL_OBJECT_ID),
    source: z.object({
      bucket: z.string().min(1), object: z.string().min(1),
      generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
    }).passthrough(),
    build: z.object({
      correlationTag: z.string().min(1),
      id: z.string().min(1),
      resourceName: z.string().min(1),
      projectId: z.string().min(1),
      projectNumber: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
    }).passthrough(),
    image: z.object({
      tag: z.string().min(1), digest: z.string().regex(/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/),
    }).passthrough(),
    job: z.object({
      name: z.string().min(1), resourceName: z.string().min(1), uid: z.string().min(1),
      generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
    }).passthrough(),
    execution: z.object({
      name: z.string().min(1), resourceName: z.string().min(1), uid: z.string().min(1),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).passthrough().parse(receipt)
  return {
    kind,
    runId: value.runId,
    head: value.head,
    sourceBucket: value.source.bucket,
    sourceObject: value.source.object,
    sourceGeneration: value.source.generation,
    buildCorrelationTag: value.build.correlationTag,
    buildId: value.build.id,
    buildResourceName: value.build.resourceName,
    buildProjectId: value.build.projectId,
    buildProjectNumber: value.build.projectNumber,
    imageTag: value.image.tag,
    digest: value.image.digest,
    job: value.job.name,
    jobResourceName: value.job.resourceName,
    jobUid: value.job.uid,
    jobGeneration: value.job.generation,
    execution: value.execution.name,
    executionResourceName: value.execution.resourceName,
    executionUid: value.execution.uid,
  }
}

export async function writePreflightEvidence(
  logPath: string,
  acceptedSha: string,
  preflightPath: string,
  iamPath: string,
  quotaPath: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const records = await readPrefixedEvidenceRecords(
    logPath,
    'cloud preflight log',
    ['FRESHELL_CLOUD_PREFLIGHT '],
    1,
  )
  const record = onePrefixedJson(
    records,
    'FRESHELL_CLOUD_PREFLIGHT ',
    preflightEvidenceSchema,
  )
  if (record.head !== acceptedSha) throw new Error('Preflight SHA differs from accepted SHA')
  const iam = effectiveIamEvidenceSchema.parse({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    acceptedSha,
    buildServiceAccount: record.buildServiceAccount,
    runtimeServiceAccount: record.runtimeServiceAccount,
    ...record.effectiveIam,
  })
  const quota = quotaEvidenceSchema.parse({
    schemaVersion: 1,
    acceptedSha,
    buildRegion: 'global',
    machineType: 'E2_HIGHCPU_32',
    logging: 'CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY',
    ...record.quota,
  })
  await Promise.all([
    writeFile(preflightPath, `${JSON.stringify(record)}\n`, { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 }),
    writeFile(iamPath, `${JSON.stringify(iam)}\n`, { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 }),
    writeFile(quotaPath, `${JSON.stringify(quota)}\n`, { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 }),
  ])
}

export async function verifyAcceptanceMain(
  argv: readonly string[],
  runCommand: RunCommand = defaultRunCommand,
): Promise<number> {
  const { command, flags } = parseExactFlags(argv)
  if (command === 'canonical-json') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, ['input'])
    const raw = await readBoundedRegularFile(flags.input, {
      label: 'canonical JSON evidence',
      maxBytes: JSON_EVIDENCE_MAX_BYTES,
    })
    let value: unknown
    try { value = JSON.parse(raw) }
    catch (error) {
      throw new Error(`Canonical JSON evidence is malformed: ${String(error)}`)
    }
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(value))
    return 0
  }
  if (command === 'identity-values') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, ['input'])
    const value = await readJsonFile(
      flags.input,
      extractedReceiptIdentitySchema,
      'extracted receipt identity',
    )
    for (const key of extractedReceiptIdentityOrder) {
      process.stdout.write(`${value[key]}\n`)
    }
    return 0
  }
  if (command === 'fixture-remove-command') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, ['command', 'input', 'output'])
    const manifest = await readJsonFile(
      flags.input,
      acceptanceManifestSchema,
      'acceptance manifest mutation input',
    )
    const removed = commandSchema.parse(flags.command)
    const artifacts = manifest.artifacts.filter(
      ({ command: candidate }) => candidate !== removed,
    )
    if (artifacts.length === manifest.artifacts.length) {
      throw new Error(`No ${removed} artifact exists to remove`)
    }
    await writeFile(
      flags.output,
      `${JSON.stringify({ ...manifest, artifacts })}\n`,
      { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 },
    )
    return 0
  }
  if (command === 'validate') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, ['manifest'])
    const manifest = await readJsonFile(flags.manifest, acceptanceManifestSchema)
    await validateAcceptanceManifest(manifest)
    process.stdout.write('acceptance manifest verified\n')
    return 0
  }
  if (command === 'extract-receipt') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, ['kind', 'log'])
    const kind = z.enum(['vitest', 'e2e']).parse(flags.kind)
    const identity = extractReceiptIdentity(await receiptFromLog(flags.log), kind)
    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(identity)}\n`)
    return 0
  }
  if (command === 'inspect-receipt') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, ['log', 'output'])
    const state = await inspectReceiptResources(await receiptFromLog(flags.log), runCommand)
    await writeFile(flags.output, `${JSON.stringify(state)}\n`, { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 })
    return 0
  }
  if (command === 'split-preflight') {
    requireFlagNames(flags, [
      'accepted-sha', 'iam-output', 'log', 'preflight-output', 'quota-output',
    ])
    await writePreflightEvidence(
      flags.log,
      flags['accepted-sha'],
      flags['preflight-output'],
      flags['iam-output'],
      flags['quota-output'],
    )
    return 0
  }
  throw new Error(`Unknown acceptance command ${command}`)
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  verifyAcceptanceMain(process.argv.slice(2)).then(
    (code) => { process.exitCode = code },
    (error) => {
      process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.stack : String(error)}\n`)
      process.exitCode = 1
    },
  )
}
```

Create `scripts/cloud-run/replay-contract-fixtures.ts` with this complete
implementation and main. It accepts only bounded raw provider-contract JSON
bundles, never lifecycle receipts. The exact production
`replayRawLiveContracts` function performs all Build/v2 Job/v2 Operation/v1
Execution/LogEntry parsing and authority checks; this CLI adds file, SHA, and
cardinality checks:

```ts
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'
import { z } from 'zod'

import { readBoundedRegularFile } from './evidence-io.js'
import {
  replayRawLiveContracts,
  type RawLiveContractsReplay,
} from './lifecycle.js'

const replaySchema = z.object({
  acceptedSha: z.string().regex(/^(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})$/),
  providerVersion: z.string().min(1),
  successCounters: z.object({
    succeededCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
    failedCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
    cancelledCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
    runningCount: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  }).strict(),
  failureCounters: z.object({
    succeededCount: z.literal(0),
    failedCount: z.literal(1),
    cancelledCount: z.literal(0),
    runningCount: z.literal(0),
  }).strict(),
  failureOmittedCounters: z.tuple([
    z.literal('succeededCount'),
    z.literal('cancelledCount'),
    z.literal('runningCount'),
  ]),
  successPartIds: z.array(z.string()).min(1),
  failureResultRejected: z.literal(true),
}).strict()
export type RawProviderReplay = z.infer<typeof replaySchema>

export async function replayRawProviderContracts(paths: readonly string[]): Promise<RawProviderReplay[]> {
  if (paths.length < 1 || paths.length > 8 || new Set(paths).size !== paths.length) {
    throw new Error('Replay requires 1 through 8 distinct raw provider fixture paths')
  }
  return Promise.all(paths.map(async (path) => {
    const raw = await readBoundedRegularFile(path, {
      label: `raw provider fixture ${path}`,
      maxBytes: 1024 * 1024,
    })
    let value: unknown
    try { value = JSON.parse(raw) }
    catch (error) { throw new Error(`Malformed raw provider fixture ${path}: ${String(error)}`) }
    const replay: RawLiveContractsReplay = replayRawLiveContracts(value)
    return replaySchema.parse(replay)
  }))
}

function replayArguments(argv: readonly string[]): {
  acceptedSha: string
  paths: string[]
} {
  if (argv[0] !== '--accepted-sha' || !argv[1]) {
    throw new Error(
      'Usage: replay-contract-fixtures --accepted-sha SHA --input raw.json',
    )
  }
  const acceptedSha = z.string()
    .regex(/^(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})$/)
    .parse(argv[1])
  const paths: string[] = []
  for (let index = 2; index < argv.length; index += 2) {
    if (argv[index] !== '--input' || !argv[index + 1]) {
      throw new Error(
        'Usage: replay-contract-fixtures --accepted-sha SHA --input raw.json',
      )
    }
    paths.push(argv[index + 1])
  }
  return { acceptedSha, paths }
}

export async function replayContractMain(argv: readonly string[]): Promise<number> {
  const { acceptedSha, paths } = replayArguments(argv)
  const replayed = await replayRawProviderContracts(paths)
  if (replayed.some((value) => value.acceptedSha !== acceptedSha)) {
    throw new Error('Raw provider characterization SHA differs from accepted SHA')
  }
  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ schemaVersion: 1, replayed })}\n`)
  return 0
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  replayContractMain(process.argv.slice(2)).then(
    (code) => { process.exitCode = code },
    (error) => {
      process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.stack : String(error)}\n`)
      process.exitCode = 1
    },
  )
}
```

Create `scripts/cloud-run/characterize-contracts.ts` with the following
closed-purpose implementation. It consumes only the opt-in local event records
from two exact public-command logs, proves their identities against those logs'
schema-valid receipts, validates the unredacted selected envelopes through
`replayRawLiveContracts`, pseudonymizes identifiers consistently, exact-compares
the recursive provider shape and stable sanitized content with the committed
fixture, and writes the sanitized evidence plus its directly parsed replay:

```ts
import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'
import { z } from 'zod'

import {
  readBoundedRegularFile,
  readBoundedSelectedLines,
} from './evidence-io.js'
import {
  parseOwnershipReceipt,
  replayRawLiveContracts,
  semanticProviderByteLimit,
  semanticProviderRecordLimit,
  type OwnershipReceipt,
  type RawLiveContractsReplay,
} from './lifecycle.js'

const FULL_OBJECT_ID = /^(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})$/
const CONTRACT_PREFIX = 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_PROVIDER_CONTRACT '
const RECEIPT_PREFIX = 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '
const MAX_EXPECTED_CLOUD_PARTS = 96
const CHARACTERIZATION_LOG_MAX_BYTES = 16 * 1024 * 1024
const CHARACTERIZATION_LOG_MAX_LINES = 100_000
const CHARACTERIZATION_LINE_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024

const providerContractRecordSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
  runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
  kind: z.enum(['build', 'job', 'operation', 'execution', 'logEntry']),
  value: z.unknown(),
}).strict()
type ProviderContractRecord = z.infer<typeof providerContractRecordSchema>

const selectedBuildSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  projectId: z.string().min(1),
  status: z.literal('SUCCESS'),
  source: z.object({ storageSource: z.object({
    bucket: z.string(), object: z.string(), generation: z.string(),
  }).passthrough() }).passthrough(),
  substitutions: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
}).passthrough()
const selectedJobSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1),
  uid: z.string().min(1),
  etag: z.string().min(1),
  generation: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
  template: z.object({
    taskCount: z.number().int().positive(),
    template: z.object({
      serviceAccount: z.string().email(),
      timeout: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*s$/),
      containers: z.array(z.object({
        image: z.string().min(1),
        env: z.array(z.object({
          name: z.string().regex(/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*$/),
          value: z.string(),
        }).passthrough()),
        resources: z.object({
          limits: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
        }).passthrough(),
      }).passthrough()).length(1),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()
const selectedOperationSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1),
  done: z.literal(true),
  response: z.object({
    name: z.string().min(1),
    uid: z.string().min(1),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()
const selectedExecutionSchema = z.object({
  metadata: z.object({
    name: z.string().min(1),
    namespace: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+$/),
    uid: z.string().min(1),
  }).passthrough(),
  status: z.object({
    conditions: z.array(z.object({
      type: z.string(),
      status: z.string(),
    }).passthrough()),
    completionTime: z.string().datetime({ offset: true }),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()
const selectedLogEntrySchema = z.object({
  resource: z.object({
    type: z.literal('cloud_run_job'),
    labels: z.object({
      project_id: z.string(),
      location: z.string(),
      job_name: z.string(),
    }).passthrough(),
  }).passthrough(),
  labels: z.record(z.string(), z.string()),
  jsonPayload: z.object({
    marker: z.literal('FRESHELL_CLOUD_RESULT'),
    runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
    execution: z.string().min(1),
    status: z.enum(['passed', 'failed']),
  }).passthrough(),
}).passthrough()

export const contractBindingsSchema = z.object({
  acceptedSha: z.string().regex(FULL_OBJECT_ID),
  providerVersion: z.string().min(1),
  success: z.object({
    runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
    buildId: z.string().min(1),
    jobName: z.string().min(1),
    jobUid: z.string().min(1),
    jobGeneration: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
    executionName: z.string().min(1),
    executionUid: z.string().min(1),
  }).strict(),
  failure: z.object({
    runId: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/),
    jobName: z.string().min(1),
    jobGeneration: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/),
    executionName: z.string().min(1),
    executionUid: z.string().min(1),
  }).strict(),
}).strict()
export type ContractBindings = z.infer<typeof contractBindingsSchema>

function oneReceipt(text: string): OwnershipReceipt {
  const rows = text.split('\n').filter((line) => line.startsWith(RECEIPT_PREFIX))
  if (rows.length !== 1) {
    throw new Error(`Expected one terminal receipt; got ${rows.length}`)
  }
  let value: unknown
  try { value = JSON.parse(rows[0].slice(RECEIPT_PREFIX.length)) }
  catch (error) { throw new Error(`Malformed terminal receipt: ${String(error)}`) }
  return parseOwnershipReceipt(value)
}

function contractRecords(
  text: string,
  expectedPartLimit: number,
): ProviderContractRecord[] {
  const rows = text.split('\n')
    .filter((line) => line.startsWith(CONTRACT_PREFIX))
  const maximum = semanticProviderRecordLimit(expectedPartLimit)
  if (rows.length === 0 || rows.length > maximum) {
    throw new Error(
      `Expected 1..${maximum} semantic provider records; got ${rows.length}`,
    )
  }
  return rows.map((line, index) => {
    let value: unknown
    try { value = JSON.parse(line.slice(CONTRACT_PREFIX.length)) }
```
