```ts
    catch (error) { throw new Error(`Malformed contract record ${index}: ${String(error)}`) }
    const parsed = providerContractRecordSchema.parse(value)
    const byteLimit = parsed.kind === 'logEntry'
      ? 64 * 1024
      : 256 * 1024
    if (Buffer.byteLength(line, 'utf8') > byteLimit) {
      throw new Error(
        `Raw ${parsed.kind} contract record ${index} exceeds ${byteLimit} bytes`,
      )
    }
    return parsed
  })
}

function requireReceiptBoundRecords(
  records: readonly ProviderContractRecord[],
  receipt: OwnershipReceipt,
  label: string,
): void {
  if (records.some(({ runId }) => runId !== receipt.runId)) {
    throw new Error(`${label}: provider record run ID differs from receipt`)
  }
}

function exactContract<T>(
  records: readonly ProviderContractRecord[],
  kind: ProviderContractRecord['kind'],
  schema: z.ZodType<T>,
  predicate: (value: T) => boolean,
  label: string,
): T {
  const distinct = new Map<string, T>()
  for (const record of records) {
    if (record.kind !== kind) continue
    const parsed = schema.safeParse(record.value)
    if (parsed.success && predicate(parsed.data)) {
      distinct.set(JSON.stringify(parsed.data), parsed.data)
    }
  }
  if (distinct.size !== 1) {
    throw new Error(`${label}: expected one distinct exact ${kind}; got ${distinct.size}`)
  }
  return [...distinct.values()][0]
}

function operationSuffix(value: string): string {
  return value.split('/').at(-1) ?? value
}

function requiredPaths(value: unknown, paths: readonly string[], label: string): string[] {
  for (const path of paths) {
    let cursor: unknown = value
    for (const segment of path.split('.')) {
      if (cursor === null || typeof cursor !== 'object' ||
          !(segment in (cursor as Record<string, unknown>))) {
        throw new Error(`${label} omits characterized field ${path}`)
      }
      cursor = (cursor as Record<string, unknown>)[segment]
    }
  }
  return [...paths]
}

export function contractShape(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(contractShape)
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
        .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
        .map(([key, child]) => [key, contractShape(child)]),
    )
  }
  if (value === null) return null
  return `<${typeof value}>`
}

const dynamicContractKeys = new Set([
  'acceptedSha',
  'capturedAt',
  'providerVersion',
  'createTime',
  'updateTime',
  'startTime',
  'finishTime',
  'completionTime',
  'lastTransitionTime',
  'timestamp',
  'receiveTimestamp',
  'insertId',
  'resourceVersion',
  'generation',
  'observedGeneration',
  'creator',
  'lastModifier',
])

export function contractStableContent(
  value: unknown,
  field = '',
): unknown {
  if (dynamicContractKeys.has(field)) {
    return `<dynamic:${value === null ? 'null' : typeof value}>`
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map((child) => contractStableContent(child))
  }
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
        .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
        .map(([key, child]) => [
          key,
          contractStableContent(child, key),
        ]),
    )
  }
  return value
}

function replaceStrings(value: unknown, replacements: readonly [string, string][]): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((child) => replaceStrings(child, replacements))
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
      .map(([key, child]) => [key, replaceStrings(child, replacements)]))
  }
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return value
  return replacements.reduce(
    (text, [from, to]) => from.length === 0 ? text : text.split(from).join(to),
    value,
  )
}

function imageCoordinates(taggedImage: string): {
  region: string
  projectId: string
  repository: string
  imageName: string
  tag: string
} {
  const match =
    /^([a-z0-9-]+)-docker\.pkg\.dev\/([^/]+)\/([^/]+)\/([^:]+):([^:]+)$/
      .exec(taggedImage)
  if (!match) throw new Error('Characterization image tag is malformed')
  return {
    region: match[1],
    projectId: match[2],
    repository: match[3],
    imageName: match[4],
    tag: match[5],
  }
}

export type CharacterizeContractsInput = {
  acceptedSha: string
  providerVersion: string
  successLog: string
  failureLog: string
  committedFixture: unknown
  allowShapeDrift?: boolean
}
export type CharacterizeContractsResult = {
  sanitized: unknown
  replay: RawLiveContractsReplay
  bindings: ContractBindings
}

export function characterizeContractLogs(
  input: CharacterizeContractsInput,
): CharacterizeContractsResult {
  if (!FULL_OBJECT_ID.test(input.acceptedSha)) throw new Error('Invalid accepted SHA')
  const successReceipt = oneReceipt(input.successLog)
  const failureReceipt = oneReceipt(input.failureLog)
  if (successReceipt.status !== 'succeeded' ||
      failureReceipt.status !== 'failed' ||
      successReceipt.head !== input.acceptedSha ||
      failureReceipt.head !== input.acceptedSha ||
      successReceipt.kind !== 'vitest' ||
      failureReceipt.kind !== 'vitest') {
    throw new Error('Characterization receipt status/SHA/kind mismatch')
  }
  const successRecords = contractRecords(
    input.successLog,
    successReceipt.parts.length,
  )
  const failureRecords = contractRecords(
    input.failureLog,
    MAX_EXPECTED_CLOUD_PARTS,
  )
  requireReceiptBoundRecords(successRecords, successReceipt, 'success')
  requireReceiptBoundRecords(failureRecords, failureReceipt, 'failure')
  const build = exactContract(
    successRecords,
    'build',
    selectedBuildSchema,
    (value) => value.id === successReceipt.build.id &&
      value.name === successReceipt.build.resourceName &&
      value.projectId === successReceipt.build.projectId,
    'success Build',
  )
  const job = exactContract(
    successRecords,
    'job',
    selectedJobSchema,
    (value) => value.name === successReceipt.job.resourceName &&
      value.uid === successReceipt.job.uid &&
      value.etag === successReceipt.job.etag &&
      value.generation === successReceipt.job.generation,
    'success Job',
  )
  const operation = exactContract(
    successRecords,
    'operation',
    selectedOperationSchema,
    (value) => operationSuffix(value.name) ===
      operationSuffix(String(successReceipt.execution.operationName)) &&
      value.response.name === successReceipt.execution.resourceName &&
      value.response.uid === successReceipt.execution.uid,
    'success Operation',
  )
  const executionSuccess = exactContract(
    successRecords,
    'execution',
    selectedExecutionSchema,
    (value) => value.metadata.name === successReceipt.execution.name &&
      value.metadata.uid === successReceipt.execution.uid,
    'success Execution',
  )
  const executionFailure = exactContract(
    failureRecords,
    'execution',
    selectedExecutionSchema,
    (value) => value.metadata.name === failureReceipt.execution.name &&
      value.metadata.uid === failureReceipt.execution.uid,
    'failure Execution',
  )
  const logEntrySuccess = exactContract(
    successRecords,
    'logEntry',
    selectedLogEntrySchema,
    (value) => value.jsonPayload.status === 'passed' &&
      value.jsonPayload.runId === successReceipt.runId &&
      value.jsonPayload.execution === successReceipt.execution.name,
    'success LogEntry',
  )
  const logEntryFailure = exactContract(
    failureRecords,
    'logEntry',
    selectedLogEntrySchema,
    (value) => value.jsonPayload.status === 'failed' &&
      value.jsonPayload.runId === failureReceipt.runId &&
      value.jsonPayload.execution === failureReceipt.execution.name,
    'failure LogEntry',
  )
  const taggedImage = String(successReceipt.image.taggedImage)
  const coordinates = imageCoordinates(taggedImage)
  if (coordinates.projectId !== successReceipt.projectId ||
      coordinates.region !== successReceipt.runRegion) {
    throw new Error('Receipt image coordinates differ from run context')
  }
  const container = job.template.template.containers[0]
  const jobEnvironment = Object.fromEntries(
    container.env.map(({ name, value }) => [name, value]),
  )
  if (Object.keys(jobEnvironment).length !== container.env.length ||
      jobEnvironment.FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND !== 'vitest' ||
      jobEnvironment.FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID !== successReceipt.runId) {
    throw new Error('Characterization Job environment is duplicate or foreign')
  }
  const {
    FRESHELL_CLOUD_KIND: _kind,
    FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN_ID: _runId,
    ...workloadEnv
  } = jobEnvironment
  const context = {
    projectId: successReceipt.projectId,
    projectNumber: String(successReceipt.projectNumber),
    runRegion: successReceipt.runRegion,
    repository: coordinates.repository,
    imageName: coordinates.imageName,
    buildServiceAccount: String(successReceipt.build.serviceAccount),
    runtimeServiceAccount: successReceipt.job.serviceAccount,
    runId: successReceipt.runId,
    failureRunId: failureReceipt.runId,
    sourceBucket: String(successReceipt.source.bucket),
    sourceObject: String(successReceipt.source.object),
    sourceGeneration: String(successReceipt.source.generation),
    imageTag: coordinates.tag,
    immutableImage: String(successReceipt.image.immutableImage),
    jobName: successReceipt.job.name,
    failureJobName: failureReceipt.job.name,
    executionSuccessName: String(successReceipt.execution.name),
    executionFailureName: String(failureReceipt.execution.name),
    taskTimeout: job.template.template.timeout,
    memory: z.string().regex(/^[1-9][0-9]*(?:Mi|Gi)$/)
      .parse(container.resources.limits.memory),
    cpu: z.coerce.number().int().positive()
      .parse(container.resources.limits.cpu),
    workloadEnv,
    expectedSuccessParts: successReceipt.parts
      .map(({ partId }: { partId: string }) => partId)
      .sort(),
  }
  const envelope = {
    schemaVersion: 1 as const,
    characterization: {
      acceptedSha: input.acceptedSha,
      capturedAt: String(failureReceipt.execution.completionTime),
      providerVersion: input.providerVersion,
      apiVersions: {
        build: 'cloudbuild.googleapis.com/v1',
        job: 'run.googleapis.com/v2',
        operation: 'run.googleapis.com/v2',
        execution: 'run.googleapis.com/v1',
        logEntry: 'logging.googleapis.com/v2',
      },
      fieldPresence: {
        build: requiredPaths(build, [
          'name', 'id', 'projectId', 'status', 'createTime', 'startTime',
          'finishTime', 'source', 'serviceAccount', 'tags', 'substitutions',
          'results',
        ], 'Build'),
        job: requiredPaths(job, [
          'name', 'uid', 'etag', 'generation', 'createTime', 'updateTime',
          'labels', 'template', 'terminalCondition',
        ], 'Job'),
        operation: requiredPaths(operation, [
          'name', 'done', 'metadata', 'response',
        ], 'Operation'),
        executionSuccess: requiredPaths(executionSuccess, [
          'apiVersion', 'kind', 'metadata', 'spec', 'status.conditions',
          'status.succeededCount', 'status.completionTime',
        ], 'success Execution'),
        executionFailure: requiredPaths(executionFailure, [
          'apiVersion', 'kind', 'metadata', 'spec', 'status.conditions',
          'status.failedCount', 'status.completionTime',
        ], 'failure Execution'),
        logEntrySuccess: requiredPaths(logEntrySuccess, [
          'logName', 'resource', 'timestamp', 'receiveTimestamp', 'severity',
          'insertId', 'labels', 'jsonPayload',
        ], 'success LogEntry'),
        logEntryFailure: requiredPaths(logEntryFailure, [
          'logName', 'resource', 'timestamp', 'receiveTimestamp', 'severity',
          'insertId', 'labels', 'jsonPayload',
        ], 'failure LogEntry'),
      },
    },
    context,
    build,
    job,
    operation,
    executionSuccess,
    executionFailure,
    logEntrySuccess,
    logEntryFailure,
  }

  // Validate actual identities and provider values before pseudonymization.
  replayRawLiveContracts(envelope)
  const replacements: [string, string][] = [
    [String(successReceipt.execution.uid), `execution-uid-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [String(failureReceipt.execution.uid), `execution-uid-${'2'.repeat(32)}`],
    [String(successReceipt.job.uid), `job-uid-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [String(successReceipt.job.etag), `etag-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [String(successReceipt.build.id), `build-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [operationSuffix(String(successReceipt.execution.operationName)),
      `run-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [String(successReceipt.execution.name), `execution-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [String(failureReceipt.execution.name), `execution-${'2'.repeat(32)}`],
    [successReceipt.job.name, `job-${'1'.repeat(32)}`],
    [failureReceipt.job.name, `job-${'2'.repeat(32)}`],
    [successReceipt.runId, '1'.repeat(32)],
    [failureReceipt.runId, '2'.repeat(32)],
    [String(successReceipt.source.object), `source/${'1'.repeat(32)}.tar.gz`],
    [String(successReceipt.source.generation), '1786766419256749'],
    [String(successReceipt.image.digest), `sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`],
    [coordinates.tag, '1'.repeat(32)],
    [String(successReceipt.source.bucket), 'test-source-bucket'],
    [successReceipt.job.serviceAccount,
      'freshell-runtime@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com'],
    [String(successReceipt.build.serviceAccount),
      'freshell-build@test-project.iam.gserviceaccount.com'],
    [successReceipt.account, 'caller@example.com'],
    [successReceipt.projectId, 'test-project'],
    [String(successReceipt.projectNumber), '123456789'],
    [coordinates.repository, 'freshell-e2e'],
    [coordinates.imageName, 'freshell-vitest'],
    [coordinates.region, 'us-west1'],
  ]
  replacements.sort(([left], [right]) => right.length - left.length)
  const sanitized = replaceStrings(envelope, replacements)
  const sanitizedReplay = replayRawLiveContracts(sanitized)
  const committedShape = contractShape(input.committedFixture)
  const sanitizedShape = contractShape(sanitized)
  if (!input.allowShapeDrift &&
      JSON.stringify(sanitizedShape) !== JSON.stringify(committedShape)) {
    throw new Error(
      'Live provider contract shape drifted; return to Task 11, run the explicit update command, commit, push, and restart Tasks 20–21',
    )
  }
  if (!input.allowShapeDrift &&
      JSON.stringify(contractStableContent(sanitized)) !==
        JSON.stringify(contractStableContent(input.committedFixture))) {
    throw new Error(
      'Live stable provider contract content differs from committed fixture',
    )
  }
  const bindings = contractBindingsSchema.parse({
    acceptedSha: input.acceptedSha,
    providerVersion: sanitizedReplay.providerVersion,
    success: {
      runId: successReceipt.runId,
      buildId: String(successReceipt.build.id),
      jobName: successReceipt.job.name,
      jobUid: String(successReceipt.job.uid),
      jobGeneration: String(successReceipt.job.generation),
      executionName: String(successReceipt.execution.name),
      executionUid: String(successReceipt.execution.uid),
    },
    failure: {
      runId: failureReceipt.runId,
      jobName: failureReceipt.job.name,
      jobGeneration: String(failureReceipt.job.generation),
      executionName: String(failureReceipt.execution.name),
      executionUid: String(failureReceipt.execution.uid),
    },
  })
  return { sanitized, replay: sanitizedReplay, bindings }
}

function exactFlags(argv: readonly string[]): {
  command: 'check' | 'update'
  flags: Record<string, string>
} {
  const [commandValue, ...tokens] = argv
  const command = z.enum(['check', 'update']).parse(commandValue)
  const flags: Record<string, string> = {}
  for (const token of tokens) {
    const match = /^--([a-z-]+)=(.+)$/.exec(token)
    if (!match || flags[match[1]] !== undefined) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid or duplicate characterization flag ${token}`)
    }
    flags[match[1]] = match[2]
  }
  const expected = [
    'accepted-sha', 'committed', 'failure-log', 'output',
    'provider-version', 'replay-output', 'success-log',
  ].sort()
  if (JSON.stringify(Object.keys(flags).sort()) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) {
    throw new Error(`Characterization flags mismatch: ${Object.keys(flags).sort()}`)
  }
  return { command, flags }
}

async function readCharacterizationRecords(
  path: string,
  label: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const maximum = semanticProviderRecordLimit(MAX_EXPECTED_CLOUD_PARTS) + 1
  const scanned = await readBoundedSelectedLines(path, {
    label,
    maxBytes: CHARACTERIZATION_LOG_MAX_BYTES,
    maxLineBytes: CHARACTERIZATION_LINE_MAX_BYTES,
    maxLines: CHARACTERIZATION_LOG_MAX_LINES,
    maxSelectedRecords: maximum,
    maxSelectedBytes:
      semanticProviderByteLimit(MAX_EXPECTED_CLOUD_PARTS) +
      1024 * 1024,
    select: (line) =>
      line.startsWith(CONTRACT_PREFIX) ||
      line.startsWith(RECEIPT_PREFIX),
  })
  return `${scanned.records.join('\n')}\n`
}

export async function characterizeContractsMain(argv: readonly string[]): Promise<number> {
  const { command, flags } = exactFlags(argv)
  const [successLog, failureLog, committedText, providerVersionText] =
    await Promise.all([
      readCharacterizationRecords(
        flags['success-log'],
        'success characterization log',
      ),
      readCharacterizationRecords(
        flags['failure-log'],
        'failure characterization log',
      ),
      readBoundedRegularFile(flags.committed, {
        label: 'committed provider fixture',
        maxBytes: 1024 * 1024,
      }),
      readBoundedRegularFile(flags['provider-version'], {
        label: 'gcloud provider version',
        maxBytes: 64 * 1024,
      }),
    ])
  const providerVersionValue = z.object({
    'Google Cloud SDK': z.string().regex(/^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$/),
  }).passthrough().parse(JSON.parse(providerVersionText))
  const result = characterizeContractLogs({
    acceptedSha: flags['accepted-sha'],
    providerVersion:
      `Google Cloud SDK ${providerVersionValue['Google Cloud SDK']}`,
    successLog,
    failureLog,
    committedFixture: JSON.parse(committedText),
    allowShapeDrift: command === 'update',
  })
  const sanitizedText = `${JSON.stringify(result.sanitized, null, 2)}\n`
  await Promise.all([
    writeFile(flags.output, sanitizedText, { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 }),
    writeFile(
      flags['replay-output'],
      `${JSON.stringify({ schemaVersion: 1, replayed: [result.replay] })}\n`,
      { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 },
    ),
  ])
  if (command === 'update') {
    const committed = structuredClone(
      result.sanitized,
    ) as { characterization: { acceptedSha: string } }
    committed.characterization.acceptedSha = 'a'.repeat(40)
    await writeFile(
      flags.committed,
      `${JSON.stringify(committed, null, 2)}\n`,
      { mode: 0o600 },
    )
  }
  return 0
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  characterizeContractsMain(process.argv.slice(2)).then(
    (code) => { process.exitCode = code },
    (error) => {
      process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.stack : String(error)}\n`)
      process.exitCode = 1
    },
  )
}
```

`update` is not part of accepted evidence. It is the deterministic

### 21.1 Push and prove exact provenance

```bash
set -euo pipefail
GATE_RECORD=/home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/.the-usual-logs/kata-sbnj/gate-a.env
test -r "$GATE_RECORD"
# shellcheck disable=SC1090
source "$GATE_RECORD"
REPO_ROOT="$REPLACEMENT_ROOT"
test "$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" rev-parse --show-toplevel)" = "$REPO_ROOT"
test "$REPO_ROOT" != /home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/kata-sbnj
BRANCH="$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" symbolic-ref --quiet --short HEAD)"
test "$BRANCH" = "$REPLACEMENT_BRANCH"
test "$BRANCH" != the-usual/kata-sbnj
test "$BRANCH" != the-usual/cloud-run-jobs
git -C "$REPO_ROOT" merge-base --is-ancestor "$GATE_A_BASE_SHA" HEAD
EVIDENCE_BASE=/home/dan/code/freshell/.worktrees/.the-usual-logs/kata-sbnj/acceptance
mkdir -p "$EVIDENCE_BASE"
for key in \
  FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT \
  FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT \
  FRESHELL_GCP_IAM_ANALYSIS_SCOPE \
  FRESHELL_GCP_SOURCE_BUCKET \
  FRESHELL_GCP_REPO \
  FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT \
  FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT
do
  value="${!key-}"
  test -n "$value" || { printf 'missing %s\n' "$key" >&2; exit 1; }
  export "$key"
done

test -z "$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" status --short)"
git -C "$REPO_ROOT" push --set-upstream origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$BRANCH"
ACCEPTED_SHA="$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" rev-parse HEAD)"
EVIDENCE="$EVIDENCE_BASE/${ACCEPTED_SHA}-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)-$$"
mkdir -m 700 "$EVIDENCE"
export REPO_ROOT ACCEPTED_SHA EVIDENCE

prove_pushed() {
  local worktree="$1" branch remote merge
  local -a REMOTES MERGES ROWS
  test -z "$(git -C "$worktree" status --short)"
  branch="$(git -C "$worktree" symbolic-ref --quiet --short HEAD)"
  test -n "$branch"
  mapfile -d '' -t REMOTES < <(
    git -C "$worktree" config --null --get-all "branch.$branch.remote"
  )
  mapfile -d '' -t MERGES < <(
    git -C "$worktree" config --null --get-all "branch.$branch.merge"
  )
  test "${#REMOTES[@]}" -eq 1
  test "${#MERGES[@]}" -eq 1
  remote="${REMOTES[0]}"
  merge="${MERGES[0]}"
  mapfile -t ROWS < <(
    git -C "$worktree" ls-remote --refs --exit-code "$remote" "$merge"
  )
  test "${#ROWS[@]}" -eq 1
  test "${ROWS[0]%%$'\t'*}" = "$(git -C "$worktree" rev-parse HEAD)"
}

prove_pushed "$REPO_ROOT"
```

### 21.3a Interrupt one exact active Build and prove owned cancellation

Launch only the accepted pushed build-only public main. The acceptance-only
`gcloud` shim records structured argv without changing provider output or retry
behavior. The trap targets only the exact child PID created here:

```bash
INTERRUPT_LOG="$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt.log"
INTERRUPT_ARGV="$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-gcloud.jsonl"
INTERRUPT_BIN="$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-bin"
INTERRUPT_PID=''
mkdir -m 700 "$INTERRUPT_BIN"
REAL_GCLOUD="$(command -v gcloud)"
test -x "$REAL_GCLOUD"
cleanup_interrupt_probe() {
  local primary=$? cleanup=0
  trap - EXIT INT TERM
  if [[ -n "$INTERRUPT_PID" ]] && kill -0 "$INTERRUPT_PID" 2>/dev/null; then
    kill -TERM "$INTERRUPT_PID" || cleanup=$?
    set +e
    wait "$INTERRUPT_PID"
    set -e
  fi
  rm -rf -- "$INTERRUPT_BIN" || cleanup=$?
  if (( primary != 0 )); then exit "$primary"; fi
  exit "$cleanup"
}
trap cleanup_interrupt_probe EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM
cat >"$INTERRUPT_BIN/gcloud" <<'SH'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
node - "$FRESHELL_GCLOUD_ARGV_LOG" "$@" <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs')
fs.appendFileSync(
  process.argv[2],
  `${JSON.stringify(process.argv.slice(3))}\n`,
  { encoding: 'utf8', mode: 0o600 },
)
NODE
exec "$FRESHELL_REAL_GCLOUD" "$@"
SH
chmod 0700 "$INTERRUPT_BIN/gcloud"
(
  cd "$REPO_ROOT"
  export PATH="$INTERRUPT_BIN:$PATH"
  export FRESHELL_REAL_GCLOUD="$REAL_GCLOUD"
  export FRESHELL_GCLOUD_ARGV_LOG="$INTERRUPT_ARGV"
  exec node --import tsx scripts/cloud-run/vitest.ts build
) >"$INTERRUPT_LOG" 2>&1 &
INTERRUPT_PID=$!
timeout 900s bash -c '
  while kill -0 "$1" 2>/dev/null; do
    if grep -F "FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP " "$2" |
       grep -Fq "\"state\":\"active\""; then exit 0; fi
    sleep 1
  done
  exit 1
' _ "$INTERRUPT_PID" "$INTERRUPT_LOG"
kill -TERM "$INTERRUPT_PID"
set +e
wait "$INTERRUPT_PID"
INTERRUPT_STATUS=$?
set -e
test "$INTERRUPT_STATUS" -ne 0
```

Parse only bounded regular files. The parser binds the unique active event,
terminal receipt, exact cancel argv, cleanup ordering, and absence of any
digest/version deletion before producing the retained summary:

```bash
node - "$INTERRUPT_LOG" "$INTERRUPT_ARGV" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt.json" "$ACCEPTED_SHA" <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs')
const [logPath, argvPath, outputPath, acceptedSha] = process.argv.slice(2)
function boundedLines(path, maxBytes, maxLines, maxLineBytes) {
  const stat = fs.lstatSync(path)
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size < 1 || stat.size > maxBytes) {
    throw new Error(`${path}: invalid bounded regular file`)
  }
  const lines = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split('\n').filter(Boolean)
  if (lines.length > maxLines ||
      lines.some((line) => Buffer.byteLength(line) > maxLineBytes)) {
    throw new Error(`${path}: line bounds exceeded`)
  }
  return lines
}
const logLines = boundedLines(logPath, 8 * 1024 * 1024, 20_000, 64 * 1024)
const argvLines = boundedLines(argvPath, 2 * 1024 * 1024, 5_000, 16 * 1024)
const ownershipPrefix = 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_OWNERSHIP '
const receiptPrefix = 'FRESHELL_CLOUD_RUN '
const ownership = logLines.filter((line) => line.startsWith(ownershipPrefix))
  .map((line) => JSON.parse(line.slice(ownershipPrefix.length)))
const receipts = logLines.filter((line) => line.startsWith(receiptPrefix))
  .map((line) => JSON.parse(line.slice(receiptPrefix.length)))
const active = ownership.filter(({ value }) => value?.state === 'active' &&
  typeof value.id === 'string')
if (active.length !== 1 || receipts.length !== 1) {
  throw new Error('expected exactly one active Build event and terminal receipt')
}
const receipt = receipts[0]
if (receipt.schemaVersion !== 1 || receipt.status !== 'failed' ||
    receipt.head !== acceptedSha || receipt.build?.id !== active[0].value.id ||
    receipt.build?.state !== 'terminal' ||
    ['PENDING', 'QUEUED', 'WORKING'].includes(receipt.build?.status) ||
    !receipt.failure?.causes?.some((value) => /SIGTERM|interrupt|aborted/i.test(value))) {
  throw new Error('interruption receipt is not exact failed terminal ownership')
}
const argv = argvLines.map((line) => JSON.parse(line))
if (argv.some((value) => !Array.isArray(value) ||
    value.some((item) => typeof item !== 'string'))) {
  throw new Error('invalid gcloud argv row')
}
const sameArgv = (left, right) =>
  JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right)
const expectedCancelArgv = [
  'builds', 'cancel', receipt.build.id,
  '--region=global',
  `--account=${receipt.account}`,
  `--project=${receipt.projectId}`,
  '--format=json',
]
const cancelIndices = argv.flatMap((value, index) =>
  value[0] === 'builds' && value[1] === 'cancel' ? [index] : [])
if (cancelIndices.length !== 1 ||
    !sameArgv(argv[cancelIndices[0]], expectedCancelArgv)) {
  throw new Error('expected one exact full Build cancel argv')
}
const cancelIndex = cancelIndices[0]
if (argv.some((value) => value[0] === 'artifacts' &&
    (value.includes('versions') || value.includes('docker')) &&
    value.includes('delete') && !value.includes('tags'))) {
  throw new Error('digest/version deletion is forbidden')
}
const expectedReadArgv = [
  'builds', 'list', '--region=global',
  `--filter=id="${receipt.build.id}"`,
  '--limit=2',
  `--account=${receipt.account}`,
  `--project=${receipt.projectId}`,
  '--format=json',
]
const expectedSourceDeleteArgv = [
  'storage', 'rm',
  `gs://${receipt.source.bucket}/${receipt.source.object}#${receipt.source.generation}`,
  `--if-generation-match=${receipt.source.generation}`,
  `--account=${receipt.account}`,
  `--project=${receipt.projectId}`,
]
const sourceDeleteIndices = argv.flatMap((value, index) =>
  value[0] === 'storage' && value[1] === 'rm' ? [index] : [])
const sourceDeleteIndex = sourceDeleteIndices[0] ?? -1
const terminalReadIndex = argv.findLastIndex((value, index) =>
  index > cancelIndex &&
  index < sourceDeleteIndex &&
  sameArgv(value, expectedReadArgv))
if (!(cancelIndex < terminalReadIndex &&
      terminalReadIndex < sourceDeleteIndex) ||
    sourceDeleteIndices.length !== 1 ||
    !sameArgv(argv[sourceDeleteIndex], expectedSourceDeleteArgv)) {
  throw new Error(
    'exact order must be cancel < post-cancel terminal read < source delete',
  )
}
const sourceCleanup = receipt.cleanup.filter(({ kind }) => kind === 'source')
const tagCleanup = receipt.cleanup.filter(({ kind }) => kind === 'tag')
const tagDeleteCommands = argv.filter((value) =>
  value[0] === 'artifacts' &&
  value[1] === 'docker' &&
  value[2] === 'tags' &&
  value[3] === 'delete')
const truthfulNoTagState =
  tagCleanup.length === 0 &&
  receipt.image.state === 'absent-after-terminal-build' &&
  tagDeleteCommands.length === 0
const ordinaryOwnedTagCleanup =
  tagCleanup.length === 1 &&
  receipt.image.state !== 'absent-after-terminal-build' &&
  typeof receipt.image.digest === 'string' &&
  /^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(receipt.image.digest) &&
  ['deleted', 'absent'].includes(tagCleanup[0].outcome)
if (sourceCleanup.length !== 1 || sourceCleanup[0].outcome !== 'deleted' ||
    (!truthfulNoTagState && !ordinaryOwnedTagCleanup) ||
    receipt.residuals.some(({ kind }) => kind === 'build' ||
      kind === 'source' || kind === 'tag') ||
    receipt.temp?.rootRemoved !== true ||
    receipt.temp?.dockerConfigRemoved !== true) {
  throw new Error('owned remote/local cleanup was not complete')
}
const summary = {
  schemaVersion: 1,
  acceptedSha,
  runId: receipt.runId,
  build: {
    id: receipt.build.id,
    resourceName: receipt.build.resourceName,
    projectId: receipt.build.projectId,
    projectNumber: receipt.build.projectNumber,
    finalStatus: receipt.build.status,
  },
  source: {
    bucket: receipt.source.bucket,
    object: receipt.source.object,
    generation: receipt.source.generation,
  },
  tag: {
    taggedImage: receipt.image.taggedImage,
    tag: receipt.image.tag,
    digest: receipt.image.digest,
    imageState: receipt.image.state,
  },
  cleanup: {
    build: receipt.cleanup.filter(({ kind }) => kind === 'build'),
    source: sourceCleanup,
    tag: tagCleanup,
    temp: receipt.temp,
  },
  commandProof: {
    cancelIndex,
    terminalReadIndex,
    sourceDeleteIndex,
    cancelArgv: argv[cancelIndex],
    terminalReadArgv: argv[terminalReadIndex],
    sourceDeleteArgv: argv[sourceDeleteIndex],
  },
}
fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, `${JSON.stringify(summary, null, 2)}\n`, {
  encoding: 'utf8',
  mode: 0o600,
})
NODE
```

Extract identities from that bounded summary and exact-read the same provider
resources. Every list is capped at two rows; a retained/unreadable source or tag
fails rather than being accepted as cleanup evidence:

```bash
mapfile -t INTERRUPT_IDS < <(node - "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt.json" <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs')
const value = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2], 'utf8'))
for (const item of [
  value.build.id,
  value.source.bucket,
  value.source.object,
  value.source.generation,
  value.tag.taggedImage,
  value.tag.tag,
]) {
  if (typeof item !== 'string' || item.length === 0 || item.includes('\n')) {
    throw new Error('invalid interrupt identity')
  }
  process.stdout.write(`${item}\n`)
}
NODE
)
test "${#INTERRUPT_IDS[@]}" -eq 6
INTERRUPT_BUILD_ID="${INTERRUPT_IDS[0]}"
INTERRUPT_SOURCE_BUCKET="${INTERRUPT_IDS[1]}"
INTERRUPT_SOURCE_OBJECT="${INTERRUPT_IDS[2]}"
INTERRUPT_SOURCE_GENERATION="${INTERRUPT_IDS[3]}"
INTERRUPT_TAGGED_IMAGE="${INTERRUPT_IDS[4]}"
INTERRUPT_TAG="${INTERRUPT_IDS[5]}"
INTERRUPT_IMAGE_PATH="${INTERRUPT_TAGGED_IMAGE%:*}"
INTERRUPT_TAG_RESOURCE="projects/$FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT/locations/"\
"${FRESHELL_GCP_REGION:-us-west1}/repositories/$FRESHELL_GCP_REPO/"\
"packages/freshell-vitest/tags/$INTERRUPT_TAG"

gcloud builds list --region=global \
  --filter="id=\"$INTERRUPT_BUILD_ID\"" --limit=2 \
  --account="$FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT" --project="$FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT" \
  --format=json >"$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-build.json"
gcloud storage objects list "gs://$INTERRUPT_SOURCE_BUCKET" \
  --filter="name=\"$INTERRUPT_SOURCE_OBJECT\"" --limit=2 \
  --account="$FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT" --project="$FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT" \
  --format=json >"$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-source.json"
gcloud artifacts docker tags list "$INTERRUPT_IMAGE_PATH" \
  --filter="tag=\"$INTERRUPT_TAG_RESOURCE\"" --limit=2 \
  --account="$FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT" --project="$FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT" \
  --format=json >"$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-tag.json"
node - \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt.json" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-build.json" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-source.json" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-tag.json" <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs')
const [summaryPath, buildPath, sourcePath, tagPath] = process.argv.slice(2)
function read(path, maxBytes) {
  const stat = fs.lstatSync(path)
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size > maxBytes) throw new Error(`${path}: invalid`)
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8'))
}
const summary = read(summaryPath, 128 * 1024)
const builds = read(buildPath, 2 * 1024 * 1024)
const sources = read(sourcePath, 512 * 1024)
const tags = read(tagPath, 512 * 1024)
if (!Array.isArray(builds) || builds.length !== 1 ||
    builds[0].id !== summary.build.id ||
    ['PENDING', 'QUEUED', 'WORKING'].includes(builds[0].status)) {
  throw new Error('exact interrupted Build is not terminal')
}
if (!Array.isArray(sources) || sources.some((row) =>
    row.bucket === summary.source.bucket &&
    row.name === summary.source.object &&
    String(row.generation) === summary.source.generation)) {
  throw new Error('exact interrupted source generation remains')
}
if (!Array.isArray(tags) || tags.length !== 0) {
  throw new Error('exact interrupted invocation tag remains')
}
NODE

rm -rf -- "$INTERRUPT_BIN"
INTERRUPT_PID=''
trap - EXIT INT TERM
export INTERRUPT_BUILD_ID INTERRUPT_SOURCE_BUCKET INTERRUPT_SOURCE_OBJECT \
  INTERRUPT_SOURCE_GENERATION INTERRUPT_TAGGED_IMAGE INTERRUPT_TAG
```

The retained `build-interrupt.log`, structured argv, summary, and bounded
provider reads prove primary interruption plus cleanup failures are distinct,
the same Build ID was cancelled once with the complete exact argv and observed
through a complete exact-ID read after cancel and before the exact source
delete, the source generation/tag are absent, no digest was deleted, and
temp/Docker state is absent. A cancelled Build that never produced full image
ownership truthfully records zero tag cleanup dispositions plus
`image.state: 'absent-after-terminal-build'` and is accepted only when the raw
argv contains zero tag-delete commands; acceptance also permits the
ordinary one-row `absent`/`deleted` tag cleanup only when image ownership was
actually acquired. It never manufactures a cleanup disposition.

### 21.4 Two simultaneous pushed-worktree `npm test` runs

```bash
COMMON="$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir)"
CHECKOUT_ROOT="$(dirname "$COMMON")"
ACCEPTANCE_RUN_ID="${ACCEPTED_SHA:0:12}-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)-$$-$(node -e \
  'process.stdout.write(require("node:crypto").randomBytes(4).toString("hex"))')"
A="$CHECKOUT_ROOT/.worktrees/kata-sbnj-cloud-$ACCEPTANCE_RUN_ID-a"
B="$CHECKOUT_ROOT/.worktrees/kata-sbnj-cloud-$ACCEPTANCE_RUN_ID-b"
BRANCH_A="the-usual/kata-sbnj-cloud-$ACCEPTANCE_RUN_ID-a"
BRANCH_B="the-usual/kata-sbnj-cloud-$ACCEPTANCE_RUN_ID-b"

A_WORKTREE_MAY_EXIST=0
B_WORKTREE_MAY_EXIST=0
A_LOCAL_REF_MAY_EXIST=0
B_LOCAL_REF_MAY_EXIST=0
A_REMOTE_REF_MAY_EXIST=0
B_REMOTE_REF_MAY_EXIST=0
CLEANUP_DONE=0

cleanup_acceptance_resources() {
  local failed=0 side path branch path_name branch_name
  local worktree_flag local_flag remote_flag
  local root live_branch head tracked sha remote_output row
  local -a rows

  for side in A B; do
    path_name="$side"
    branch_name="BRANCH_$side"
    worktree_flag="${side}_WORKTREE_MAY_EXIST"
    local_flag="${side}_LOCAL_REF_MAY_EXIST"
    remote_flag="${side}_REMOTE_REF_MAY_EXIST"
    path="${!path_name}"
    branch="${!branch_name}"

    if (( ${!worktree_flag} )) && [[ -e "$path" ]]; then
      root=''; live_branch=''; head=''; tracked=''
      if root="$(git -C "$path" rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null)" &&
         live_branch="$(git -C "$path" symbolic-ref --quiet --short HEAD 2>/dev/null)" &&
         head="$(git -C "$path" rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null)" &&
         tracked="$(git -C "$path" status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=no 2>/dev/null)" &&
         [[ "$root" = "$path" && "$live_branch" = "$branch" &&
            "$head" = "$ACCEPTED_SHA" && -z "$tracked" ]]; then
        if ! git -C "$REPO_ROOT" worktree remove "$path"; then
          printf 'cleanup failed removing owned worktree %s\n' "$path" >&2
          failed=1
        fi
      else
        printf 'refusing to remove non-owned or changed worktree %s\n' "$path" >&2
        failed=1
      fi
    fi

    if (( ${!local_flag} )); then
      if sha="$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" show-ref --verify --hash \
          "refs/heads/$branch" 2>/dev/null)"; then
        if [[ "$sha" = "$ACCEPTED_SHA" && ! -e "$path" ]]; then
          if ! git -C "$REPO_ROOT" branch -D "$branch"; then
            printf 'cleanup failed deleting owned local ref %s\n' "$branch" >&2
            failed=1
          fi
        else
          printf 'refusing to delete non-owned or checked-out local ref %s\n' \
            "$branch" >&2
          failed=1
        fi
      fi
    fi

    if (( ${!remote_flag} )); then
      rows=()
      if remote_output="$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" ls-remote --refs origin \
          "refs/heads/$branch")"; then
        while IFS= read -r row; do
          [[ -n "$row" ]] && rows+=("$row")
        done <<<"$remote_output"
        if (( ${#rows[@]} == 1 )) &&
           [[ "${rows[0]%%$'\t'*}" = "$ACCEPTED_SHA" ]]; then
          if ! git -C "$REPO_ROOT" push origin --delete "$branch"; then
            printf 'cleanup failed deleting owned remote ref %s\n' "$branch" >&2
            failed=1
          fi
        elif (( ${#rows[@]} != 0 )); then
          printf 'refusing to delete non-owned remote ref %s\n' "$branch" >&2
          failed=1
        fi
      else
        printf 'cleanup failed reading remote ref %s\n' "$branch" >&2
        failed=1
      fi
    fi
  done
  return "$failed"
}

on_acceptance_exit() {
  local primary=$? cleanup=0
  trap - EXIT INT TERM
  if (( ! CLEANUP_DONE )); then
    cleanup_acceptance_resources || cleanup=$?
  fi
  if (( cleanup != 0 )); then
    printf 'acceptance cleanup failed with status %s\n' "$cleanup" >&2
  fi
  if (( primary != 0 )); then
    exit "$primary"
  fi
  exit "$cleanup"
}

trap on_acceptance_exit EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM

for side in A B; do
  path_name="$side"
  branch_name="BRANCH_$side"
  path="${!path_name}"
  branch="${!branch_name}"
  test ! -e "$path"
  ! git -C "$REPO_ROOT" show-ref --verify --quiet "refs/heads/$branch"
  test -z "$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" ls-remote --refs origin \
    "refs/heads/$branch")"
done

A_WORKTREE_MAY_EXIST=1
A_LOCAL_REF_MAY_EXIST=1
git -C "$REPO_ROOT" worktree add -b "$BRANCH_A" "$A" "$ACCEPTED_SHA"
B_WORKTREE_MAY_EXIST=1
B_LOCAL_REF_MAY_EXIST=1
git -C "$REPO_ROOT" worktree add -b "$BRANCH_B" "$B" "$ACCEPTED_SHA"
A_REMOTE_REF_MAY_EXIST=1
git -C "$A" push --set-upstream origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$BRANCH_A"
B_REMOTE_REF_MAY_EXIST=1
git -C "$B" push --set-upstream origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$BRANCH_B"
prove_pushed "$A"
prove_pushed "$B"
npm --prefix "$A" ci
npm --prefix "$B" ci
prove_pushed "$A"
prove_pushed "$B"

(
  cd "$A"
  FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=cloud \
    trace_public coordinator-test-a npm test
) & PID_A=$!
(
  cd "$B"
  FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=cloud \
    trace_public coordinator-test-b npm test
) & PID_B=$!
set +e
wait "$PID_A"; STATUS_A=$?
wait "$PID_B"; STATUS_B=$?
set -e
test "$STATUS_A" -eq 0
test "$STATUS_B" -eq 0
prove_pushed "$A"
prove_pushed "$B"
inspect_receipt coordinator-test-a
inspect_receipt coordinator-test-b
```

Both clean symbolic branches must independently pass the same cardinality/live
equality proof as 21.1. Require overlapping active global Build intervals and
overlapping Execution intervals, exact all-suite parts, no local heavy child,
and distinct run/temp/source generation/tag/Build/Job/Execution identities.

### 21.5 Trace the three previously missing coordinator public commands

Run and inspect each exact retained public command independently; do not infer
one from another:

```bash
(
  cd "$REPO_ROOT"
  FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=cloud \
    trace_public coordinator-check npm run check
)
inspect_receipt coordinator-check
(
  cd "$REPO_ROOT"
  FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=cloud \
    trace_public coordinator-verify npm run verify
)
inspect_receipt coordinator-verify
(
  cd "$REPO_ROOT"
  FRESHELL_VITEST_BACKEND=cloud \
    trace_public coordinator-test-all npm run test:all
)
inspect_receipt coordinator-test-all
```

Each must exit 0 and emit exactly one route receipt plus one terminal lifecycle
receipt. `coordinator-check` must show completed `typecheck` before first cloud
mutation; `coordinator-verify` must show `typecheck`, then `build`, before first
cloud mutation; `coordinator-test-all` must show no pre-phase. Every route says
`coordinatorEffects:false`; every independent trace is bound to its exact npm
argv and has no holder/wait selector, local Vitest/Electron/test-balanced child,
or banned cloud command. Each lifecycle receipt independently validates exact
SHA/source/Build/image/Job/Execution/parts/cleanup/digest retention.

and Build-SA token mint, push only a unique tag, resolve its digest, create no

parts plus one bound with no list/latest.

### 21.7 Validate one exact manifest

Generate the complete closed manifest deterministically. Every retained command
kind, exact argv, trace/log/receipt path, expected route/pre-phase, accepted SHA,
worker identity, expected part set, and provider-state path is literal here:

```bash
node <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs')
const path = require('node:path')
const env = process.env
for (const key of [
  'EVIDENCE', 'REPO_ROOT', 'ACCEPTED_SHA',
  'FRESHELL_GCP_BUILD_SERVICE_ACCOUNT',
  'FRESHELL_GCP_RUNTIME_SERVICE_ACCOUNT',
  ...['VITEST', 'E2E'].flatMap((prefix) => [
    'RUN_ID', 'HEAD', 'SOURCE_BUCKET', 'SOURCE_OBJECT', 'SOURCE_GENERATION',
    'BUILD_CORRELATION', 'BUILD_ID', 'BUILD_RESOURCE', 'BUILD_PROJECT_ID',
    'BUILD_PROJECT_NUMBER', 'IMAGE_TAG', 'DIGEST', 'JOB', 'JOB_RESOURCE',
    'JOB_UID', 'JOB_GENERATION', 'EXECUTION', 'EXECUTION_RESOURCE', 'EXECUTION_UID',
  ].map((suffix) => `${prefix}_${suffix}`)),
]) {
  if (!env[key]) throw new Error(`missing generator environment ${key}`)
}
const evidence = env.EVIDENCE
const acceptedSha = env.ACCEPTED_SHA
const p = (name) => path.join(evidence, name)
const resources = (prefix) => ({

  sourceBucket: env[`${prefix}_SOURCE_BUCKET`],

  buildProjectNumber: env[`${prefix}_BUILD_PROJECT_NUMBER`],

  digest: env[`${prefix}_DIGEST`],
  job: env[`${prefix}_JOB`],
  jobResourceName: env[`${prefix}_JOB_RESOURCE`],
  jobUid: env[`${prefix}_JOB_UID`],
  jobGeneration: env[`${prefix}_JOB_GENERATION`],
  execution: env[`${prefix}_EXECUTION`],
  executionResourceName: env[`${prefix}_EXECUTION_RESOURCE`],
  executionUid: env[`${prefix}_EXECUTION_UID`],
})
const vitestResources = resources('VITEST')
const e2eResources = resources('E2E')
const artifact = ({
  id, command, argv, log = `${id}.log`, receipt = log,
  trace = `${id}.trace`, state = `${id}.provider-state.json`,
  origin, status = 'succeeded', kind = 'vitest', buildMode = 'cloud',
  pre = [], resources,
}) => ({
  id,
  command,
  expectedArgv: argv,
  commandLogPath: p(log),
  receiptLogPath: p(receipt),
  tracePrefix: p(trace),
  providerStatePath: p(state),
  expectedOrigin: origin,
  expectedStatus: status,
  expectedKind: kind,
  expectedBuildMode: buildMode,
  ...(resources === undefined ? {} : { expectedResources: resources }),
  expectedPrePhases: pre,
  acceptedSha,
})
const artifacts = [
  artifact({
    id: 'coordinator-test-a', command: 'coordinator-test',
    argv: ['npm', 'test'], origin: 'coordinator-test',
  }),
  artifact({
    id: 'coordinator-test-b', command: 'coordinator-test',
    argv: ['npm', 'test'], origin: 'coordinator-test',
  }),
  artifact({

    argv: ['npm', 'run', 'check'], origin: 'coordinator-check',

  }),

    ],

  }),
  artifact({
    id: 'vitest-build', command: 'vitest-build', origin: 'vitest-build',
    argv: ['npm', 'run', 'test:cloud:build'],
  }),
  artifact({
    id: 'vitest-push', command: 'vitest-push', origin: 'vitest-push',
    argv: ['bash', 'scripts/vitest-cloud.sh', 'push'],
    buildMode: 'local',
  }),
  artifact({
    id: 'vitest-logs', command: 'vitest-logs', origin: 'vitest-run',
    argv: [
      'bash', 'scripts/vitest-cloud.sh', 'logs',
      `--job=${env.VITEST_JOB}`, `--execution=${env.VITEST_EXECUTION}`,
      '--config=server', '--shards=1',
    ],
    receipt: 'vitest-run.log',
    state: 'vitest-run.provider-state.json',
    resources: vitestResources,
  }),
  artifact({
    id: 'e2e-run', command: 'e2e-run', origin: 'e2e-run',
    argv: [
      'npm', 'run', 'test:e2e:cloud', '--', '--project=chromium',
      'test/e2e-browser/specs/auth.spec.ts', '--reporter=line',
    ],
    kind: 'e2e', resources: e2eResources,
  }),
  artifact({
    id: 'e2e-build', command: 'e2e-build', origin: 'e2e-build',
    argv: ['npm', 'run', 'test:e2e:cloud:build'],
    kind: 'e2e',
  }),
  artifact({

# Prove the untouched generated manifest before producing a negative fixture.
npm --prefix "$REPO_ROOT" run cloud:verify-acceptance -- \
  validate --manifest="$EVIDENCE/manifest.json"

# Prove the same bounded validator CLI fails closed for a missing exact public command.

  fixture-remove-command \

  --input="$EVIDENCE/manifest.json" \

npm --prefix "$REPO_ROOT" run cloud:verify-acceptance -- \

EXPECTED_FAILURE_STATUS=$?

npm --prefix "$REPO_ROOT" run cloud:verify-acceptance -- \
```

Expected: `acceptance manifest verified`. It must machine-prove one receipt per
run, exact origin/SHA/parts/self-attribution, all four exact coordinator public
commands, pre-phase order, no coordinator/heavy-local/banned command, distinct

Job/temp/Docker/local-image absence, digest presence, exact failed-run cleanup,
and exact logs.

### 21.8 Freshness, temporary-worktree cleanup, and stop

```bash
test "$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$ACCEPTED_SHA"
prove_pushed "$REPO_ROOT"
prove_pushed "$A"
prove_pushed "$B"
npm --prefix "$REPO_ROOT" run cloud:verify-acceptance -- \
  validate --manifest="$EVIDENCE/manifest.json"
node - \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt.json" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-gcloud.jsonl" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-build.json" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-source.json" \
  "$EVIDENCE/build-interrupt-tag.json" \
  "$ACCEPTED_SHA" <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs')
const [summaryPath, argvPath, buildPath, sourcePath, tagPath, acceptedSha] =
  process.argv.slice(2)
for (const [path, cap] of [
  [summaryPath, 128 * 1024],
  [argvPath, 2 * 1024 * 1024],
  [buildPath, 2 * 1024 * 1024],
  [sourcePath, 512 * 1024],
  [tagPath, 512 * 1024],
]) {
  const stat = fs.lstatSync(path)
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size < 1 || stat.size > cap) {
    throw new Error(`invalid interruption artifact ${path}`)
  }
}
const summary = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(summaryPath, 'utf8'))
if (summary.schemaVersion !== 1 || summary.acceptedSha !== acceptedSha ||
    summary.build.id !== process.env.INTERRUPT_BUILD_ID ||
    summary.source.bucket !== process.env.INTERRUPT_SOURCE_BUCKET ||
    summary.source.object !== process.env.INTERRUPT_SOURCE_OBJECT ||
    summary.source.generation !== process.env.INTERRUPT_SOURCE_GENERATION ||
    summary.tag.taggedImage !== process.env.INTERRUPT_TAGGED_IMAGE ||
    summary.tag.tag !== process.env.INTERRUPT_TAG ||
    typeof summary.tag.imageState !== 'string' ||
    summary.tag.imageState.length === 0) {
  throw new Error('interruption artifact freshness/identity mismatch')
}
const argv = fs.readFileSync(argvPath, 'utf8').trim().split('\n').map(JSON.parse)
const sameArgv = (left, right) =>
  JSON.stringify(left) === JSON.stringify(right)
const expectedCancelArgv = [
  'builds', 'cancel', summary.build.id,
  '--region=global',
  `--account=${process.env.FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT}`,
  `--project=${process.env.FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT}`,
  '--format=json',
]
const expectedReadArgv = [
  'builds', 'list', '--region=global',
  `--filter=id="${summary.build.id}"`,
  '--limit=2',
  `--account=${process.env.FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT}`,
  `--project=${process.env.FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT}`,
  '--format=json',
]
const expectedSourceDeleteArgv = [
  'storage', 'rm',
  `gs://${summary.source.bucket}/${summary.source.object}#${summary.source.generation}`,
  `--if-generation-match=${summary.source.generation}`,
  `--account=${process.env.FRESHELL_GCP_ACCOUNT}`,
  `--project=${process.env.FRESHELL_GCP_PROJECT}`,
]
const proof = summary.commandProof
if (!Number.isInteger(proof.cancelIndex) ||
    !Number.isInteger(proof.terminalReadIndex) ||
    !Number.isInteger(proof.sourceDeleteIndex) ||
    !(proof.cancelIndex < proof.terminalReadIndex &&
      proof.terminalReadIndex < proof.sourceDeleteIndex) ||
    !sameArgv(argv[proof.cancelIndex], expectedCancelArgv) ||
    !sameArgv(argv[proof.terminalReadIndex], expectedReadArgv) ||
    !sameArgv(argv[proof.sourceDeleteIndex], expectedSourceDeleteArgv) ||
    !sameArgv(proof.cancelArgv, expectedCancelArgv) ||
    !sameArgv(proof.terminalReadArgv, expectedReadArgv) ||
    !sameArgv(proof.sourceDeleteArgv, expectedSourceDeleteArgv) ||
    argv.filter((value) => value[0] === 'builds' &&
      value[1] === 'cancel').length !== 1 ||
    argv.filter((value) => value[0] === 'storage' &&
      value[1] === 'rm').length !== 1 ||
    argv.some((value) => value[0] === 'artifacts' &&
      value.includes('delete') && !value.includes('tags'))) {
  throw new Error('interruption exact argv/order/digest invariant failed')
}
const tagCleanup = summary.cleanup.tag
const tagDeleteCommands = argv.filter((value) =>
  value[0] === 'artifacts' &&
  value[1] === 'docker' &&
  value[2] === 'tags' &&
  value[3] === 'delete')
const truthfulNoTagState =
  summary.tag.imageState === 'absent-after-terminal-build' &&
  tagCleanup.length === 0 &&
  tagDeleteCommands.length === 0
const ordinaryOwnedTagCleanup =
  tagCleanup.length === 1 &&
  summary.tag.imageState !== 'absent-after-terminal-build' &&
  typeof summary.tag.digest === 'string' &&
  /^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(summary.tag.digest) &&
  ['deleted', 'absent'].includes(tagCleanup[0].outcome)
if (!truthfulNoTagState && !ordinaryOwnedTagCleanup) {
  throw new Error('interruption tag receipt state is inconsistent')
}
const builds = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(buildPath, 'utf8'))
const sources = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(sourcePath, 'utf8'))
const tags = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(tagPath, 'utf8'))
if (builds.length !== 1 || builds[0].id !== summary.build.id ||
    ['PENDING', 'QUEUED', 'WORKING'].includes(builds[0].status) ||
    sources.some((row) => row.bucket === summary.source.bucket &&
      row.name === summary.source.object &&
      String(row.generation) === summary.source.generation) ||
    tags.length !== 0) {
  throw new Error('interruption provider state is not terminal/absent')
}
NODE
cleanup_acceptance_resources
CLEANUP_DONE=1
trap - EXIT INT TERM
test "$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$ACCEPTED_SHA"
test -z "$(git -C "$REPO_ROOT" status --short)"
```

Expected: temporary acceptance worktrees/branches are gone; feature branch and
evidence remain. Do not create a PR, restart/deploy the live server, or begin
another child task.

## Step 1 Self-Review (Final-Blocker Rerun)

prerequisites are Gate A's correctly ordered explicit-local baseline/replacement
worktree and Gate B's separately authorized IAM provisioning. Ordinary RED,
provider characterization differences, and acceptance falsifications return to
the owning task.

FE1–FE16 are covered without a new subsystem. Gate A closes FE13 with the
complete NUL-delimited inventory/archive/restore prefix plus the exact hashed
five-case executable harness: process-group INT/TERM, status/tar equality,
collision retention, the path/type matrix, and zero replacement creation. Task 9
closes FE14 by aligning producer/type/value and by mutation-testing missing and
malformed copies of the real emitted record at the focused schema. Tasks
1/5/6/7/9/10/11 close FE15 with one converter, pre-work guards, exact provider
comparison, a consumed comparator call whose only timeout change is caller to
immutable intent, malformed legacy E2E zero-work rejection, digit-only
Playwright dispatch plus consumed temporary/final production help, and an exact
contextual edit to the consumed nested selected-Job timeout; private receipt
`300s` remains structural by construction. Tasks 6/11 close FE16 through an
uncommitted base cleanup GREEN and complete consumed `checkedCommand` calls
whose argv differ only by one `--quiet` token. Existing FE1–FE12 coverage
remains intact. No FE is deferred.

The iteration-2 remediation review blockers B1–B5 and integration blockers
B1–B3 are closed at their owning seams: cleanup reconstructs and compares the
current provider row before cancel; a drifted full-authority row fails with no
cancel or Storage call; every identity/config/terminal-direction/terminal-state
rejection preserves the original ledger; exact AggregateError children are
ordered; Task 7 allows the legitimate pre-Build Storage read and inspects only
post-cancel calls; and 21.3a accepts the truthful
`absent-after-terminal-build`/zero-tag-disposition state while still requiring
provider tag absence and no digest deletion. FE9's literal missing-field rows
now match its strict schema and GREEN claim.

The final test-coverage recheck blockers B1–B2 are also closed. The
drift-before-cancel negative uses 50ms Build/command budgets, a 1,000ms explicit
guard, and a runner that returns the drifted row once then rejects every
forbidden follow-up command; GREEN must return promptly with the intended drift
message, one exact read, no cancel, and no Storage. The deterministic cancelled
Task 7 row exact-checks `absent-after-terminal-build`, no digest/version/image

Both live acceptance parsers additionally require zero tag-delete commands
when selecting the truthful no-tag alternative, while retaining the separate

The final re-audit blockers map concretely to Task 7's precise

### 1b. No silent deferrals

The production-proof and double-bridge tables name an observable real outcome
for every user-facing requirement. In particular, a real `npm test` is not used
to infer behavior of `npm run check`, `npm run verify`, or
`npm run test:all`; Task 21 invokes and validates each exact command. The fixed
Run child is exercised against real Cloud Run operations, dedicated Artifact
Registry/Storage commands are exercised against those providers, and the same
machine validator used in synthetic RED cases checks all real evidence. Raw
provider-contract proof is not inferred from canonical receipts: Task 21
captures a bounded semantic set of Build/v2 Job/v2 Operation/v1
success+failure Execution and LogEntry events in one invocation-local object,
includes that invocation's run ID on every emitted record, validates actual
receipt identities, sanitizes them deterministically, compares their recursive
shape to the committed fixture, compares stable content directly, and binds
actual logs/receipts/SHA/provider version without a second integrity protocol.

The oversize/growth/trace/prefix and 4,500/2,100-poll cases are controlled
falsifications of final production functions, not substitutes for a real
outcome. Task 21.3 feeds real success/failure provider logs through the same
bounded reader and semantic capture, while 21.7 feeds every real command log,
receipt, trace, fixture, replay, and manifest through the same validator.
Task 7's fake Docker diagnostic is separately bridged by both real local-push
facades in 21.6 and exact local-image/provider-state inspection in 21.7.
Task 11's lifecycle-level pre-abort, concurrent invocation, setup failure,
cleanup failure, and bounded flush tests use the real lifecycle body rather
than calling only the capture object. Task 21.3 exercises that same scoped
object through separate real characterized success/failure public commands;
Task 21.4 independently proves simultaneous real lifecycle isolation.

The raw IAM fixture is not the real-provider substitute: 21.2 runs the exact
organization analyzer/ancestry preflight before mutation. The Artifact Registry
doubles retain `{image,tag,version}` until the production parser; 21.6–21.7
bridge them to live tag absence and retained version state. Public E2E dry-run
rejection is proved at the real facade process boundary with no child launch,
while the retained direct-container diagnostic is intentionally non-authoritative
and normal E2E is bridged by the real image smoke and 21.6 cloud run.

No behavior task commits known-wrong production code for a later task to
replace. Task 6, Task 10, and Task 11 use explicit uncommitted predecessor forms
only inside nested RGR microcycles. Each predecessor and final is shown as the
complete consumed in-function call, production help function, or actual nested
schema—not as a detached declaration. Each prerequisite surface first goes
green, each exact behavior RED is reached, and each predecessor is replaced
before the task's final GREEN/refactor/one commit. Final process/signal,
provenance, IAM/build, fixed Run operations, cleanup/receipt, and lifecycle
dependencies precede their consumers. Every public main is signal-supervised in
its first committed implementation.

### 3. Type and contract consistency

All tasks consistently use `RunCommand` with `settlementTimeoutMs`,
`LifecycleBudgets.settlementMs`, `SourceSnapshot.sourceArchive/sourceDir/
buildTemplatePath/dockerConfigDir`, `buildRegion: 'global'`, `runRegion`,
`CloudInvocationOrigin`, `OwnershipReceipt`, `ExecutionCounters` with all four
keys, `expectedParts.length + 1`, exact Job UID/generation/fresh etag, exact Execution identity,
source generation/size, Build normalized config, image tag/digest resource, and
structured cleanup/residuals. Production lifecycle input has no run ID.
`CloudPreflightReceipt`, its staged `Omit`, focused schema, emitted value, strict
acceptance schema, and effective-IAM equality all carry the same
`iamAnalysisScope`. Public adapters accept documented s/m/h spellings. For the
legacy E2E equals form, digit-only `--timeout=<decimal>` is Playwright
milliseconds and falls through unchanged; every other equals-form value reaches
`canonicalizeTaskTimeout` and either canonicalizes or rejects. Every
`Parsed*Args.taskTimeout`, lifecycle input, Job intent, gcloud create argv,
receipt-inspection input, provider replay context, and exact Job comparison uses
canonical whole seconds. Task 5 owns the only parser/converter; Task 7 and Task
11 retain the same pre-work assertion. Active Execution cleanup uses one exact
`gcloud run jobs executions cancel <name> --quiet ... --format=json` command,
then the existing exact terminal describe, with no stdin policy or async semantic
change. Task 6's nested comparator and cancel predecessor/final pairs are
complete calls at those real consumers; Task 10's pair is the real
`printE2EHelp`; Task 11's pair is the real nested timeout member in the consumed
`selectedJobSchema`. Their surrounding arguments/body/member context is
identical across each pair except for the intended timeout value, `--quiet`
token, or help text.
`CorrelatedBuildIdentity` carries exact active-or-terminal authority/config;
`BuildIdentity` is its terminal refinement. `OwnedResources.build` uses the
former and `BuiltImage.build` uses the latter. The Build branch of
`markMutationPossiblyAccepted` is the sole pre-identity source-risk transition;
`acquireBuildOwnership` alone may install/advance identity and derive terminal
source risk afterward. Static and behavioral tests reject every other write.
Every lifecycle input and terminal receipt carries `iamAnalysisScope`; every
effective IAM producer/consumer uses exactly `analysisScope`, `caller`,
`buildEffectiveGrantKeys`, `runtimeEffectiveGrantKeys`, `actAsResources`, and
`tokenMintResources`. Every complete image uses the shared package/tag/version
constructors and `{image,tag,version}` provider rows. Residuals have only
`kind`, `identity`, and `reason`. Task 21.7 is plain JavaScript and carries all
19 `expectedResources` fields.
Vitest defaults to four tasks; E2E defaults to one; both accept 1–32. The Node
child consumes only private `ClosedRunCommand` values and exposes no URL/verb/
body transport contract. Artifact and Storage operations remain dedicated
structured gcloud argv. `LocalPushMutationIntent.correlationTag` is always the
reserved `InvocationIdentity.buildCorrelationTag`; the local Build ID uses the
`local-build-` prefix and no separate local-only correlation authority remains. Task 11 uses
only `readBoundedRegularFile`/`readBoundedSelectedLines` for acceptance
evidence, with `TraceLimits` and `semanticProviderRecordLimit(expectedParts)`
consumed consistently by capture, characterization, replay, tests, and live
acceptance. `ProviderContractCapture` is created only after the pre-aborted
guard, is passed explicitly through Build/Job/Operation/Execution/log and
cleanup calls, has no module-global active state, and is flushed/released by
one outer lifecycle finalization path. Every provider record carries the same
run ID required by its terminal receipt.
Direct local multi-config Vitest children alone receive
`--passWithNoTests`; cloud parser/container paths retain positive-test-count
enforcement. Multi-task E2E accepts an empty individual shard only after a
successful nonempty global Playwright inventory and has no filesystem fallback.

The focused B1–B5/B1–B3 structural rerun additionally requires exactly two
production `buildMayReadSource` assignments (the sanctioned pre-identity
transition and the ownership-derived transition), one `buildTerminal`
assignment, and no direct test assignment. It verifies immutable comparison
textually precedes cancel, all monotonic cases and FE9 missing fields are
literal, obsolete whole-transcript Storage assertions are absent, deterministic
and live expected argv arrays are complete, and both acceptance passes enforce
ordered stored indices plus the truthful no-tag state.
The final coverage-specific rerun also requires the drift runner's one-read
branch, forbidden-follow-up error, tiny budgets, explicit 1,000ms guard, prompt
elapsed assertion, deterministic Task 7 no-tag field/disposition/argv
assertions, and `tagDeleteCommands.length === 0` in both live truthful branches.

The post-edit structural rerun finds, for every Task 1–11, ordered literal RED
code, a full focused-file RED command and stated behavioral failure, literal
final GREEN bodies, a full focused-file GREEN command and stated pass, literal
refactor code, and exactly one commit. No name filter can silently skip a listed
RED case. All TypeScript fences pass parser diagnostics; semantic compilation
passes for the exact Task 3 interface fence, exact IAM fault-mutator table, and
exact former-command argv map. The implementation plan requires the complete
focused files and `npm run typecheck:scripts` to pass in each owning GREEN; this
self-review does not substitute isolated plan checks for those future cumulative
implementation commands.
The real manifest additionally binds schema-extracted run/source/Build/tag/digest/
Job/Execution identities and exact cleanup multisets for both public log bridges.
It contains no manifest-authoritative task count or expected-part set:

closed command kind, exact argv, and shared canonical suite data.
